### [1] [Aleksandrov-Bakelman-Pucci maximum principle for $L^n$-viscosity solutions of uniformly elliptic equations with unbounded terms: The critical case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03918)
*Andrzej Święch*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为带漂移项的完全非线性一致椭圆方程建立了Aleksandrov-Bakelman-Pucci极大值原理，漂移系数满足 $\\gamma(\\ln(1+\\gamma))^{\\frac{n-1}{n}}\\in L^n(\\Omega)$，证明基于凸对偶与Trudinger-Moser型不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典ABP极大值原理的漂移项通常要求 $\\gamma\\in L^n(\\Omega)$，本文旨在将该可积性条件放宽到对数修正的临界Orlicz型空间，拓展ABP原理的适用范围。

Method: 研究对象为 $\\Omega\\subset\\mathbb R^n$ 上完全非线性一致椭圆方程的 $L^n$-粘性次/超解；证明思路结合凸对偶方法（convex duality）与Trudinger-Moser型不等式，以处理漂移项的对数临界可积性。

Result: 在漂移系数 $\\gamma$ 满足 $\\gamma(\\ln(1+\\gamma))^{\\frac{n-1}{n}}\\in L^n(\\Omega)$ 的条件下，成功证明了相应的ABP极大值原理对 $L^n$-粘性次/超解成立。

Conclusion: ABP极大值原理可在比 $L^n$ 更弱的对数修正临界可积性假设下保持成立，凸对偶与Trudinger-Moser型不等式是处理此类临界漂移项的有效工具。

Abstract: We prove the Aleksandrov-Bakelman-Pucci maximum principle for $L^n$-viscosity sub/super-solutions of fully nonlinear uniformly elliptic equations in $Ω\subset\mathbb R^n$ with the drift coefficient $γ$ such that $γ(\ln(1+γ))^{\frac{n-1}{n}}\in L^n(Ω)$. The approach is based on convex duality and Trudinger-Moser type inequalities.

</details>


### [2] [Distance Geometry and a Hydrodynamical Hopf-Rinow Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03926)
*Theodore D. Drivas,Patrick Heslin,Gerard Misiołek*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究理想流体的hydrodynamical Hopf-Rinow问题，在平坦二维环面上用度量介于性给出了强版本问题的等价判据，并证明保体积微分同胚群中足够小的法邻域是测地凸的，同时建立了内蕴测地距离与外蕴L²距离的局部双Lipschitz等价以及测地距离平方的C^{1,1}延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: hydrodynamical Hopf-Rinow问题关注给定的理想流体重构能否由Euler流实现，其中强版本进一步要求流体运动最小化所需总动能。本文旨在为该问题提供几何刻画，并在局部层面解决其强版本。

Method: 在平坦n维环面（n≥2）的保体积微分同胚群上赋予动能度量，证明足够小的法邻域是测地凸的，从而在H^s拓扑下局部解决强问题；并建立内蕴测地距离与外蕴L²距离的局部双Lipschitz等价，以及从恒等元出发的测地距离平方从小法邻域到周围L²空间的C^{1,1}延拓。

Result: 在平坦二维环面上，用度量介于性给出了强版本hydrodynamical Hopf-Rinow问题的等价判据；证明足够小的法邻域测地凸；建立内蕴测地距离与外蕴L²距离的局部双Lipschitz等价；并证明测地距离平方具有C^{1,1}延拓。

Conclusion: 该工作为hydrodynamical Hopf-Rinow问题提供了基于度量介于性的等价刻画，并在局部H^s拓扑下解决了强版本问题，同时给出了测地距离与外蕴L²距离的定量等价及测地距离平方的正则性延拓结果。

Abstract: The hydrodynamical Hopf-Rinow problem asks whether a given reconfiguration of an ideal fluid can be attained by an Euler flow. On the flat two-dimensional torus, we give an equivalent criterion for the strong version of this problem (which requires that the fluid motion minimizes the total kinetic energy required) in terms of metric betweenness. Toward this, we prove that sufficiently small normal neighborhoods in the volume-preserving diffeomorphism group of the flat $n$-dimensional torus, $n \geq 2$, equipped with the kinetic energy metric, are geodesically convex; resolving the strong problem locally in the $H^s$ topology. We further establish local bi-Lipschitz equivalence of the intrinsic geodesic distance and the extrinsic $L^2$ distance. Finally, we prove that the squared geodesic distance from the identity admits a $C^{1,1}$ extension from a sufficiently small normal neighborhood to the ambient $L^2$ space.

</details>


### [3] [Vanishing discount selection for constant-sign local perturbations of periodic Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04149)
*Andrea Davini*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究 ${\mathbb R}^d$ 上临界 Hamilton--Jacobi 方程 $G=H-V$ 的消失贴现选择问题：$V\leqslant0$ 时证明贴现解局部一致收敛到可用 Mather 测度刻画的特异临界解；$V\geqslant0$ 时在零均值条件下得到收敛，并给出反例说明自然候选公式失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界 Hamilton--Jacobi 方程在消失贴现极限下的选择问题（即贴现解收敛到哪一个临界解）是弱 KAM 理论中的核心问题。本文关注非紧空间 ${\mathbb R}^d$ 上带紧支势 $V$ 的扰动 $G=H-V$，其中未扰动的周期问题本身可能存在多个临界解，需要刻画扰动如何"选择"极限解，并厘清现有候选公式的适用范围与局限。

Method: 设 $H$ 连续、关于空间周期、对动量凸且超线性，$V$ 为紧支且定号的势。关键在于对极限贴现测度进行分解：将其拆分为一个"保留"分量（集中在紧集上、对应扰动效应）和一个周期分量（刻画在无穷远处损失的质量）。对 $V\leqslant 0$ 情形，结合 Mather 测度与未扰动周期问题所选解来刻画特异临界解；对 $V\geqslant 0$ 情形，额外假设未扰动所选解关于每个周期 Mather 测度均值为零以证明收敛。最后通过显式构造例子检验候选极限公式。

Result: $V\leqslant0$ 时证明了贴现解的局部一致收敛，并给出基于 Mather 测度与未扰动周期问题所选解的特异临界解刻画；$V\geqslant0$ 时在零均值假设下同样建立了收敛性。此外给出显式例子：贴现解确实收敛，但若干自然候选极限公式无法识别被选中的解，说明这些公式在非紧扰动情形下不成立。

Conclusion: 本文完整处理了 ${\mathbb R}^d$ 上带紧支势的临界 Hamilton--Jacobi 方程消失贴现选择问题，其测度分解方法是刻画无穷远处质量损失的要点。结果既推广了周期/紧空间情形的选择理论，也通过反例警示依赖朴素候选公式的危险，为弱 KAM 中非紧选择问题的后续研究提供了框架与边界。

Abstract: We study the vanishing discount selection problem for critical Hamil\-ton--Jacobi equations posed on ${\mathbb R}^d$ with Hamiltonian $G=H-V$, where $H$ is continuous, periodic in space, convex and superlinear in the momentum, and $V$ is a compactly supported potential of constant sign. For $V\leqslant0$, we prove local uniform convergence of the discounted solutions to a distinguished critical solution, which we characterize in terms of Mather measures and of the solution selected by the unperturbed periodic problem. The proof relies on a decomposition of limiting discounted measures into a retained component and a periodic component accounting for the mass lost at infinity. For $V\geqslant0$, we establish convergence under the additional assumption that the unperturbed selected solution has zero average with respect to every periodic Mather measure. We also give explicit examples in which the discounted solutions converge, but natural formulas for the candidate limit fail to identify the selected solution.

</details>


### [4] [Slow motion for the time-fractional Allen-Cahn equation with linear and nonlinear diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04208)
*Davide Cusseddu,Raffaele Folino,Luis Fernando López Ríos*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究分数阶Allen--Cahn方程(0<α<1)相变层的慢运动，揭示分数记忆保持非退化与退化势对应的指数/代数慢动力学区分，并推广到非线性扩散算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察Caputo时间分数阶导数引入的记忆效应如何影响Allen--Cahn方程中相变界面层的慢运动时间尺度，厘清指数型与代数型慢动力学结构是否被分数记忆所改变。

Method: 对含Caputo时间导数(阶α∈(0,1))的时间分数阶Allen--Cahn方程，通过渐近/匹配分析相变层演化；分别处理非退化势与退化势两种情形；再将框架推广至p-Laplacian与Perona--Malik型非线性扩散算子。

Result: 分数记忆保留了指数与代数慢动力学的区分：非退化情形下分层解存活于量级为exp(A/(αε))的指数长时间尺度(A>0)；退化情形下代数时间尺度被因子1/α修正；在p-Laplacian及Perona--Malik型扩散下同样保持该分数慢运动机制。

Conclusion: 分数阶记忆不破坏相变层慢运动的基本理论结构，仅对时间尺度作出依赖于α的显式修正，且该机制对更一般的非线性扩散算子具鲁棒性。

Abstract: We study the slow motion of phase transition layers for a time-fractional Allen--Cahn equation with a Caputo time derivative of order $α\in(0,1)$. We show that the fractional memory preserves the distinction between exponentially and algebraically slow dynamics associated with non-degenerate and degenerate potentials, respectively. In the non-degenerate case, layered solutions persist on an exponentially long time scale of order $\exp(A/(α\varepsilon))$, $A>0$, while in the degenerate case the corresponding algebraic time scale is modified by the factor $1/α$. Finally, we extend the analysis to nonlinear diffusion operators, considering both the $p$-Laplacian and a Perona--Malik type diffusion, showing that the same fractional slow-motion mechanism persists in these more general settings.

</details>


### [5] [Existence and stability of shock profiles for a one-dimensional full compressible Navier--Stokes--Poisson equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04257)
*Yeping Li,Yuan Yuan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究一维完全可压缩Navier–Stokes–Poisson方程组小振幅激波行波解的存在性、唯一性与大时间渐近稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该方程组刻画自洽静电场中黏性导热离子的运动，激波行波解的存在性与稳定性是理解等离子体等物理体系中激波传播结构的基础问题，此前对完全可压缩且含Poisson耦合情形缺乏系统结果。

Method: 先用中心流形定理证明小振幅光滑激波剖面的存在唯一性；随后基于由原方程组构造的积分系统进行能量估计，引入恰当的反导数变量，将自洽静电势与热传导效应纳入其中，从而把原系统改写为耗散型积分系统，再细致处理由此产生的各类耦合项完成能量估计。

Result: 证明了小振幅光滑激波剖面的存在性与唯一性，并建立了其在适当小的光滑扰动下的大时间渐近稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了完全可压缩Navier–Stokes–Poisson方程组激波行波解存在性与渐近稳定性的完整框架，所提出的反导数变量与耗散型积分系统方法为处理静电耦合与热传导耦合提供了有效途径。

Abstract: This paper studies the existence and asymptotic stability of traveling shock waves for the one-dimensional full compressible Navier--Stokes--Poisson equations, which model viscous heat-conducting ions in a self-consistent electrostatic field. We first prove the existence and uniqueness of a smooth shock profile of small amplitude by the center manifold theorem, and then establish its large-time asymptotic stability under suitably small smooth perturbations. The proof is based on an energy estimate for a specially constructed integrated system derived from the full compressible Navier--Stokes--Poisson equations. To carry out this estimate, we introduce suitable anti-derivative variables that incorporate the effects of the self-consistent electrostatic potential and heat conduction, thereby reformulating the original system as a dissipative integrated one. The subsequent energy estimates are completed by carefully handling the induced couplings.

</details>


### [6] [Sharp Poincaré Inequalities for Families of Nonlocal Nonconvex Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04259)
*Feng Dai,Dachun Yang,Wen Yuan,Yirui Zhao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文建立了非局部非凸泛函Φ^γ_{λ,p}的尖锐Poincaré不等式，证明其成立的充要条件为γ∈(-∞,-1]，并给出Γ-收敛下界、Sobolev与BV空间渐近刻画及VMO正则性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nguyen此前对γ=-p情形给出了Poincaré不等式，但一般γ下该不等式是否成立、最优γ范围为何尚不清楚；同时该泛函在正则性与紧性研究中缺乏系统刻画，Nguyen及Brezis等人提出了相关公开问题。

Method: 针对定义在Q_0=(0,1)^N上的可测函数g，引入非局部非凸泛函Φ^γ_{λ,p}(g;Q_0)，对满足|g(x)-g(y)|>λ|x-y|^{1+γ/p}的(x,y)积分|x-y|^{γ-N}；通过构造性与反例相结合的方式确定不等式∫∫|g(x)-g(y)|^p ≲ λ^p + Φ^γ_{λ,p}成立的充要条件，并将其推广至Γ-收敛、Sobolev/BV渐近刻画与VMO判据。

Result: 证明了该Poincaré型估计对任意g和λ成立当且仅当γ∈(-∞,-1]，且隐含常数仅依赖于N、p、γ；由此得到Φ^γ_{λ,p}的Γ-收敛下界，建立Sobolev与BV空间的渐近刻画，并确定Rellich–Kondrachov定理及固定阈值下VMO正则性判据成立的最优γ范围。

Conclusion: 该工作将Nguyen的Poincaré不等式推广到γ∈(-∞,-1]的一般情形，回答了Nguyen及Brezis等人提出的非局部Poincaré、Sobolev与BV刻画等公开问题，为借助此类泛函研究正则性与紧性提供了统一的尖锐工具。

Abstract: Let $N\in\mathbb N$, $p\in[1,\infty)$, and $Q_0:=(0,1)^N$. In this article, we establish sharp Poincaré inequalities for the nonlocal nonconvex functionals $$ Φ_{λ,p}^γ(g;Q_0) :=λ^p\iint_{\{(x,y)\in Q_0^2:\,x\ne y,\,|g(x)-g(y)|>λ|x-y|^{1+\fracγ{p}}\}} |x-y|^{γ-N}\,dx\,dy, $$ where $g:Q_0\to\mathbb R$ is measurable, $λ\in(0,\infty)$, and $γ\in\mathbb R$. Specifically, we prove that the estimate $$ \int_{Q_0}\int_{Q_0}|g(x)-g(y)|^p\,dx\,dy \lesssimλ^p+Φ_{λ,p}^γ(g;Q_0) $$ holds for any such $g$ and $λ$ if and only if $γ\in(-\infty,-1]$, where the implicit positive constant depends only on $N$, $p$, and $γ$. This extends Nguyen's Poincaré inequality for $γ=-p$ and provides a useful tool for studying regularity and compactness through these functionals. As applications, we obtain a lower bound for the $Γ$-convergence of $Φ^γ_{λ,p}$ and related asymptotic characterizations for Sobolev and BV spaces. Moreover, we identify the optimal range of $γ$ for which both the Rellich--Kondrachov theorem and a criterion for VMO regularity involving $Φ^γ_{λ,p}$ at a fixed threshold hold. These results also answer the question on nonlocal Poincaré inequalities posed by H.-M. Nguyen in [C. R. Math. Acad. Sci. Paris 363 (2025)]. For $γ\in(-\infty,-1]$, they further answer questions on Sobolev and BV characterizations posed in the same article and by H. Brezis et al. in [Anal. PDE 17 (2024)].

</details>


### [7] [Complete classification of solutions for spin-1 Bose-Einstein condensates in R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04411)
*Xiao Luo,Cunxin Wei,Juncheng Wei,Maoding Zhen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维自旋-1玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体解的完整分类，采用非标准Hirota双线性方法、行列式公式与广义左唯一性策略，给出若干新显式解及解的完备分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-1玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体是探索量子磁性与相干自旋动力学的基础平台，其三个Zeeman分量在粒子数与磁化守恒下由自旋交换相互作用耦合，产生丰富磁相与非线性自旋结构，因而对定态进行严格分类可厘清可能的自旋构型及其物理来源。

Method: 作为前期工作的延续，在实数轴上研究自旋-1玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体解的分类，应用非标准Hirota双线性方法、行列式公式以及广义左唯一性策略。相比经典Hirota方法，非标准方法需引入辅助函数构造双线性形式，从而对波函数施加额外约束，以处理更复杂的方程。

Result: 给出若干新的显式解，并完成解的完整分类；据作者所知，这是首次对自旋-1玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体解进行系统完整的分类研究。

Conclusion: 分类结果为自旋布居、相对相位和磁结构提供可实验检验的预测。

Abstract: Spin-1 Bose-Einstein condensates provide a fundamental platform for exploring quantum magnetism and coherent spin dynamics. Their three Zeeman components are coupled by spin-exchange interactions under conserved particle number and magnetization, giving rise to rich magnetic phases and nonlinear spin structures; a rigorous classification of stationary states therefore clarifies the possible spin configurations and their physical origins. In this paper, as a continuation of our previous work \cite{LLWZ23,LLWZ24,LLWZ26}, we study the classification of solution for the spin-1 Bose-Einstein condensates in $\mathbb{R}$, by applying the non-standard Hirota's bilinear method, the determinant formula and the general left-uniqueness strategy, we present some novel explicit solution and give the complete classification of the solutions. In comparison with the classical Hirota's method, the non-standard Hirota's method needs to introduce auxiliary functions to construct Hirota's bilinear form, which essentially adds additional constraint on the wave functions, so that it possible to deal with more complex equations. As far as we know, this is the first systematic study the complete classification of solutions to the spin-1 Bose-Einstein condensates. Our classification results yields experimentally testable predictions for spin populations, relative phases, and magnetic textures

</details>


### [8] [On some concepts of convergence and their connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04478)
*Tatiana Danielsson,Liselott Flodén,Anders Holmbom,Pernilla Johnsen,Marianne Olsson Lindberg,Jens Persson*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文将经典双尺度收敛与周期展开方法统一归结为一般双尺度收敛框架，并比较了极弱双尺度收敛与周期展开在Sobolev空间中有界序列（经微振荡尺度放大）下的关系，指出二者对放大序列并不直接等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典双尺度收敛与周期展开方法均依赖表示周期微振荡所需的附加尺度，二者关系及与更一般的双尺度收敛的联系需要厘清，尤其对经尺度放大的Sobolev空间有界序列，不同方法是否等价尚不明确。

Conclusion: 论文兼具文献综述与新结果：既通过示例和图示梳理收敛概念，又给出上述非等价性等新结论。

Abstract: The notions of classical two-scale convergence and the periodic unfolding method, which both rely on an additional scale for the representation of periodic micro-oscillations, are in this paper traced back to the wider concept of general two-scale convergence. Moreover, a comparison is made between very weak two-scale convergence and periodic unfolding with respect to sequences bounded in Sobolev spaces, amplified by the scale of the micro-oscillations. It turns out that, for amplified sequences, these approaches are not equivalent in an immediate sense. The paper both serves as a literature overview, with examples and illustrations of the concepts of convergence, and contains new results.

</details>


### [9] [Global and nonglobal solutions for an abstract semilinear heat equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04550)
*Ricardo Castillo,Ricardo Freire,Miguel Loayza*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究抽象半线性热方程在L^p(X,μ)空间中全局与非全局非负解的存在性，并将结果应用于次黎曼流形和局部有限图上的Laplace算子，得到Fujita型结论，推广了Lenz、Schmidt和Zimmermann 2023年的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study a log-Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev (log-HLS) inequality under constraints and a naturally induced Wasserstein flow. Abstract: We investigate the conditions under which a Hessian operator satisfies the integral comparison principle. Consequently, neither equation admits solutions of finite Morse index in these ranges. In the borderline case $N=p$, we prove that the solutions of $-Δ_N^H u=e^u$ that are stable outside a compact set are precisely the finite-mass solutions classified by Ciraolo and Li. A main ingredient is independent of stability: if $N>qτ$, where $τ=\frac{p}{q+p-1}$, a positive exterior solution of $Δ_p^H u=u^{-q}$ cannot remain above $θ$ times the singular solution near infinity for any $θ>1$. An analogous obstruction holds for the exponential equation. Both obstructions are proved by comparison with explicit radial barriers having frozen right-hand sides. We also prove sharpness when $N>p$, and for the negative-power equation when $N=p>2$. Finally, for operators $\operatorname{div}(B'(H(\nabla u))\nabla H(\nabla u))$ with $p$-growth, we establish the integral estimates needed for the stability arguments with fully justified truncation and absorption procedures. These arguments also repair gaps in earlier proofs for the corresponding isotropic and anisotropic problems.

Abstract: We study the surface Stokes-Cahn-Hilliard system governing tangential flow and phase separation in viscous fluid membranes. Abstract: In this work, we study transparent boundary conditions for the time-fractional heat equation on a metric star graph. This equation is the standard continuum model for subdiffusive heat and mass transport in disordered and porous branched media. Using the Laplace transform to solve the exterior branch problems, we demonstrate that the non-local vertex operator observed by the incoming bond is an Abel-type convolution kernel of order $β/2$, which generalizes the classical half-derivative kernel recovered at $β=1$. Our main result is that the diffusion-coefficient sum rule $κ_1=κ_2+κ_3$, known to eliminate thermal backflow for the classical heat equation, remains unchanged for every fractional order $β$: the transparency of the junction depends only on the network's diffusivities and not on the memory of the transport law. We rigorously prove this and uniqueness for the coupled vertex problem. We confirm our results numerically at the level of the underlying coupled partial differential equation system with an implicit L1 finite-difference scheme. Abstract: We consider the defocusing quintic wave equation $\partial_t^2u-Δu+u^5=0$ for real-valued functions on $\mathbb{R}^{1+10}$, which is energy-supercritical. We construct a discretely self-similar solution in a backward light cone, of the form $$u(t,x)=(T-t)^{-1/2}W^*\big(ω^*\log\frac1{T-t},\frac{|x|}{T-t}\big),$$ where the nonzero profile $W^*$ is $2π$-periodic in its first variable and real-analytic up to and across the light cone. Cutting off its initial data gives smooth, compactly supported, radial data whose solution coincides with the discretely self-similar solution in the backward light cone, is smooth on $[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{10}$ except at the vertex $(T,0)$ of the cone, and blows up there at the self-similar rate. The solutions we construct here appear to be the first real-valued blow-up solutions of defocusing energy-supercritical NLW.

We find the approximate profile by numerically continuing rotating self-similar profiles of the complex-valued equation. This step is not essential for the blow-up proof, so it is left as non-rigorous numerics. The main analytic ingredients are an exact description of the mode operators for the linear part, which are hypergeometric and upper triangular in the Chebyshev basis, and a bound for their inverses that is uniform at high frequencies. All computer-assisted proofs, including the requisite codes and the approximate objects, are accessible on GitHub at \cite{code}.

</details>

Abstract: We construct an odd smooth initial density for the unforced incompressible porous media (IPM) equation on $\mathbb T^2$ whose solution develops a finite-time blowup. Motivation: 退化$k$-Hessian方程Dirichlet问题的全局高阶正则性（尤其$C^{1,1}$可解性）长期未决，$k=2$情形是其中的关键开问题；同时需澄清边界正则性假设的最优性。

Method: 在有界严格平均凸$C^{3,1}$区域$\Omega$上研究退化2-Hessian方程，边界值与右端项$f$取$C^{3,1}$、非负$C^{1,1}$；通过证明$σ_{3}[D^{2}u]$的下界与$\inf_\Omega f$无关，建立一般2-可容许解的全局半凸性；并推广至$3\le k\le n-1$情形（单位球、$C^{3}$边界值、$f^{1/(k-1)}\in C^{1,1}$）。

Result: 证明了Dirichlet问题存在全局$C^{1,1}$解，解决$k=2$的开问题；对一般2-可容许解得到与$\inf_\Omega f$无关的$σ_{3}[D^{2}u]$下界及全局半凸性；对$3\le k\le n-1$在单位球上亦得全局半凸估计且不依赖$\inf f$；构造反例表明$C^{3,1}$边界值假设在Hölder尺度下是尖锐的。

Conclusion: 退化2-Hessian方程Dirichlet问题具全局$C^{1,1}$可解性，解的全局半凸性由不依赖右端项下确界的$σ_{3}$下界保证；高阶$k$情形在单位球上有类似结论，且$C^{3,1}$正则性假设最优。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish global $C^{1,1}$ solvability of the Dirichlet problem for the degenerate $2$-Hessian equation on bounded strictly mean convex $C^{3,1}$ domains $Ω$, with general $C^{3,1}$ boundary values and nonnegative right-hand sides $f\in C^{1,1}(\overlineΩ)$, resolving the $k=2$ case of a longstanding open problem. We uncover a global semiconvexity structure for general $2$-admissible solutions by proving a lower bound for $σ_{3}[D^{2}u]$ independent of $\inf_Ωf$. For $3\leq k\leq n-1$, we also establish global semiconvexity for $k$-admissible solutions on the unit ball with $C^{3}$ boundary values and $f^{1/(k-1)}\in C^{1,1}(\overline{B}_{1})$, with a bound independent of $\inf_{B_{1}}f$. A counterexample shows that the $C^{3,1}$ boundary value assumption for global $C^{1,1}$ solvability is sharp in the Hölder scale.

</details>

Result: 单值性特征空间给出纵向位移：正情况为0与1/2，负情况为1/4与3/4。谱由单一未配对横向分支与配对分支组成，后者纵向位移随横向宇称交替。核与谱对称性依赖提升与边界条件；两个负Pin提升在给定约定下eta值分别为1/2与-1/2。

Conclusion: 莫比乌斯带上Pin旋量的Dirac边界实现与其提升及局部边界条件密切相关，纵向单值性是决定谱位移、核结构与eta不变量的关键；所得久期方程与核判据为全平移不变局部自伴椭圆边界族提供了精确刻画。

Abstract: We study complex rank-two pinor fields on the flat Möbius band and the relation between their deck equivariance and Dirac boundary realizations. With explicit Clifford conventions, the two lifts for each Pin type are represented by $R=cσ_1$, where $c=\pm1$ for Pin+ and $c=\pm i$ for Pin-. The relevant longitudinal monodromy is $cJ$, with $Jφ(w)=σ_1φ(-w)$, rather than the fiber matrix alone. Its eigenspaces give shifts $0,1/2$ in the positive case and $1/4,3/4$ in the negative case. We identify the resulting geometric Sobolev spaces and construct self-adjoint elliptic realizations of $D=-i(σ_1\partial_x+σ_2\partial_w)$ with a local boundary condition defined by the longitudinal tangent field. An exact graph estimate gives compact resolvent and Fredholm mappings on nonnegative Sobolev levels. The spectrum consists of a single unpaired transverse branch and paired branches whose longitudinal shifts alternate with transverse parity. Consequently, the kernel and spectral symmetry depend on the lift and boundary condition; the two negative Pin lifts have eta values $1/2$ and $-1/2$ in the stated convention. We also give a secular equation and an exact kernel criterion for the full translation-invariant family of local self-adjoint elliptic boundary lines.

</details>

Abstract: We prove that plane waves are the only gravitational pp-wave spacetimes which are distinguishing; in particular, they are the only strongly causal ones. This is used to prove a null version of Anderson's rigidity theorem, conjectured by Costa e Silva and Flores: every geodesically complete, transversally complete, strongly causal, Ricci-flat Brinkmann spacetime of dimension $4$ is isometric to a quotient of a plane wave spacetime.

</details>

Abstract: Let $G$ be a compact connected semisimple Lie group, let $Q$ be a bi-invariant metric and let $ρ$ be its Cartan three-form. We prove that every left-invariant generalized Ricci flow with $[H_0]=[ρ]$ exists for all positive time and converges smoothly to a Bismut-Ricci-flat pair without rescaling or pullback. The standard pair uniquely maximizes generalized scalar curvature in its torsion class, and every left-invariant Bismut-Ricci-flat pair on a compact simple group is standard, answering questions of Fusi, Lafuente and Stanfield and of Lauret and Will. On the product of two copies of a compact simple group, nonstandard stationary pairs in the Cartan class are known; we construct nonstationary flows with the exact initial form $[H_0]=[ρ]$ and nonstandard limits, disproving the general semisimple case of the convergence conjecture of Garcia-Fernandez and Streets. The proofs use a proper nonincreasing energy and a decomposition of Killing fields that parametrizes the stationary pairs by nilpotent Lie algebra endomorphisms.

Conclusion: 结果给出了水平周长在特征点处二阶变分的正则性，并通过完整谱分析确定了 Pansu 球的稳定性：它是保体积意义下的稳定极小曲面，其唯一的负方向与零空间均有明确的几何解释。

Abstract: For $n\ge2$, we establish second differentiability of horizontal perimeter for compact $C^2$ boundaries in $\mathbb H^n$ and the continuous extension of their affine second variation to $C^1$ vector fields along the boundary. For the unit Pansu sphere, the Hessian agrees with the closed Jacobi form across both characteristic poles, including variations which move them. We compute the full Friedrichs spectrum of its Jacobi operator. The operator has compact resolvent, Morse index one and nullity $2n+1$; its kernel consists exactly of normalized translation speeds. Consequently, the sphere is stable under volume-preserving ambient variations.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a theory of orientability for metric manifolds and singular metric spaces using locally integral currents. For topological $n$-manifolds with locally finite Hausdorff measure, we introduce local degree and measure-theoretic conditions under which topological orientations correspond canonically to boundaryless, locally integer rectifiable $n$-currents of multiplicity one on the rectifiable part. We extend these currents to a class of singular spaces whose manifold part is dense and of full measure, and establish constancy and top-dimensional homology results under local uniqueness of orientation. For orientable non-collapsed $\RCD(κ,n)$ spaces, we prove a current-theoretic Stokes theorem: the boundary of the orientation current is the induced orientation current of the geometric boundary, with multiplicity one. In particular, its mass measure is precisely the $(n-1)$-dimensional Hausdorff measure on that boundary. For purely $n$-dimensional, locally geodesically complete spaces with local upper curvature bounds, we show that local uniqueness of orientation excludes codimension-one branching and implies a local $1$-Poincaré inequality. TL;DR: 该论文证明任意维数非正曲率空间形式中的双调和超曲面必为极小，解决了广义陈省身猜想在双曲空间中的超曲面情形，并给出单位球面中非定常平均曲率双调和超曲面的定量限制。

Conclusion: 该工作统一处理了非正曲率空间形式的超曲面情形，解决双曲空间中广义陈省身猜想的超曲面情形，并为BMO猜想提供了新的支持证据。

Abstract: We prove that every biharmonic hypersurface in a space form of nonpositive sectional curvature is minimal in arbitrary dimension. This settles the hypersurface case of the generalized Chen's conjecture in hyperbolic space and gives a unified treatment of nonpositive space forms. For biharmonic hypersurfaces in the unit sphere, we derive quantitative restrictions on every possible nonconstant-mean-curvature solution. In particular, we obtain pointwise criteria forcing constant mean curvature, a strict scalar-curvature bound in dimensions at least five, and local classification results above the classical CMC gap threshold. These results provide further evidence for the BMO conjecture.

</details>


### [82] [Clifford Actions and the Algebra of Double Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04428)
*Abdelhadi Belkhirat*

Main category: math.DG

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双重形式代数上天然存在两族（因式分解的）外乘与内乘算子，从而诱导两个交换的 Clifford 作用；作者希望不借助外部构造、内蕴地在 Greub 复合代数中实现这些 Clifford 作用，并阐明其与 Bianchi 算子、$\mathfrak{sl}_2$ 作用及 sharp 积之间的关系，为曲率张量的代数运算（如 Ricci 流反应项中的 sharp 贡献）提供统一框架。

Method: 对 $a\in V^*$ 引入非齐次双重形式 $\chi_a=((1\otimes a)-(a\otimes1))e^g$，证明 $\chi_a$ 满足复合积下的 Clifford 关系，并建立正则作用公式 $C_R(a)\omega=\chi_a\circ\omega$、$C_L(a)\omega=-\omega\circ\chi_a$；对 $h\in\D^{1,1}$ 将耦合左右 Clifford 作用分解为外乘、双重缩并与两个广义 Bianchi 算子；对度规双重形式 $g$ 分析这四个算子生成的 $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ 作用，并把构造推广到任意双次数，在 $(2,2)$ 次比较保次分量与 sharp 积的双重形式推广。

Result: 证明了 $\chi_a$ 满足 Clifford 关系并给出左右正则作用的精确公式；得到耦合左右 Clifford 作用的四分量分解；对 $g$ 得到两个交换的 $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ 作用；在双次数 $(2,2)$ 上证明保次分量满足 $\Gamma_{1,1}(R)(\omega)=\frac14 R\#\omega$，即 $R\#$ 作为高阶左右 Clifford 变换的中心齐次分量自然出现；作为应用，代数二次映射 $\mathcal Q(R)=-\frac14 R\#R$ 满足 $\mathcal Q(R)=-\Gamma_{1,1}(R)R$，其在 $R$ 处的线性化为 $-2\Gamma_{1,1}(R)$，并对应曲率张量 Ricci 流反应项中的 sharp 贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了双重形式 Clifford 作用在 Greub 复合代数中的内蕴实现与系统分解，把 sharp 积、Bianchi 算子与 $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ 结构统一在同一框架下，为曲率张量代数及 Ricci 流反应项的 sharp 部分提供了自然的代数解释。

Abstract: Let $V$ be an oriented Euclidean vector space and let $\D(V)=ΛV^*\otimesΛV^*$ be the algebra of double forms. The two exterior factors carry natural factorwise exterior and interior multiplication operators and therefore two commuting Clifford actions. The central result of the paper is an intrinsic realization of these Clifford actions inside Greub's composition algebra. For $a\in V^*$ we introduce the inhomogeneous double form \[ χ_a=((1\otimes a)-(a\otimes1))e^g, \] prove that the $χ_a$ satisfy the Clifford relations for the composition product, and establish the exact regular-action formulas \[ C_R(a)ω=χ_a\circω, \qquad C_L(a)ω=-ω\circχ_a. \] For $h\in\D^{1,1}$ this yields a canonical four-component decomposition of the coupled left--right Clifford action into exterior multiplication, double contraction and two generalized Bianchi operators. For the metric double form $g$ these four operators generate two commuting $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ actions. The construction extends canonically to arbitrary bidegree. In bidegree $(2,2)$ its degree-preserving component admits a direct comparison with the double-form extension of the sharp product: for every $R\in\D^{2,2}$, \[ Γ_{1,1}(R)(ω)=\frac14R\#ω. \] The point of this identity is not a new definition of $R\#$, which is already constructed from Clifford commutators, but the fact that it appears canonically as the central homogeneous component of the higher left--right Clifford transform. As an application, the algebraic quadratic map $\mathcal Q(R)=-\frac14R\#R$ satisfies \[ \mathcal Q(R)=-Γ_{1,1}(R)R, \] and its linearization at $R$ is the operator $-2Γ_{1,1}(R)$. For curvature tensors this is the sharp contribution to the Ricci-flow reaction term.

</details>


### [83] [Frobenius conjugation and Jacobi functions for minimal immersions in $\mathbb{S}^3$ and CMC $1$ immersions in $\mathbb{R}^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04484)
*José M. Espinar,Joaquín Pérez*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文将共轭极小浸入用一阶Frobenius描述刻画，据此对任意Jacobi函数定义共轭，无需其经由极小曲面法向变形可积，并分析边界条件在共轭下的交换及CMC~1情形的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共轭极小浸入是极小曲面几何中的经典对象，但传统定义依赖于浸入的可积性与法向变形。作者希望在仅线性化层面（即Jacobi函数层面）内蕴地定义共轭，从而摆脱可积性限制，并理解Dirichlet/Neumann边界条件在共轭下如何转换。

Method: 在三维球面中给出共轭极小浸入的一阶Frobenius描述，用它定义任意Jacobi函数的共轭；研究大圆边界弧与反射曲线上的边界条件交换，并附加以适当环境规范（gauge）；随后将构造推广到R^3中CMC~1浸入的共轭Jacobi函数。

Result: 沿大圆边界弧，Dirichlet Jacobi函数在合适环境规范下的共轭具有消失的Neumann数据；沿反射曲线，消失的Neumann数据给出共轭的消失Dirichlet数据（模Jacobi--Killing函数）。同时给出柱面反例，表明两种纯边界交换即使在模环境Killing场意义下也不成立。

Conclusion: 共轭可在Jacobi函数层面内蕴定义而无需可积性，边界条件交换在大圆与反射曲线情形成立（至多模Killing场），但柱面反例说明不存在一般性的纯边界交换。

Abstract: We give a first-order Frobenius description of conjugate minimal immersions in the round three-sphere and use it to define the conjugate of an arbitrary Jacobi function. The construction is intrinsic at the linearized level and does not require the Jacobi function to be integrable through a normal deformation by minimal surfaces. We then describe how the boundary conditions are exchanged under conjugation. Along a great-circle boundary arc, a Dirichlet Jacobi function has, after a suitable ambient gauge, a conjugate Jacobi function with vanishing Neumann data. Conversely, along a reflection curve, vanishing Neumann data yield vanishing Dirichlet data for the conjugate modulo a Jacobi--Killing function. We also construct conjugate Jacobi functions for conjugate CMC~$1$ immersions in $\mathbb{R}^3$ and exhibit cylindrical counterexamples to both pure boundary exchanges, even modulo ambient Killing fields.

</details>


### [84] [Analyticity and tautness of smooth Dupin hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04679)
*Stephan Wiesendorf*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在满足Dupin条件（主重数局部常数的区域）的光滑浸入超曲面中，每个局部像分支都是实解析且局部Nash的，无需正则轨迹局部有限性或奇异轨迹正则性假设；由此平方距离函数为Morse-Bott型，进而推出紧致嵌入超曲面是taut的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于Dupin超曲面的解析性与Nash性质的结果通常依赖对正则轨迹的局部有限性假设或对奇异轨迹的额外正则性假设，本文旨在去掉这些限制，并建立该几何条件与taut性之间的联系。

Method: 将对proper Dupin片段适用的、依赖于维数的代数次数上界，与基于有限jet和resultant的延拓论证相结合；随后用解析Hessian判别法分析平方距离函数。

Result: 证明了每个局部像分支实解析且局部Nash（即实解析且半代数）；证明了每个平方距离函数是Morse-Bott的；结合作者此前给出的taut性刻画，推出每个满足该条件的紧致嵌入超曲面都是taut的。

Conclusion: 在仅假设Dupin条件于主重数局部常数处成立的前提下，超曲面仍具有强解析与半代数结构，且紧致嵌入情形自动蕴含taut性。

Abstract: We consider smooth immersed Euclidean hypersurfaces satisfying the Dupin condition wherever the principal multiplicities are locally constant. We prove that in this case, every local image branch is real-analytic and locally Nash, i.e. real-analytic and semialgebraic, without any local finiteness assumption on the regular locus or any regularity assumption on the singular locus. The proof combines a dimension-dependent algebraic degree bound on proper Dupin pieces with a continuation argument using finite jets and resultants. In addition, an analytic Hessian criterion then shows that every squared distance function is Morse-Bott. The author's earlier characterization of tautness consequently implies that every compact embedded hypersurface satisfying this condition is taut.

</details>


### [85] [Boundary behaviour of stable Allen-Cahn limit-interfaces in Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04833)

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文是Allen-Cahn方程自由边界极小超曲面min-max理论系列工作的第二篇，研究Neumann边界条件下稳定（或有限Morse指标）Allen-Cahn解的极限界面整变分在边界处的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在通过Allen-Cahn方程发展带边界Riemann流形中自由边界极小超曲面的min-max理论，特别是为后续工作实现最优边界正则性提供关键工具。此前对一般临界点的研究已奠定基础，尚需深入理解稳定或有限Morse指标解的极限界面在边界处的性质。

Method: 在Neumann边界条件下，分析稳定（或有限Morse指标）Allen-Cahn解的极限界面整变分在边界附近的行为。证明极限界面是Mantegazza意义下带边界的曲率变分，并满足广义稳定性不等式。

Result: 证明了极限界面是满足广义稳定性不等式的带边界曲率变分；排除了边界处的经典切锥，并证明极限界面变分的第一变分关于其权重测度是奇异的。由此证实了Malchiodi、Ni和Wei给出的边界集中例子具有无界Morse指标这一猜想。

Conclusion: 本文结果确认了关于边界集中例子Morse指标无界的猜想，并为即将开展的工作中Allen-Cahn自由边界min-max理论的最优边界正则性提供了关键要素。

Abstract: This is the second paper in our series of works to develop the min-max theory for free boundary minimal hypersurfaces in Riemannian manifolds with boundary via the Allen-Cahn equation. Building on our previous work for general critical points, we study the boundary behaviour of limit-interface integral varifolds arising from stable (or finite Morse index) solutions to the Allen-Cahn equation with Neumann boundary condition. More precisely, we prove that the limit-interface is a curvature varifold with boundary in the sense of Mantegazza satisfying a generalized stability inequality. Furthermore, we rule out classical tangent cones at the boundary and show that the first variation of the limit-interface varifold is singular with respect to its weight measure. In particular, our result confirms a folklore conjecture that the boundary concentration examples of Malchiodi, Ni and Wei have unbounded Morse index. As will be evident in our forthcoming work, the results obtained in this paper are crucial ingredients towards the optimal boundary regularity in the Allen-Cahn free boundary min-max theory.

</details>


### [86] [Spectral Gap Estimates on Nearly Einstein Manifolds with Integral Curvature Deviation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04846)
*Shunzi Guo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明：在紧致（带凸边界的）Riemann流形上，若用 $p>n/2$ 范数的Einstein偏离 $κ_{\mathrm{Ein}}(p)$ 度量“$\varepsilon$-近Einstein”，且直径有界，则第一非零特征值 $\lambda_1$ 可任意逼近Bakry–Qian一维模型问题的特征值 $\lambda_1(n,K_E,D)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Bakry–Qian定理给出Ricci曲率下界对 $\lambda_1$ 的最优估计，但仅依赖Ricci下界无法刻画“接近Einstein”这一几何情形下 $\lambda_1$ 是否更接近模型值。作者希望在近Einstein、直径受控条件下得到 $\lambda_1$ 的稳定性/刚性型下界，并当偏离趋于零时恢复经典定理。

Method: 引入参考曲率 $R_E>0$、$K_E=R_E/(n(n-1))$、无迹Ricci张量 $\mathring{\mathrm{Ric}}$ 与Einstein偏离 $Θ=|\mathring{\mathrm{Ric}}|+|R-R_E|/n$，用 $κ_{\mathrm{Ein}}(p)$（$p>n/2$）定义 $\varepsilon$-近Einstein。证明结合Bochner公式与分解 $\mathrm{Ric}=(n-1)K_E g+\theta g+\mathring{\mathrm{Ric}}$，构造满足 $\Delta J-\tau|\nabla J|^2/J-2JΘ=-\sigma J$ 及Neumann边条件的辅助函数 $J$，并运用Moser迭代与最大值原理。

Result: 对任意 $\alpha\in(0,1)$，存在 $\varepsilon_0=\varepsilon_0(n,p,\alpha,D,R_E)>0$，使得若 $\mathrm{diam}(M)\le D$ 且 $κ_{\mathrm{Ein}}(p)<\varepsilon_0$，则 $\lambda_1(M)\ge \alpha\,\lambda_1(n,K_E,D)$；其中 $\lambda_1(n,K,D)$ 为Bakry–Qian一维模型问题的第一非零特征值，约定当 $K>0$ 且 $D\ge\pi/\sqrt{K}$ 时 $\lambda_1(n,K,D)=nK$。

Conclusion: 当 $κ_{\mathrm{Ein}}(p)\to0$ 时，估计恢复经典Bakry–Qian定理，表明 $\lambda_1$ 的下界在近Einstein且直径受控条件下具有稳定性，并为接近Einstein几何的最优谱估计提供了定量框架。

Abstract: Let $(M^n,g)$ be a compact Riemannian manifold of dimension $n\ge 2$, possibly with nonempty convex $C^2$ boundary. We study the first nonzero eigenvalue $λ_1(M)$ of the Laplace--Beltrami operator, subject to the Neumann boundary condition when $\partial M\neq\emptyset$. Given a reference scalar curvature $R_E>0$, set $K_E:=R_E/(n(n-1))$, and define the traceless Ricci tensor $\mathring{\Ric}:=\Ric-\frac{R}{n}g$ and the Einstein deviation \[ Θ:=|\mathring{\Ric}|+\frac{|R-R_E|}{n},\qquad κ_{\mathrm{Ein}}(p):=\left(\frac{1}{\vol(M)}\int_MΘ^p\,dV\right)^{1/p}, \quad p>n/2. \] We say that $M$ is $\varepsilon$-nearly Einstein if $κ_{\mathrm{Ein}}(p)<\varepsilon$. The main result states that for every $α\in(0,1)$ there exists $\varepsilon_0=\varepsilon_0(n,p,α,D,R_E)>0$ such that if $\diam(M)\le D$ and $κ_{\mathrm{Ein}}(p)<\varepsilon_0$, then \[ λ_1(M)\ge α\,λ_1(n,K_E,D), \] where $λ_1(n,K,D)$ is the first nonzero eigenvalue of the Bakry--Qian one-dimensional model problem with curvature parameter $K$, with the convention $λ_1(n,K,D)=nK$ when $K>0$ and $D\geπ/\sqrt{K}$. The proof combines the Bochner formula for the decomposition $\Ric=(n-1)K_Eg+θg+\mathring{\Ric}$ with an auxiliary function $J$ satisfying \[ ΔJ-τ\frac{|\nabla J|^2}{J}-2JΘ=-σJ \] and Neumann boundary conditions, together with Moser iteration and a maximum principle. As $κ_{\mathrm{Ein}}(p)\to0$, the estimate recovers the classical Bakry--Qian theorem.

</details>


### [87] [Measure Concentration around Minimal Hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05056)
*Vicent Gimeno i Garcia,Vicente Palmer*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文将标准球面上测度集中现象（"胖赤道效应"）从全测地赤道推广到任意闭极小超曲面，借助布朗运动均值逃逸时间（MET）与Schwarz对称化的结合，证明高维下极小性本身即可保证测度集中于极小超曲面邻域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典测度集中现象仅适用于标准球面 S^n 上的全测地赤道 S^{n-1}，其管状邻域在高维下几乎占据整个球面体积。作者希望探究这一"胖赤道效应"是否能推广到更一般的子流形，即任意闭极小超曲面是否也具备类似的测度集中性质。

Method: 作者结合解析框架与几何工具：一方面采用布朗运动及其均值逃逸时间（MET）函数作为分析手段，另一方面运用Schwarz对称化这一几何工具，以此研究闭极小超曲面 Σ^{n-1} ⊂ S^n 邻域的测度集中行为。

Result: 研究表明，仅凭极小性这一条件，就足以保证在高维情形下测度集中于任意闭极小超曲面 Σ^{n-1} 的邻域，即将"胖赤道效应"成功推广至任意闭极小超曲面。

Conclusion: 极小性是驱使高维球面上测度集中于极小超曲面邻域（胖赤道效应的推广）的充分条件，该结论通过布朗运动MET函数与Schwarz对称化的结合得以建立。

Abstract: The classical measure concentration phenomenon on the standard sphere $\mathbb{S}^n$ asserts that the volume of any tubular neighborhood of a totally geodesic equator $\mathbb{S}^{n-1}$ concentrates almost the entire volume of $\mathbb{S}^n$ as the dimension $n$ grows to infinity. In this paper, we extend this \lq\lq Fat Equator Effect'' to arbitrary closed minimal hypersurfaces $Σ^{n-1} \subset \mathbb{S}^n$. By combining the analytic framework of Brownian motion and its Mean Exit Time (MET) function with the geometric tool of Schwarz symmetrization, we establish that minimality alone is sufficient to guarantee measure concentration around $Σ^{n-1}$ in high dimensions.

</details>


### [88] [Collapse to nonsmoothable manifolds under lower sectional and positive Ricci curvature bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05117)
*Jian Ge,Chao Qian*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 构造截面曲率下界为 $-1$ 的光滑坍缩序列，其极限是 Kirby--Siebenmann 类非零的拓扑流形，并给出自旋、透镜空间同伦型及不可约流形等例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究坍缩极限的拓扑性质，特别是极限流形是否携带非平凡的 Kirby--Siebenmann 类，以及曲率下界（Ricci、数量曲率）对极限与逼近的约束。

Method: 构造光滑坍缩序列，令度量满足截面曲率 $\ge -1$ 与统一的 Ricci 下界；通过取 Chern 四维流形与 $S^{n-4}$ 的乘积等方式得到极限，并利用有限商与同伦型分析构造自旋与五维透镜空间例子；对非球面情形使用数量曲率一致趋于 $+\infty$ 的坍缩并给出逼近障碍。

Result: 得到极限为 Kirby--Siebenmann 类非零的拓扑流形；对每个 $n\ge6$，源与极限单连通，维数分别为 $n+3$ 与 $n$，且度量具有共同的 Ricci 下界；极限同胚于 Chern 四维流形与 $S^{n-4}$ 的乘积。另获自旋例子及由固定 $2$-连通八维流形的有限商得到的五维透镜空间同伦型；并实现数量曲率一致趋于 $+\infty$ 的非球面坍缩极限，同时建立其几乎非负 Ricci 曲率逼近的障碍。

Conclusion: 在正 Ricci 下界下仍可出现 Kirby--Siebenmann 类非零的坍缩极限，说明该类拓扑障碍与曲率下界相容；数量曲率趋于 $+\infty$ 的坍缩可产生无非闭合 PL 代表的非球面流形，并阻碍几乎非负 Ricci 逼近。

Abstract: We construct smooth collapsing sequences with sectional curvature at least $-1$ whose limits are topological manifolds with nonzero Kirby--Siebenmann class. For every $n\geq6$, the source and limit are simply connected, have dimensions $n+3$ and $n$, and the metrics have a common positive Ricci lower bound. The limits are homeomorphic to the product of the Chern four-manifold with $S^{n-4}$. We also obtain spin examples and five-dimensional lens-space homotopy types, the latter from finite quotients of one fixed $2$-connected eight-manifold. Finally, we realize aspherical manifolds with no closed PL representatives of their homotopy types as collapsing limits with scalar curvatures tending uniformly to $+\infty$, and establish an obstruction to their approximation with almost nonnegative Ricci curvature.

</details>


### [89] [Schwarz lemmas and rigidity of \(\bar\partial_b\)-harmonic maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05181)
*Xin Huang,Hui Liu*

Abstract: We prove Schwarz lemmas for \(\bar\partial_b\)-harmonic maps from complete pseudo-Hermitian manifolds into K"ahler manifolds of negative curvature. For CR maps a coupled Bochner identity yields rank-dependent horizontal estimates and a bound for the Reeb differential. On Sasakian sources and complex hyperbolic targets we classify the maps attaining the horizontal bound, bound the deviation from equality in integral form by the deficit at one point, and prove compactness of almost extremal sequences. Method: 研究(2,1)维AdS时空中的反问题。将边界诱导度量(每个分支曲率K<-1)作为边界值,利用Gauss公式给出的必要约束,构造并证明存在唯一光滑AdS度量的对应;进而将该度量对应关系与凸核边界的实测弯曲层联系起来以处理Mess猜想。

Conclusion: 在(2,1)维AdS时空中,边界曲率K<-1的诱导度量唯一决定内部度量;任意两个共同充满S的实测层唯一决定GHMC AdS时空。Mess猜想成立。

Abstract: Let $S$ be a closed oriented surface of genus at least $2$. Let $X$ be a $(2,1)$-dimensional anti-de Sitter spacetime homeomorphic to $S\times[-1,1]$ with smooth, spacelike, strongly convex boundary. By Gauss' formula, the induced metric on each of the two components of $\partial X$ has curvature $K<-1$. In this paper we address the inverse problem. We show that, for any two smooth metrics $h_-, h_+$ on $S$ with curvature $K<-1$, there exists a unique, smooth anti-de Sitter metric on $X:=S\times [-1,1]$ with respect to which the boundary is locally strongly convex and has induced metric isotopic to $h_-$ on its past component and isotopic to $h_+$ on its future component. Existence has already been proven by Tamburelli.
  We use this result to solve affirmatively a conjecture of Mess: given any two measured laminations $l_-, l_+\in\mathcal{ML}_S$ that together fill $S$, there exists a unique $(2,1)$-dimensional, globally hyperbolic, maximal, Cauchy compact (GHMC) anti-de Sitter spacetime such that the measured bending laminations of the past and future boundary components of its convex core are respectively $l_-$ and $l_+$. Here, existence has already been proven by Bonsante--Schlenker.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Dang与Wrochna关于偶数维Lorentz情形Feynman复幂的局部分析推广到任意维、混合签名的伪黎曼散射空间。

Conclusion: 在任意维混合签名伪黎曼散射空间上建立了谱zeta密度的亚纯延拓理论，推广了既有Lorentz情形结果。

Abstract: We study spectral zeta densities associated with complex powers of principally scalar Laplace-type operators on pseudo-Riemannian scattering spaces of mixed signature and arbitrary dimension. The construction extends, beyond the even-dimensional Lorentzian case, the local analysis of Feynman complex powers developed by Dang and Wrochna. Namely, for $\varepsilon > 0$, we consider complex powers $(P\pm i\varepsilon)^{-α}$ and prove that the diagonal trace densities admit meromorphic continuation. We relate their poles to local geometric invariants, in particular to the pseudo-Riemannian scalar curvature. Our proof combines uniform Feynman resolvent estimates with a direct analysis of the Hadamard parametrix in pseudo-Riemannian signature. Beyond the Lorentzian case, the use of causal convexity is replaced by a geometric no-return argument.

</details>

Abstract: Constant curvature is one of the most fundamental intrinsic conditions in submanifold geometry. Investigating the existence and classification of biharmonic submanifolds under intrinsic curvature constraints is generally difficult, since the biharmonic equation is expressed in terms of extrinsic data. In this paper, we obtain a complete classification of biharmonic Lagrangian surfaces with constant Gaussian curvature in 2-dimensional complex space forms. As a consequence, we classify the biharmonic Lagrangian surfaces with flat normal bundle and we prove that pseudo-umbilical biharmonic Lagrangian surfaces are necessarily minimal.

Abstract: We show that the fundamental group is not locally stable under non-collapsed Gromov--Hausdorff convergence, even among closed Ricci-flat Kähler four-manifolds. Conclusion: 该工作将Wentzell谱理论从函数推广到微分形式，提供了显式谱、变分刻画、锐估计及跨阶间隙下界，为带边流形上形式型Steklov/Wentzell问题的后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We extend the Wentzell operator to differential forms on compact manifolds with boundary, investigate its spectrum, and establish a variational characterization of the first eigenvalue. We explicitly compute the eigenvalues on the unit ball in Euclidean space. We also derive several eigenvalue estimates for the first eigenvalue, some of which are shown to be sharp. Finally, we obtain a lower bound for the gap between the lowest eigenvalues in consecutive degrees.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在完备连通非紧无边黎曼流形 $(M^n,g)$ 上，假定其标量曲率 $R_g>0$，并施加无穷远处标量曲率衰减条件 $\liminf_{d_g(o,x)\to\infty}d_g(o,x)^2R_g(x)>\frac{n-1}{n}$（$o$ 为固定点）。据此构造一个新的完备光滑度量，使其标量曲率一致为正。技术细节（如共形形变或曲率控制手段）未在摘要中说明，但核心是通过该下界条件保证远离原点区域标量曲率的可控性。

Conclusion: 该结果将 Chen（三维、可定向）以及作者此前（$4\le n\le7$、可定向）的存在性定理统一推广为：对所有 $n\ge3$、无定向性要求的完备连通非紧流形均成立。

Abstract: Let $(M^n,g)$ be a complete connected noncompact smooth Riemannian manifold without boundary, with $n\ge3$ and positive scalar curvature. We prove that $M$ admits a complete smooth metric of uniformly positive scalar curvature if $$
  \liminf_{d_g(o,x)\to\infty}d_g(o,x)^2R_g(x)>\frac{n-1}{n} $$ for a fixed point $o\in M$. This extends earlier existence results for orientable manifolds, due to Chen in dimension three and to the authors in dimensions $4\le n\le7$, to every dimension $n\ge3$ without an orientability assumption.

</details>

Result: 建立了四类Manton涡旋方程解与X×(P¹/Δ/C)上等变全纯丛（带反自对偶联络的不变(伪)Hermitian度量）之间的一一对应（双向构造）。

Conclusion: Manton五种涡旋方程中的四种可统一理解为X×(P¹/Δ/C)上具有反自对偶联络的等变全纯丛的几何对象，群作用对应SU(2)、SU(1,1)、SE(2)；正反两方向构造成立。

Abstract: We study four of Manton's five vortex equations on a Riemann surface $X$ in relation to anti-self-dual connections. From a solution of each vortex equation, we construct an equivariant holomorphic bundle over $X$$\times$$\mathbb{P}^1$, $X$$\times$$Δ$, or $X$$\times$$\mathbb{C}$, together with an invariant Hermitian or pseudo-Hermitian metric whose associated connection is anti-self-dual. The equivariant structures are given by the actions of $SU(2)$, $SU(1,1)$, and $SE(2)$, respectively. Conversely, we show that an equivariant holomorphic bundle equipped with an invariant (pseudo-)Hermitian metric gives rise to a solution of the corresponding vortex equation when the associated connection is anti-self-dual.

</details>


### [99] [A Converse to the Archimedes Sphere Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06261)
*Mohammad Ghomi,Ralph Howard,Mijia Lai*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明：若欧氏3维空间中凸曲面被某固定宽度平板截得的面积只依赖于平板方向，则该曲面必为球面；结论对任意光滑闭嵌入曲面亦成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Blaschke与Stamm的定理。原定理要求对所有宽度都满足平板面积性质，本文将其弱化为仅需某一个固定宽度，并扩展到一般光滑闭嵌入曲面。

Method: 基于一个平均恒等式，将平板面积与曲面的牛顿位势联系起来；结合Mendez与Reichel关于球面的静电刻画，以及Radon变换的解析微局部分析性质；借助Kashiwara和Boman的定理，证明平板面积性质迫使曲面实解析。

Result: 证明了对某个固定宽度仅依赖方向的平板面积性质即可推出曲面为球面，并且该结论适用于任意光滑闭嵌入曲面，而不仅限于凸曲面。

Conclusion: 通过将平板面积性质与牛顿位势、静电刻画及Radon变换的解析微局部性质相联系，成功弱化了经典假设并推广了球面刻画定理。

Abstract: We show that a convex surface in Euclidean 3-space is a sphere if the area cut off from it by a slab of some fixed width depends only on the direction of the slab. This result generalizes theorems of Blaschke and Stamm, which assumed the slab-area property for every width. It holds also for any smooth closed embedded surface. The proof is based on an averaging identity, which relates slab areas to the Newtonian potential of the surface, the electrostatic characterization of spheres by Mendez and Reichel, and analytic microlocal properties of the Radon transform, which are used to show that the slab-area property forces real analyticity, via theorems of Kashiwara and Boman.

</details>


### [100] [The Lebrun--Salamon conjecture is true in dimension up to 56](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06336)
*Jordi Daura Serrano,Aron Gohr,Marie-Amélie Lawn,Travis Schedler*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明了四元数维数 n ≤ 14 的正四元数Kähler流形上的 LeBrun–Salamon 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明 LeBrun–Salamon 猜想在 n ≤ 14 情形成立，即排除正四元数Kähler流形的极小反例。

Method: 先证极小反例 M 的等距群 Isom(M) 秩至多为一、维数至多三；再考虑特征数（n 偶时为 dim Isom(M)-(n+3)，n 奇时为 dim Isom(M)-(n+1)），并在 n ≤ 14 时将其表示为若干正量之和以导出矛盾。正性工具分段升级：投影曲率矩（至 n=8）、半正定曲率矩（至 n=10）、带符号标量轨道矩（n=11,12），以及标量轨道矩结合上同调类平方的 Hodge–Riemann 正性（n=13,14）。

Result: 成功证明 n ≤ 14 的 LeBrun–Salamon 猜想，并给出解释各阶段方法切换的精确分离障碍。

Conclusion: LeBrun–Salamon 猜想在四元数维数不超过 14 时成立；证明中不同矩不等式与 Hodge–Riemann 正性的组合是克服各维数障碍的关键。

Abstract: We prove the LeBrun--Salamon conjecture for positive quaternionic Kähler manifolds of quaternionic dimension n <= 14. To do so, we first show that the isometry group Isom(M) of any minimal counterexample M has rank at most one, hence dimension at most three. On the other hand, we consider a characteristic number which equals dim Isom(M) - (n+3) if n is even and dim Isom(M) - (n+1) if n is odd, and show that this index is a sum of positive quantities when n <= 14, yielding a contradiction.
  The toolset used to show positivity enlarges in stages. Projection curvature moments suffice through n=8; positive-semidefinite curvature moments extend the argument through n=10; and signed scalar orbital moments give the new inequalities needed for n=11,12. Scalar orbital moments alone fail at n=13; combining them with Hodge--Riemann positivity for squares of cohomology classes extends the proof to n=13,14. We give exact separating obstructions explaining the changes of method.

</details>


### [101] [Geodesic orbit pseudo-Riemannian $H$-type nilmanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06375)
*Kenro Furutani,Irina Markina,Yurii Nikonorov*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了伪H-型群$N_{r,s}$的测地轨道(GO)性质：证明自然约化当且仅当$(r,s)\in\{(0,1),(1,2)\}$且此时对任意可容许模均为GO；$N_{3,4}$当且仅当$\mathfrak v$为极小可容许模时GO但非自然约化；其余情形均非GO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼H-型群的GO性质已由Kaplan与Riehm用中心维数和底Clifford模结构完全刻画，而伪H-型群（中心度量为不定号，$s\ge1$）的对应问题尚未解决。作者希望弄清：在中心度量不定时，GO性质是否如同黎曼情形仅由中心维数决定，还是受可容许模的明显限制。

Method: 研究对象为2步幂零Lie群$N_{r,s}$，其Lie代数$\mathfrak n_{r,s}=\mathfrak z\oplus\mathfrak v$由Clifford代数$\mathrm{Cl}(\mathbb R^{r,s})$的可容许模$\mathfrak v$构造，左不变度量在中心上限制为不定号。通过分析自然约化条件与GO条件的代数判据，结合可容许模的分类（含极小可容许模），对参数$(r,s)$分情形讨论。

Result: 1) $N_{r,s}$自然约化$\iff(r,s)\in\{(0,1),(1,2)\}$，且此时对每个可容许模都是GO的。2) $N_{3,4}$是GO但非自然约化，当且仅当$\mathfrak v$是极小可容许模。3) 其余所有情形$N_{r,s}$均非GO。4) 黎曼情形下7维中心可在维数8、16、24的同型模上带GO度量，而伪黎曼例外情形$N_{3,4}$仅对最小可能模GO。5) 对$N_{3,4}(\mathfrak v_{\min})$显式构造了生成全部测地线（含类光测地线）的单参数等距群，并描述了其生成元的仿射族。

Conclusion: 伪H-型群的GO性质与黎曼情形有本质差异：自然约化仅在$(0,1)$和$(1,2)$出现；$N_{3,4}$的GO性被压缩到极小的可容许模上，体现了不定中心度量对GO结构的强限制。该结果给出了伪H-型群GO问题的完整分类，并通过对极小模显式构造等距生成元刻画了例外情形。

Abstract: A pseudo-Riemannian manifold is called geodesic orbit (GO) if every geodesic is an orbit of a one-parameter group of isometries. For Riemannian $H$-type groups, the GO property was completely characterized by A. Kaplan and C. Riehm in terms of the dimension of the centre and the structure of the underlying Clifford module. We solve the corresponding problem for pseudo $H$-type groups $N_{r,s}$, that is, 2-step nilpotent Lie groups whose Lie algebra $\mathfrak n_{r,s}=\mathfrak z\oplus\mathfrak v$ is built from an admissible module $\mathfrak v$ of the Clifford algebra $\mathrm{Cl}(\mathbb R^{r,s})$, endowed with the left-invariant metric whose restriction to the centre is indefinite ($s\ge 1$). We prove that $N_{r,s}$ is naturally reductive if and only if $(r,s)\in\{(0,1),(1,2)\}$, and then it is GO for every admissible module. We also prove that $N_{3,4}$ is GO, but not naturally reductive, exactly when $\mathfrak v$ is a minimal admissible module, and that in all remaining cases $N_{r,s}$ is not GO. Thus, in contrast with the Riemannian situation, where a seven-dimensional centre admits GO metrics on isotypic modules of dimensions 8, 16 and 24, the exceptional pseudo-Riemannian case is GO only for the smallest possible module. For $N_{3,4}(\mathfrak v_{\min})$ we explicitly construct the one-parameter isometry groups generating all geodesics, including the null ones, and describe the affine family of their generators.

</details>


### [102] [Remarks on the critical catenoid and an overdetermined eigenvalue problem in $\Bbb S^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06386)
*Rabah Souam*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了单位球内浸入（可能带分支）且边界嵌入的自由边界极小环面必无分支点且嵌入，从而在更弱假设下确立critical catenoid的唯一性，并应用于S²上过定特征值问题的环域刚性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前结果仅对嵌入的自由边界极小环面证明其为critical catenoid。作者希望放宽假设：只要求浸入（允许分支）、且仅边界嵌入，考察唯一性是否仍成立，并将结论用于过定特征值问题的环域分类。

Method: 对流形上的浸入自由边界极小环面进行分析（结合分支点分析与自由边界理论），证明其边界嵌入条件下无分支点且整体嵌入；随后将该刚性结果应用于S²上的过定特征值问题（Δu+2u=0, u=0, |∇u|=1），提取环域几何结构。

Result: 浸入（可能带分支）、边界嵌入的自由边界极小环面在B³中必无分支点且为嵌入，即critical catenoid（旋转意义下唯一）。进一步，若S²中的环域Ω上存在满足过定特征值问题的光滑函数，则Ω在旋转意义下为具赤道对称性的旋转环域。

Conclusion: 将critical catenoid的唯一性推广到更弱的浸入假设；并给出S²过定特征值问题环域解的刚性刻画。

Abstract: It was recently proved that an embedded minimal annulus with free boundary in the unit ball $\Bbb B^3$ of the Euclidean space is, up to a rotation, the critical catenoid. We prove that an immersed, possibly branched, free boundary minimal annulus in $\Bbb B^3$ with embedded boundary components is necessarily free of branch points and is embedded. This shows the uniqueness of the critical catenoid holds under these weaker hypotheses. We then apply these results to prove that if $Ω$ is an annulus in $\Bbb S^2$ for which there exists a smooth function satisfying the overdetermined eigenvalue problem \begin{equation*} \begin{cases} Δu+ 2u = 0 \quad \text{on} \quad Ω\\ \qquad \quad u=0 \quad \text{in}\quad\partialΩ\\ \quad\,\,\,\, |\nabla u|=1 \quad \text{in}\quad \partialΩ. \end{cases} \end{equation*} then $Ω$ is, up to rotation, a rotational annulus with equatorial symmetry.

</details>


### [103] [Characterization of Sasakian Manifolds by Curvature Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06463)
*Sourav Nayak,Vladimir Rovenski,Dhriti Sundar Patra*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 利用部分Ricci流，在$(κ,μ)$-零化度条件下刻画了w.c.m.流形中的Sasakian结构，并给出了bi-Legendrian结构与分类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sasakian流形在微分几何与数学物理中处于核心地位，而弱接触度量（w.c.m.）结构是其更广泛的推广。核心问题是理解曲率条件如何约束底层接触度量结构，特别是如何将Sasakian几何与其更弱的变体区分开来。

Method: 采用部分Ricci流（partial Ricci flow）作为主要工具，结合与曲率相关的$(κ,μ)$-零化度条件进行分析。

Result: 在$(κ,μ)$-零化度条件下刻画了w.c.m.流形中的Sasakian结构；当$κ<1$时，给出了w.c.m.流形容许bi-Legendrian结构的条件；当$κ=μ=0$时，建立了分裂与分类结果。

Conclusion: 该工作通过部分Ricci流将曲率零化度条件与Sasakian几何的刻画联系起来，深化了对弱接触度量结构的分类理解。

Abstract: Sasakian manifolds, a subclass of contact metric manifolds, play a central role in modern differential geometry and mathematical physics, where they arise naturally in geometric mechanics, CR geometry, and supersymmetric field theories. The weak contact metric (w.c.m.) structure generalizes the contact metric structure and provides a broader framework for studying contact geometry and its applications. A fundamental problem is to understand how curvature conditions constrain the underlying contact metric structure and, in particular, how they distinguish Sasakian geometry from its weaker variants. Using the partial Ricci flow, we characterize Sasakian structure (among w.c.m. manifolds) under the $(κ,μ)$-nullity condition related to curvature. For $κ<1$ we find conditions under which a w.c.m. manifold admits a bi-Legendrian structure, and for $κ=μ=0$ establish splitting and classification results.

</details>


### [104] [Volume Entropy and Scalar Curvature near a Hyperbolic Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06466)
*Xiaodong Wang,Bo Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在闭双曲流形上计算了体积熵在标量曲率约束下的Hessian，用无迹横向对称二张量表示；三维情形Hessian负定，任何标量曲率≥-6的足够近度量熵至多为2且等号仅当为g0的拉回；n≥4时Hessian符号由普适谱阈值决定，非零无迹Codazzi张量给出正方向，双曲弯曲给出光滑约束熵鞍点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究体积熵在标量曲率约束下的变分稳定性，判断双曲度量在固定标量曲率类中是否为熵的极大或极小点，并揭示维数对稳定性行为的影响。

Method: 在闭双曲流形(M^n,g0)（n≥3）上，于约束Sc_g=-n(n-1)下计算体积熵的Hessian；将其表示为作用在无迹横向（transverse-traceless）对称二张量上的二次型；三维时分析其负定性，高维时通过谱阈值分析符号，并考察无迹Codazzi张量的贡献及双曲弯曲构造。

Result: 三维中Hessian负定，且一切足够C^∞接近、满足Sc_g≥-6的度量熵≤2，等号恰为g0的拉回。n≥4时Hessian符号由普适谱阈值决定；非零无迹Codazzi张量给出正方向，从而在任意n≥4维给出光滑的约束熵鞍点。

Conclusion: 体积熵在双曲度量处的约束稳定性强烈依赖于维数：三维是严格的局部极大，而高维出现由谱阈值与Codazzi张量导致的鞍点现象。

Abstract: Let $(M^n,g_0)$ be a closed hyperbolic manifold, $n\geq3$. We compute the Hessian of volume entropy at $g_0$ under the constraint $Sc_g=-n(n-1)$ and express it as a quadratic form on transverse-traceless symmetric two-tensors. In dimension three, the Hessian is negative definite, and every sufficiently $C^\infty$-close metric with $Sc_g\geq-6$ has entropy at most $2$, with equality precisely for pullbacks of $g_0$. In dimensions $n\geq4$, a universal spectral threshold determines the Hessian sign. Nonzero trace-free Codazzi tensors give positive directions, and hyperbolic bending provides smooth constrained entropy saddles in every such dimension.

</details>


### [105] [On Polynomial Complex Hessian Equations I: Non-Linear Non-Spectral Elliptic Hessian Equations on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06529)
*Rémi Delloque*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究紧致黎曼流形上一类完全非线性椭圆Hessian方程，将Caffarelli-Nirenberg-Spruck关于方程是Hessian特征值对称函数的假设减弱，在适当假设下建立先验估计，并用连续性方法求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Caffarelli-Nirenberg-Spruck工作要求方程是Hessian特征值的对称函数，这一假设较强、限制了适用范围。本文旨在放宽该对称性假设，研究更一般的完全非线性椭圆Hessian方程。

Method: 在紧致黎曼流形上研究一般完全非线性椭圆Hessian方程；在比对称性更弱的假设下，先建立解的先验估计，再通过连续性方法证明解的存在性。

Result: 在对方程的适当假设下成功导出了解的先验估计，并借助连续性方法完成了方程的求解。

Conclusion: 减弱对称性假设后仍可对完全非线性椭圆Hessian方程建立先验估计并求解，推广了Caffarelli-Nirenberg-Spruck的经典结果。

Abstract: We study a general class of fully non-linear elliptic Hessian equations on compact Riemannian manifolds. In particular, we replace the standard assumption that the equation is a symmetric function of the eigenvalues of the Hessian made by Caffarelli, Nirenberg and Spruck by a weaker one. After deriving a priori estimates for solutions under suitable assumptions on the equation, we solve it with a continuity method.

</details>


### [106] [Rigidity of conformal metrics with constant sixth-order $Q$-curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06573)
*Daowen Lin,Wangzhe Wu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在$n\ge6$维闭连通正数量曲率Einstein流形上，利用Obata型恒等式证明：与$g_0$共形的常六阶$Q$-曲率光滑度量必为Einstein度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高阶$Q$-曲率（六阶）在共形类中的刚性，推广经典Obata型刚性结果到高阶共形不变量框架。

Method: 采用Obata型恒等式作为核心工具，结合共形几何中六阶$Q$-曲率的变分/积分估计，分析常$Q$-曲率共形度量的结构。

Result: 证明任意与$g_0$共形且具常六阶$Q$-曲率的光滑度量$g$必为Einstein度量，即共形类内具常六阶$Q$-曲率的度量为Einstein度量。

Conclusion: 在$n\ge6$、闭连通、正数量曲率Einstein条件下，常六阶$Q$-曲率给出强刚性，迫使共形度量回到Einstein情形。

Abstract: Let $(M^n,g_0)$ be a closed connected Einstein manifold with positive scalar curvature, where $n\ge6$. Using an Obata-type identity, we prove that every smooth metric conformal to $g_0$ with constant sixth-order $Q$-curvature is Einstein.

</details>


<div id='math.CO'></div>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [All polyhedral graphs with at most 16 vertices are Schurian: a computational verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03729)
*David Reh*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文对多面体图的Schurian性质进行穷举计算验证：所有顶点数不超过16的多面体图（共413,024,847,068个）均非反例，均为Schurian图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Li、Ponomarenko和Zeman猜想每个多面体图（3连通平面图）都是Schurian的，即其相干闭包WL(X)与Aut(X)的轨道构型一致；结合其Theorem 1.1将推出每个多面体图的Weisfeiler-Leman维数至多为2。作者希望以计算方式验证这一猜想。已有相干构型枚举结果仅能覆盖n≤13，n≥14缺乏文献支撑，故需独立计算验证。

Method: 使用plantri生成所有多面体图；先施加单侧1-WL离散性过滤器筛除，再对存活图完整比较Ω^2上的稳定2-WL划分与Aut(X)的轨道划分。整个过程受已发表锚点计数、两个最大阶段的种子重枚举、独立nauty交叉校验以及数学判定固定的对照图守护；代码、检查点与验证脚本作为附属文件提供。

Result: 共验证413,024,847,068个顶点数≤16的多面体图，无一非Schurian。n≤13的结果与已发表相干构型枚举经不同路径所得一致；n=14起从该侧推导需要文献中未找到的步骤；n=16时全图断言失败——Shrikhande图非Schurian，说明此处多面体限制起关键作用。伴随笔记指出环面一侧在n=16时确实出现非Schurian三角剖分图。

Conclusion: 该结论由穷举计算建立，相对于一般猜想属于实例证据而非证明。它支持Li-Ponomarenko-Zeman猜想，并将多面体图Weisfeiler-Leman维数上界2的推断扩展到n=16的情形。

Abstract: Li, Ponomarenko and Zeman state their belief that every polyhedral graph (i.e. every $3$-connected planar graph) is Schurian: that its coherent closure $WL(X)$ coincides with the orbital configuration of $Aut(X)$. Combined with their Theorem 1.1, this would imply that every polyhedral graph has Weisfeiler-Leman dimension at most $2$. We report a computational verification of the Schurian property for all polyhedral graphs with at most $16$ vertices, a total of 413,024,847,068 graphs, none of which is non-Schurian. The computation combines the generator plantri with a one-sided $1$-WL discreteness filter and, for every graph surviving that filter, a full comparison of the stable $2$-WL partition of $Ω^2$ with the orbit partition of $Aut(X)$. The computation is guarded by the published anchor counts, a seeded re-enumeration of the two largest stages, independent nauty cross-checks and control graphs with mathematically fixed verdicts; code, checkpoints and a verification script accompany the note as ancillary files. For $n\le 13$ the result reproduces what already follows from the published enumeration of coherent configurations, by an entirely different route; from $n=14$ on, deriving it from that side would require a step we could not find in the literature, and at $n=16$ the all-graphs statement fails outright - the Shrikhande graph is non-Schurian - so there the polyhedral restriction carries the result. The statement is established by exhaustive computation; with respect to the general conjecture it is instance evidence, not a proof. A companion note treats the torus side, where non-Schurian triangulation graphs do occur at $n = 16$.

</details>


### [108] [Branch-Sensitive Density Increments for $k$-Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03750)
*Yao Zhi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文改进了Beker关于$k$-configuration的密度增量论证，通过在图递归中保留固定矩以避免输出被压缩为单一均匀情形，得到三类输出并以带权迭代预算控制局部成本，最终将指数从$68$降至$36$，并得到指数$c<1/36$的sum-free下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Beker的$k$-configuration密度增量论证在将图计数输出压缩为单一均匀替代时会丢失信息。作者希望保留这些被丢失的信息以改进密度增量，从而降低最终界中的指数。

Method: 针对$k$-configuration问题中出现的传递$K_k$，在图递归过程中保持一个固定矩。由此得到三种真正不同的输出：行度异常、非中心网格异常、中心网格异常。中心情形通过相应Gram矩的正性直接处理，避免通常的unbalancing步骤。每种输出通过相应的局部Kelley–Meka机制处理，各自带有密度增益、秩代价和宽度代价，并用带权迭代预算避免最昂贵的局部分支被计入最大次数。

Result: 对奇阶有限交换群$G$及密度为$α$的$A\subseteq G$，令$L=\log(2/α)$、$Λ=L+\log(2k)$，得到下界$\Pp_{x_1,\ldots,x_k\in G}((x_i+x_j)/2\in A\ \forall 1\le i\le j\le k)\ge\exp(-CΦ(k,L,Λ))$，其中$Φ=k^{36}L^6Λ^6+k^{34}L^8Λ^5+k^{32}L^{14}Λ+k^{30}L^{16}$。在Erdős–Moser应用中将Beker路线中的指数$68$替换为$36$，并得到指数$c<1/36$的sum-free下界。

Conclusion: 通过保留图递归中的固定矩并细分输出类型，可以改进密度增量论证，显著降低$k$-configuration及sum-free问题中的指数（由$68$降至$36$，sum-free指数$c<1/36$）。

Abstract: We refine the density-increment argument for $k$-configurations developed by Beker. The point is not to introduce a new inverse theorem, but to retain information that is lost when the graph-counting output is compressed into a single uniform alternative. For the transitive $K_k$ appearing in the $k$-configuration problem we keep a fixed moment throughout the graph recursion and obtain three genuinely different outputs: a row-degree anomaly, a non-centred grid anomaly, and a centred grid anomaly. The centred case is treated directly through positivity of the associated Gram moments, avoiding the usual unbalancing step. Each output is then routed through the appropriate local Kelley--Meka mechanism with its own density gain, rank cost, and width cost. A weighted iteration budget prevents the most expensive local branches from being charged the maximal number of times.
  For a finite abelian group $G$ of odd order and $A\subseteq G$ of density $α$, write $L=\log(2/α)$ and $Λ=L+\log(2k)$. We obtain a lower bound of the form \[ \Pp_{x_1,\ldots,x_k\in G}\!\left(\frac{x_i+x_j}{2}\in A\ \text{for all }1\le i\le j\le k\right) \ge \exp\!\bigl(-CΦ(k,L,Λ)\bigr), \] where \[ Φ(k,L,Λ)=k^{36}L^6Λ^6+k^{34}L^8Λ^5+k^{32}L^{14}Λ+k^{30}L^{16}. \] Consequently, in the Erdős--Moser application one may replace the exponent $68$ in Beker's $k$-configuration route by $36$; in particular the same reduction yields the sum-free lower bound with every exponent $c<1/36$.

</details>


### [109] [$\ell_{1}$-Embeddability of Regular Quadrilateral Maps on the Torus and the Klein Bottle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03770)
*Guangfu Wang,Wenchao Cong,Chunxiao Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文对环面与克莱因瓶上的正则四边形地图的 $\ell_1$-可嵌入性给出了完整的参数级分类：环面家族中恰为 $Q_{k,m,0}, Q_{k,2,1}, Q_{k,1,e}, Q_{5,2,e}, Q_{2i,2,e}\,(i\ge2)$ 可嵌入，克莱因瓶家族中恰为 $Q_{2,2,a}, Q_{4,2,a}, Q_{2,m,b}, Q_{1,2,h}$ 可嵌入，其余均不可嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\ell_1$-可嵌入性是图度量几何中的核心性质，等价于（有限情形下）可标度嵌入超立方体，与图的超立方体性、部分立方体结构及中位数图等密切相关。环面与克莱因瓶上的正则四边形地图是一类自然且结构丰富的曲面网格图，此前缺乏对其 $\ell_1$-可嵌入性的系统性刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白并给出完全分类。

Method: 将环面上的正则四边形地图归结为两个环面家族 $Q_{k,m,r}$ 与 $Q_{k,m,e}$，二者由有限方格网经适当粘合得到；利用克莱因瓶四边形地图之间已知的同构关系，将其归结为三个本质家族 $Q_{k,m,a}$、$Q_{k,m,b}$、$Q_{k,m,h}$。在此基础上，对这些参数化家族逐一分析其最短路度量是否可等距嵌入 $\ell_1$-空间（等价地是否具有超立方体标度嵌入），从而给出参数级的判定条件。

Result: $\ell_1$-可嵌入的环面地图恰为 $Q_{k,m,0}$、$Q_{k,2,1}$、$Q_{k,1,e}$、$Q_{5,2,e}$、$Q_{2i,2,e}\,(i\ge2)$；$\ell_1$-可嵌入的克莱因瓶地图恰为 $Q_{2,2,a}$、$Q_{4,2,a}$、$Q_{2,m,b}$、$Q_{1,2,h}$。这五个家族的所有其他成员均不可 $\ell_1$-嵌入。

Conclusion: 本文完成了环面与克莱因瓶上正则四边形地图 $\ell_1$-可嵌入性的完整参数级分类，明确列出了两个曲面族中所有可嵌入的参数条件，并证明其余情形均不可嵌入。

Abstract: A connected graph is called $\ell_1$-embeddable if its shortest-path metric admits an isometric embedding into the $\ell_1$-space; equivalently, in the finite case, it admits a scale embedding into a hypercube. This paper gives a complete parameter-level classification of $\ell_1$-embeddability for regular quadrilateral maps on the torus and the Klein bottle. We treat the two toroidal families $Q_{k,m,r}$ and $Q_{k,m,e}$, obtained from suitable identifications of finite square grids, and, using the known isomorphisms among Klein-bottle quadrilateral maps, the three essential Klein-bottle families $Q_{k,m,a}$, $Q_{k,m,b}$, and $Q_{k,m,h}$. The $\ell_1$-embeddable toroidal maps are exactly
  $Q_{k,m,0}, Q_{k,2,1}, Q_{k,1,e}, Q_{5,2,e}, Q_{2i,2,e} (i\ge 2)$,
  and the $\ell_1$-embeddable Klein-bottle maps are exactly $
  Q_{2,2,a}, Q_{4,2,a}, Q_{2,m,b}, Q_{1,2,h}.$
  All other members of these five families are not $\ell_1$-embeddable.

</details>


### [110] [Upper Domination in Rectangular Grid Graphs and Its Relation to Sub-Quorum Colorings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03785)
*Rafik Sahbi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文指出，矩形网格图 \(G_{m,n}=P_m\square P_n\) 的上支配数可由二部图的经典等式 \(\Gamma(G)=\alpha(G)\) 直接确定，即 \(\Gamma(G_{m,n})=\lceil mn/2\rceil\)；随后比较其与 2-独立数和 sub-quorum 着色数的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2023 年的一项计算研究将方格图（square grid graphs）的上支配数列为尚无一般公式的情形。作者注意到，对于二部图，经典结论 \(\Gamma(G)=\alpha(G)\) 可直接推广到任意矩形网格图，从而填补这一空白，并进一步研究上支配数与相关参数的关系。

Method: 给出两个简短证明：一是通过二部图的上支配定理（bipartite upper-domination theorem），二是通过匹配界 \(\Gamma(G)+\alpha'(G)\le |V(G)|\)。随后将上支配数与网格图的 2-独立数及 sub-quorum 着色数进行比较。

Result: 得到矩形网格图上支配数的精确公式 \(\Gamma(G_{m,n})=\lceil mn/2\rceil\)；并建立无条件不等式链 \(\Gamma(G_{m,n})\le \beta_2(G_{m,n})\le \psi_{\mathrm{sq}}(G_{m,n})\)，同时给出 \(\Gamma(G_{m,n})\) 与 \(\beta_2(G_{m,n})\) 之间间隙的显式公式。

Conclusion: 矩形网格图的上支配数可由二部图性质完全确定，无需依赖复杂计算方法；该结果进一步统一并细化了网格图上上支配数、2-独立数与 sub-quorum 着色数之间的定量关系。

Abstract: For a graph $G$, the upper domination number $Γ(G)$ is the maximum cardinality of a minimal dominating set. A 2023 computational study listed the upper domination number of square grid graphs among the cases for which no general formula was known. We observe that the classical equality $Γ(G)=α(G)$ for bipartite graphs immediately determines the parameter for every rectangular grid graph $G_{m,n}=P_m\square P_n$. Namely, \[ Γ(G_{m,n})=\left\lceil\frac{mn}{2}\right\rceil. \] We give two short proofs, one through the classical bipartite upper-domination theorem and one through the matching bound $Γ(G)+α'(G)\le |V(G)|$. We then compare upper domination with the $2$-independence number and the sub-quorum coloring number of grid graphs. This yields the unconditional chain \[ Γ(G_{m,n})\le β_2(G_{m,n})\le ψ_{\mathrm{sq}}(G_{m,n}), \] and an explicit formula for the gap between $Γ(G_{m,n})$ and $β_2(G_{m,n})$.

</details>


### [111] [Asymptotic Spectral Bounds under Odd-Girth Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05172)
*Chen Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究在奇围长约束下，图的最大与最小邻接特征值之和（λ₁+λₙ）/n 的渐近行为，通过抽象矩问题给出上下界，并在正则图上得到更优且阶数最优的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇围长与（λ₁+λₙ）之和都是衡量图接近二部图程度的常用指标。论文旨在刻画在奇围长至少为 g 的约束下，这一特征值和随 n 归一化后的渐近上确界，从而量化奇围长对该和的影响。

Method: 将问题抽象为有限实数序列上的矩问题：施加由奇围长至少为 g 所要求的奇数阶矩消失恒等式，以及图谱的标准二阶矩上界。在该约束集上分析 (λ₁+λₙ)/n 的上确界，并针对正则图（奇围长 g≥5）推导更强的组合/谱界。

Result: 当 g 沿奇数趋于无穷时，该上确界介于 (1-o(1))(log g)²/g³ 与 (10/7+o(1))(log g)²/g³ 之间；上界特别适用于奇围长至少为 g 的图。对奇围长 g≥5 的正则图，得到更强界 (λ₁+λₙ)/n ≤ 12/((g-1)(g-2)(g-3))，且在常数因子意义下最优。

Conclusion: 奇围长约束下 (λ₁+λₙ)/n 的渐近量级为 Θ((log g)²/g³)；正则图情形可由 g 的多项式倒数精确控制，且所给界在常数因子意义下最优。

Abstract: Odd girth and the sum of the largest and smallest adjacency eigenvalues are two common indicators of how close a graph is to being bipartite. This paper investigates the asymptotic behavior of this eigenvalue sum under odd-girth constraints. We study the associated abstract moment problem over finite real lists satisfying the vanishing odd-moment identities imposed by odd girth at least $g$ and the standard second-moment bound for graph spectra. We prove that the supremum of $(λ_1+λ_n)/n$ over these lists lies between $(1-o(1))(\log g)^2/g^3$ and $(10/7+o(1))(\log g)^2/g^3$ as $g\to\infty$ through the odd integers. The upper bound applies in particular to graphs of odd girth at least $g$.
  For regular graphs of odd girth $g\geq5$, we obtain a stronger bound $(λ_1+λ_n)/n \leq 12/((g-1)(g-2)(g-3))$, which is optimal up to a constant factor.

</details>


### [112] [Spectral Incidence Bounds over Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03819)
*Yao Zhi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限域上一类双参数族曲线的关联计数问题，证明了一致谱估计，并通过系数曲线的Lüroth分解揭示误差尺度本质上取决于曲线度数而非参数化多项式的原始次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限域上研究参数化曲线族的关联（incidence）估计，特别是当参数化多项式F、G的原始次数很大时，误差尺度是否必然变差。作者希望揭示：真正的度量应是系数曲线本身的复杂度，而非所选参数化的次数，从而在任意大原始次数下仍能获得Vinh尺度的误差。

Method: 结合两次加性傅里叶变换与Kloosterman矩阵的四阶矩估计，证明一般情形下的谱估计；对度数1≤d,e≤2且特征大于3的情形，采用中点-差分坐标与有限Weyl量子化将误差改进到Vinh尺度。进一步利用多项式Lüroth分解（F=f∘H, G=g∘H，唯一到H的仿射变换），通过有限纤维拉回控制中心化关联范数。

Result: 当char(Fq)>d+e时，对任意子集P,Ω得到|I(P,Ω)-|P||Ω|/q| ≪_{d,e} q^{5/8}√(|P||Ω|)；若1≤d,e≤2且char(Fq)>3，误差改进为O(q^{1/2}√(|P||Ω|))。更一般地，若系数曲线Γ度数≤2且char(Fq)>3，则误差为O(√κ_H · q^{1/2}√(|P||Ω|))，即使F、G原始次数任意大，其中κ_H为H纤维最大重数。该结果对J(x)+yF(x)+zG(x)+λyz的展开估计无需输入集的下界。

Conclusion: 关联误差尺度本质上由系数曲线的几何（度数）决定，而非参数化多项式的原始次数；Lüroth分解与有限纤维拉回给出误差因子的显式控制。均衡情形下q^3缺失值尺度已由Arala–Chow定理覆盖，而本文关联定理进一步给出强非均衡情形下的展开推论。

Abstract: Let $\Fq$ be a finite field, let $F,G\in\Fq[T]$ be nonconstant, and let $J\in\Fq[T]$. We study the parameterized two-parameter family \[ C_{a,b}=\{(x,t)\in\Fq^2:t=J(x)+aF(x)+bG(x)+λab\}, \qquad (a,b)\in\Fq^2, \] where $λ\in\Fq^\times$, with incidences counted with parameter multiplicity. If $d=°F$, $e=°G$, and $\charac(\Fq)>d+e$, we prove the uniform spectral estimate \[ \left|I(\cP,Ω)-\frac{|\cP||Ω|}{q}\right| \ll_{d,e}q^{5/8}\sqrt{|\cP||Ω|} \] for arbitrary $\cP,Ω\subseteq\Fq^2$. The proof combines two additive Fourier transforms with a fourth-moment estimate for Kloosterman matrices. If $1\le d,e\le2$ and $\charac(\Fq)>3$, midpoint--difference coordinates and finite Weyl quantization sharpen the error to the Vinh scale $O(q^{1/2}\sqrt{|\cP||Ω|})$.
  We further show that the latter phenomenon is intrinsic to the coefficient curve rather than to the degree of a chosen parametrization. There is a polynomial Lüroth factorization \[ F=f\circ H,\qquad G=g\circ H,\qquad \Fq(f,g)=\Fq(T), \] unique up to an affine change of $H$. If $Γ$ is the plane curve parametrized properly by $(f,g)$ and \[ κ_H=\max_{s\in\Fq}|H^{-1}(s)|, \] then finite-fibre pullback multiplies the centered incidence norm by at most $\sqrt{κ_H}$. Consequently, if $\degΓ\le2$ and $\charac(\Fq)>3$, then \[ \left|I(\cP,Ω)-\frac{|\cP||Ω|}{q}\right| \ll \sqrt{κ_H}\,q^{1/2}\sqrt{|\cP||Ω|}, \] even when $F$ and $G$ have arbitrarily large raw degree. The resulting expansion estimates for $J(x)+yF(x)+zG(x)+λyz$ hold without lower bounds on the individual input sets. In the balanced regime, the $q^3$ missing-value scale for this family is already covered by a theorem of Arala--Chow; the incidence theorems here additionally yield expansion consequences in strongly unbalanced regimes.

</details>


### [113] [An Improved Upper Bound for Recovering Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03851)
*Simone Costa*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了n元集合上任意非空子集族恢复对(A,B)满足|A||B| ≤ (2.23949)^n，改进了此前由Mond-Souza-Versteegen与Fang-Huang结果结合所得的(2.2499)^n界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恢复对(recovering pairs)的极值界此前为(2.2499)^n，源于Mond、Souza和Versteegen的结果与Fang和Huang关于消去对(cancellative pairs)的(9/4)^n紧界相结合。作者希望收紧该指数常数，得到更优的上界。

Method: 证明结合了两个熵不等式：其一直接由恢复性质推导；其二对通过Mond-Souza-Versteegen构造从原对得到的族应用Fang-Huang界。随后通过凸组合将两个不等式合并。

Result: 证明了任意n元集合上非空子集族的恢复对(A,B)满足|A||B| ≤ (223949/100000)^n = (2.23949)^n，较此前的(2.2499)^n有明显改进。

Conclusion: 通过熵方法与凸组合技术，恢复对的乘积上界被改进为(2.23949)^n，推进了该领域极值问题的研究。

Abstract: We prove that every recovering pair $(\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B})$ of nonempty families of subsets of an $n$-element set satisfies $$|\mathcal{A}||\mathcal{B}|\le\left(\frac{223949}{100000}\right)^n=(2.23949)^n.$$ A result of Mond, Souza and Versteegen, combined with the sharp $(9/4)^n$ bound of Fang and Huang for cancellative pairs, gives $(2.2499)^n$ for recovering pairs. We improve this to $(2.23949)^n$. The proof combines two entropy inequalities. One is derived directly from the recovering property, while the other applies the Fang-Huang bound to families obtained from the original pair by a construction of Mond, Souza and Versteegen. The two inequalities are then combined by a convex combination.

</details>


### [114] [Exact and asymptotic enumeration of unrestricted binary phylogenetic networks through automorphism weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03856)
*Josep Batle*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文为带 $\ell$ 个叶、$k$ 个网状结点的无平行边根二叉系统发育网络建立了计数理论：将总数分解为加权计数 $W_k(\ell)$ 与缺陷 $D_k(\ell)$ 并给出递推与闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统发育网络（含网状结构）的精确计数此前只到 $k\le4$，可用的递推往往需要枚举组件图或区分对称/非对称情形，且自同构群在 $k$ 较大时不再只是初等阿贝尔群，因此需要更具结构性、可推广的计数框架。

Method: 定义 $\cP_{\ell,k}$（$\ell$ 叶、$k$ 个网状结点、无平行边的根二叉网络），写 $|\cP_{\ell,k}|=W_k(\ell)+D_k(\ell)$，其中 $W_k$ 以叶固定自同构群阶的倒数加权，$D_k$ 为缺陷。对每个 $k$，沿树组件结构的源层建立不依赖组件图列表、也不区分对称/非对称配置的递推，其指数生成函数是 $\sqrt{1-2x}$ 的 Laurent 多项式。对 $k\le5$，证明缺陷等于带一个被标记对合的网络的加权计数，并满足同一递推的扩展形式，再进行精确符号求解。

Result: 对 $k\le5$ 给出 $|\cP_{\ell,k}|$ 的闭式（$k=5$ 为新结果），重现了 $k\le4$ 的已发表计数，并修正了 $k=3$ 的已发表生成函数中的一个系数。证明网络自同构群为 $2$-群，$k\le5$ 时为初等阿贝尔群但一般不是。对每个 $k$（在显式 $k$ 区间上一致地）证明非树子网络的数目主阶为树子网络的 $2k(k-1)/\ell$ 倍，从而给出 $|\cP_{\ell,k}|$ 渐近展开的第三项；还证明 reticulation-visible 网络超出树子网络的比例为 $k(k-1)/\ell$，且 $\cP_{\ell,k}$ 中一致随机网络以概率 $k(k-1)/(4\ell^3)$ 具非平凡自同构（主阶）。

Conclusion: 该框架通过加权计数与缺陷的分解，避免了组件枚举与对称性分类，统一给出了小 $k$ 的精确闭式与大 $k$、大 $\ell$ 的渐近主阶项，并量化了自同构的稀有性与各类网络类之间的比例关系。

Abstract: Let $\cP_{\ell,k}$ be the set of rooted binary phylogenetic networks with $\ell$ labelled leaves, $k$ reticulations and no parallel edges. We write $|\cP_{\ell,k}|=W_k(\ell)+D_k(\ell)$, where the weighted count $W_k(\ell)$ adds the inverse orders of the leaf-fixing automorphism groups and the defect $D_k(\ell)$ collects the remainder. The weighted count satisfies, for every $k$, a recursion over the source layers of the tree-component structure that involves neither a list of component graphs nor any distinction between symmetric and asymmetric configurations, and its exponential generating function is a Laurent polynomial in $\sqrt{1-2x}$. Automorphism groups of networks are $2$-groups, elementary abelian for $k\le5$ but not in general. For $k\le5$ the defect is the weighted count of networks with a distinguished involution, which obeys an extension of the same recursion. An exact symbolic evaluation of the two recursions yields $|\cP_{\ell,k}|$ in closed form for $k\le5$, the case $k=5$ being new; it reproduces the published counts for $k\le4$ and corrects a coefficient in a published generating function for $k=3$. For every $k$, uniformly over explicit ranges of $k$, we prove that the non-tree-child networks are a fraction $2k(k-1)/\ell$ of the tree-child networks to leading order, which gives the third term of the asymptotic expansion of $|\cP_{\ell,k}|$. We also prove that reticulation-visible networks exceed tree-child networks by the fraction $k(k-1)/\ell$, and that a uniformly random network in $\cP_{\ell,k}$ has a nontrivial automorphism with probability $k(k-1)/(4\ell^3)$ to leading order.

</details>


### [115] [The Zero Forcing Number of Graph Powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03892)
*Aida Abiad,Mary Flagg,Sina Ghasemi Nezhad,Sjanne Zeijlemaker*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究简单图 $k$ 次幂 $G^k$ 的零迫数，证明零迫参数随取幂单调，确定路与圈的幂的零迫数，给出蜘蛛图与网格图幂的上界，并建立基于基图谱的谱下界及线性规划计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图的幂更稠密，通常不被现有零迫数结果所涵盖，需采用不同方法研究；且与边删除下零迫行为形成对比，探索取幂下零迫参数的单调性。

Method: 结合图论、线性代数与多项式优化技术：证明零迫参数（及其变体）关于取幂单调；确定路与圈的幂的零迫数并给出蜘蛛图、网格图幂的上界；利用基图谱建立谱下界，并用线性规划方法计算这些界。

Result: 零迫参数及其变体在取幂下单调；确定了路与圈的幂的零迫数；得到蜘蛛图与网格图幂的零迫数上界；给出仅依赖基图谱的谱下界，并提供线性规划计算方法，同时研究所得界的紧致性。

Conclusion: 图幂的零迫数可通过图论、线性代数与多项式优化相结合的方法有效研究，取幂下零迫参数呈现单调性，谱与线性规划为下界估计提供了新工具。

Abstract: The $k$-th power of a simple graph $G$, denoted $G^k$, is the graph with vertex set $V(G)$ where two vertices are adjacent if they are within distance $k$ in $G$. We investigate the zero forcing number of graph powers. Powers of graphs are much denser and generally not encompassed by existing results on zero forcing of graphs, hence their study requires a different approach. In contrast with the usual zero forcing behavior under edge deletion, we show that the zero forcing parameter (and variations of it) is monotone with respect to taking powers. We also determine the zero forcing number of powers of paths and cycles, together with upper bounds for powers of spiders and of grids. We then present spectral lower bounds on the zero forcing number of graph powers which uniquely use the spectrum of the base graph, as well as linear programming methods to compute these bounds. Finally, we study the sharpness of the derived bounds. To derive our results we use techniques ranging from graph theory, linear algebra and polynomial optimization.

</details>


### [116] [Restrictions of the dot action representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04110)
*Hsin-Chieh Liao,Martha Precup,John Shareshian*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将任意Weyl群的dot action表示限制到A型抛物子群，证明其同构于A型dot action表示的直接和，并对B/C、D型给出染色对称函数组合公式，进而证明Lesnevich猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受"所有抛物子群上的类似限制性质"这一猜想驱动，研究正半单Hessenberg簇上调和表示（dot action表示）在抛物子群限制下的结构，并解决Lesnevich关于B、C型理想对应表示同构性的猜想。

Method: 利用A型下正半单Hessenberg簇上调和可通过自然标号单位区间图的染色拟对称函数计算这一事实，将任意Weyl群的dot action表示限制到A型抛物子群并进行分解；对B/C、D型经典Weyl群使用染色对称函数给出限制到特定极大A型抛物子群的组合公式。

Result: 证明任意Weyl群的dot action表示限制到A型抛物子群同构于若干A型dot action表示的直接和；对B/C、D型给出限制到极大A型抛物子群的简洁组合公式；证明Lesnevich猜想：B型与C型理想在标准偏序同构下对应的dot action表示同构当且仅当两理想在各自根系中确定相同的反射集；并证明此类理想总数由Catalan数计数。

Conclusion: A型抛物限制下的分解性得到验证，为该性质推广到所有抛物子群的猜想提供支持；染色对称函数是研究经典Weyl群dot action表示限制的有效工具，并由此完整刻画了B、C型理想对应的表示同构条件及其计数。

Abstract: Regular semisimple Hessenberg varieties are subvarieties of flag varieties defined for any reductive group. Their cohomology carries a representation of the associated Weyl group, known as the dot action representation. In type $A$, these representations can be computed using the chromatic quasisymmetric functions of naturally labeled unit interval graphs.
  We prove that restricting the dot action representation of any Weyl group to a type $A$ parabolic subgroup yields a representation isomorphic to a direct sum of type $A$ dot action representations.
