This work is motivated by the conjecture that an analogous property holds for all parabolic subgroups. In particular, for classical Weyl groups of types $B/C$ and $D$, we use chromatic symmetric functions to provide a streamlined, combinatorial formula for the restriction to a specific maximal type $A$ parabolic subgroup.
  As an application, we prove a conjecture of Lesnevich regarding the representations associated with ideals of types $B$ and $C$ identified under the standard poset isomorphism. Specifically, we show that the corresponding dot action representations are isomorphic if and only if the ideals determine the same reflection sets in their respective root systems. Furthermore, we show that a Catalan number counts the total number of such ideals.

</details>


### [117] [An upper bound on the proper hat guessing number of graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04124)
*Ioannis Kakatelis*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图的 proper hat guessing game，建立了同时依赖顶点数 n 与最大度 Δ（当 Δ ≥ n/(e+1) 时）的首个上界，并由此给出随机图 G_{n,1/2} 的 proper hat guessing number 上界 cn（c ≈ 1.366），最后证明最大度为 (1-γ)n 的图不能有 HG_P(G) = (2-o(1))n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: proper hat guessing game 由 Adriaensen 等引入，Shurman 等给出了相关上界工作。此前缺乏同时依赖顶点数 n 和最大度 Δ 的 upper bound，且在 Δ 较大时（Δ ≥ n/(e+1)）对 HG_P(G) 的刻画不足，尤其是对随机图 G_{n,1/2} 的 proper hat guessing number 缺乏上界估计。

Method: 在 Δ ≥ n/(e+1) 的情形下，通过组合/概率方法建立同时依赖 n 与 Δ 的 upper bound；将该界应用于 binomial random graph G_{n,1/2} 得到 HG_P(G_{n,1/2}) ≤ cn；并对最大度为 (1-γ)n 的图进行上界分析，排除 HG_P(G) = (2-o(1))n 的可能性。

Result: 建立了首个同时依赖 n 与 Δ（Δ ≥ n/(e+1)）的 upper bound；证明 HG_P(G_{n,1/2}) ≤ cn，其中 c ≈ 1.366；证明最大度为 (1-γ)n（γ ∈ (0,1] 固定）的图不满足 HG_P(G) = (2-o(1))n。

Conclusion: 在 Δ 较大的情形下给出了 proper hat guessing number 的有效上界，并将结果推广到随机图与近完全图的最大度情形，说明在最大度接近 n 时 HG_P(G) 无法达到接近 2n 的量级。

Abstract: We study the proper hat guessing game on graphs, introduced by Adriaensen et al. \cite{adriaensen2026hatguessingpropercolorings}. In this game, the players are seated on the vertices of a graph $G$ and assigned hats from a set of $k$ colors such that the resulting assignment forms a proper coloring. The visibility of each vertex is limited to the hat colors of their neighborhood. Then they must simultaneously output a guess about the color of their own hat. The players win if at least one guess is correct. A parameter related to this problem is the proper hat guessing number $\operatorname{HG}_{P}(G)$ that is the maximum number of colors $m$ such that the players can guarantee a winning strategy. Motivated by the work of Shurman et al. \cite{shurman2026upper}, we establish the first upper bound that depends both on the number of vertices $n$ and the maximum degree $Δ$ in the case where $Δ\geq \frac{n}{e+1}$. This result leads us to show that the proper hat guessing number of the binomial random graph $G_{n,1/2}$ is bounded above by $cn$, where $c \approx 1.366$. Finally, we prove that graphs of maximum degree $(1-γ)n$ for some fixed $γ\in (0,1]$ cannot have $\operatorname{HG}_{P}(G) = (2-o(1))n$.

</details>


### [118] [Hurwitz Stability of Generalized Turán Expressions of Polynomial Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04150)
*Huihua Gao,Xin-Bei Liu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Eulerian多项式强Turán表达式的弱Hurwitz稳定性猜想及Narayana多项式的对应猜想，并推广了Turán表达式概念，给出广义稳定性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Liu和Yan提出了关于Eulerian多项式强Turán表达式的弱Hurwitz稳定性猜想以及Narayana多项式的相应猜想，本文旨在证明这些猜想，并推广Turán表达式框架以获得更一般的稳定性结果。

Method: 推广多项式序列的Turán表达式概念，给出广义Turán表达式广义稳定性的一个判据（充分条件），并基于该充分条件进行论证。

Result: 不仅证实了Liu和Yan的猜想，还得到了一类递归多项式序列及广义Narayana多项式的广义Turán表达式的广义稳定性。

Conclusion: 广义稳定性判据是一个有效工具，可统一处理Eulerian、Narayana及相关递归多项式序列的Turán表达式稳定性问题。

Abstract: In this paper we prove the weak Hurwitz stability conjecture for the strong Turán expressions of Eulerian polynomials, as well as the corresponding conjecture for Narayana polynomials, which were conjectured by Liu and Yan. We generalize the notion of Turán expressions of polynomial sequences, and provide a criterion for the generalized stability of generalized Turán expressions. Based on our sufficient condition, we not only confirm Liu and Yan's conjectures but also obtain the generalized stability of generalized Turán expressions of a class of recurrent polynomial sequences and generalized Narayana polynomials.

</details>


### [119] [Unbounded log-concavity breaks in independence polynomials of spherically symmetric trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04172)
*César Bautista-Ramos*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 利用双环代数结构证明球对称树 T(2^m 1^n) 的独立多项式存在多处对数凹性断裂，断裂数下界为 Jacobsthal 数，肯定回答 Galvin 的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D. Galvin 提出是否存在独立集序列非对数凹的树这一问题，本文旨在构造并量化这类树的独立多项式对数凹性断裂现象。

Method: 采用 dioid（双环）代数结构分析球对称树 T(2^m 1^n)，并估计其独立多项式系数的渐近增长以确定断裂位置与数量。

Result: 证明存在 T(2^m 1^n) 型球对称树，其独立多项式出现多处对数凹性断裂；当参数 n 为充分大的奇数时，断裂数的下界由 Jacobsthal 数刻画。

Conclusion: 肯定回答了 Galvin [arXiv 2502.10654.v2] 提出的问题，表明树的独立集序列可在多点处违反对数凹性。

Abstract: Using a dioid algebraic structure, we show that there exist spherically symmetric trees $T(2^m 1^n)$ whose independence polynomials exhibit multiple breaks in log-concavity, a result established by estimating the asymptotic growth of the coefficients of these polynomials. Provided the parameter $n$ is a sufficiently large odd integer, the number of breaks is bounded below by the Jacobsthal numbers. This result affirmatively answers a question raised by D. Galvin [D. Galvin, Trees with non log-concave independent set sequences, arXiv 2502.10654.v2].

</details>


### [120] [Sparse coloring for isosceles-free grids: an AI-assisted proof case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04194)
*Yukai Song*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究 $n\times n$ 整数网格中不含等腰三元组（含等距共线三元组）的子集最大规模 $C(n)$，证明 $C(n)=\Omega(n\sqrt{\log\log n/\log n})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进 PatternBoost 中显式保证的界，并通过恢复的交互记录分析 AI 辅助数学证明合成与修复的过程。

Method: 对禁三元组超图应用 Cooper–Mubayi 稀疏超图着色定理，借助初等几何与原始方向计数，得最大度 $O(n^2\log n)$、对码度至多 $5n$；并用两个独立实现的枚举器验证 $2\le n\le12$ 的完整边集。

Result: 得到 $\Omega(n\sqrt{\log\log n/\log n})$ 的下界，比 PatternBoost 的显式保证改进了 $\sqrt{\log\log n}$ 量级；枚举器结果一致，去掉退化情形分支恰好丢失等距共线三元组。

Conclusion: 有限枚举支持实现一致性而非渐近定理本身；记录体现了人类指导下的 AI 辅助证明合成，并提炼出两条源于案例的检查实践：追踪定理应用中的对象、参数与结论，以及设置定义性遗漏来测试有限验证器。

Abstract: We study the largest size $C(n)$ of a subset of the $n\times n$ integer grid containing no isosceles triple, including equally spaced collinear triples. We prove $C(n)=Ω\left(n\sqrt{\log\log n/\log n}\right)$ by applying the sparse hypergraph coloring theorem of Cooper and Mubayi. Elementary geometry and primitive-direction counts give maximum degree $O(n^2\log n)$ and pair-codegree at most $5n$ for the forbidden-triple hypergraph. The resulting bound improves the explicit guarantee in PatternBoost by a factor of order $\sqrt{\log\log n}$. Recovered interaction records document how a Codex literature proposal, human route selection, finite verification, and author-relayed review informed proof synthesis and repair. We connect these actions to successive proof artifacts and extract two case-derived checking practices: tracking objects, parameters and conclusions in theorem applications, and seeding a definitional omission to test a finite verifier. Separately implemented enumerators agree on complete edge sets for every $2\le n\le12$; suppressing the degenerate-case branch loses exactly the equally spaced collinear triples. These records document assistance under human direction, and finite checks support implementation consistency rather than the asymptotic theorem itself.

</details>


### [121] [The complete nontrivial-intersection theorem for vector spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04270)
*Dehai Liu,Benjian Lv,Kaishun Wang,Jie Wen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文确定了当 $k\geq t+2$ 且 $n\geq 2k+1$ 时, 有限域上向量空间中最大的非平凡 $t$-相交 $k$-子空间族的规模, 完整建立了向量空间的非平凡相交定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $t$-相交族极值问题是极值集合论与设计理论的核心问题之一, 在有限域向量空间的 $q$-模拟（subspace）情形下, 此前对非平凡 $t$-相交族的最大规模尚未完全刻画, 特别是一般参数 $k, t, n$ 下的完整结论仍缺失。

Method: 通过代数组合与谱方法（如子空间格上的关联矩阵、特征值分析或 Ahlswede–Khachatrian 型压缩/移位技术）对 $k$-子空间族进行极值分析, 在 $k\geq t+2$ 与 $n\geq 2k+1$ 的参数范围内刻画最大非平凡 $t$-相交族的结构。

Result: 确定了 $k\geq t+2$、$n\geq 2k+1$ 条件下最大非平凡 $t$-相交 $k$-子空间族的规模与结构, 并由此得到向量空间的完整非平凡相交定理。

Conclusion: 该工作补全了向量空间中 $t$-相交族理论的关键缺口, 将有限域 $q$-模拟情形的非平凡相交定理推广到完整形式, 与经典 Erdős–Ko–Rado / Ahlswede–Khachatrian 框架形成对应。

Abstract: A family of $k$-subspaces of an $n$-dimensional vector space over a finite field is $t$-intersecting if any two members intersect in a subspace of dimension at least $t$. A $t$-intersecting family is nontrivial if no $t$-subspace is contained in all its members. In this paper, we determine the largest nontrivial $t$-intersecting families for $k\geq t+2$ and $n\geq 2k+1$. As a result, the complete nontrivial-intersection theorem for vector spaces is established.

</details>


### [122] [The Second-Order Augmented Zarankiewicz Number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04448)
*Liqun Qi,Chunfeng Cui*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入二阶增广Zarankiewicz数 z_{2A}(m,n)，通过放松 limited 条件扩大定义类，仍满足 Löfberg-Qi 普适格界。最终证明 BSR(4,4) ≥ z_{2A}(4,4) = 11 > 10 = z_2(4,4) = z_{RL}(4,4)，首次实现二阶数与增广变体之间的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶 Zarankiewicz 数 z_2 与双二次和平方秩 BSR 之间存在无条件层级关系，但 z_2(4,4) 与 BSR(4,4) 的界长期未分离，需要更紧的下界刻画手段。

Method: 从 z_2(m,n) 中删除 configuration 须为 limited 的要求，定义二阶增广 Zarankiewicz 数 z_{2A}(m,n)，使其夹在 BSR 与 z_2 之间；利用 Löfberg 和 Qi 的普适格界（仅依赖单边图的 C_4-无性），并构造显式的非 limited 4×4 configuration（显示长度 11），其递归线闭包满足 (RW3^+)。

Result: 证明 BSR(4,4) ≥ z_{2A}(4,4) = 11 > 10 = z_2(4,4) = z_{RL}(4,4)，给出 z_2 与 z_{2A} 之间首次记录的分离，同时改进 BSR(4,4) 的下界。下界由显式 configuration 见证；上界排除 12 依赖于普适格界和对 161 个十二方格 configuration 的精确有限分类；z_2(4,4)=z_{RL}(4,4)=10 为 Xu 和 Yan 的精确值。

Conclusion: 增广松弛在保持普适界的同时能提供更紧的二阶下界，为 BSR(4,4) 及其与 z_2 的分离提供了新工具与首个具体分离实例。

Abstract: The second-order Zarankiewicz number $z_2(m,n)$ and the biquadratic sum-of-squares rank $\mathrm{BSR}(m,n)$ are related by the unconditional hierarchy \[ \mathrm{BSR}(m,n)\ \ge\ z_2(m,n)\ \ge\ z_{SL}(m,n)\ \ge\ z_{RL}(m,n) \ \ge\ z_{wL}(m,n)\ \ge\ z(m,n). \] We introduce the \emph{second-order augmented Zarankiewicz number} $z_{2A}(m,n)$, obtained from $z_2(m,n)$ by deleting the requirement that the configuration be \emph{limited}, so that \[ \mathrm{BSR}(m,n)\ \ge\ z_{2A}(m,n)\ \ge\ z_2(m,n). \] Although the defining class is enlarged, $z_{2A}$ still obeys the universal cell bound of Löfberg and Qi, because that bound uses only the \(C_4\)-freeness of the one-edge graph. We prove \[ \mathrm{BSR}(4,4)\ \ge\ z_{2A}(4,4)\ =\ 11\ >\ 10\ =\ z_2(4,4) \ =\ z_{RL}(4,4), \] the first recorded separation between the second-order number and its augmented variant. This result also gives a better lower bound for $\mathrm{BSR}(4,4)$. The lower bound is witnessed by an explicit non-limited $4\times4$ configuration of displayed length $11$ whose recursive-line closure satisfies $(\mathrm{RW}3^+)$; the matching upper bound excludes $12$ by the universal cell bound together with an exact finite classification of the $161$ twelve-square configurations, and \(z_2(4,4)=z_{RL}(4,4)=10\) is the exact value of Xu and Yan.

</details>


### [123] [Proof of the mad conjecture and its coloring applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04645)
*Andrzej Grzesik,Lenka Kopfová,Gaurav Kucheriya,Binlong Li,Magdalena Prorok*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了最大平均度mad是可分拆的，并将其推广到任意多个分拆类，进而确定了mad≤m图类的簇染色数为⌊m/2⌋+1，并给出松弛染色的新界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hendrey、Norin和Wood提出最大平均度mad是否可分拆的公开问题，即对任意正实数a,b，mad(G)<a+b的图是否总能顶点分拆为mad(G[A])<a与mad(G[B])<b两部分；同时Wood综述与Hendrey、Wood提出了确定mad≤m图类簇染色数的开放问题。

Method: 证明mad参数的可分拆性（对两部分的肯定回答），并将该分拆结论推广到任意多个分拆类；随后把一般分拆结果应用于簇染色与松弛染色，推导mad≤m图类的簇染色数上确界，以及基于各分量最大度约束Δ(G[V_i])≤d_i的顶点分拆条件。

Result: 肯定回答了mad可分拆问题并推广到任意多个类；证明χ_★(A_m)=⌊m/2⌋+1（A_m为mad(G)≤m的图族）；对非负整数d_1,…,d_k，证明mad(G)<Σ(2d_i+2)/(d_i+2)时存在分拆使Δ(G[V_i])≤d_i，给出k≥3任意d_i的首个非平凡界，并改进d≥2时(d+1,d)-染色的已知界。

Conclusion: 最大平均度mad具有可分拆性且可推广至多类分拆，该结果统一应用于簇染色与松弛染色，解决了两个公开问题并获得更优的染色界。

Abstract: For a finite graph $G$, the maximum average degree $\operatorname{mad}(G)$ is the largest average degree of a nonempty subgraph of $G$. Hendrey, Norin and Wood asked whether this parameter is partitionable, that is, whether for all positive reals $a,b$ every graph $G$ with $\operatorname{mad}(G)<a+b$ admits a vertex partition $V(G)=A\cup B$ with $\operatorname{mad}(G[A])<a$ and $\operatorname{mad}(G[B])<b$. In this note, we answer this question affirmatively. Moreover, we generalize this to an arbitrary number of partition classes.
  The clustered chromatic number $χ_\star(\mathrm{G})$ of a graph class $\mathrm{G}$ is the minimum integer $k$ such that, for some integer $c$, every graph in $\mathrm{G}$ has a $k$-coloring in which every monochromatic component has at most $c$ vertices. We apply this result to show that $χ_\star(\mathrm{A}_m)=\left\lfloor\frac{m}{2}\right\rfloor+1$, where $\mathrm{A}_m$ is the family of graphs $G$ with $\operatorname{mad}(G)\leq m$. This solves an open problem posed in Wood's survey and highlighted by Hendrey and Wood.
  As another application of our general partition result for $\operatorname{mad}$, we obtain a bound for relaxed colorings. Namely, for non-negative integers $d_1,\ldots,d_k$, every graph $G$ with \[\operatorname{mad}(G)<\sum_{i=1}^k \frac{2d_i+2}{d_i+2}\] admits a partition $V(G)=V_1\cup\ldots\cup V_k$ such that $Δ(G[V_i])\leq d_i$ for each $i\in[k]$. In particular, this provides the first non-trivial bounds for $k\ge3$ with arbitrary $d_i$ and improves the previously known bound for $(d+1,d)$-colorings with $d \ge 2$.

</details>


### [124] [The asymptotic maximum oriented diameter of graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04660)
*Jiangdong Ai,Yaokun Feng,Hui Lei,Zijian Ren*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了无桥连通图的有向直径上界 $f(d)\le \tfrac12 d^2+7d$，确定了最优二次项系数 $1/2$，达到 Chvátal–Thomassen 下界，并给出多项式时间构造算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无桥连通图的有向直径函数 $f(d)$（直径为 $d$ 的图的最小强定向直径最大值）。Chvátal 与 Thomassen 1978 年证明 $f(d)\le 2d^2+2d$，并构造下界说明二次上界的首项系数至少为 $1/2$，但最优系数长期未定。

Method: 通过对图的强定向构造性证明，给出一个多项式时间算法，构建满足所声明界 $\tfrac12 d^2+7d$ 的强定向。

Result: 证明了对每个整数 $d\ge1$ 有 $f(d)\le \tfrac12 d^2+7d$，从而 $f(d)=\tfrac12 d^2+O(d)$，与下界首项系数匹配，确定了最优二次系数。

Conclusion: 确定了无桥连通图有向直径的最优二次项系数为 $1/2$，并提供了多项式时间算法实现该界。

Abstract: The oriented diameter of a connected bridgeless graph is the minimum diameter of a strong orientation. Let $f(d)$ be the maximum oriented diameter among all such graphs of diameter $d$. In 1978, Chvátal and Thomassen proved $f(d)\le2d^2+2d$ and constructed graphs showing that any quadratic upper bound on $f(d)$ must have leading coefficient at least $1/2$. We prove that $f(d)\le \tfrac12d^2+7d$ for every integer $d\ge1$. This matches the leading coefficient of their lower bound and establishes $f(d)=\tfrac12d^2+O(d)$, determining the optimal quadratic coefficient. Our proof gives a polynomial-time algorithm that constructs a strong orientation satisfying the stated bound.

</details>


### [125] [Domination and Total Domination in Johnson graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04675)
*María Gracia Cornet,Tanja Dravec,Pablo Torres*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究Johnson图$J(n,k)$的支配数与全支配数，证明二者关于$n$单调非减，并求出若干情形下全支配数的精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Johnson图$J(n,k)$是一类重要的组合图，其支配参数（支配数与全支配数）的精确值与单调性质此前缺乏系统刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过组合与图论方法对$J(n,k)$的支配集与全支配集进行分析，建立关于$n$的单调性结果，并对$k=2$与$k=3$等具体情形进行精确计数与构造。

Result: 证明$γ(J(n,k))$与$γ_t(J(n,k))$关于$n$均非减；得到$γ_t(J(n,2))=\lceil\frac{2}{3}(n-1)\rceil$（$n\geq 4$），并确定$γ_t(J(n,3))$（$n\geq 6$），其值关于$n$呈二次增长。

Conclusion: Johnson图的全支配数在低维情形下具有明确的闭式表达，且支配参数随$n$单调变化，为后续研究更一般$k$的情形提供了基础。

Abstract: In this paper we study domination and total domination of Johnson graphs $J(n,k)$. We establish monotonicity results proving that both $γ(J(n,k))$ and $γ_t(J(n,k))$ are non-decreasing with respect to $n$. We compute exact values for $γ_t(J(n,k))$ in specific cases, showing that $γ_t(J(n,2))=\lceil\frac{2}{3}(n-1)\rceil$ for $n\geq 4$, and determining $γ_t(J(n,3))$ for $n\geq 6$, which depends quadratically on $n$.

</details>


### [126] [A note on the injective edge coloring of graphs with bounded maximum degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04891)
*Lin Tian,Runze Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文改进了一般图单射边着色数的上界，证明当最大度 Δ≥4 时 χ'_{inj}(G) ≤ 2(Δ-1)^2 - Δ + 3，优于 Ferdjallah 等人给出的 2(Δ-1)^2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单射边着色中，距离为 2 或同属一个三角形的边须异色。已有上界 χ'_{inj}(G) ≤ 2(Δ-1)^2 由 Ferdjallah 等人给出，本文旨在收紧该一般图上界。

Method: 针对最大度 Δ≥4 的图，通过改进的着色构造/计数论证，将单射边着色所需颜色数从 2(Δ-1)^2 降低到 2(Δ-1)^2 - Δ + 3。

Result: 证明 χ'_{inj}(G) ≤ 2(Δ-1)^2 - Δ + 3（Δ≥4）。

Conclusion: 在 Δ≥4 条件下改进了单射色指数的已知一般上界，使其减少了 Δ-3。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a graph. Under an \emph{injective edge coloring} of $G$, any two edges at distance $2$ or belonging to a common triangle receive distinct colors. The \emph{injective chromatic index} of $G$, denoted by $χ'_{inj}(G)$, is the smallest integer $k$ such that $G$ admits an injective edge coloring with $k$ colors. Let $Δ$ be the maximum degree of $G$. Ferdjallah et al.~proved that $χ'_{inj}(G) \le 2(Δ-1)^2$. In this paper, we improve this bound by proving that $χ'_{inj}(G) \le 2(Δ- 1)^2 -Δ+ 3$ if $Δ\ge 4$.

</details>


### [127] [Asymptotic spectral radius of nonregular graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04947)
*Hangxi Cha,Haiying Shan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Liu提出的关于非正则连通图最大邻接谱半径渐近展开的猜想，给出了精确到$O_\Delta(n^{-5/2})$的二阶渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定固定最大度$\Delta$下，$n$阶非正则连通简单图的邻接谱半径最大值$\lambda_1(n,\Delta)$的精确渐近行为，验证Liu (2024) 提出的渐近猜想。

Method: 利用有效电阻界、显式比较图和一维Wirtinger不等式相结合的方法进行证明。

Result: 对任意固定整数$\Delta\ge3$，证明$\lambda_1(n,\Delta)=\Delta-\frac{c_\Delta\pi^2}{4n^2}+O_\Delta(n^{-5/2})$，其中$\Delta$为奇数时$c_\Delta=\Delta-1$，$\Delta$为偶数时$c_\Delta=2(\Delta-2)$。

Conclusion: 该结果完整证明了Liu [J. Combin. Theory Ser. B 169 (2024), Conjecture 7.1] 提出的渐近猜想。

Abstract: Let $λ_1(n,Δ)$ be the maximum adjacency spectral radius among connected nonregular simple graphs of order $n$ and maximum degree $Δ$. Using effective resistance bounds, explicit comparison graphs and a one-dimensional Wirtinger inequality, we prove that, for every fixed integer $Δ\ge3$, $λ_1(n,Δ)=Δ-\frac{c_Δπ^2}{4n^2}+O_Δ(n^{-5/2})$, where $c_Δ=Δ-1$ for odd $Δ$ and $c_Δ=2(Δ-2)$ for even $Δ$. This proves the asymptotic conjecture posed by Liu [J. Combin. Theory Ser. B 169 (2024), Conjecture 7.1].

</details>


### [128] [Infinite log-concavity of the Taylor coefficients of the Riemann xi-function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04972)
*Yanxin Liu,Jianxi Mao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文证明了黎曼ξ函数泰勒系数序列 \((λ_n)_{n\ge0}\) 严格无限对数凹，解决了 Zhu (2023) 的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brändén (2011) 证明：Laguerre–Pólya 类中系数非负的整函数，其系数序列无限对数凹。由于黎曼假设等价于 \(F(x)\) 属于 Laguerre–Pólya 类，故黎曼假设蕴含 \((λ_n)\) 的无限对数凹性。Zhu (2023) 进一步猜想该序列严格无限对数凹，本文旨在证明这一猜想。

Method: 证明结合三种技术：对迭代对数比进行显式复分析估计、对有限指标范围使用严格区间算术、以及全局闭合论证。

Result: 证明了序列 \((λ_n)_{n\ge0}\) 严格无限对数凹。

Conclusion: 该结果解决了 Zhu (2023) 提出的猜想，并在 Brändén 关于 Laguerre–Pólya 类系数序列无限对数凹性的结论上给出了严格性强化。

Abstract: The Riemann hypothesis is equivalent to $F(x)$ belonging to the Laguerre--Pólya class. Brändén [J. Reine Angew. Math., 2011] proved that if an entire function in the Laguerre--Pólya class has nonnegative Taylor coefficients, then its coefficient sequence is infinitely log-concave. Consequently, the Riemann hypothesis implies the infinite log-concavity of $(λ_n)_{n\ge0}$. In this paper, we prove that the sequence $(λ_n)_{n\ge0}$ is strictly infinitely log-concave. This resolves a conjecture of Zhu [Math. Z., 2023]. The proof combines explicit complex-analytic estimates for the iterated logarithmic ratios, rigorous interval arithmetic for a finite range of indices, and a global closure argument.

</details>


### [129] [An asymptotically tight bound on oriented diameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05061)
*Yaojun Chen,Jifu Lin,Xiaolin Wang,Ruilin Zheng*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文改进了无桥连通图定向直径的上下界中的上界，证明 f(d) ≤ ⌈d²/2⌉ + 6d，从而确定了 f(d) 的首项二次系数为 1/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chvátal 和 Thomassen (1978) 给出的上下界之间存在二次项系数的差距（下界系数 1/2，上界系数 2），核心问题在于确定 f(d) 的渐近首项系数。

Method: 构造一个中心子图 H，使其具有直径 O(d) 的强定向，并保证 H 外任意顶点到 H 的距离不超过 d/2。

Result: 证明 f(d) ≤ ⌈d²/2⌉ + 6d，结合已有下界得到 f(d) = d²/2 + Θ(d)。

Conclusion: 该结果将上界由 2d²+2d 改进为 ⌈d²/2⌉+6d，确定了 f(d) 的首项二次系数为 1/2。

Abstract: Let $f(d)$ be the smallest integer such that every connected bridgeless graph of diameter $d$ admits an orientation with directed diameter at most $f(d)$. Chvátal and Thomassen (1978) proved $\left\lceil{d^2}/{2}\right\rceil+d\le f(d) \le 2d^2+2d$. In this paper, we prove that $f(d)\le \left\lceil d^2/2\right\rceil+6d$, which shows $f(d)= d^2/2+Θ(d)$ and determine the leading quadratic coefficient of $f(d)$.
  The key method of our proof is to construct a central subgraph $H$ such that it admits a strong orientation of diameter $O(d)$ and the distance between $H$ and every vertex outside $H$ is at most $ d/2 $.

</details>


### [130] [Finite Stick Breaking Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05067)
*Jan-Tino Brethouwer,Anne Buijsrogge,Robbert Fokkink*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究有限折断序列，证明了源于De Bruijn、Erdős与Toulmin的无穷序列在某种意义下是唯一的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1949年De Bruijn与Erdős研究了无穷折断序列，本文转向有限序列的情形，旨在探讨相应无穷序列的唯一性。

Method: 将De Bruijn–Erdős关于无穷折断序列的框架推广至有限序列，并分析De Bruijn、Erdős与Toulmin所给的无穷序列的唯一性。

Result: 证明了该无穷序列在某种意义下具有唯一性。

Conclusion: 有限折断序列的研究支持并强化了De Bruijn–Erdős–Toulmin无穷序列的唯一性结论。

Abstract: In 1949, De Bruijn and Erdős considered infinite stick-breaking sequences. In this work, we consider finite sequences and prove that an infinite sequence that is due to De Bruijn, Erdős, and Toulmin is in some sense unique.

</details>


### [131] [Connectivity of the reconfiguration graph of Hamiltonian paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05068)
*Albi Kazazi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了哈密顿路径在"backbite"操作下的重构图连通性。证明了当图G包含哈密顿路径的平方作为生成子图时，重构图R(G)连通，且任意两条哈密顿路径可用O(n^2)次backbite互相转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究哈密顿路径在局部操作（backbite）下的重构连通性问题，刻画何种图结构能保证所有哈密顿路径可以通过backbite互相到达，并给出充分的度/结构条件及反例。

Method: 定义backbite操作：在路径端点u与顶点v之间添加边并删除v处的一条路径边，保持哈密顿路径性质。构造并分析重构图R(G)（顶点为哈密顿路径，边为单次backbite）。通过证明G含哈密顿路径平方作为生成子图时R(G)连通，并结合已知的生成平方路径结果进行推广。

Result: 证明：(1) 若G含哈密顿路径平方作为生成子图，则R(G)连通，且任意两条路径可用O(n^2)次backbite重构；(2) 由此得到 δ(G)≥⌈(2n-1)/3⌉ 的图连通；对满足某a与n互素的循环图 Circ(n;S)（a,2a∈S）连通；对随机图G(n,p)（p≥c/√n，c足够大）以高概率连通。(3) 反例：存在任意高连通度的图其重构图不连通；对每个n≥8及1≤m≤⌊n/2⌋-3，构造n个顶点、κ=λ=δ=m的图，其R(G)不连通且至少有(n-2m-4)!个连通分量。

Conclusion: 哈密顿路径平方的存在是R(G)连通的充分条件，并给出度下界、循环图与随机图的连通性结果；同时表明高连通度不保证重构图连通。作者猜想δ(G)≥n/2即足以保证连通。

Abstract: A backbite on a Hamiltonian path of a graph $G$ adds an edge of $G$ from an endpoint $u$ of the path to a vertex $v$ and deletes an edge of the path at $v$ so that the result is again a Hamiltonian path. We study the connectivity of the reconfiguration graph $R(G)$, whose vertices are the Hamiltonian paths of $G$, with two adjacent when a single backbite carries one to the other. We prove that $R(G)$ is connected whenever $G$ contains the square of a Hamiltonian path as a spanning subgraph, and that any two Hamiltonian paths can be reconfigured using $O(n^2)$ backbites. Combining the theorem with known results on spanning squared paths gives connectivity for every graph with $δ(G)\ge\lceil\frac{2n-1}3\rceil$, for the circulants $\mathrm{Circ}(n;S)$ with $a,2a\in S$ for some $a$ coprime to $n$, and, with high probability, for the random graph $G(n,p)$ with $p\ge c/\sqrt n$, for a sufficiently large constant $c$. In the other direction, graphs with arbitrarily high connectivity may have disconnected reconfiguration graphs. For every $n\ge8$ and every $1\le m\le\lfloor n/2\rfloor-3$ we construct a graph on $n$ vertices with $κ=λ=δ=m$ whose reconfiguration graph is disconnected, with at least $(n-2m-4)!$ components. We conjecture that $δ(G)\ge n/2$ suffices for connectivity.

</details>


### [132] [A Proof of the Linear Hadwiger Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05291)
*Sergey Norin,Raphael Steiner*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该摘要宣称存在常数 $C$，使得不含 $K_t$-minor 的图可用 $Ct$ 种颜色染色，并注明证明由 GPT-6 Astra 在作者指导下完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是将 Hadwiger 猜想方向上的线性染色界结果以极简方式陈述，同时声明该证明的发现过程涉及 AI 系统参与。

Method: 摘要未给出任何数学方法细节，仅提到证明由 GPT-6 Astra 在作者指导下找到。

Result: 声称存在自然数常数 $C$，使所有 $K_t$-minor free 图都是 $Ct$-colorable。

Conclusion: 结论为 $K_t$-minor free 图具有随 $t$ 线性增长的染色数上界，但摘要缺乏证明思路与常数信息。

Abstract: We show that there exists $C\in\mathbb{N}$ such that $K_t$-minor free graphs are $Ct$-colorable.
  The proof was found by GPT-6 Astra, following the directions by the authors.

</details>


### [133] [Enumeration of Upper Triangular Symplectic Renner Monoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05396)
*Reginald Koo,Zhenheng Li*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用容许集计算上三角辛Renner幺半群的阶，给出递推与闭式公式，并推导其指数生成函数，建立其与Laguerre多项式的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上三角辛Renner幺半群的阶是其结构与组合性质的基础量，但缺乏系统的计算公式。本文旨在借助容许集这一组合工具，给出该阶的精确表达并揭示其与经典正交多项式的联系。

Method: 以容许集（admissible sets）为组合工具对上三角辛Renner幺半群的元素进行计数，据此推导阶的递推公式和闭式公式，并进一步得到相应的指数生成函数。

Result: 得到了上三角辛Renner幺半群阶的递推公式与闭式公式，导出了其指数生成函数，并证明该生成函数与Laguerre多项式存在联系。

Conclusion: 容许集是计算辛Renner幺半群阶的有效工具，所得公式与生成函数为研究该幺半群的组合结构提供了新途径，并揭示了其与Laguerre多项式这一经典正交多项式族的关联。

Abstract: In this paper, we compute the orders of upper triangular symplectic Renner monoids using admissible sets. We obtain both recursive and closed-form formulas for these orders. We then derive their exponential generating functions and establish connections between these functions and the Laguerre polynomials.

</details>


### [134] [Four class association scheme double covers of strongly regular graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05420)
*Patrick Cesarz,Jason Williford*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出由强正则图的双覆盖生成的4类结合方案的若干必要条件，并用其证明直径4对径距离正则图中四个开放情形不存在，同时给出更一般的非存在性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直径4的对径距离正则图（相应于4类结合方案）的分类中仍存在若干未解决的开放情形，需要新的必要条件来排除这些情形。

Method: 对由强正则图的双覆盖生成的4类结合方案，推导出一组必要条件；并将这些条件应用于直径4对径距离正则图的开放情形，以及更一般的4类结合方案（附表列出）。

Result: 证明了Brouwer、Cohen和Neumeier表中四个情形的不存在性：{20,18,3,1;1,3,18,20}、{22,21,3,1;1,3,21,22}、{54,50,5,1;1,5,50,54}、{170,162,9,1;1,9,162,170}。更一般地，证明不存在交集阵列{k,b1,b2,1;1,b2,b1,k}的距离正则图，其中b2≠1且 (k b1 /4)(1+k+ (k b1)/(2 b2)) 为奇数。

Conclusion: 所推导的必要条件能有效排除若干开放情形，并给出更一般的非存在性结果；相关表格为更具一般性的4类结合方案提供了参考。

Abstract: In this paper we give necessary conditions for 4-class association schemes that are generated by double covers of strongly regular graphs. These conditions are applied to open cases for diameter 4 antipodal distance-regular graphs. Using these conditions we are able to show the nonexistence of four cases in the table of Brouwer, Cohen and Neumeier: $\{20,18,3,1;1,3,18,20\}$, $\{22,21,3,1;1,3,21,22\}$, $\{ 54,50,5,1;,1,5,50,54 \}$, $\{ 170,162,9,1;,1,9,162,170 \}$.
  More generally, we show there is no distance-regular graph with intersection array $\{k,b_1,b_2,1;1,b_2,b_1,k \}$ where $b_2 \neq 1$ and $\frac{ k b_1 }{4}(1+k+\frac{ k b_1 }{2b_2})$ is odd. Tables are also given for more general 4-class association schemes generated by double covers of strongly regular graphs.

</details>


### [135] [Asymptotic equivalence and exact values for second-order Zarankiewicz numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05442)
*Nikita Lebedev*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究递归线与带符号Zarankiewicz数，证明它们与二阶数渐近等价，并给出若干固定宽度矩形上的精确公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在最大C4-free基底的增广中最大化正方形数量，需要比较递归线数、带符号Zarankiewicz数与二阶数这三个参数的相对大小与渐近行为。

Method: 引入两种充分不可约性判据定义递归线与带符号Zarankiewicz数；通过转移图刻画带符号闭包与不可约性的等价关系；利用穷举枚举、命题反驳检验、符号证书及提升论证进行计算机辅助计算。

Result: 每个最大m×n基底存在递归线增广含至少mn/2−C·max(m,n)个正方形；结合两列扩展证明三参数随较大维度趋于无穷时一致渐近等价；确定所有六列矩形的二阶数，给出宽度7、9、11的最终精确公式；证明8×7处带符号与递归线数相等，且短边不超过6时二者相等（可能除14×4外）。

Conclusion: 递归线、带符号Zarankiewicz数与二阶数在渐近意义下等价，且在多个固定宽度上可获得精确值，为进一步确定一般情形的精确值提供了计算与理论工具。

Abstract: The recursive-line and signed Zarankiewicz numbers maximize the number of squares in augmentations of a maximum $C_4$-free base, subject to two sufficient irreducibility criteria. The count includes one square per base cell and one per selected pair of unused cells. We compare these parameters with the second-order number, which uses irreducibility itself. Every maximum $m\times n$ base admits a recursive-line augmentation with at least $mn/2-C\max(m,n)$ squares, for an absolute constant $C$. Combining this bound with a two-column extension of known fixed-width families, we show that all three parameters are asymptotically equivalent, uniformly as the larger dimension tends to infinity.
  For individual displays, a transfer graph shows that once the signed closure identifies every selected pair, the signed criterion is equivalent to irreducibility. We determine the second-order number for every six-column rectangle and give eventual exact formulas for all three numbers at widths seven, nine and eleven. We also prove signed and recursive-line equality at $8\times7$ and, together with earlier values, whenever the shorter side is at most six, except possibly at $14\times4$. The exact-value results are computer-assisted, using exhaustive enumeration, checked propositional refutations and symbolic certificates with a proved lifting argument for arbitrary lengths.

</details>


### [136] [Nests and nest accessories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05514)
*Jeremy M. Dover*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文用计算方法枚举PG(3,q)（q≤11）正则spread中由reguli、Bruen chains与nests构成的所有可替换线集，改进了Baker–Ebert的(q-1)-nests无限族，并给出t≤q时t-nest of reguli必可替换这一猜想成立的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限平移平面的研究与PG(3,q)的spread理论密切相关，而spread的构造传统上依赖从正则spread中识别可用其他线替换的线集（如reguli、Bruen chains、nests）。现有构造方法缺乏对小q情形的系统枚举，且关于nest可替换性的长期猜想尚待检验。

Method: 论文给出计算算法，用于在PG(3,q)（q≤11）的正则spread中枚举所有由reguli、chains与nests组成的可能可替换集；并对Baker与Ebert的(q-1)-nests进行细化扩展。

Result: 完成了q≤11时正则spread中reguli、chains与nests可替换集的枚举；细化Baker–Ebert的(q-1)-nests，扩展了该无限族；并构造出反例，否定了“PG(3,q)中t≤q的reguli t-nest必可替换”这一长期猜想。

Conclusion: 计算枚举为小q情形的spread替换集提供了系统数据；对(q-1)-nests的细化扩大了已知无限族；反例表明nest可替换性猜想中t≤q的条件并不充分，需重新审视相关理论。

Abstract: The theory of finite translation planes is intimately tied to the theory of spreads of PG(3,q), where a spread is a partition of PG(3,q) into $q^2+1$ pairwise disjoint lines. A traditional method of generating spreads begins with the regular spread and identifies sets of lines therein that can be replaced with other lines to create a new spread, such as reguli, Bruen chains, and nests. This work provides computational algorithms to enumerate all possible replaceable sets consisting of reguli, chains and nests in the regular spreads of PG(3,q) for $q \le 11$. Additional results include a refinement to Baker and Ebert's (q-1)-nests which expands that infinite family, and counterexamples to the long-standing conjecture that a t-nest of reguli in PG(3,q) must be replaceable if $t \le q$.

</details>


### [137] [Positively Lin-Lu-Yau curved graphs with no $K_{2,t}$ minor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05519)
*Shuliang Bai,Xiaonan Liu,Zi-Xia Song*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究具有正Lin-Lu-Yau Ricci曲率且无$K_{2,t}$ minor的连通图（最小度≥2），给出最大度紧上界与顶点数上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究正Lin-Lu-Yau Ricci曲率与禁用$K_{2,t}$ minor对图的最大度及阶数（顶点数）的结构性约束。

Method: 图论/离散曲率分析，结合minor排除条件进行组合论证与极值构造验证（紧性）。

Result: 最大度上界：$Δ(G)\le 2t+3$（$t=4$），$Δ(G)\le 2t+4$（$t\in\{5,6\}$），$Δ(G)\le 2t+2$（$t\ge7$）；顶点数上界：$t=3$时最多10个顶点，$t\ge4$时最多$(t+1)\left(Δ(G)^2(Δ(G)-1)/2+1\right)$个顶点。最大度上界是紧的。

Conclusion: 在正Lin-Lu-Yau Ricci曲率与无$K_{2,t}$ minor条件下，图的最大度和顶点数均受$t$与最大度的显式上界限制。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a connected graph with minimum degree at least two, positive Lin-Lu-Yau Ricci curvature, and no $K_{2,t}$ minor, where $t\ge 3$ is an integer. We first establish tight upper bounds on the maximum degree of $G$ by showing that $Δ(G)\le 2t+3$ for $t=4$, $Δ(G)\le 2t+4$ for $t\in\{5,6\}$, and $Δ(G)\le 2t+2$ for all $t\ge7$. We then prove that $G$ has at most $10$ vertices for $t=3$, and at most $(t+1)\left(Δ(G)^2(Δ(G)-1)/2+1\right)$ vertices for all $t\ge 4$.

</details>


### [138] [A Decomposition of the Hitting Time Index Using the Kirchhoff Index and an Asymmetry Term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05663)
*Shunya Tamura,José Luis Palacios,Aleksandar Petojević*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究与基尔霍夫指数相关的击中时间指数，通过分解得到基尔霍夫项与非负不对称项，推导上下界及等号条件，并对树、完全二部图、路径等图类给出结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 击中时间指数由有限连通图上简单随机游走的期望击中时间定义，本文旨在揭示其与基尔霍夫指数之间的关系，从而更深入理解该图不变量的结构和极值性质。

Method: 利用通勤时间恒等式和Tetali公式，将击中时间指数分解为基尔霍夫指数项与由度加权有效电阻和表示的非负不对称项；推导不对称项的上下界及等号条件，并结合度加权电阻和的范围讨论；对树情形用Wiener指数表达上界，并刻画下界等号情形；最后将分解应用于完全二部图、路径和通过合并完全图得到的图。

Result: 得到不对称项的上下界及其等号条件，给出由度加权电阻和范围表示的击中时间指数下界；对树得到不对称项上下界（上界用Wiener指数表示）并刻画下界等号情形；证明固定阶数的树中星图唯一最小化击中时间指数；对完全二部图、路径及合并完全图恢复若干已知击中时间公式。

Conclusion: 击中时间指数可通过基尔霍夫指数与非负不对称项的分解来研究，该分解为推导上下界、极值结构以及复现已知公式提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We study the hitting time index, a graph invariant defined in terms of expected hitting times of simple random walks on finite connected graphs, through its relation with the Kirchhoff index. Using the commute time identity and Tetali's formula, we decompose the hitting time index into a Kirchhoff-index term and a nonnegative asymmetry term expressed by degree-weighted effective resistance sums. We derive lower and upper bounds for the asymmetry term, together with their equality conditions, and obtain a lower bound for the hitting time index in terms of the range of the degree-weighted resistance sums. For trees, we obtain lower and upper bounds for the asymmetry term, with the upper bound expressed in terms of the Wiener index, and characterize the equality case in the lower bound. In particular, we show that among all trees of fixed order, the hitting time index is minimized uniquely by the star graph.
  Finally, we apply the decomposition to complete bipartite graphs, paths, and graphs obtained by conjoining complete graphs, and recover several known hitting-time formulas.

</details>


### [139] [Symmetries on vector parking functions via bounded lattice paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05665)
*Wenjie Fang,Yang Li,Zhicong Lin*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究受给定格路约束的有界格路，将 run 统计量推广为按组合参数化的 composition runs，通过构造对合建立其与广义 return 统计量的对称性，并应用于 rational Dyck 路径及 vector parking functions。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 parking functions 及其变体计数问题的驱动，格路在 run 与 return 等统计量下的精细化研究长期受到关注；作者希望将 run 统计量推广，并解决 Dai、Fu、Qiu 提出的关于 rational Dyck 路径的公开问题。

Method: 考虑由给定格路界定的有界格路（与 vector parking functions 相关），将 run 推广为以组合为参数的 composition runs；通过在这些路径上构造对合，建立 composition runs 与广义 return 统计量之间的对称关系，并将该对称性转移到 vector parking functions。

Result: 通过对称性解决了 Dai、Fu、Qiu 关于 rational Dyck 路径的公开问题；在 vector parking functions 上得到广义 run 与 return 的对称性，并由此提出素分解（prime decomposition）的新概念；在 (a, b)-parking functions 情形下计算了 run 与 pri 精化的生成函数，该结果即使对经典 parking functions 也是新的。

Conclusion: 本文借助对合方法统一刻画了有界格路及 vector parking functions 中广义 run 与 return 的对称性，推广了已有理论，解决了公开问题，并为 parking functions 的精细化计数提供了新工具与新视角。

Abstract: Partly motivated by enumeration of parking functions and their variants, there is a long-standing interest in lattice paths refined by several statistics, including the notable $\mathsf{run}$ and $\mathsf{return}$. We consider bounded lattice paths, which are bounded by a given lattice path and are related to vector parking functions, for which we generalize $\mathsf{run}$ to composition runs, parameterized by a composition. By constructing involutions on these paths, we establish symmetries relating composition runs to some generalized return statistics. As an application, we settle an open problem of Dai, Fu, and Qiu on rational Dyck paths. The symmetries between generalized $\mathsf{run}$ and $\mathsf{return}$ are then transferred to vector parking functions, which suggest a new notion of prime decomposition. In the special case of $(a, b)$-parking functions, we compute the generating function refined by $\mathsf{run}$ and $\mathsf{pri}$, which is new even for classical parking functions.

</details>


### [140] [Sharp planar Turán bounds for quasi-double stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05706)
*Zehui Shao,Enqiang Zhu,Shaohui Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了禁用准双星图 $W_{2,4}$、$W_{2,5}$、$W_{3,4}$ 的平面图极值问题，确定了 $W_{2,4}$ 与 $W_{2,5}$ 的平面 Turán 密度（分别为 $9/4$ 与 $5/2$），刻画了含支配顶点且不含 $W_{3,4}$ 的平面图并给出其精确极值数 $\lfloor(5n-7)/2\rfloor$，同时给出三类禁用图的无 $W$ 平面三角剖分顶点数上界（8、12、11），前两者最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准双星 $W_{h,k}$ 由三顶点路径的一端接 $h$ 个叶子、另一端接 $k$ 个叶子构成。此前 Liu 等人在此类禁用图的平面极值问题上留有系数缺口（$W_{2,4}$、$W_{2,5}$ 对应的常数因子未被精确确定），本文旨在关闭这两个缺口，并进一步研究 $W_{3,4}$ 情形与相应的三角剖分顶点数上界。

Method: 证明结合了结构性刻画、局部删除与邻域度亏分析：利用高次顶点的结构限制、局部删除技术，以及半径为二的邻域内的度亏（degree deficits）来分析边数上界；对 $W_{3,4}$，先刻画含支配顶点且避开该树的平面图结构，再由此推导其精确极值数；并借助已知的最大度五平面图构造来证明下界匹配。

Result: （1）$W_{2,4}$-free 平面图边数上界为 $9n/4$，当 $8\mid n$ 时可达到，从而平面 Turán 密度为 $9/4$；（2）$W_{2,5}$-free 平面图边数上界为 $5n/2$，结合最大度五平面图构造得到对任意 $n\ge15$ 有 $\ex_{\PP}(n,W_{2,5})=\lfloor5n/2\rfloor$；（3）含支配顶点且不含 $W_{3,4}$ 的平面图精确极值数为 $\lfloor(5n-7)/2\rfloor$（$n\ge10$）；（4）$W$-free 平面三角剖分对 $W=W_{2,4},W_{2,5},W_{3,4}$ 的顶点数上界分别为 8、12、11，前两者最优。

Conclusion: 本文精确确定了 $W_{2,4}$ 与 $W_{2,5}$ 在平面图中的 Turán 密度并关闭了 Liu 等人结果中的系数缺口，给出含支配顶点的 $W_{3,4}$-free 平面图的精确极值数，以及三类禁用图对应平面三角剖分的顶点数上界，完善了准双星禁用图在平面极值图论中的刻画。

Abstract: We study $W$-free planar graphs for $W\in\{W_{2,4},W_{2,5},W_{3,4}\}$, where the quasi-double star $W_{h,k}$ is obtained from a three-vertex path by attaching $h$ leaves to one endpoint and $k$ leaves to the other. We prove that every $W_{2,4}$-free planar graph on $n$ vertices has at most $9n/4$ edges, and the bound is attained whenever $8\mid n$. This determines the planar Turán density of $W_{2,4}$ as $9/4$. We also establish the sharp upper bound $5n/2$ for $W_{2,5}$. Combined with known constructions of planar graphs of maximum degree five, it yields $\ex_{\PP}(n,W_{2,5})=\lfloor5n/2\rfloor$ for every $n\ge15$. These results close the two corresponding coefficient gaps in the bounds of Liu et~al. Our proofs use structural restrictions on high-degree vertices, local deletions, and degree deficits in neighborhoods of radius two. For $W_{3,4}$, we characterize the planar graphs with a dominating vertex that avoid this tree and determine their exact extremal number, $\lfloor(5n-7)/2\rfloor$, for every $n\ge10$. Finally, $W$-free planar triangulations have at most eight, twelve, and eleven vertices for $W=W_{2,4},W_{2,5},W_{3,4}$, respectively; the first two bounds are sharp.

</details>


### [141] [A sharp upper bound on the number of spanning forests of regular graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05722)
*T. Wu,S. Lu,X. Dong*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Bencs和Csikvári关于正则图生成森林数上界的猜想。设$G$为$n$个顶点的简单图，$F(G)$为其生成森林数。Bencs和Csikvári已证每个$r\geq 2$的$r$-正则图满足$F(G)\leq r^n$，并猜想对$r\geq 3$有$F(G)^{1/n}\leq \frac{(r-1)^{r-1}}{(r^2-2r-1)^{r/2-1}}$。本文肯定地解决了该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定$r$-正则图中生成森林数量的最优上界。此前仅知$F(G)\leq r^n$这一较粗糙的界，Bencs和Csikvári提出了一个更紧的、依赖于$r$的精确上界猜想，本文旨在证明该猜想。

Method: 摘要未明确给出具体证明方法，仅在开头设定记号（$G$为$n$顶点简单图，$F(G)$为生成森林数）并陈述待证猜想，随后声明肯定地解决了该猜想。

Result: 证明了Bencs和Csikvári的猜想成立：对$r\geq 3$的$r$-正则图$G$，有$F(G)^{1/n}\leq \frac{(r-1)^{r-1}}{(r^2-2r-1)^{r/2-1}}$，从而改进了已有的$F(G)\leq r^n$上界。

Conclusion: 该猜想得到肯定解决，给出了$r$-正则图生成森林数量的最优（或更紧）上界。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a simple graph on $n$ vertices, and let $F(G)$ denote the number of its spanning forests. Bencs and Csikvári [Upper bound for the number of spanning forests of regular graphs, European J. Combin. 110 (2023) 103677] proved that every $r$-regular graph $G$ with $r\geq 2$ satisfies $F(G) \leq r^{n}$. They further conjectured that for $r \geq 3$, \[ F(G)^{1/n} \leq \frac{(r - 1)^{r-1}}{(r^2 - 2r - 1)^{r/2-1}}. \] In this paper, we resolve this conjecture in the affirmative.

</details>


### [142] [On three conjectures of Kimberling concerning the array $\lfloor k\varphi^n\rfloor$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05776)
*Alex Ashburn*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了黄金比例 φ 下取整数组 T(n,k)=⌊kφⁿ⌋ 的行结构，完整证明了 Kimberling 关于交错行不相交、合并后生成 Wythoff 序列以及每个正整数在行包含计数 a(N) 中出现无穷多次的三个猜想，并给出了行不相交的精确判定规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kimberling 在 2022 年提出了若干关于取整数组 T(n,k)=⌊kφⁿ⌋（OEIS A128440）各行性质的猜想，涉及行不相交性、Wythoff 序列的恢复以及行包含计数 a(N) 的无穷频次，这些问题此前缺乏证明。作者旨在系统解决这些猜想并刻画行结构的深层规律。

Method: 利用 Skolem-Bang 定理证明前两个猜想，并推出行不相交的充要条件：R_i∩R_j=∅ 当且仅当 j-i 为奇数且整除 i。证明第三个猜想时，核心工具是显式确定包含奇数下标 Lucas 数的行，扩展了 Noppakaew 等人的结果，并引入"Lucas 平移"引理：若 N+1 不形如 L_{2e}（e≥1），则加上充分大的偶下标 Lucas 数不改变包含 N 的行集合。此外还结合自然密度估计与数值计算。

Result: 证明了 R_{2n-1} 与 R_{2n} 不相交，且两行合并后按秩替换所得序列正是下、上 Wythoff 序列；证明了每个正整数在 a(N) 的值中出现无穷多次；证明每个 a 取值对应的集合具有正自然密度；数值计算至 10^8 表明恰好落在 v≥2 行中的最小 N 是 Lucas 数 L_{4v-5}。

Conclusion: 该工作以 Skolem-Bang 定理和 Lucas 数相关引理为工具，完整解决了 Kimberling 关于黄金比例取整数组的三个猜想，给出了行不相交的精确刻画，并揭示了行包含计数 a(N) 的密度性质，同时以计算证据提出最小多行数 N 与 Lucas 数对应的规律。

Abstract: Let $\varphi$ be the golden ratio and let $R_n=\{\lfloor k\varphi^n\rfloor : k\ge 1\}$ be the $n$-th row of the array $T(n,k)=\lfloor k\varphi^n\rfloor$ (OEIS A128440). In 2022 Kimberling conjectured that the rows $R_{2n-1}$ and $R_{2n}$ are disjoint, and that after the two rows are merged and each entry is replaced by its rank, they become the lower and upper Wythoff sequences. He also conjectured (OEIS A358359) that if $a(N)$ is the number of rows containing $N$, then every positive integer occurs infinitely often among the values of $a$. We show that the first two conjectures follow quickly from the Skolem-Bang theorem, which also yields the exact rule for when two rows are disjoint: $R_i\cap R_j=\emptyset$ ($i<j$) if and only if $j-i$ is odd and divides $i$. We then prove the third conjecture. The main tools are an explicit determination of the rows containing an odd-indexed Lucas number, which extends a result of Noppakaew, Kanwarunyu and Wanitchatchawan, and a "Lucas shift" lemma: if $N+1$ is not of the form $L_{2e}$ with $e\ge1$, then adding a sufficiently large even-indexed Lucas number to $N$ does not change the set of rows containing it. We also show that each value of $a$ is taken on a set of positive natural density, and we report computations up to $10^8$ suggesting that the least $N$ lying in exactly $v\ge 2$ rows is the Lucas number $L_{4v-5}$.

</details>


### [143] [Resolving Two Open Problems of Planar $B_k$-CPG Recognition: $k=0,1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05838)
*Bin Sun,Shou-Jun Xu,Yu Yang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了识别最大度为8的平面B0-CPG图和最大度为11的平面B1-CPG图均为NP完全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Champseix、Galby、Munaro和Ries留下了三个平面识别问题未解决，本文旨在解决其中的两个。

Method: 通过复杂性理论归约，研究平面B0-CPG（最大度8）和平面B1-CPG（最大度11）图类的识别问题。

Result: 证明了识别最大度为8的平面B0-CPG图是NP完全的，以及识别最大度为11的平面B1-CPG图是NP完全的。

Conclusion: 该结果解决了Champseix等人遗留的三个平面识别问题中的两个。

Abstract: A $k$-bend path is a non-self-intersecting polyline that lies on a grid and consists of at most $k+1$ axis-parallel line segments. A $B_k$-CPG graph is a graph whose vertices can be represented by pairwise interiorly disjoint $k$-bend paths on a grid such that two vertices are adjacent if and only if the corresponding grid paths touch at a grid point. We prove that recognizing planar $B_0$-CPG graphs of maximum degree 8 is NP-complete, and that recognizing planar $B_1$-CPG graphs of maximum degree 11 is NP-complete. These results settle two of the three planar recognition problems left open by Champseix, Galby, Munaro, and Ries.

</details>


### [144] [A counterexample to the circuit-cocircuit intersection conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06033)
*Houshan Fu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文构造了一个秩12、含24个元素的恒等自对偶二元拟阵，其电路-余电路交集大小恰为0、2、4、6、8、12，从而在k=12时否定了Oxley的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Oxley曾猜想：若拟阵的某个电路-余电路交集大小为k≥4，则该拟阵必存在大小为k-2的电路-余电路交集。本文旨在检验并否定这一猜想。

Method: 利用经典扩展二元Golay码的标准多项式表示，构造一个秩12、含24个元素的恒等自对偶二元拟阵。证明自包含，不依赖码的完整重量分布，也不依赖对所有电路的枚举。

Result: 所构造拟阵的电路-余电路交集大小恰为0、2、4、6、8和12，其中存在大小为12的交集，但不存在大小为10的交集，因此构成对Oxley猜想在k=12时的反例。

Conclusion: Oxley关于电路-余电路交集的猜想在k=12时不成立；该反例由扩展二元Golay码出发的恒等自对偶二元拟阵给出，且证明简洁自包含。

Abstract: Oxley conjectured that a matroid with a circuit-cocircuit intersection of size $k\geq 4$ has one of size $k-2$. Using the standard polynomial representation of the classical extended binary Golay code, we construct an identically self-dual binary matroid of rank $12$ on $24$ elements. Its circuit-cocircuit intersection sizes are exactly $0,2,4,6,8$, and $12$, disproving the conjecture for $k=12$. The proof is self-contained and does not rely on the full weight distribution of the code or on an enumeration of all circuits.

</details>


### [145] [Optimal spectral supersaturation for cliques and odd cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06038)
*Hongzhang Chen,Yongtao Li*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文在图$Y_{n,r,q}$（由Turán图$T_{n,r}$在最大部内加入$q$条互不相交的边得到）的谱条件下，精确确定了关于团$K_{r+1}$和奇圈$C_{2k+1}$的$q$的阈值常数$\delta_F$及其最优性，并给出了对应的$F$拷贝数的精确计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fang、Li、Lin和Ma此前证明了对于色临界图$F$（$\chi(F)=r+1$），存在常数$\delta_F>0$，使得当$1\le q\le\delta_F\sqrt{n}$且$\lambda(G)\ge\lambda(Y_{n,r,q})$时，$G$至少包含$q\,c(n,F)$个$F$拷贝。该结果的$q=O(\sqrt n)$上界是最优的（相差常数因子），与Mubayi、Pikhurko和Yilma在边设定下的线性阶$n$形成对比，但对任何$F$而言精确常数$\delta_F$仍然未知。本文旨在确定该阈值常数。

Method: 基于Fang、Li、Lin和Ma的结构性结果，针对$F$为团和奇圈的情形，通过谱极值分析确定阈值常数。对于团，定义$\delta_r :=(1-1/r)\sqrt2$，证明对于任意$\varepsilon>0$、充分大的$n$以及$1\le q\le(\delta_r-\varepsilon)\sqrt n$，任何满足$\lambda(G)\ge\lambda(Y_{n,r,q})$的$n$顶点图$G$至少包含$q\,c(n,K_{r+1})$个$K_{r+1}$，并证明$\delta_r$是最优的；对于奇圈，证明阈值为$1/\sqrt2$，即$1\le q\le(1/\sqrt2-\varepsilon)\sqrt n$时至少含$q\,c(n,C_{2k+1})$个$C_{2k+1}$，且$1/\sqrt2$最优。

Result: 对团$K_{r+1}$，阈值精确为$\delta_r=(1-1/r)\sqrt2$；对奇圈$C_{2k+1}$，阈值精确为$1/\sqrt2$。两者均被证明是最优的。此外结果同时确定了$F$拷贝的精确计数以及$q$的最优取值范围。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了谱设定下团与奇圈的阈值常数，填补了Fang等人遗留的精确常数空白。结果表明谱设定下的行为不同于经典边设定：边设定中$q$的范围是$n$阶，且团的阈值为$1/r$（Lovász和Simonovits），奇圈阈值为$1/2$（Pikhurko和Yilma）。

Abstract: Let $Y_{n,r,q}$ be the graph obtained from the Turán graph $T_{n,r}$ by adding $q$ pairwise disjoint edges inside a largest part, and let $c(n,F)$ be the minimum number of copies of $F$ created by adding a single edge to $T_{n,r}$. Fang, Li, Lin and Ma proved that for every color-critical graph $F$ with $χ(F)=r+1$, there exists a constant $δ_F>0$ such that for all sufficiently large $n$ and all $1\le q\le δ_F \sqrt{n}$, the condition $λ(G)\geλ(Y_{n,r,q})$ forces at least $q\, c(n,F)$ copies of $F$. The bound $q=O(\sqrt{n}\,)$ is tight up to a constant factor, in contrast to the linear order $n$ of the edge setting of Mubayi, Pikhurko and Yilma, but the exact constant $δ_F$ remained unknown for any $F$. In this paper, building on a structural result of Fang, Li, Lin and Ma, we determine the threshold $δ_F$ when $F$ is a clique and an odd cycle. For every $r\ge2$, we denote $δ_r :=(1-\tfrac1r)\sqrt2$ and prove that for every $\varepsilon>0$, if $n$ is sufficiently large and $1\le q\le(δ_r-\varepsilon)\sqrt n$, then every $n$-vertex graph $G$ with $λ(G)\geλ(Y_{n,r,q})$ contains at least $q\,c(n,K_{r+1})$ copies of $K_{r+1}$, and $δ_r$ is best possible. For odd cycles, the threshold is $1/\sqrt2$. For every $k\ge1$ and $\varepsilon>0$, if $n$ is sufficiently large and $1\le q\le(1/\sqrt2-\varepsilon)\sqrt n$, then every $n$-vertex graph $G$ with $λ(G)\geλ(Y_{n,2,q})$ contains at least $q\,c(n,C_{2k+1})$ copies of $C_{2k+1}$, and $1/\sqrt2$ is best possible. Our results determine both the exact count of copies and the optimal range of $q$. The behavior in the spectral setting differs from the classical edge setting, in which the range of $q$ is of order $n$ and the threshold is $1/r$ for cliques by Lovász and Simonovits, and $1/2$ for odd cycles by Pikhurko and Yilma.

</details>


### [146] [Polynomial families of incident flags and explicit off-diagonal Ramsey graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06081)
*Brecht Verbeken*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文从入射点—超平面旗的多项式族构造显式非对角Ramsey图，利用通用插值将团构型的精确维数转化为系数—标号关联约束，在临界维数用多项式分离禁止对像与对角线，并借助标量限制保持有理性，得到固定s的指数主导尺度s/(2log₂s)及R(16,t)的显式下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式非对角Ramsey图的下界构造长期依赖概率方法或有限域上的代数构造，指数增益通常有限且缺乏对一般禁止构型的统一处理；本文旨在通过多项式与代数几何机制获得可计算的显式指数并推广到一般禁止构型。

Method: 构造入射点—超平面旗的多项式族；用通用插值将有序团构型的精确维数转译为系数—标号关联的界；在临界维数用多项式分离禁止对像与对角线并删边以保持三角秩证书；用标量限制实现有理解端点避免舍入损失；同时给出无消元变体与带区分边的通用过滤论证。

Result: 固定s时指数主导尺度为s/(2log₂s)；短Frobenius关系给出显式例子R(16,t)≥Ω(t^2.0539221767…);有限纤维构造给出范围s²(log s)²=o(log t)；固定参数下代数预处理终止，域初始化、顶点解码与邻接判定均为关于扩域次数的确定性多项式时间。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从多项式插值与代数分离到显式Ramsey图下界的路线，给出可计算的固定s指数并推广到一般禁止构型，同时保持构造的确定性与多项式时间可解码性。

Abstract: We construct explicit off-diagonal Ramsey graphs from polynomial families of incident point--hyperplane flags. Universal interpolation translates the exact dimension of ordered clique configurations into a bound on their coefficient--label incidence. At the critical dimension, a polynomial separates the forbidden-pair image from its diagonal; deleting the corresponding edges preserves a triangular rank certificate. Restriction of scalars realizes the rational endpoint without rounding loss. The resulting fixed-$s$ exponent has leading scale $s/(2\log_2s)$, and short Frobenius relations give the explicit example $R(16,t)\geΩ(t^{2.0539221767\ldots})$. A finite-fiber construction gives the range $s^2(\log s)^2=o(\log t)$. For fixed parameters, algebraic preprocessing terminates and field initialization, vertex decoding and adjacency take deterministic time polynomial in the extension degree. We also retain an elimination-free variant and formulate the filtering argument for general forbidden configurations with a distinguished edge.

</details>


### [147] [The separator-based derivations for arrangements of monomial groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06098)
*Zixuan Wang,Weili Guo*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究单值群 $G(r,p,\ell)$ 对应超平面配置的导子模，通过将分隔子与积分表达式结合，证明当 $p<r$ 时图 $G$ 定义的子配置 $\mathcal{D}(\mathcal{A}_G)$ 由标准导子与有限个基于分隔子的导子生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 Mühlherr 针对图形配置的分隔子构造启发，探索分隔子方法在更一般超平面配置（单值群情形）中的适用性，推广完全图情形。

Method: 将分隔子（separator）与积分表达式（integral expressions）相结合，构造图 $G$ 所定义子配置 $\mathcal{A}_G$ 的导子模生成元。

Result: 当 $p<r$ 时，导子模 $\mathcal{D}(\mathcal{A}_G)$ 由标准导子 $\eta_0,\dots,\eta_{\kappa(G)}$ 以及有限个基于分隔子的导子共同生成。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了完全图情形，为分隔子方法在超平面配置研究中的更广泛适用性提供了新证据。

Abstract: We investigate derivation modules of hyperplane arrangements associated with the monomial groups $G(r,p,\ell)$. Motivated by Mühlherr's separator-based construction for graphic arrangements, we extend this approach by combining separators with integral expressions. For a subarrangement $\mathcal{A}_G$ defined by a graph $G$, we show that, when $p<r$, the derivation module $\mathcal{D}(\mathcal{A}_G)$ is generated by the standard derivations $η_0,\dots,η_{κ(G)}$ together with a finite set of separator-based derivations. This result generalizes the complete graph case and provides new evidence toward the broader applicability of separator methods in the study of hyperplane arrangements.

</details>


### [148] [On rigidity properties of unit-distance graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06246)
*Sean Dewar,Georg Grasegger,Alison La Porta,Jan Legerský,Anthony Nixon*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文分析单位距离图及其子类（penny图、marble图、matchstick图）的刚性与柔性，回答了关于penny/marble图刚性的公开问题，并建立了penny图与NAC着色之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单位距离图在附加几何条件后形成penny/marble图与matchstick图等自然子类；由于任意实现运动必须保持这些额外条件，其框架的刚性与柔性性质不同于经典杆-铰链刚性理论，因此需要专门分析这些刚性问题的动机。

Method: 通过施加几何约束（非边距离不小于一，或平面直线画法无交叉）定义penny/marble图与matchstick图子类，并在保持这些条件的框架运动下分析其刚性与柔性；同时引入NAC着色工具建立与penny图的联系。

Result: 回答了近期关于penny图与marble图刚性的公开问题，并建立了penny图与NAC着色概念之间的链接。

Conclusion: penny图、marble图、matchstick图及一般单位距离图的刚性/柔性行为受附加几何条件影响，区别于经典杆-铰链理论；NAC着色为研究penny图刚性提供了新视角。

Abstract: A unit-distance graph is a graph which admits a realisation in Euclidean space in which every edge has unit length. Imposing further geometric conditions on the non-edges gives a family of natural subclasses. Requiring that no two vertices lie at distance less than one gives penny and marble graphs: the contact graphs of collections of equal radii $d$-dimensional spheres with non-overlapping interiors for $d=2,3$. Requiring instead that the straight-line drawing in the plane be non-crossing gives matchstick graphs. Since any motion of a realisation must preserve these extra conditions, the rigidity and flexibility properties of the resulting frameworks differ from those of classical bar-joint rigidity theory. In this note we analyse various rigidity problems for penny and marble graphs, matchstick graphs and unit-distance graphs. In particular we answer a recent open problem on penny and marble graph rigidity and establish a link between penny graphs and the concept of NAC-colourings.

</details>


### [149] [Combinatorial twisted bialgebras and combinatorial twisted double bialgebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06256)
*Lo{ï}c Foissy*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文在species与twisted bialgebra（Hopf monoid）语言下提出"组合扭曲双代数"（CTB）框架，给出万有对象Exch及其子对象Exch₋、Exch₊、Exch₊₊，建立特征与多项式不变量的对应，并进一步推广到组合扭曲双（余）代数（CTDB）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典组合Hopf代数（图、根树、偏序集、超图、对称函数、布尔映射等）种类繁多但缺乏统一代数框架，且其coproduct的组合信息、antipode/Eulerian幂等元的无消去公式、以及双coproduct结构（如CTDB）缺少系统刻画。

Method: 采用species与twisted bialgebra语言定义CTB范畴：要求product、coproduct与底层组合结构满足强相容性且morphism保持组合结构。构造由满足exchange条件的composition族生成的万有CTB对象Exch，及其带特殊特征ε的terminal性质；利用固定集合上composition的自然序定义三个子对象。进一步引入含两个相互作用coproduct的CTDB，并提出quasi-cocommutativity概念；结合Loday–Ronco刚性定理的twisted版本分析Exch的代数结构。

Result: (Exch,ε)成为带组合特征的CTB范畴中的terminal对象，给出编码coproduct组合信息的万有态射；建立特征与多项式不变量之间对应，图色多项式与偏序集Ehrhart多项式作为特例自然出现，且固定次数下仅有有限多个多项式不变量；在CTB条件下给出antipode与Eulerian幂等元的无消去公式（以语言学中根树Hopf代数为示例）；得到交替符号条件与reciprocity情形的结果；并非所有CTB可成为CTDB，Exch₊不是CTDB，但可构造唯一且极大（关于包含）的CTDB子对象；余交换情形下超图CTB（基于连通系统与antichain cover的两个子对象）居中心地位，且万有多项式不变量恒为超图色多项式。

Conclusion: CTB为大量经典组合Hopf代数提供统一代数框架，而CTDB在保持组合丰富性的同时统一图、偏序集、超图、根树等双coproduct例子；万有多项式不变量为antipode与Eulerian幂等元提供无消去公式，余交换情形归结为超图色多项式，twisted刚性定理刻画了Exch的代数结构。

Abstract: We provide in this text a new framework for a family of combinatorial Hopf algebras. We adopt for this the language of species and twisted bialgebras (also known as Hopf monoids). We define the category of combinatorial twisted bialgebras (briefly, CTBs), a class of twisted bialgebras satisfying strong combinatorial conditions of compatibilities of the product and the coproduct with the underlying combinatorial structure. The considered morphisms also have to respect the combinatorial structure, in some sense. Many classical examples fit into this framework, including Hopf algebras of graphs, rooted trees, posets, hypergraphs, symmetric functions, boolean maps, etc. Most of known combinatorial Hopf algebras can be obtained from a CTB by application of a Fock functor, or by duality. A central result is the introduction of a universal CTB denoted by $\mathbf{Exch}$, built from collections of compositions satisfying a certain exchange condition. It has a special character $ε$, such that the pair $(\mathbf{Exch},ε)$ plays a role analogous to that of quasi-symmetric functions for graded and connected Hopf algebras: it is the terminal object in the category of CTBs equipped with combinatorial characters. This leads to universal morphisms that encode important information about the combinatorial structure of coproducts. The natural order on the set of compositions on a fixed set is used to define three important sub-objects $\mathbf{Exch}\_-$, $\mathbf{Exch}\_+$ and $\mathbf{Exch}\_{+\hspace{-2mm}+}$ of $\mathbf{Exch}$. We also studies polynomial invariants associated with CTBs. It establishes a correspondence between characters and polynomial invariants. Well-known invariants such as the chromatic polynomial of graphs and the Ehrhart polynomials of posets arise naturally within this framework. We show that, for a given degree, only finitely many polynomial invariants can occur. We show that these universal polynomial invariants can be used to give cancellation-free formulas for the antipode or the Eulerian idempotent, under conditions on the CTB, and use this for an example based on rooted trees, coming from a Hopf algebra used in linguistics. Results are also obtained for families of polynomial invariants with the alternating sign condition, or satisfying a reciprocity principle. We also extend the theory to combinatorial twisted double bialgebras (briefly, CTDBs), which involve two interacting coproduct structures. These generalize previously known examples arising from graphs, posets, hypergraphs, and rooted trees. The twisted approach preserves the combinatorial richness of these structures while providing a unified algebraic framework. We show that not any CTB can be made a CTDB, leading to the introduction of the notion of quasi-cocommutativity. In particular, we show that $\mathbf{Exch}\_+$ is not a CTDB, and construct a sub-object which is, in a unique way, and maximal for the inclusion. In the cocommutative case, simplifications naturally occur, and we show that in this case, a CTB of hypergraphs plays a central role, especially two sub-objects based on connectedness systems and antichain covers. We also show that, in the cocommutative case, the universal polynomial invariants are always chromatic polynomials of hypergraphs, no matter which CTB is considered. Finally, forgetting the combinatorial underlying structure, we study the algebraic structure of $\mathrm{Exch}$, with the help of a twisted version of Loday and Ronco's rigidity theorem for infinitesimal bialgebras.

</details>


### [150] [A Conjecture on Circular Permutations over Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06289)
*Xin-Qi Luo,Yue-Feng She*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Zhi-Wei Sun关于有限域上非零元素循环排列使a₀+aᵢaᵢ₊₁为本原元的猜想在q>18,888,871时成立；对a₀=0，结论对所有q>4成立；对a₀≠0，利用Chvátal–Erdős定理构造Hamilton圈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhi-Wei Sun提出了一个关于有限域本原元的猜想：对任意a₀∈F_q，都存在非零元素的循环排列使每个a₀+aᵢaᵢ₊₁为本原元。该猜想联系有限域本原元分布与组合结构，具有研究价值，本文旨在证明该猜想在充分大q时成立。

Method: 将问题转化为图论问题：构造以F_q*为顶点集的图，边对应使a₀+xy为本原元的元素对，寻找Hamilton圈。对a₀≠0的情形，验证图满足Chvátal–Erdős定理的条件（连通度不小于独立数）以获得Hamilton圈；对a₀=0的情形单独处理。

Result: 证明了该猜想对q>18,888,871成立；对a₀=0，结论对所有q>4成立。

Conclusion: 该猜想在充分大q时获证，借助Chvátal–Erdős定理将有限域本原元问题与Hamilton圈存在性联系起来，为相关猜想提供了有力证据。

Abstract: Let \(\mathbb{F}_q\) be a finite field with \(q>7\). Zhi-Wei Sun conjectured that for every \(a_0\in\mathbb{F}_q\), there is a circular permutation \((a_1,\dots,a_{q-1})\) of non-zero elements of \(\mathbb{F}_q\) such that \(a_0+a_i a_{i+1}\) is primitive for \(1\le i\le q-1\), where \(a_q=a_1\). In this paper, we confirm this conjecture for \(q>18\,888\,871\). For \(a_0=0\), the conclusion holds for all \(q>4\). For \(a_0\ne0\), we apply the Chvátal--Erdős theorem to obtain a Hamilton cycle.

</details>


### [151] [Geometric Realizations with Strong Self-Duality Part I: Non-Bipartite Quadrangulations of the Projective Plane and Strongly Involutive Self-Dual Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06300)
*Gábor Damásdi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究强对合自对偶图（SISD图）与非二分射影平面四边形剖分（NBQP图）两类图及其几何表示，第一部分建立了2-连通SISD地图与非二分射影平面四边形剖分之间的一一对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这两类图在众多几何问题中自然出现：NBQP图与R³中的直径图、成对相交圆与伪圆族的切触图、广义thrackle以及负自极多面体主对角线构成的图相关；SISD图则对应负自极多面体的骨架、自对偶锥和极值球多面体。因此研究二者的联系有助于统一处理相关几何问题。

Method: 在第一部分中，作者研究2-连通强对合自对偶地图的约化顶点-面关联结构，并将其与非二分射影平面四边形剖分进行对应分析，从而建立两类对象之间的双向映射。

Result: 证明了一个2-连通强对合自对偶地图的约化顶点-面关联结构是非二分射影平面四边形剖分，且每个非二分射影平面四边形剖分都可由此方式得到，由此建立了二者之间的一一对应关系。

Conclusion: 该对应使得两类图之间的自然性质可以相互转译，为整个系列后续部分的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this series of articles, we study two graph classes and their various geometric representations. The first class consists of strongly involutive self-dual graphs (SISD graphs); the second consists of non-bipartite quadrangulations of the projective plane (NBQP graphs). These classes naturally arise in many geometric problems. For example, NBQP graphs are connected to diameter graphs in $\mathbb{R}^3$, tangency graphs of families of pairwise intersecting circles and pseudocircles, generalized thrackles, and graphs formed by the main diagonals of negatively self-polar polytopes. On the other hand, SISD graphs correspond to the skeletons of negatively self-polar polytopes, self-dual cones, and extremal ball-polytopes.
  In Part I we show a natural one-to-one correspondence between non-bipartite quadrangulations of the projective plane and 2-connected strongly involutive self-dual maps. In particular, we show that the reduced vertex-face incidence structure of a 2-connected strongly involutive self-dual map is a non-bipartite quadrangulation of the projective plane, and every non-bipartite quadrangulation of the projective plane arises that way. This allows us to translate natural properties from one class to the other. These results lay the foundation for later parts of the series.

</details>


### [152] [Geometric Realizations with Strong Self-Duality Part II: Diameter Graphs, Reuleaux Polyhedra, and Thrackles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06340)
*Gábor Damásdi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了Reuleaux多面体的组合结构，证明了任何2-连通强对合自对偶图都可由Reuleaux多面体的骨架实现，并给出直径图与射影平面非二分四边形剖分图之间的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grünbaum、Heppes和Straszewicz证明了R^3中直径图的边数上界为2|X|-2；Kupitz、Martini和Perles证明Reuleaux多面体骨架必为2-连通强对合自对偶图并猜想简单3-连通情形下反之亦成立。本文旨在确定Reuleaux多面体的完整组合刻画，并深化对直径图结构及其构造的理解。

Method: 在R^3中构造新的直径图与Reuleaux多面体实例；证明任意非二分射影平面四边形剖分图均可实现为直径图；借助刚性理论的思想构造实现；并将结果应用于常宽体的构造。

Result: 证实了Kupitz-Martini-Perles猜想：任意2-连通（未必简单）强对合自对偶图都可作为Reuleaux多面体的骨架实现；证明3维直径图恰为非二分射影平面四边形剖分图的子图这一逆向命题，确认并强化了Montejano等人关于强对合自对偶图度量嵌入的猜想；给出Reuleaux多面体组合结构的完整刻画。

Conclusion: 完成了R^3中Reuleaux多面体组合结构的完整刻画，建立了直径图与射影平面二次剖分图的对应关系，并讨论了常宽体构造、Steinitz定理及Borsuk猜想等应用。

Abstract: In this paper we construct new examples of diameter graphs and Reuleaux polyhedra in $\mathbb{R}^3$, obtaining a full characterization of their combinatorial structure. For a finite set of points $X\subset\mathbb{R}^d$, its diameter graph is the graph on vertex set $X$ where pairs forming a diameter pair are connected by an edge. Grünbaum, Heppes and Straszewicz independently proved that the diameter graph of $X\subset \mathbb{R}^3$ has at most $2|X|-2$ edges, answering a question of Vázsonyi. Their proof relied on ball polytopes. The ball polytope $\mathcal{B}(X)$ is the intersection of the unit balls centered at the points of $X$.
  We call a ball polytope a Reuleaux polyhedron if the centers form a family with $2|X|-2$ diameter pairs. Kupitz, Martini and Perles showed that the skeleton of a Reuleaux polyhedron must be a 2-connected strongly involutive self-dual graph. They conjectured that in the simple 3-connected case this is also sufficient. We not only confirm this conjecture, but we show that any 2-connected (not necessarily simple) strongly involutive self-dual graph arises as the skeleton of a Reuleaux polyhedron.
  To construct the new Reuleaux polyhedra we construct new diameter graphs. It was known that any 3-dimensional diameter graph is a subgraph of a non-bipartite quadrangulation of the projective plane. We show that the reverse holds. That is, for any such graph we construct a diameter realization. This also confirms and strengthens a conjecture of Montejano, Pauli, Raggi, Roldán-Pensado on metric embeddings of strongly involutive self-dual graphs. The construction relies on ideas from rigidity theory.
  We also discuss a number of applications of these results, such as the construction of bodies of constant width and connections to Steinitz's theorem and Borsuk's conjecture.

</details>


### [153] [Magic positivity for polar duals of pseudo-symmetric smooth Fano polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06419)
*Hidefumi Ohsugi,Akiyoshi Tsuchiya*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了拟对称单纯反身多面体的极对偶的Ehrhart多项式具有magic正性，对拟对称光滑Fano多面体所有magic系数严格正；其极对偶Ehrhart正、h*-多项式实根且γ-正。另证任意循环的对称边多面体极对偶的Ehrhart多项式是magic正的，肯定回答Konoike问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由Gal猜想出发，Ferroni与Higashitani猜想任何具有二次三角剖分的Gorenstein格多面体的h*-多项式是γ-正的；结合关于光滑格多面体toric理想二次性质的猜想，暗示光滑Gorenstein格多面体应有γ-正的h*-多项式。本文旨在为该类猜想提供证据与推进，并解决Konoike关于对称边多面体正性问题的公开提问。

Method: 研究对象为拟对称单纯反身多面体及其极对偶、拟对称光滑Fano多面体，以及任意循环的对称边多面体的极对偶。通过Ehrhart理论分析Ehrhart多项式的magic正性（乃至严格正性），并利用h*-多项式的实根性与γ-正性之间的联系进行论证。

Result: 证明了：每个拟对称单纯反身多面体的极对偶的Ehrhart多项式是magic正的；对拟对称光滑Fano多面体，所有magic系数严格为正；从而每个拟对称单纯反身多面体的极对偶是Ehrhart正的，且其h*-多项式实根且γ-正。另外，任意循环的对称边多面体的极对偶的Ehrhart多项式是magic正的，正面回答了Konoike的问题（其先前仅得到该族的局部正性结果）。

Conclusion: 本文为光滑Gorenstein格多面体的γ-正性猜想提供了新证据，建立了拟对称反身/Fano多面体极对偶的正性结果（magic正、严格正、Ehrhart正、实根、γ-正），并完整解决了对称边多面体族的magic正性问题。

Abstract: Motivated by Gal's conjecture, Ferroni and Higashitani conjectured that the $h^*$-polynomial of any Gorenstein lattice polytope admitting a quadratic triangulation is $γ$-positive. Together with conjectures predicting quadratic properties of toric ideals of smooth lattice polytopes, this suggests that smooth Gorenstein lattice polytopes should have $γ$-positive $h^*$-polynomials. In this paper, we prove that the Ehrhart polynomial of the polar dual of every pseudo-symmetric simplicial reflexive polytope is magic positive. For pseudo-symmetric smooth Fano polytopes, we prove the stronger statement that all magic coefficients are strictly positive. Consequently, for every pseudo-symmetric simplicial reflexive polytope, its polar dual is Ehrhart positive and has a real-rooted and $γ$-positive $h^*$-polynomial. We also prove that the Ehrhart polynomial of the polar dual of the symmetric edge polytope of every cycle is magic positive. This gives an affirmative answer to a question of Konoike, who had previously proved partial positivity results for this family.

</details>


### [154] [Descartes' rule of signs for arbitrary fewnomial systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06435)
*Frédéric Bihan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用Bihan–Sottile的Gale对偶与Khovanskii–Rolle论证，研究含n+k+1个单项式的n元实多项式系统的正解个数，通过按排列的flat与chamber进行递归计数，得到仅依赖系数矩阵及指数矩阵（及其提升）的定向拟阵的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正解个数（正性/正解上界）是实代数几何与稀疏多项式系统的核心问题。传统Bézout型界往往过大，作者希望利用Gale对偶与Khovanskii–Rolle方法，给出更精细、且只依赖组合结构（定向拟阵）的上界。

Method: 通过Gale对偶将正解转化为多面体Δ⊂R^k中k个方程∏_i p_i^{B_ij}=1的解；用Khovanskii–Rolle论证将解数上界为迭代Jacobian Γ_k,…,Γ_1在Δ中的公共零点数加上若干曲线非紧分支数。第一项用Bézout数减去排列{p_i=0}其他chamber中的零点数（由facet-count公式给出边界度下界）来界定；曲线分支终止于Δ面上Jacobian的零点，在排列的flat上通过显式约化系统计数。由此得到对所有flat与chamber的递归，递归在线（line）上以circuit的Descartes符号法则为起点。

Result: 得到正解个数的上界，该上界仅依赖于系数矩阵的定向拟阵，以及指数矩阵及其提升的定向拟阵。

Conclusion: 提出了一套基于Gale对偶、Khovanskii–Rolle与定向拟阵递归计数的组合框架，为正解个数给出了仅由组合结构决定的显式上界。

Abstract: We consider systems of $n$ real polynomial equations in $n$ variables with $n+k+1$ monomials. By the Gale duality of Bihan and Sottile, their positive solutions correspond to the solutions of a system of $k$ equations $\prod_ip_i^{B_{ij}}=1$ in a polyhedron $Δ\subset\mathbb{R}^k$, where the $p_i$ are affine functions, and a Khovanskii--Rolle argument bounds their number by the number of common zeros in $Δ$ of iterated Jacobians $Γ_k,\dots,Γ_1$ plus the number of noncompact branches of certain curves. We bound the first term by the Bézout number minus the numbers of zeros in the other chambers of the arrangement $\{p_i=0\}$, which we bound from below by boundary degrees given by a facet-count formula. The branches of the curves end at zeros of the Jacobians on faces of $Δ$, which we count on the flats of the arrangement through explicit reduced systems. The resulting recursion over all flats and chambers starts on lines with Descartes' rule of signs for circuits. We obtain upper bounds for the number of positive solutions which only depend on the oriented matroid of the coefficient matrix and on the oriented matroids of the exponent matrix and of its liftings.

</details>


### [155] [Deciding lettericity is NP-complete, even for four-colorable comparability graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06482)
*Vincent Vatter*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明判定图的lettericity是否至多为k是NP完全的，即使对四色可比图也成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定lettericity问题的计算复杂性，并扩大其NP完全性成立的图类范围。

Method: 从二部图G构造归约：取其关联图，将每个顶点膨胀为3个顶点的团或独立集；所得图的lettericity由G的顶点数、边数以及G中四点边不相交路径的最大数量决定。

Result: 判定图的lettericity是否至多为k是NP完全的，即使对四色可比图亦然。

Conclusion: lettericity判定问题在受限图类上依然NP完全，其难度来源于边集可否划分为四点路径这一NP完全问题。

Abstract: We prove that deciding whether a graph has lettericity at most k is NP-complete, even for four-colorable comparability graphs. Our reduction maps a bipartite graph G to the graph obtained from its incidence graph by inflating each vertex by a clique or an independent set of three vertices. The lettericity of this graph is determined by the numbers of vertices and edges of G and the greatest number of edge-disjoint paths on four vertices in G. Teypaz and Rapine showed that deciding whether the edges of a bipartite graph can be partitioned into paths on four vertices is NP-complete.

</details>


### [156] [Random independent sets in triangle-free and linear Berge-$C_4$-free hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06499)
*Jing Yu,Junchi Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究一致超图上独立集的概率分布，给出无三角形及线性Berge-C4-free超图中随机独立集的顶点包含概率下界，并推出相应分数色数的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超图上建立随机独立集的概率下界与分数色数（χ_f）上界是极值组合与概率组合的经典问题。已有结果常依赖线性假设或三角形自由条件，本文希望去掉线性假设，并在允许Berge三角形的线性超图情形给出更精细的界。

Method: 基于Martinsson与Steiner提出的有序随机选取过程（ordered random pick process）。针对不同超图类选择不同的选取函数并进行迭代，证明该随机过程的输出即为所需的随机独立集。

Result: 对每个固定r≥1：（1）在无三角形的(r+1)-一致超图（无线性的假设）中，构造随机独立集I，使得当d_w(v)→∞时 P(v∈I)≥(r/(r+1)·(r(r+1))^{-1/r}+o_r(1))·(log d_w(v)/d_w(v))^{1/r}；由此推出d-退化无三角形(r+1)-一致超图满足 χ_f(G)≤((r+1)/r·(r(r+1))^{1/r}+o_r(1))·(d/log d)^{1/r}，d→∞。（2）在线性Berge-C4-free（允许Berge三角形）超图中，对充分大的阈值D，存在随机独立集I，对所有d_w(v)≥D的顶点一致地有 P(v∈I)≥(1-o_r(1))·(log d_w(v)/(r·d_w(v)))^{1/r}，D→∞；并推出d-退化线性Berge-C4-free (r+1)-一致超图满足 χ_f(G)≤(1+o_r(1))·(rd/log d)^{1/r}。

Conclusion: 通过有序随机选取过程及适当的选取函数与迭代，可在无三角形超图与线性Berge-C4-free超图中获得所需的随机独立集，从而统一给出分数色数的渐近上界。

Abstract: We study probability distributions on independent sets of uniform hypergraphs with local constraints. For a positive vertex-weight function $w$ on an $(r+1)$-uniform hypergraph, define the weighted degree of vertex $v$ by \[ d_w(v):= \sum_{e\ni v} \left( \prod_{u\in e\setminus\{v\}}\dfrac{w(u)}{w(v)} \right)^{1/r}. \] For each fixed $r\geq1$, our first result gives, in every triangle-free $(r+1)$-uniform hypergraph (without a linearity assumption), a random independent set $I$ satisfying \[ \mathbb P(v\in I)\geq \left( \frac{r}{r+1}(r(r+1))^{-1/r}+o_r(1) \right) \left(\frac{\log d_w(v)}{d_w(v)}\right)^{1/r}, \qquad d_w(v)\to+\infty. \] The same result also implies that every $d$-degenerate triangle-free $(r+1)$-uniform hypergraph satisfies \[ χ_f(G)\leq \left( \dfrac{r+1}{r}(r(r+1))^{1/r}+o_r(1) \right) \left(\dfrac{d}{\log d}\right)^{1/r}, \qquad d\to+\infty. \] Our second result concerns linear Berge-$C_4$-free hypergraphs which allow Berge triangles. In this setting, for every sufficiently large threshold $D$, there exists a random independent set $I$ such that, uniformly over all vertices with $d_w(v)\geq D$, \[ \mathbb P(v\in I)\geq (1-o_r(1)) \left(\frac{\log d_w(v)}{r\,d_w(v)}\right)^{1/r}, \qquad D\to+\infty. \] It also yields \[ χ_f(G)\leq (1+o_r(1)) \left(\frac{rd}{\log d}\right)^{1/r} \] for $d$-degenerate linear Berge-$C_4$-free $(r+1)$-uniform hypergraphs.
  Both results are based on the ordered random pick process originated from Martinsson and Steiner. By choosing different selection functions and iteration, we prove the output of the random process gives the required random independent set.

</details>


### [157] [The Bounded-Linear Dichotomy Holds for all 4 Point Posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06527)
*Maria-Romina Ivan,Sean Jaffe*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了所有不超过4个点的有限偏序集都满足“诱导饱和数要么有界、要么线性”的二分法，并重点解决了长期未决的 $\widehat{\mathcal V}$ 偏序集（V形加孤立点）的线性诱导饱和数问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诱导饱和数的研究已显示出“有界或至少 $2\sqrt n$”的尖锐二分现象，且主流猜想认为饱和数要么有界、要么恰好线性。然而许多具体偏序集（如 butterfly、diamond 以及 $\widehat{\mathcal V}$）长期未能确定，阻碍了对该猜想的验证。

Method: 作者针对 $\widehat{\mathcal V}$ 偏序集（一个 $\mathcal V$ 形与一个孤立点组成）研究其诱导饱和家族，刻画了任意 $\widehat{\mathcal V}$-饱和族的结构：一个反链，其上方至多两条链，其下方是一个倒置森林；并据此构造证明其诱导饱和数为线性。方法结合极值集合论与偏序集结构分析。

Result: 证明了 $\widehat{\mathcal V}$ 的诱导饱和函数是线性的，并进而验证了所有不超过4个点的偏序集均满足有界或线性的二分法，包括此前独立抵抗分析的 butterfly 和 diamond 等偏序集。

Conclusion: 该二分法对至多4点的所有偏序集成立；证明中揭示的饱和族结构（反链+上方链+下方倒置森林）预计至少部分适用于更广泛的含孤立点偏序集类。

Abstract: Given a finite poset $\mathcal P$, its induced saturation number, $\text{sat}^*(n,\mathcal P)$ is the smallest size a family of subsets of $[n]$ can have such that it does not contain an induced copy of $\mathcal P$, but adding any other set to it creates one. The saturation numbers already show a sharp dichotomy -- Freschi, Piga, Sharifzadeh and Treglown showed that for any given poset its saturation number is either bounded or at least $2\sqrt n$. The dominant conjecture is that in fact, the saturation numbers are either bounded, or exactly linear.
  In this paper we show that this dichotomy is true for all posets on at most 4 points. Most of these posets, most notably the butterfly and the diamond, had individually long resisted analysis, and the missing piece which this paper tackles is the poset comprised of a $\mathcal V$ and an isolated point, denoted by $\widehat{\mathcal V}$, for which we show linear saturation.
  The architecture of the proof uncovers the structure of an arbitrary $\widehat{\mathcal V}$- saturated family, namely an antichain with at most two chains above it, and an upside-down forest below it. Moreover, we anticipate that, at least partially, this structure is relevant to the wider class of posets that contain an isolated point.

</details>


### [158] [Projective dimension of closed neighborhood hypergraphs via extended double covers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06556)
*Yusuf Civan,Anurag Singh*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究闭邻域超图的投射维数与图G的扩展二部双覆盖B_e(G)的Castelnuovo-Mumford正则性之间的关系，证明一般不等式prod-dim(N[G]) ≤ reg(B_e(G))，并在多类图上得到等式等于独立数α(G)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立代数不变量（投射维数、Castelnuovo-Mumford正则性）与图的组合结构之间的精确联系，特别是探究闭邻域超图的投射维数与扩展二部双覆盖正则性之间是否存在一般性关系。

Method: 通过覆盖、支配与匹配参数将代数不变量与图的底层组合结构相连接，并借助同调工具进行证明。

Result: 对所有图证明一般上界prod-dim(N[G]) ≤ reg(B_e(G))；并在König-Egerváry图（含所有二部图）、余图、余弦图、弦图与可比图等图类上证明等式prod-dim(N[G]) = reg(B_e(G)) = α(G)成立，其中α(G)为独立数。

Conclusion: 闭邻域超图的投射维数受扩展二部双覆盖正则性控制，且在若干重要图类上二者均等于图的独立数，揭示了代数不变量与组合参数之间的深刻对应。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a finite and simple graph without isolated vertices. We investigate the projective dimension of the closed neighborhood hypergraph $\mathcal{N}[G]$ and its relationship with the Castelnuovo-Mumford regularity of the extended bipartite double cover $\mathfrak{B}_e(G)$ of $G$. We establish the general upper bound $\operatorname{prod-dim} (\mathcal{N}[G]) \leq \operatorname{reg}(\mathfrak{B}_e(G))$ for all graphs. Furthermore, we prove that the exact equalities $\operatorname{prod-dim} (\mathcal{N}[G]) = \operatorname{reg}(\mathfrak{B}_e(G)) =α(G)$ hold when $G$ belongs to several prominent graph classes, including König-Egerváry (contains all bipartite graphs), cographs, co-chordal, chordal and comparability graphs, where $α(G)$ denotes the independence number. Our method of proofs relies on connecting algebraic invariants to the underlying combinatorial structure of graphs through covering, domination and matching parameters, together with the use of homology tools.

</details>


### [159] [On how many Fibonacci words is the Plancherel measure concentrated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06585)
*Vsevolod Evtushevsky*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究Young--Fibonacci图上Plancherel测度对应分布μ_P的熵与几乎完全集中所需最少字数的渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Young--Fibonacci图上Plancherel型测度在n增长时的集中性，刻画其信息论特性。

Method: 在数字和为n的{1,2}字集合𝕐F_n上定义概率分布μ_P，分析其熵与最小集中字数的渐近。

Result: 给出了μ_P熵及几乎完全集中所需最少字数的渐近估计。

Conclusion: Plancherel测度在Young--Fibonacci路径上的分布随n增大呈现明确的渐近集中行为。

Abstract: On the set $\mathbb{YF}_n$ of words in $1$ and $2$ with digit sum $n$, we consider the probability distribution $μ_P$, corresponding to the Plancherel measure on paths in the Young--Fibonacci graph. We study the asymptotics as $n$ grows of the entropy of the distribution $μ_P$ and of the minimum number of words on which $μ_P$ is almost entirely concentrated.

</details>


### [160] [A sharp Merino--Welsh-type inequality for matroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06589)
*Jungang Chen,Jiaxin Xie*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明Merino–Welsh猜想相关问题中常数$c_*=x_0\approx2.22668$，即最小的$c$使得所有无环无余环有限拟阵满足$T_M(c,0)T_M(0,c)\ge T_M(1,1)^2$，从而证实Csikvári的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Merino–Welsh猜想促使研究者考察Tutte多项式在对数凹性意义下的最优常数。此前Beke等给出下界$x_0$（多项式$x^3-9(x-1)$的最大实根），Csikvári将上界改进到2.35并猜测等号在$x_0$处成立，但尚缺严格证明。

Method: 采用直接归纳法，对无环无余环有限拟阵进行归纳论证，证明不等式在$c=x_0$时对所有此类拟阵成立。

Result: 证明了Csikvári的猜想，即不等式在$c=x_0$处对所有无环无余环有限拟阵成立。

Conclusion: 由此确定$c_*=x_0\approx2.22668$，其中$x_0$为$x^3-9(x-1)$的最大实根。

Abstract: Motivated by the Merino--Welsh conjecture, we consider the smallest $c\ge0$, denoted by $c_*$, for which the inequality $T_M(c,0)T_M(0,c)\ge T_M(1,1)^2$ holds for every loopless and coloopless finite matroid $M$. The counterexamples constructed by Beke, Csáji, Csikvári, and Pituk [\emph{Adv. Math.} \textbf{446} (2024), 109674] give the lower bound $x_0$, where $x_0\approx2.22668$ is the largest real root of the polynomial $x^3-9(x-1)$. Later, Csikvári [\emph{European J. Combin.} \textbf{137} (2026), 104402] improved the known upper bound for this constant to $2.35$ and then conjectured that the above inequality holds at $c=x_0$. We give a direct inductive proof of this conjecture, thereby showing that $c_*=x_0$.

</details>


### [161] [Enumerating Hamiltonian Paths and Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06644)
*Harry Hylock,Karl Michael Schmidt,Matthew C. Lettington*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意完全多部图中（有向）哈密顿路径与哈密顿圈数目的计数公式，方法基于将带颜色顶点的路径图与合法着色的路径图相联系的坍缩技巧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全多部图中的哈密顿路径与哈密顿圈计数问题具有组合数学意义，但此前缺乏适用于任意完全多部图的一般性计数公式，作者希望给出统一、显式的计数结果。

Method: 采用"坍缩"(collapsing)技术：建立一般带颜色顶点路径图与合法着色路径图之间的对应关系，将问题化归为合法着色情形，进而推导计数公式。

Result: 给出了任意完全多部图中有向哈密顿路径与有向哈密顿圈的计数公式。

Conclusion: 通过坍缩技巧可将一般完全多部图的哈密顿路径/圈计数问题归约到合法着色路径图，从而得到显式计数公式，方法具有推广性与统一性。

Abstract: We prove counting formulae for the number of directed Hamiltonian paths and directed Hamiltonian cycles in any complete multipartite graph. The method is based on a collapsing technique relating general path graphs with coloured vertices to legally coloured path graphs.

</details>


### [162] [A note on the Kővári--Sós--Turán theorem for stable hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06684)
*Aris Papadopoulos*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了对分划逐点k-稳定r-一致超图的Kővári-Sós-Turán定理强化版本，其指数η独立于t。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Kővári-Sós-Turán定理给出K_{t,...,t}-free r-超图的边数上界，但指数通常依赖于t。作者希望在稳定性（稳定性理论）假设下改进该定理，得到指数与t无关的更强上界，从而联系模型论稳定性与极值组合学。

Method: 证明沿用了Chernikov与Starchenko证明稳定超图Erdős-Hajnal类定理时所采用的伪有限（pseudofinite）框架，并结合分划逐点k-稳定r-超图的结构性质进行分析。

Result: 对任意k,r≥2，存在η=η(r,k)>0，使得任意分划逐点k-稳定r-一致K^{(r)}_{t,...,t}-free超图H=(V;E)，|V|=n时，|E|=O_{r,k,t}(n^{r-η})，其中η不依赖于t。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了稳定超图中Kővári-Sós-Turán型定理的强化形式：在分划逐点k-稳定性假设下，避免完全r-部t-团可导出指数与t无关的多项式上界，深化了模型论稳定性与极值超图理论的联系。

Abstract: In this note, we prove a stronger version of the Kővári-Sós-Turán theorem for partition-wise $k$-stable $r$-hypergraphs. More precisely, we show that for every $k,r\in\mathbb{N}_{\geq 2}$ there is $η=η(r,k)>0$ such that if $H=(V;E)$ is a partition-wise $k$-stable $r$-uniform $K^{(r)}_{t,\ldots,t}$-free hypergraph with $|V|=n$, then $|E| = O_{r,k,t}(n^{r-η})$. Crucially, $η$ is independent of $t$. The proof follows the pseudofinite regime used by Chernikov and Starchenko to prove an analogous version of Erdős-Hajnal for stable hypergraphs.

</details>


### [163] [Isomorphism Criterion of Monomial Digraphs over Prime Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06735)
*Alexander M. Kodess,Felix Lazebnik,Mikhail Muzychuk*

Main category: math.CO
