TL;DR: 该文研究有限域上由方程 x2+y2=x1^m y1^n 定义的有向图 D(q;m,n) 的同构分类猜想，在素域上证明该猜想成立，并在扩域上构造出无穷多反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作提出了关于有向图 D(q;m,n) 同构判定的猜想（同构当且仅当存在与 q-1 互素的 k 使 m2≡km1 且 n2≡kn1 mod q-1），需要验证该猜想的适用范围并考察其在扩域上是否依然成立。

Method: 对任意有限域 F_q 及正整数 m,n，定义顶点集为 F_q×F_q 的有向图 D(q;m,n)，弧 (x1,x2)→(y1,y2) 当且仅当 x2+y2=x1^m y1^n；通过分析该有向图的结构与自同构来判定同构等价条件。

Result: 在素域上证明了原猜想成立；同时在扩域上构造出一个无穷反例族，表明猜想在扩域情形下不成立。

Conclusion: D(q;m,n) 的同构分类猜想在素域上成立，但在扩域上失效；扩域情形需要新的、更精细的同构判据。

Abstract: For any Galois field $\mathbb{F}_q$ with $q$ elements and any positive integers $m$ and $n$, the directed graph $D(q;m,n)$ has vertex set $\mathbb{F}_q\times\mathbb{F}_q$, and there is an arc from vertex $(x_1,x_2)$ to vertex $(y_1,y_2)$ if and only if $x_2+y_2=x_1^my_1^n$. It was conjectured in earlier work that two digraphs $D(q;m_1,n_1)$ and $D(q;m_2,n_2)$ are isomorphic if and only if there exists an integer $k$ relatively prime to $q-1$ such that $m_2\equiv km_1$ and $n_2 \equiv kn_1$, where both congruences are modulo $q-1$. We prove this conjecture over prime fields and construct an infinite family of counterexamples over extension fields.

</details>


### [164] [Symmetric Grassmann Formulas: Monotone Dimension-Defect Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06739)
*Masoud Gharahi,Diego Ponterio*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究任意域上有限多个子空间的和空间维数亏损，给出对称非递归的Grassmann型维数公式，并用Shapley值、子模函数、离散曲率及熵等方法刻画该亏损结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限多个子空间和空间的维数计算通常依赖递归公式，缺乏对称、非递归且具组合解释的表达；同时维数亏损与Shapley值、子模性、熵恒等式等结构存在联系，需要统一刻画。

Method: 推导关于总和维数的对称非递归Grassmann型公式，用某一子空间与其余子空间部分和的交来表达总维数损失，系数由参与子空间数量确定；给出可表示多拟阵的Shapley值解释；按参与子空间数量对修正项分组得到非负维数亏损剖面；分析其单调性、相邻差与平均秩剖面离散曲率的关系；对Kinser松弛作几何分解，并在置换与收缩下取平均；给出加权与对偶公式及熵类比。

Result: 提出对称非递归的Grassmann型维数公式，将总维数损失表示为区分子空间与部分和交的贡献；证明公式具有Shapley值解释；证明亏损剖面非负且单调，其相邻差等于平均秩剖面的离散曲率，并给出双侧亏损界的精确余项；证明某侧等号成立当且仅当各子空间在公共交商空间中的像构成内直和；给出Kinser松弛的非负商维数精确分解及等号刻画；通过置换与收缩平均可恢复除最后一个外的所有亏损曲率，个体有序松弛中仍保留可表示性约束；给出加权、对偶公式，并用互信息与条件互信息的平均表达亏损层级与曲率。

Conclusion: 该工作为多个子空间维数亏损提供了统一的对称代数、子模与信息论框架，刻画了亏损剖面、离散曲率、Kinser松弛及等号条件，并揭示可表示性约束与熵类比。

Abstract: We study the dimension defect of finitely many subspaces over an arbitrary field. We derive a symmetric, nonrecursive Grassmann-type formula for the dimension of their sum. The formula expresses the total dimension loss through intersections of a distinguished subspace with partial sums of the remaining subspaces, with coefficients determined by the number of subspaces involved. We also show that the formula admits a Shapley-value interpretation for the associated representable polymatroid. Grouping the correction terms by the number of participating subspaces yields a nonnegative dimension-defect profile. We prove that this profile is monotone and identify its successive gaps with the discrete curvatures of the average-rank profile. These gaps give exact remainders in two-sided defect bounds. Equality in either bound holds precisely when the images of the subspaces in the quotient by their common intersection form an internal direct sum. We also give an exact geometric decomposition of the Kinser slack into nonnegative quotient dimensions and characterize equality. Averaging these slacks over permutations and contractions recovers every defect curvature except the final one; additional representability constraints remain in the individual ordered slacks. Weighted and dual formulas accompany the expansion, and entropy analogues express the defect levels and curvatures as averages of mutual and conditional mutual information, respectively.

</details>


<div id='math.CA'></div>

# math.CA [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [A note on the asymmetric discretised ring theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04064)
*Willliam O'Regan*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明离散环定理的asymmetric（非对称）版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散环定理是调和分析与组合几何中的经典结果，其非对称推广可覆盖更一般的集合尺度关系，具有理论拓展价值。

Method: 采用非对称形式的离散环定理证明方法，针对两个尺度不同的集合建立环状关联估计。

Result: 给出了非对称版本离散环定理的完整证明。

Conclusion: 该结果将离散环定理推广至非对称情形。

Abstract: We prove an asymmetric version of the discretised ring theorem.

</details>


### [166] [Projection theorems for macroscopic intermediate dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04395)
*Jinjun Li,Lifeng Xi*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该文引入无界集合的宏观中间维度（上、下），并证明其对几乎所有m维线性子空间的投影等于相应的内在容量剖面；该维度在参数0处退化为宏观Hausdorff维度，具有拟等距不变性与正参数连续性，并用于数字Moran集、Lipschitz图与算术和集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无界子集的投影与容量关系缺乏统一的刻画工具；已有离散中间维度等概念未覆盖宏观尺度与投影行为，需要一个能融合各二进环覆盖贡献、并在投影下保持稳定的宏观维度理论。

Method: 通过综合给定尺度之外所有二进环的覆盖贡献，定义上、下宏观中间维度；证明中结合覆盖—容量比较与指数间隙、投影覆盖的分数矩估计，以及源壳的容量加权保留。

Result: 对每个无界Borel集E⊂R^d与m∈{1,…,d-1}，几乎所有m维线性子空间V都满足P_VE的宏观中间维度等于对应内在容量剖面，且同一满测度集对所有参数θ∈[0,1]与两个维度同时有效；上宏观中间维度与文献[LXZ]的上离散中间维度一致，下者可能不同；并计算了有界基离散数字Moran集（含自相似情形）的投影维度。

Conclusion: 宏观中间维度在θ=0时还原宏观Hausdorff维度，具有拟等距不变性并在正参数处连续；该框架统一了宏观与离散中间维度，并为Lipschitz图、算术和集等问题提供投影维度应用。

Abstract: We introduce lower and upper macroscopic intermediate dimensions of unbounded subsets of Euclidean space by combining covering contributions from all dyadic annuli beyond a given scale. For every unbounded Borel set $E\subset\R^d$ and $m\in \{1,\ldots,d-1\}$, we prove that the macroscopic intermediate dimensions of $P_VE$ equal the corresponding intrinsic capacity profiles for almost every $m$-dimensional linear subspaces $V\subset \R^d$. One full-measure set works for both dimensions and every parameter $θ\in[0,1]$. The proof combines a covering--capacity comparison with an exponent gap, fractional-moment estimates for projected coverings, and capacity-weighted retention of source shells. The dimensions recover macroscopic Hausdorff dimension at parameter zero, are invariant under quasi-isometries, and are continuous at positive parameters. The upper macroscopic intermediate dimension agrees with the upper discrete intermediate dimension introduced in \cite{LXZ}, whereas the lower one can differ. We compute the projected dimensions of bounded-base discrete digit Moran sets, including the self-similar case obtained when the bases and digit sets are constant, and give applications to Lipschitz graphs and arithmetic sumsets.

</details>


### [167] [Multiple solutions of singular periodic problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04501)
*Milan Tvrdý*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 研究奇异周期问题 u''+ku=g(u)+e(t) 的两解存在性，并给出比原证明更清晰的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原论文关于奇异周期问题至少存在两个解的结果，其证明不够透明，作者希望重新审视并给出更简洁清晰的证明。

Method: 考虑周期边值问题 u''+ku=g(u)+e(t)，u(0)=u(2π)，u'(0)=u'(2π)，其中 g 在 x=0 处可能存在奇异性；通过重新组织证明思路，给出比原证明更透明的新证明。

Result: 证明了该奇异周期问题至少存在两个解（沿用此前结果），并提供了更清晰的新证明。

Conclusion: 该工作重申了奇异周期问题至少存在两个解的结论，其价值主要在于给出更透明的证明，便于理解与推广。

Abstract: In this contribution we consider the singular periodic problem \[
  u''+k\,u=g(u)+e(t),\quad u(0)=u(2π),\,\,u'(0)=u'(2π), \] where $k\in\R,$ $e:\J\to\R$ is Lebesgue integrable and $g:\I\mapsto\R$ is continuous, but can have a singularity at $x=0.$ In particular, I wish to revisit our earlier result regarding the existence of at least two its solutions and present a proof that is somewhat more transparent than the original one.

</details>


### [168] [On the mapping properties of a radially power-weighted k-plane transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04503)
*Aniruddha Deshmukh,Ashisha Kumar*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究带径向幂权重的 $k$-平面变换的加权类比，刻画其在径向幂权 Lebesgue 空间上的存在性、径向函数上的映射性质（加权 $L^p$-改进有界性与端点 Lorentz 空间估计），以及作用于一般函数时的加权 $L^p$-$L^p$ 有界性和算子范数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 $k$-平面变换的加权理论尚不完整，特别是当 $k$-维测度带径向距离幂权重时，算子在加权 Lebesgue 空间上的存在性、径向对称情形下的改进估计以及一般函数上的范数刻画缺乏系统结果。

Method: 在 $k$-维测度上引入径向距离幂权重，构造 $k$-平面变换的加权类比；分别在带径向幂权的 Lebesgue 空间上分析算子存在性；针对径向函数证明加权 $L^p$-改进有界性与端点 Lorentz 空间估计；对一般函数建立加权 $L^p$-$L^p$ 有界性并计算算子范数。

Result: 得到了带径向幂权 $k$-平面变换在加权 Lebesgue 空间上的存在性条件；对径向函数建立了加权 $L^p$-改进有界性和端点 Lorentz 空间估计；对一般函数给出了加权 $L^p$-$L^p$ 有界性并求得其算子范数。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典 $k$-平面变换的加权理论推广到径向幂权情形，系统给出存在性、径向改进估计及一般 $L^p$ 范数结果，完善了加权 $k$-平面变换的映射性质框架。

Abstract: In this article, we study a weighted analogue of the $k$-plane transform, where the weight on the $k$-dimensional measure is given by a radial power of distance. Particularly, we first consider the existence of such operators on Lebesgue spaces imbibed with radial power weights. Then, we proceed to see the mapping properties of the said transform when it acts on radial functions. In this regard, we prove certain weighted $L^p$-improving boundedness and end-point Lorentz space estimates. Finally, we look at the weighted $L^p$-$L^p$ boundedness of the operator acting on general functions, and evaluate its operator norm.

</details>


### [169] [On pointwise convergence of singular integrals of Christ-Journé type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04673)
*Ankit Bhojak,Sangita Kundu,Saurabh Shrivastava*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文建立了 Christ-Journé 型奇异积分的几乎处处收敛性，通过证明相应极大算子的弱 (1,1) 估计，解决了 Seeger (2014) 提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立 Christ-Journé 型奇异积分的点态几乎处处收敛性，并解决 Seeger 在 2014 年提出的公开问题。

Method: 针对由 Calderón–Zygmund 核 K 与 a∈L∞ 定义的极大算子 T*，证明其弱 (1,1) 估计。

Result: 证明了 Christ-Journé 型奇异积分对应极大算子的弱 (1,1) 估计，从而得到其几乎处处收敛性。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了 Seeger (Rev. Mat. Iberoam., 2014) 提出的问题，推进了 Christ-Journé 型奇异积分理论。

Abstract: The primary objective of this paper is to establish the pointwise almost everywhere convergence of the singular integrals of Christ-Journé type. To this end, we prove the weak type $(1,1)$ estimate for the corresponding maximal operator defined as
  \[
  T^*[a] f(x):= \sup_{r>0}\left|\int_{|x-y| > r} K(x-y) \int_0^1 a(tx + (1-t)y)\, dt f(y)\, dy\right|,
  \]
  where $K$ is a Calderón--Zygmund kernel and $a\in L^\infty$. This result resolves a problem posed by Seeger (Rev. Mat. Iberoam., 2014).

</details>


### [170] [Lower bounds for parameter-dependent oscillatory integrals and Fourier frames on hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04799)
*Shilei Fan,Bochen Liu*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该论文证明了有限型凸超曲面上曲面测度Fourier变换的下界，在每点至多一个主曲率消失时与经典上界匹配，并由此证明此类曲面测度不存在Fourier框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始动机是研究曲面测度的Fourier框架。此前Iosevich、Lai等人仅证明非零高斯曲率曲面的测度不存在Fourier框架，作者希望弱化"非零曲率"条件，刻画更一般的有限型曲面。

Method: 核心是参数依赖的振荡积分在R上、相位为凸函数时的一致下界估计。通过法向量方向与频率平行的局部化分析，结合有限型假设与至多一个主曲率消失的条件，得到与Bruna-Nagel-Wainger经典上界相匹配的曲面测度Fourier变换下界。

Result: 证明了在有限型凸超曲面、每点至多一个主曲率消失时，|ψdσ^(ξ)| 的均匀下界与Bruna-Nagel-Wainger上界一致；给出两个主曲率消失的凸例子，其Fourier变换在法向频率上超多项式衰减；并证明此类曲面测度（包括球面部分区域）不存在Fourier框架。

Conclusion: 在有限型且至多一个主曲率消失的凸超曲面上，曲面测度不容许任何Fourier框架，弱化了此前"非零曲率"的条件；对中心对称非零高斯曲率曲面及球面带上区域，也得到更多无Fourier框架的域，推广了半球面的相关结果。

Abstract: We prove lower bounds for Fourier transforms of surface measures $$|\widehat{ψ\,dσ}(ξ)| \gtrsim_{ψ, K, σ, R_0} σ(B(p, |ξ|^{-1})), \text{ given }p\in K\Subset\{ψ>0\} ,\ \vec{n}_{p}\parallel ξ,\ |ξ|\ge R_0,$$ on convex hypersurfaces of finite type with at most one vanishing principal curvature at each point. These bounds are uniform in the choice of $p\in K\Subset \{ψ>0\}$ and match the classical upper bound of Bruna-Nagel-Wainger. The key new analytic ingredient is a uniform lower bound for parameter-dependent oscillatory integrals on $\mathbb{R}$ with convex phases. We also give convex examples of finite type with two vanishing principal curvatures for which $$\inf_{p\in K}|\widehat{ψ\,dσ}(R \vec{n}_p)|\lesssim_N R^{-N},\ \text{ for arbitrary fixed } \{0\}\Subset K\Subset\{ψ>0\}.$$ Though the estimate above has its own interest, our original motivation is to study Fourier frames for surface measures. In particular, we show that, if $Γ$ is a closed convex smooth surface in $\mathbb{R}^d, d\geq 2$, of finite type and at most one principal curvature vanishes at each point, then the surface measure on $Γ$ does not admit any Fourier frame. This weakens the non-vanishing curvature condition in a previous result of Iosevich, Lai, the second author, and Wyman. On centrally symmetric surfaces of non-vanishing Gaussian curvature, we obtain more domains without Fourier frames. For example, we show that $\{x\in S^{d-1}:0<x_d<δ\}, d\geq 3$, does not admit any Fourier frame for all $δ>0$, which generalizes the result of Chen and the second author on the hemisphere.

</details>


### [171] [Sparse domination and the bi-parameter Hilbert transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04812)
*Bingyang Hu*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究双参数Hilbert变换能否被轴平行矩形上的(1,1)型稀疏形式控制，证明即使对紧支于(0,1)^2的光滑测试函数该控制也失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏控制（sparse domination）是调和分析中估计奇异积分算子的强有力工具，但多参数情形下的稀疏控制理论尚不完善。本文旨在考察双参数Hilbert变换是否可被(1,1)型稀疏形式控制，以厘清多参数稀疏理论的适用边界。

Method: 证明结合了两个关键要素：一是基于ℤ[√2]算术显式构造两个有限格（lattices）；二是采用随机化（randomization）论证。两者结合用于构造反例。

Result: 证明该稀疏控制失败，即使测试函数取紧支于(0,1)^2的光滑函数时也成立，表明双参数Hilbert变换无法被轴平行矩形上的(1,1)型稀疏形式控制。

Conclusion: 双参数Hilbert变换不存在(1,1)型稀疏控制，说明单参数稀疏控制理论不能直接推广到多参数情形，需寻求其他估计途径。

Abstract: We study domination of the bi-parameter Hilbert transform by $(1,1)$-type sparse forms over axis-parallel rectangles, and show that such domination fails even for smooth test functions compactly supported in $(0,1)^2$. Our proof combines an explicit construction of two finite lattices based on the arithmetic of $\mathbb Z[\sqrt2]$ with a randomization argument.

</details>


### [172] [The strong $\ell^r$ spherical maximal function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04968)
*Jin Bong Lee,Sanghyuk Lee,Jeongtae Oh*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 研究 $\ell^r$ 球面平均的强极大算子，在独立坐标伸缩群下证明其在 $L^p$ 上有界的充要条件为 $p>\max\{(d+1)/(d-1),\,r/(d-1)\}$，并给出局部极大算子的 $L^p\to L^q$ 最佳（端点除外）估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euclidean 球面平均的强极大算子（沿各坐标方向的伸缩）是多参数调和分析中的自然对象，其 $L^p$ 有界性阈值与坐标伸缩群的几何结构密切相关。作者希望将球面平均情形推广到 $\ell^r$ 单位球面，并刻画阈值对 $r$ 的依赖，同时给出局部算子的 $L^p\to L^q$ 定量估计。

Method: 采用二进曲面的分解（dyadic surface decomposition）与频率局部化 $L^2$ 估计；通过迭代平稳相位法得到乘子的精确分解，再结合标量 Fourier 反演与 $TT^*$ 论证建立这些估计；在三维情形进一步与定量多参数局部光滑（multiparameter local smoothing）相结合。

Result: 强极大算子在 $L^p(\mathbb R^d)$ 上有界当且仅当 $p>\max\{(d+1)/(d-1),\,r/(d-1)\}$（$d\ge3,\,2\le r<\infty$）；对相应的局部极大算子得到 $L^p\to L^q$ 界，且在给定 $p$ 的若干范围内所得 $q$ 的取值范围在端点之外是最优的。

Conclusion: $\ell^r$ 球面平均强极大算子的 $L^p$ 有界性阈值由 $\max\{(d+1)/(d-1),\,r/(d-1)\}$ 完全刻画，表明多参数结构与球面几何共同决定临界指标；所用二进分解、频率局部化 $L^2$ 估计与多参数局部光滑的组合为处理此类问题提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Let $d\ge3$ and $2\le r<\infty$. We consider the strong maximal operator associated with Euclidean surface averages over the $\ell^r$ unit sphere and independent coordinate dilations. We prove that this operator is bounded on $L^p(\mathbb R^d)$ if and only if \[
  p>\max\left\{\frac{d+1}{d-1},\frac{r}{d-1}\right\}. \] We also obtain $L^p\to L^q$ bounds for the corresponding local maximal operator. For each fixed $p$ in certain ranges, the resulting range of $q$ is optimal up to endpoints. The proof uses a dyadic surface decomposition and frequency-localized $L^2$ estimates. We establish these estimates by combining exact multiplier factorization via iterated stationary phase with scalar Fourier inversion and $TT^*$ arguments. In dimension three, these estimates are combined with quantitative multiparameter local smoothing.

</details>


### [173] [Lebesgue measure of distance sets and $L^2$ ball inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05015)
*Shengwen Gan,Bochen Liu,Shukun Wu*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文给出Falconer距离猜想中$5/4$结果的一个新证明，并在packing维数$<2$时突破$5/4$阈值。方法基于$L^2$球膨胀，不使用decoupling不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guth-Iosevich-Ou-Wang证明了平面上Hausdorff维数$>5/4$的紧集存在点$x$使钉住距离集$\Delta_x(E)$具有正Lebesgue测度。作者希望给出替代证明，并在packing维数$<2$时改进该阈值。

Method: 基于$L^2$球膨胀（ball inflation）技术，不依赖decoupling不等式。

Result: 若紧集$E\subset\mathbb{R}^2$的Hausdorff维数$>5/4$，则存在$x\in E$使$\Delta_x(E)$具有正Lebesgue测度；当$E$的packing维数$<2$时可突破$5/4$壁垒。更一般地，给出了同时涉及Hausdorff与packing维数的充分条件，改进了$5/4$阈值并恢复了$\dim_H E=\dim_P E>1$的已有结果。

Conclusion: 该工作以不依赖decoupling的$L^2$球膨胀方法重新证明了$5/4$结果，并通过引入packing维数在特定情形下改进阈值。

Abstract: We give an alternative proof of the $5/4$ result of Guth-Iosevich-Ou-Wang on the Falconer distance conjecture: if a compact set $E$ in the plane has Hausdorff dimension $>5/4$, then there exists $x\in E$ such that the pinned distance set $$Δ_x(E):=\{|x-y|: y\in E\} $$ has positive Lebesgue measure. Moreover, we are able to beat the $5/4$ barrier if the packing dimension of $E$ is $<2$. More generally, we obtain sufficient conditions involving both the Hausdorff and packing dimensions of $E$. These conditions improve the $5/4$ threshold when the packing dimension of $E$ is $<2$, and recover the second author's result for $\dim_H E=\dim_P E>1$. The proof is based on $L^2$ ball inflation and does not use decoupling inequalities.

</details>


### [174] [Dunkl--BMO functions representable in terms of generalized balayage of Carleson measures and an application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05092)
*Qingdong Guo*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文用Dunkl--Poisson半群相关的Carleson测度的广义balayage刻画了紧支集Dunkl--BMO函数，并由此给出Dunkl框架下Hardy空间的一个主值积分新刻画，结论对Dunkl--热半群同样成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典欧氏调和分析中，BMO函数可由Carleson测度的balayage表示，Hardy空间也可借助此类表示刻画；作者希望将这一系刻画推广到带有反射群结构的Dunkl设定，处理Dunkl--Poisson与Dunkl--热半群的非对称、非局部性带来的困难。

Method: 对紧支集Dunkl--BMO函数$f$，证明分解$f=g+\widetilde{S}_{\mu,\mathcal{P}}$，其中$g$有界，$\widetilde{S}_{\mu,\mathcal{P}}$是$\mathbb{R}^N\times(0,\infty)$上Carleson测度关于Dunkl--Poisson半群的广义balayage；并利用该表示，借助与$\widetilde{S}_{\mu,\mathcal{P}}$相关的主值积分刻画Dunkl--Hardy空间；对Dunkl--热半群给出平行论证。

Result: 建立了紧支集Dunkl--BMO函数的balayage表示，得到Dunkl--Hardy空间的一个新的主值积分等价刻画，且相应结论对Dunkl--热半群同样成立。

Conclusion: Dunkl设定下BMO与Hardy空间可通过半群相关的Carleson测度balayage统一刻画，表明经典表示定理在该框架中具有良好推广性，并为后续原子分解、对偶与奇异积分研究提供工具。

Abstract: We characterize compactly supported functions $f$ in Dunkl--BMO spaces as those that can be written as \begin{equation*}
  f=g+\widetilde{S}_{μ,\mathcal{P}}, \end{equation*} the sum of some bounded function $g$ and the generalized balayage $\widetilde{S}_{μ,\mathcal{P}}$ of a Carleson measure on $\mathbb{R}^{N}\times(0,\infty)$ associated with the Dunkl--Poisson semigroup. As a direct application of this representation of Dunkl--BMO functions, we establish a new characterization of the Hardy space in the Dunkl setting via a principal value integral related to $\widetilde{S}_{μ,\mathcal{P}}$. Moreover, analogous conclusions hold for the Dunkl--heat semigroup as well.

</details>


### [175] [Null-additive sets and packing measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05283)
*Ond{ř}ej Zindulka*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 严格测度零定义为：可分度量空间中，任意gauge诱导的packing measure均为零。本文给出该概念的若干刻画，讨论其笛卡尔积性质，并证明在Polish局部紧群中严格测度零集具有null-additive性质，且在Cantor集、欧氏空间与环面上，严格测度零与null-additive两概念等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: packing measure依赖于gauge的选取，因此"对任意gauge其packing measure为零"这一更强条件（严格测度零）值得系统研究；同时需厘清它与null-additivity之间的关系，以及在典型空间中的等价性。

Method: 通过给出严格测度零的多种等价刻画来展开分析；考察笛卡尔积在该性质下的表现；在Polish局部紧群框架下论证严格测度零集的null-additivity；在Cantor集、欧氏空间及环面上证明两种概念的等价。

Result: 给出严格测度零的若干刻画；获得其笛卡尔积的相关结论；证明Polish局部紧群中每个严格测度零集都是null-additive的；在Cantor集、欧氏空间与环面上严格测度零与null-additive等价。

Conclusion: 严格测度零作为一个不依赖gauge选取的强零测度概念，具有良好结构性质，并与null-additivity在若干经典空间中等价，深化了对packing measure与null-additivity关系的理解。

Abstract: A separable metric space has strict measure zero if its packing measure induced by any gauge is zero. We provide a few characterizations of strict measure zero, look at cartesian products and prove that in every Polish locally compact group every set of strict measure zero is null-additive and that in the Cantor set, Euclidean spaces and tori the two notions are equivalent.

</details>


### [176] [Addition formula for two families of orthogonal polynomials in several variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05314)
*Yuan Xu*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文推导了两族多元正交多项式的加法公式，每族由单位球上的经典Gegenbauer多项式与单纯形上的Jacobi多项式或乘积Laguerre多项式的"缠绕乘积"构成，并显式表示为单变量经典正交多项式的多层积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加法公式是多元正交多项式再生核的闭式表达，是正交投影算子的核。已有分析往往需要直接处理多元结构，缺乏将多元Fourier正交展开问题化归为单变量问题的工具，因此有必要推导新的加法公式以简化分析。

Method: 针对两类多元正交多项式族——单位球上Gegenbauer多项式与单纯形上Jacobi多项式（或乘积Laguerre多项式）的缠绕乘积——推导其加法公式，并将每个加法公式显式表达为单变量经典正交多项式的多层积分。

Result: 获得了这两族正交多项式的加法公式，且均以单变量经典正交多项式的多层积分形式给出显式表达。

Conclusion: 这些加法公式提供了强有力的分析工具，特别地可将所论区域上Fourier正交展开的相当一部分分析化归为单变量分析。

Abstract: An addition formula is a closed-form formula for reproducing kernels of orthogonal polynomials of several variables; the latter are kernels of orthogonal projection operators. We derive addition formulas for two families of orthogonal polynomials, which consist of wrapped products of classical Gegenbauer polynomials on the unit ball with either classical Jacobi polynomials on the simplex or product Laguerre polynomials, each of which is given explicitly as a multilayer of integrals of a classical orthogonal polynomial of one variable. These formulas provide a powerful tool for analysis and allow us, in particular, to reduce a substantial portion of analysis on the Fourier orthogonal expansions on the domains to that of one variable.

</details>


### [177] [Weak reverse Hölder inequalities for doubling measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05320)
*Martí Prats*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该文在倍增Radon测度框架下证明了Bennewitz–Lewis定理的局部定量形式，并建立相应二进工具与Muckenhoupt型权类，统一推导出反Hölder不等式与局部Gehring引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Bennewitz–Lewis定理表明，满足某种绝对连续性条件的测度其密度具有反Hölder性质，但缺乏局部化、定量的版本；同时相关论证依赖的二进结构在一般倍增测度下需要重新建立。

Method: 设μ为$\mathbb R^d$上支撑为X的倍增Radon测度。发展二进工具：由邻居定义的三重立方体、基$\mathcal{D}\cup3\mathcal{D}$以及Whitney分解，并整理该基上的Muckenhoupt型权类。核心是一个统一的bootstrapping引理，分别对p=1与p>1陈述，由此同时推出主要结论。

Result: 若Radon测度ν在有界相对开集$V\subset X$上满足常数足够小的Bennewitz–Lewis条件，则$\nu|_V\ll\mu|_V$，且其密度在每个紧子集$K\subset V$上满足反Hölder不等式。同一机制还给出放大球的局部Gehring引理。

Conclusion: 该工作将Bennewitz–Lewis定理推广为倍增测度下的局部定量形式，并建立了一套可复用的二进分析框架；反Hölder不等式与局部Gehring引理由同一bootstrapping引理统一导出，显示两类结果的内在联系。

Abstract: Let $μ$ be a doubling Radon measure in $\mathbb R^d$ with support $X$. We prove a local and quantitative form of the Bennewitz--Lewis theorem in this setting: if a Radon measure $ν$ satisfies the Bennewitz--Lewis condition on a bounded relatively open set $V\subset X$ with a small enough constant, then $ν|_V\llμ|_V$ and its density satisfies a reverse Hölder inequality on every compact subset $K\subset V$. Along the way we develop the dyadic toolkit these arguments run on --- triple cubes defined through neighbours, the basis $\mathcal{D}\cup3\mathcal{D}$, and a Whitney decomposition --- and we collect the Muckenhoupt-type classes of weights over that basis. The same machinery yields a local Gehring lemma for enlarged balls: both theorems are deduced from a single bootstrapping lemma, stated once for $p=1$ and $p>1$.

</details>


### [178] [A Fraction Dimensional Kakeya Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05404)
*Jiahan Du*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文构造了一族不满足最坏压缩现象的曲线，但其并集的Hausdorff维数与上盒维数均为5/2，说明除集中在超曲面附近外还存在其他Kakeya压缩现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: arXiv:2607.27629 证明在非常一般条件下，R^3 中非平凡地包含于超曲面的通用弯曲 Kakeya 集族其 Hausdorff 维数严格大于 2。该文第 1.9 节提出是否存在其他 Kakeya 压缩现象的问题，本文旨在回答该问题。

Method: 利用大语言模型（ChatGPT 5.6 Sol 与 ChatGPT 6 Astra）给出了一族曲线的显式构造，该曲线族不满足 arXiv:2607.27629 中的最坏压缩现象。

Result: 所构造曲线族的并集其 Hausdorff 维数和上盒维数均为 5/2。

Conclusion: 该例子表明，除集中在超曲面附近的平凡例子外，确实存在其他 Kakeya 压缩现象，从而回答了 arXiv:2607.27629 第 1.9 节提出的问题。

Abstract: Recently, it was shown in arXiv:2607.27629 that under a very general condition, a generic family of curved Kakeya sets in $\mathbb{R}^3$ not trivially contained in a hypersurface has Hausdorff dimension strictly greater than 2. In this paper we leverage large language models to give an explicit construction of a family of curves failing the worst compression phenomenon in arXiv:2607.27629, but whose union has Hausdorff dimension and upper box dimension $5/2$. The example shows that there are Kakeya compression phenomena besides the trivial example of concentrating near a hypersurface. This answers a question raised in section 1.9 of arXiv:2607.27629. The models used are ChatGPT 5.6 Sol and ChatGPT 6 Astra.

</details>


### [179] [Improved dimension bounds for Falconer's distance problem in the plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05500)
*Alex Iosevich,Alexia Yavicoli*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究平面解析集 E（Hausdorff 维数 t=dim_H E>1）的距离集与钉住距离集的维数下界。作者保留 Kolmogorov 复杂度估计中两端点的信息，反转端点应用划分估计，得到 dim_H ΔE≥4/5+η_0 及在 1<t≤(11−√41)/4 时的有理下界 (t²−2t+5)/(2t²−7t+10)（右端点达 7/8）。钉住距离方面，对几乎处处 x 给出 dim_H Δ_xE≥q_*=0.7813…，并给出更强 t 依赖界、例外集估计，以及 Frostman 测度下的盒维数与 Hausdorff 维数结论。等号成立迫使信息剖面在共同尺度区间上有超量信息，转化为点—圆构型与丰富垂直平分线，与 Katz–Tardos、Ren–Wang 下界和 Fu–Gan–Ren 上界矛盾，由紧性得 η_0>0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面距离集问题（Falconer 距离问题）长期关注：若 dim_H E>1，则 dim_H ΔE 至少多少。已有工作给出 4/5 等下界，但既有 Kolmogorov 复杂度方法丢失了端点信息，无法捕捉更精细的 t 依赖界与钉住距离的一致下界，也不能说明最优下界扰动常数 η_0>0 的来源。本文旨在改进距离集与钉住距离集的维数下界，并通过刚性论证说明严格改进的存在性。

Method: 在 Stull 与 Fiedler–Stull 的 Kolmogorov 复杂度框架中保留两端点信息；反转端点应用划分估计以导出 4/5 与有理下界。若等号成立，则两个信息剖面在共同尺度区间上均有超量信息。将单一源测度编码到所有钉住量，把该约束转化为具有许多丰富垂直平分线的点—圆构型；用 Katz–Tardos 与 Ren–Wang 的下界与该构型上界（Fu–Gan–Ren）矛盾，借紧性得到 η_0>0。另用独立的有限尺度选择证明钉住盒维数下界。

Result: 对解析 E⊂R²，t=dim_H E>1，证得 dim_H ΔE≥4/5+η_0（η_0 为绝对非显式常数），且当 1<t≤(11−√41)/4 时 dim_H ΔE≥(t²−2t+5)/(2t²−7t+10)，右端点达 7/8。钉住距离：对例外集（Hausdorff 维数至多 1）之外的每个 x∈R²，dim_H Δ_xE≥q_*=0.7813…，并有依赖 t 的更强界与进一步例外集估计。若 E 紧且支撑 s-Frostman 测度 μ（s>1），则对 μ-几乎处处的 x，下盒维数 max{4/5+η_0, min{(3s+1)/5, 7/8}} 成立；在共同二进质量律（容许尺度振荡）下，同一界对钉住 Hausdorff 维数成立。

Conclusion: 本文利用带端点信息的 Kolmogorov 复杂度与刚性论证，改进了平面距离集与钉住距离集的维数下界，证明 4/5 可被严格超越（η_0>0），并在小 t 区间给出精确有理下界、在 Frostman 情形给出更强界。刚性与等号分析为该类问题提供了新的结构判据，其思路与 Héra–Shmerkin–Yavicoli 关于 Furstenberg 集的工作（借助 Bourgain 投影定理）相呼应。

Abstract: Let $E\subset\mathbb R^2$ be analytic with $t=\dim_{\mathrm H}E>1$. We prove $\dim_{\mathrm H}ΔE\ge 4/5+η_0$ for an absolute, non-explicit constant $η_0>0$, and \[ \dim_{\mathrm H}ΔE\ge \frac{t^2-2t+5}{2t^2-7t+10} \quad\text{for }1<t\le\frac{11-\sqrt{41}}{4}, \] which reaches $7/8$ at the right endpoint. For pinned distances, $\dim_{\mathrm H}Δ_xE\ge q_*=0.7813\ldots$ for every $x\in\mathbb R^2$ outside an exceptional set of Hausdorff dimension at most one, with stronger bounds depending on $t$ and further exceptional-set estimates. If $E$ is compact and supports an $s$-Frostman measure $μ$, $s>1$, then \[ \underline{\dim}_{\mathrm B}Δ_xE\ge \max\left\{\frac45+η_0,\min\left\{\frac{3s+1}{5},\frac78\right\}\right\} \] for $μ$-almost every $x$. The same bound holds for pinned Hausdorff dimension under a common dyadic mass law, allowing oscillation with scale.
  We retain information about both endpoints in the Kolmogorov complexity estimates of Stull and Fiedler--Stull. Applying the partition estimates with the endpoints reversed yields $4/5$ and the rational bound. Equality forces both information profiles to have excess information on a common interval of scales. Encoding one source measure for all pins converts this constraint into a configuration of points and circles with many rich perpendicular bisectors. Lower bounds of Katz--Tardos and Ren--Wang contradict the upper bound of Fu--Gan--Ren; compactness then gives $η_0>0$. This rigidity argument has a precedent in the work of Héra, Shmerkin and Yavicoli on Furstenberg sets, using Bourgain's projection theorem. A separate finite-scale selection proves the pinned lower box bound.

</details>


### [180] [Multilinear transference on product spaces and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05973)
*Árpád Bényi,Tadahiro Oh*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该论文建立了从欧氏空间到混合空间 $\mathbb R^{d_1}\times\mathbb T^{d_2}$ 的多线性傅里叶乘子算子的转移结果，并给出应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为将欧氏空间上已知的多线性算子有界性推广到乘积空间（欧氏空间与环面的混合），需要一套保持算子界的转移原理。

Method: 证明强型与弱型转移原理，使欧氏空间对应的多线性傅里叶乘子算子界在 $\mathbb R^{d_1}\times\mathbb T^{d_2}$ 上得以保持。

Result: 获得强型和弱型转移定理，保持欧氏空间算子界；并据此导出若干多线性算子及PDE中自然出现的乘子算子的有界性估计。

Conclusion: 转移原理为混合欧氏-环面设置下的多线性算子有界性提供了统一框架，并可用于分析PDE中的乘子算子。

Abstract: We establish transference results for multilinear Fourier multiplier operators from Euclidean spaces to product spaces of the form $\mathbb R^{d_1}\times\mathbb T^{d_2}$. We prove strong and weak-type transference principles that preserve the corresponding Euclidean operator bounds. As applications, we obtain bounds for certain multilinear operators, as well as for some multiplier operators arising naturally in PDEs.

</details>


### [181] [A uniqueness result for two-step indefinite $p$-Laplacian equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06068)
*Alberto Cagnetta*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Boscaggin–Feltrin–Zanolin 关于两步不定权方程正解唯一性的猜想，并将结论推广至一般 p-Laplace 情形，给出完整的正解存在唯一性分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 Boscaggin、Feltrin 与 Zanolin 提出的关于两步不定权（two-step indefinite weight）方程正解唯一性的猜想，本文旨在证明该猜想，并进一步将结论纳入更一般的非线性框架。同时，原有结果未覆盖对数势情形 γ=-1，且对一般 p-Laplace 方程缺乏完整的存在唯一性刻画。

Method: 考虑方程 (|u'|^{p-2}u')' + a(t)u^γ = 0，在 Neumann 或周期边界条件下，其中 a(t) 为两步不定权。对 γ∈R\{0,p-1} 进行系统分析，结合必要的均值条件 γ∫₀^T a(t)dt<0，刻画存在性与唯一性。对 γ=-1 的对数势情形作专门处理。末段通过一个数值例子检验单次变号但内部剖面非常数的权重下是否会出现多正解。

Result: 证明了上述猜想：当 a(t) 为两步不定权时，Neumann 与周期问题对任意 γ∈R\{-1,0,1} 至多有一个正解；并补全 γ=-1 的对数势情形。对一般 p>1，给出 γ∈R\{0,p-1} 的完整存在唯一性分类：均值条件 γ∫₀^T a(t)dt<0 在 γ≤-1 或 γ>p-1 时为充分条件；在中间区间 γ∈(-1,p-1)\{0} 还需额外的关于权重均值的尖锐条件。数值例子表明，权重虽只一次变号但内部剖面非常数时，Neumann 问题可存在多个正解。

Conclusion: 两步不定权（即在支撑区间内为常值且仅一次变号）是保证正解唯一性的关键；一旦内部剖面非常数，即使仅有单次变号也可能出现多重正解，凸显了“两步”假设的必要性。结果统一并推广了原猜想，并给出对数势情形下的完整刻画。

Abstract: Boscaggin, Feltrin and Zanolin conjectured that the Neumann and periodic problems associated with the equation
  \begin{equation*}
  u''+a(t)u^γ=0
  \end{equation*} with a two-step indefinite weight $a(t)$ have at most one positive solution for every $γ\in\mathbb{R}\setminus\left\{-1,0,1\right\}$. We prove this conjecture and complete the uniqueness picture by also including the logarithmic potential case $γ=-1$. More generally, for every $p>1$, we consider the equation
  \begin{equation*}
  \left(\left\lvert u'\right\rvert^{p-2}u'\right)'+a(t)u^γ=0
  \end{equation*} under Neumann or periodic boundary conditions and establish a complete existence and uniqueness classification for all $γ\in\mathbb{R}\setminus\left\{0,p-1\right\}$. The necessary mean-value condition $γ\int_{0}^{T}a(t)\,\mathrm{d} t<0$ is also sufficient for existence when $γ\leq-1$ or $γ>p-1$, while an additional sharp condition on the mean of the weight is required in the intermediate range $γ\in(-1,p-1)\setminus\{0\}$. Finally, a numerical example provides evidence of multiple positive solutions for the Neumann problem whose weight has the same single change of sign but a non-constant internal profile, highlighting the role of the two-step assumption.

</details>


### [182] [Refinements on the Restriction and Kakeya problems range in $\mathbb{R}^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06181)
*Tiago Moreira*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 在 $\mathbb{R}^4$ 中获得改进的 Fourier 限制性与 Kakeya 极大估计：通过证明两类 two-ends incidence 估计，将限制范围扩展到 $p>2+\frac{176}{221}$，Kakeya 极大估计扩展到维数 $3.06737$，并研究了其对 Kakeya 集 Hausdorff 维数下界与 Bochner–Riesz 猜想的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fourier 限制性估计与 Kakeya 极大估计是调和分析中的核心问题，二者与 Kakeya 集维数、Bochner–Riesz 猜想等著名猜想密切相关。在 $\mathbb{R}^4$ 中改进这些估计的适用范围，能够推进对相关猜想下界的理解。

Method: 通过证明两个 two-ends incidence 估计 $\mathbf{TE}\left(\frac{133}{44},\frac{177}{88},\frac34\right)$ 与 $\mathbf{TE}\left(\frac{386453}{125988},\frac{386453}{125988},\frac{358951}{377964}\right)$，并将这些 incidence 结果应用于 Fourier 限制性与 Kakeya 极大估计的推导。

Result: 限制范围扩展到 $p>2+\frac{176}{221}$，Kakeya 极大估计扩展到维数 $3.06737$；同时得到关于 $\mathbb{R}^4$ 中 Kakeya 集 Hausdorff 维数下界以及 Bochner–Riesz 猜想的推论。

Conclusion: 该工作通过 two-ends incidence 估计改进了 $\mathbb{R}^4$ 中 Fourier 限制性与 Kakeya 极大估计的范围，并为 Kakeya 集 Hausdorff 维数下界与 Bochner–Riesz 猜想提供了新的进展。

Abstract: We obtain improved Fourier restriction and Kakeya maximal estimates in $\mathbb R^4$. More precisely, by proving the two-ends incidence estimates $\mathbf{TE}\left(\frac{133}{44},\frac{177}{88},\frac34\right)$ and $\mathbf{TE}\left(\frac{386453}{125988},\frac{386453}{125988},\frac{358951}{377964}\right)$, we extend the restriction range to $p>2+ \frac{176}{221}$ and the Kakeya maximal estimate up to dimension $3.06737$. The implications to the known lower-bounds for the Hausdorff dimension of Kakeya sets in $\mathbb{R}^4$ and the Bochner--Riesz conjecture are also studied.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [The Riemann-Siegel remainder as a fractional summand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03728)
*Paul Stahura*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将 Siegel 1932 年 ζ(s) 的 Riemann-Siegel 分解中的耦合变量 (t, m) 通过变换 t=I(T) 归约为单实指标 T，把单个余项积分 R 精确拆分为 R₁ₚₛ 与 R₂ₚₛ，得 ζ=Σ₁+R₁ₚₛ+Σ₂+R₂ₚₛ；并证明每个余项等价于 Dirichlet 和上追加的一个分数部分和项，其系数 d̂₁、d̂₂ 为实数（临界线上恒正），且在 σ=1/2 时 d₁=d₂（已用 Lean 形式化验证）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Siegel 的 Riemann-Siegel 分解中，虚部 t 与求和指标 m 是耦合的一对变量，且余项 R 以单一积分形式出现，其结构与 Dirichlet 部分和的关系不够透明。作者希望引入单实指标 T 解耦该配对，并将余项拆成结构清晰的精确片段，以揭示余项实为 Dirichlet 和的一个附加分数项，从而刻画临界线上系数的对称性与余项在 T 的分数部分上的近周期行为。

Method: 1) 从 Siegel 1932 年给出的 ζ(s) Riemann-Siegel 分解出发，引入变量替换 t=I(T)，把"虚部 t / 求和指标 m"的耦合对替换为单一实指标 T；2) 将 Siegel 的单一余项积分 R 拆分为 R₁ₚₛ 与 R₂ₚₛ 两个精确片段，证明 R=R₁ₚₛ+R₂ₚₛ；3) 将每个余项解释为 Dirichlet 和的一个额外分数部分和项，得到 ζ(s)=Σ_{n=1}^m n^{-s}+d̂₁(m+1)^{-s}+χ(s)Σ_{n=1}^m n^{s-1}+d̂₂χ(s)(m+1)^{s-1}；4) 用 Lean 对 σ=1/2 时 d₁=d₂（等价 d̂₁=d̂₂）进行形式化验证；5) 通过实验数学（部分和螺旋可视化）发现该分解，并讨论 yin yang 曲线、零点计数函数与等长度腿轨迹椭圆等观察。

Result: 得到 ζ(s)=Σ_{n=1}^m n^{-s}+d̂₁(m+1)^{-s}+χ(s)Σ_{n=1}^m n^{s-1}+d̂₂χ(s)(m+1)^{s-1} 的精确分解，其中 d̂₁、d̂₂ 为实数且临界线上恒正，代表两个被使用的求和项的分数。作为推论，σ=1/2 时 d₁=d₂（等价 d̂₁=d̂₂），该事实已在 Lean 中形式化验证。经此重标度后，余项在 T 中近似以 1 为周期，并在 T 的分数部分上收敛到固定波形。此外还给出 yin yang 曲线、零点计数函数以及等长度腿轨迹椭圆等多项观察。

Conclusion: 通过变量替换 t=I(T) 与余项的精确二拆分，Siegel 的 Riemann-Siegel 余项被重新表述为 Dirichlet 和的分数附加项，揭示了临界线上系数的对称性 d̂₁=d̂₂ 与余项在 T 分数部分上的近周期收敛行为；该分解源于部分和螺旋可视化的实验数学发现，并附带若干相关几何与计数观察。

Abstract: Starting from the Riemann-Siegel decomposition of $ζ(s)$ given in Siegel's 1932 paper, we introduce a change of variable, $t=I(T)$, that replaces Siegel's coupled pair "imaginary part $t$ / summation index $m$" with one real index $T$. We then split Siegel's single remainder integral $R$ into two exact pieces, $R_{1ps}$ and $R_{2ps}$, and prove that $R=R_{1ps}+R_{2ps}$, so that $ζ=Σ_1+R_{1ps}+Σ_2+R_{2ps}$. Our central observation is that each of these remainders is nothing more than one additional, fractional partial summand appended to its Dirichlet sum: $ζ(s)=\sum_{n=1}^{m}n^{-s}+\hat{d}_1(m+1)^{-s}+χ(s)\sum_{n=1}^{m}n^{s-1}+\hat{d}_2χ(s)(m+1)^{s-1}$, with $\hat{d}_1,\hat{d}_2$ real numbers (always positive on the critical line), the fractions of those two summands that are used. As a corollary, when $σ=\frac{1}{2}$ one has $d_1=d_2$ (equivalently $\hat{d}_1=\hat{d}_2$); this fact is formally verified in Lean. Also, with this rescaling the remainder terms are nearly periodic in $T$ with period one, converging to a fixed waveform in the fractional part of $T$. This decomposition of Siegel's $R$ was discovered through experimental mathematics using a spiral visualization of the partial sums, described later in the paper. We also discuss a number of other observations, including what we call the yin yang curves, the zero counting function, and ovals of equal length leg loci.

</details>


### [184] [Gaps, isolated points, and the origin of the ray in the one-dimensional Dirichlet spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03787)
*Maksim Lavrov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究一维Dirichlet谱，给出其射线上原点μ的改进上界与下界，证明Ivanov引入的点Z_k为孤立点，并刻画其邻近间隙的渐近行为及相关无理数的等价类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维Dirichlet谱中射线起点μ及其孤立点的位置长期缺乏精确估计，需要改进μ的上下界并厘清Z_k点的结构性质。

Method: 通过连分数词刻画Dirichlet谱，结合解析估计得到μ的上界；对下界进行精确的计算机辅助有限搜索，枚举容许的连分数词；并分析Z_k附近间隙的渐近展开与等价类判别。

Result: 证明μ ≤ (448875+1611√67773)/46982 = 18.4809…；构造显式间隙(L,R)得到μ ≥ R = 5.9596…；证明所有Z_k（k≥2）均为Dirichlet谱的孤立点；确定Z_k周围相邻间隙的渐近行为并描述Dirichlet值为Z_k的无理数等价类。

Conclusion: 改进了Dirichlet谱射线起点μ的上下界，确立了Z_k的孤立性并给出其邻域结构，深化了对一维Dirichlet谱精细结构的理解。

Abstract: We study the one-dimensional Dirichlet spectrum and obtain new results concerning the origin $μ$ of the ray contained in the spectrum and its isolated points. First, we prove the improved upper bound $μ\leq (448875+1611\sqrt{67773})/46982 = 18.4809\ldots$. We also exhibit an explicit gap $(L,R)$ in the Dirichlet spectrum, which yields the improved lower bound $μ\geq R = 5.9596\ldots$. The proof of the lower bound includes an exact computer-assisted finite search over admissible continued-fraction words. We further prove that all points $Z_k$, $k\geq 2$, introduced by Ivanov are isolated points of the Dirichlet spectrum. Finally, we determine the asymptotic behavior of the neighboring gaps around $Z_k$ and describe the equivalence classes of all irrational numbers whose Dirichlet value is $Z_k$.

</details>


### [185] [Exponential generating functions for Apostol-Dedekind and related sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03868)
*Yilmaz Simsek*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了Apostol-Dedekind和的指数生成函数，定义了高阶Apostol-Dedekind和及一类有限和，并借助生成函数推导出若干新公式与关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apostol-Dedekind和的相关新公式与关系较难直接发现，需要一种系统化的工具来统一处理并推广到高阶情形。

Method: 构造Apostol-Dedekind和的指数生成函数；定义高阶Apostol-Dedekind和及其他有限和族，并为其建立生成函数，涉及的函数包括Bernoulli多项式与数、Euler多项式与数、Array多项式、Stirling数、Carlitz多项式及其偏微分方程、Hardy和等。

Result: 生成函数有效促进了涉及Apostol-Dedekind和的新公式与关系的发现；利用所构造的生成函数推导出许多高阶Apostol-Dedekind和的新公式。

Conclusion: 生成函数方法是研究Apostol-Dedekind和及其高阶推广的有效统一框架，可系统产生新的恒等式与关系。

Abstract: One of the primary objectives of this paper is to construct generating functions for Apostol-Dedekind sums. These exponential generating functions greatly facilitate the discovery of new formulas and relations involving these sums. Other primary objectives of this paper are to define Apostol-Dedekind sums of higher-order and other certain family of finite sums, and to construct their generating functions. These generating functions include the Bernoulli polynomials and numbers, the Euler polynomials and numbers, the Array polynomials, the Stirling numbers, the Carlitz polynomials with their partial derivative equations, and the Hardy sums etc. Moreover, by using these functions, many novel formulas for the Apostol-Dedekind sums of higher-order are derived.

</details>


### [186] [Nonvanishing of special $L$-values of cusp forms on $\mathrm{GL}(2)$ with totally split prime power twists: without the class number hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03976)
*Ruichen Xu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于数域 F 上 GL(2) 的归一化尖点 Hecke 特征形式 f，其 L 函数的中心临界点为 k/2；在 f 的 level 理想与某个完全分裂的奇素数 p 的素理想 𝔭 互素时，对充分分歧的 p 幂阶、𝔭 幂导体 Hecke 特征 φ，L(f⊗φ, k/2) 不消失，从而在取消“p 不整除类数”假设的情况下改进了 Jaesung Kwon 与 Hae-Sang Sun 的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关注自守 L 函数在中心临界点处的非消失问题。已有 Jaesung Kwon 与 Hae-Sang Sun 的结果需要额外假设 p 不整除 F 的类数；本文旨在减弱/取消该限制，得到更一般数域情形下的非消失定理。

Method: 研究对象为数域 F 上 GL(2) 的归一化尖点 Hecke 特征形式 f，中心临界点为 k/2；取完全分裂的奇素数 p 的素理想 𝔭，且与 f 的 level 理想互素。对 p 幂阶、𝔭 幂导体的充分分歧 Hecke 特征 φ，考察扭曲 L 值 L(f⊗φ, k/2) 的非消失。摘要未给出具体技术路线，仅说明通过上述设定下的论证实现非消失结论。

Result: 证明 L(f⊗φ, k/2) 对充分分歧的 p 幂阶、𝔭 幂导体 Hecke 特征 φ 不为零。该结果去除了此前 Kwon–Sun 工作中对 p 不整除 F 类数的额外条件。

Conclusion: 在更一般的数域与素数条件下确立了 GL(2) 自守 L 函数中心临界点的非消失性，改进了 Jaesung Kwon 与 Hae-Sang Sun 的相关结果。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a number field and $f$ a normalized cuspidal Hecke eigenform on $\mathrm{GL}(2)$ over $F$, with $k/2$ as the central critical point of its $L$-function $L(f,s)$. Let $\mathfrak{p}$ be a totally split prime ideal of $F$ lying above an odd prime $p$ and coprime to the level ideal of $f$. In this article, we show that $L(f\otimesφ, k/2)$ does not vanish for sufficiently ramified Hecke characters of $F$ of $p$-power order and $\mathfrak{p}$-power conductor. Our result improves previous works of Jaesung Kwon and Hae-Sang Sun, in which the additional hypothesis that $p$ does not divide the class number of $F$ is required.

</details>


### [187] [On Mahler $U$-numbers under rational maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03988)
*Diego Marques*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文刻画了代数系数有理函数作用于 $U$-数后可能的类型，并给出保持 $U_m$ 类的刻划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maillet（1906）证明了有理函数将 Liouville 数映为 Liouville 数，即保持 $U$-数的特殊情形。自然的问题是：对更一般的 $U$-数（按类型 $m$ 分层），有理函数作用后类型如何变化？哪些有理函数能保持某一类型类？这是对 Maillet 经典结果的精细化与推广。

Method: 设 $R$ 为非常数有理函数，系数为实代数数，次数为 $d$，其极小定义域在 $\mathbb{Q}$ 上的次数为 $e$。通过对 $R$ 在 $U$-数上的逼近性质进行定量分析（利用高度、逼近阶与代数次数的关系），确定 $\xi$ 遍历类型 $m$ 的 $U$-数时 $R(\xi)$ 的可能类型集合。

Result: 对任意 $m\geq 1$，$R(\xi)$（$\xi$ 取遍类型 $m$ 的 $U$-数）的可能类型恰为所有满足 $\lceil m/(ed)\rceil\leq r\leq em$ 的整数 $r$。而且，每个这样的类型都在任意非空开区间内的某个类型 $m$ 的 $\xi$ 处实现。

Conclusion: 当 $m\geq2$ 时，唯一保持类型 $m$ 的 $U$-数类的实代数系数有理函数是 $\mathbb{Q}$ 上的 Möbius 变换。

Abstract: In 1906, Maillet proved that every nonconstant rational function over $\mathbb{Q}$ maps Liouville numbers to Liouville numbers. Let $R$ be a nonconstant rational function with real algebraic coefficients, of degree $d$, and let $e$ be the degree of its minimal field of definition over $\mathbb{Q}$. We prove that, for every $m\geq 1$, the possible types of $R(ξ)$, as $ξ$ ranges over $U$-numbers of type $m$, are exactly the integers $r$ with $\lceil m/(ed)\rceil\leq r\leq em$. Moreover, every such type occurs for some $ξ$ of type $m$ in every nonempty open interval. Consequently, for $m\geq2$, the only rational functions with real algebraic coefficients preserving the class of $U$-numbers of type $m$ are the Möbius transformations over $\mathbb{Q}$.

</details>


### [188] [Optimal large sieve for fixed order characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04045)
*Alexandre de Faveri*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了一般数域上$n$阶Hecke特征的大筛不等式，预期该界在所有参数范围内最优，并给出三次特征第二矩、无平方模数下渐近展开以及各阶矩与零点密度估计等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典大筛不等式对高阶Hecke特征（$n\ge3$）的估计在一般数域上不够精细，限制了对高阶特征矩和零点密度等问题的研究。

Method: 针对包含$n$次单位根的数域，改进$n$阶Hecke特征的大筛不等式，并由此推导相关应用。

Result: 得到预期在所有参数范围内最优的大筛界；导出按导子排序的三次特征第二矩的上界、无平方模数上三次第二矩带低阶项的渐近式，以及每个$n\ge3$的矩估计与零点密度估计的改进。

Conclusion: 改进的大筛不等式是核心工具，为高阶Hecke特征的矩问题与零点密度问题提供了更强的定量结果。

Abstract: Let $n \geq 3$. We improve the large sieve inequality for $n$-th order Hecke characters over any number field containing the $n$-th roots of unity. Our bound is expected to be optimal in all ranges of parameters. Several applications are deduced, including a tight upper bound for the second moment of cubic characters ordered by conductor, asymptotics with lower order terms for the cubic second moment over squarefree moduli, and improved moment and zero density estimates for every $n \geq 3$.

</details>


### [189] [Moments and Large Geometric Monodromy for Linearly Constrained Kloosterman Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04099)
*Hamed Ebadi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文确定了线性约束Kloosterman族在任意维数 n≥4 且特征 p>max(2,n) 时顶权层（top-weight sheaves）的连通几何单值群，证明其单位分支为 SL_{r(n)}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是刻画线性约束Kloosterman族顶权层所对应的连通几何单值群。核心难点在于：以往方法（如Guralnick–Tiep路线）依赖六阶矩与大量例外情形的有限计算/分类，本文希望在不使用有限群分类、也不依赖数值例外情形验证的前提下，给出统一且随固定维数一致成立的证明。

Method: 关键定量输入是一条四阶矩恒等式，其辅助几何只依赖于矩的阶数而非环境族的维数；经精确解耦后，一般层（generic stratum）由Cayley三次曲面的平面截面控制，并用射影射线（projective-ray）论证在每个固定维数下一致地获得所需的幂节省（power saving）。几何侧通过局部Fourier变换计算与Rojas-Leon乘性卷积公式构造出非单位幂幺边界元（nonidentity unipotent boundary element）。随后结合四阶矩、可约性（reductivity）、无穷性（infinitude）以及Larsen alternative，得到 G_geom^0(W_n)=SL_{r(n)}。

Result: 得到 G_geom^0(W_n) = SL_{r(n)}，且证明过程不依赖有限群分类，也不需数值例外情形证书。作为对照，文中保留独立的Guralnick–Tiep路线（含其六阶矩与精确有限计算），但该路线并未用于主定理的证明。

Conclusion: 线性约束Kloosterman族的连通几何单值群单位分支即为 SL_{r(n)}；四阶矩恒等式配合Cayley三次曲面的平面截面分析与射影射线论证，提供了无需有限群分类和例外情形验证的统一证明框架。

Abstract: We determine the connected geometric monodromy of the top-weight sheaves attached to linearly constrained Kloosterman families in every dimension n >= 4 and every characteristic p > max(2,n). The quantitative input is a fourth-moment identity whose auxiliary geometry depends on the moment degree rather than on the ambient family dimension. After an exact decoupling, the generic stratum is controlled by plane sections of the Cayley cubic, and a projective-ray argument gives the required power saving uniformly in each fixed dimension. On the geometric side, a local Fourier-transform calculation and Rojas-Leon's multiplicative-convolution formula produce a nonidentity unipotent boundary element. The fourth moment, reductivity, infinitude, and Larsen's alternative then give G_geom^0(W_n) = SL_r(n) without finite-group classification or numerical exceptional-case certificates. For comparison, we retain the independent Guralnick-Tiep route, including its sixth-moment and exact finite computations, but it is not used in the proof of the main theorem.

</details>


### [190] [Sequential exponential-motivic periods in spatially cut-off $φ^4_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04148)
*Juan Carlos Sampedro*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文用指数-动机周期序列表示空间截断φ⁴₂理论中的构造性积分，研究Galois变换对配分周期收敛性的影响，并刻画了能在自由场L¹空间上有界实现的变换子群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造性量子场论中，积分（配分周期）的逼近序列在Galois变换下的行为尚不清楚，需要厘清哪些变换保持物理值、哪些导致发散或非物理极限。

Method: 将空间截断φ⁴₂的构造性积分表示为指数-动机周期序列；分析Galois变换作用于这些周期时的模长与收敛性；刻画允许在自由场L¹空间上界定有界实现的变换子群，并通过单变量四次方例子进行验证。

Result: 对非零空间截断，单个Galois变换即可使配分周期在满足构造性估计的每个逼近序列上模长发散。对固定截断表示，刻画了容许有界实现的变换子群，并证明该子群上所有固定可观测极限都是其物理值的公共标量倍。单变量四次方例子表明，所有变换后的固定可观测序列可以在无需有界实现的情况下收敛，其极限不与物理值成比例。

Conclusion: Galois变换与有界可实现的联系决定了截断φ⁴₂构造性积分的极限行为：有界可实现子群保证极限为物理值的公共标量倍，而超出该子群则可能出现非比例极限，说明该子群的刻画具有本质意义。

Abstract: We represent constructive integrals in spatially cut-off $φ^4_2$ by sequences of exponential-motivic periods. For a nonzero spatial cutoff, a single Galois transformation makes the partition periods diverge in modulus along every sequence of approximations satisfying the constructive estimates. For a fixed cutoff presentation, we characterize the subgroup of transformations admitting a bounded realization on the free field's $L^1$ space and prove that all fixed-observable limits on this subgroup are a common scalar multiple of their physical values. A one-variable quartic example shows that all transformed fixed-observable sequences can converge without such a bounded realization, with limits that are not proportional to the physical values.

</details>


### [191] [Iterated cotangent-difference integrals over totally real cyclotomic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04214)
*Jin Cao,Hidekazu Furusho*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究带分圆平移的迭代余切积分，将其沿指定参考路径的值用分圆多重zeta值表示，并刻画其在全实分圆域上产生的Tate混合动机周期结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广迭代积分与多重zeta值理论，将余切形式的差视为全实分圆域上带穿孔射影直线的形式，从而引入"实分圆多重zeta值"这一族新周期，并探究其动机论与Hopf代数结构。

Method: 研究带分圆平移的迭代余切积分；对余切形式取差使其延拓到全实分圆域上的穿孔射影直线；区分有理切端点与整端点两类数据；利用Goncharov余积考察相关framed动机类生成的Hopf子代数。

Result: 参考路径上的值可由分圆多重zeta值表示；有理切端点情形下差积分是全实分圆域上Tate混合动机的周期，整端点数据则给出其整数环（倒置整除level的素数后）上的周期；相关framed动机类在Goncharov余积下生成Hopf子代数。

Conclusion: 迭代余切积分与分圆多重zeta值建立了联系，并通过动机与Hopf代数框架揭示了实分圆多重zeta值的代数结构。

Abstract: We study iterated cotangent integrals with cyclotomic shifts and express their values along specified reference paths in terms of cyclotomic multiple zeta values. Differences of cotangent forms extend to a punctured projective line over a totally real cyclotomic field, giving a family of "real cyclotomic multiple zeta values." With rational tangential endpoints, these difference integrals are periods of mixed Tate motives over that field; integral endpoint data yield periods over its ring of integers after inverting primes dividing the level. The associated framed motivic classes generate a Hopf subalgebra under Goncharov's coproduct.

</details>


### [192] [Prescribed ideal classes and elliptic points in Ennola cubic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04254)
*Junyu Lu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 对每个素数ℓ≥5与整数n≥2，构造具有指定ℓ上素理想、理想类阶恰为2^n的Ennola三次域；并用平方类障碍得到相关椭圆曲线上四个独立有理点、Pell方程刻画参数、亏格一基变换给出五个独立截面及秩至少五的有理纤维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Ennola三次域的理想类群结构（特别是2幂次阶）与相关椭圆曲线有理点、亏格一曲线截面的联系，揭示构造参数需满足的平方与同余条件。

Method: 构造Ennola三次域，指定ℓ上素理想并控制理想类阶为2^n；利用平方类障碍构造椭圆曲线的四个独立有理点；用Pell方程刻画满足平方与同余条件的全部参数；对亏格一曲线做基变换得到五个独立截面。

Result: 对任意素数ℓ≥5、n≥2，得到理想类阶恰为2^n的Ennola三次域；相关椭圆曲线上有四个独立有理点；Pell方程给出全部可行参数；基变换所得五个独立截面生成的子群在2处饱和；并得到无穷多个几何不同构、秩至少五的有理纤维。

Conclusion: 该构造将Ennola三次域的类群2幂阶与椭圆曲线有理点、亏格一曲线截面及纤维秩统一起来，给出参数化的族与无穷多高秩有理纤维。

Abstract: For every prime $\ell\geq5$ and integer $n\geq2$, we construct an Ennola cubic field with a specified prime above $\ell$ of exact ideal-class order $2^n$. The same squareclass obstructions give four independent rational points on associated elliptic curves. A Pell equation describes all parameters satisfying the required square and congruence conditions. A genus-one base change gives five independent sections whose generated subgroup is saturated at two. It also yields infinitely many geometrically nonisomorphic rational fibres of rank at least five.

</details>


### [193] [Cubic Progressions on Smooth Plane Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04312)
*Eslam Badr*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明数域上次数 d≥5 的光滑射影平面曲线 C 上的任意三次算术或几何递进序列均为有限，并给出四次条件下的条件性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究曲线上的三次递进序列的有限性问题，推广经典算术/几何递进在射影平面曲线上的情形。

Method: 构造由 C 上线性形式商除子控制的循环 Kummer 覆盖的素数 p 塔，结合 Kadets--Vogt 无穷多三次点曲线分类、Abramovich--Harris gonality 下降、连续层上椭圆映射的 Castelnuovo--Severi 分解及甲板变换障碍。

Result: d≥5 时任意三次算术或几何递进序列有限；在分圆非分裂假设下得到条件性四次结果；d=4 且 C(k)≠∅ 时存在无限三次递进。

Conclusion: d≥5 的曲线无无限三次递进序列，d=4 时主定理的类比不成立。

Abstract: Let $C \subset \Pbb^2_k$ be a smooth projective plane curve of degree $d \ge 5$ over a number field $k$. We prove that any cubic arithmetic or geometric progression sequence on $C$ is finite. The proof rests on a prime-$p$ tower of cyclic Kummer covers controlled by the divisor of a linear-form quotient on $C$, combined with the Kadets--Vogt classification of curves with infinitely many cubic points, the Abramovich--Harris gonality descent, a Castelnuovo--Severi factorization for the elliptic maps supplied by Kadets--Vogt independently at consecutive levels of the tower, and a deck-transformation obstruction. We also obtain a conditional quartic result under a cyclotomic non-splitting hypothesis. The case $d = 4$ with $C(k) \neq \emptyset$ admits infinite cubic progressions, so no analogue of the main theorem can hold there.

</details>


### [194] [Note on fractional sum of the divisor function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04579)
*Zhiyuan Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究除数函数在取整除法下的求和问题，给出了误差项指数 $\theta_c$ 的新上界，改进了 L. Wu (2025) 的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进 L. Wu (2025) 关于 $\sum_{n\leq x^{1/c}}d([x/n^c])$ 渐近公式中误差项指数的估计，获得更优的上界。

Method: 通过引入由递推关系 $(κ_1,λ_1)=(1/14,11/14)$、$(κ_{i+1},λ_{i+1})=(κ_i/(2κ_i+2),(κ_i+λ_i+1)/(2κ_i+2))$ 定义的参数对序列，并据此按区间 $2κ_{i+1}/(1-κ_{i+1}+λ_{i+1})\leq c<2κ_i/(1-κ_i+λ_i)$ 分段确定误差指数 $\theta_c$。

Result: 证明了 $\sum_{n\leq x^{1/c}}d([x/n^c])=d_cx^{1/c}+O_{\varepsilon,c}(x^{\theta_c+\varepsilon})$，其中 $\theta_c=2(c+1)/(2c^2+(5+i)c+2)$，$d_c$ 为适当常数。

Conclusion: 该结果改进了 L. Wu (2025) 的相应估计，给出误差项指数 $\theta_c$ 的更优上界。

Abstract: Let $d(n)$ be the divisor function and denote by $[t]$ the integral part of the real number $t$. We prove that
  \begin{align*}
  \sum_{n\leq x^{1/c}}d\left(\left[\frac{x}{n^c}\right]\right)=d_cx^{1/c}+O_{\varepsilon,c}(x^{θ_c+\varepsilon}),
  \end{align*} where $d_c$ is a suitable constant,
  \begin{align*}
  θ_c=\frac{2(c+1)}{2c^2+(5+i)c+2}, \quad \text{for}\ 2κ_{i+1}/(1-κ_{i+1}+λ_{i+1})\leq c<2κ_i/(1-κ_i+λ_i),
  \end{align*} the pair $(κ_i,λ_i)$ satisfies
  \begin{align*}
  (κ_1,λ_1)=\left(\frac{1}{14},\frac{11}{14}\right),\quad (κ_{i+1},λ_{i+1})=\left(\frac{κ_i}{2κ_i+2},\frac{κ_i+λ_i+1}{2κ_i+2}\right), \quad i\geq 1.
  \end{align*} This result constitutes an improvement upon that of L. Wu (2025).

</details>


### [195] [Few small primes suffice to cover unit groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04678)
*Scott D. Hughes*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文改进了覆盖模 q 单位群所需的素数个数上界，从 (log Q)^{1+ε} 降至最优阶 (1/log2+o(1))log Q。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Klurman、Shparlinski 与 Teräväinen 之前证明了几乎全部模 q≤Q 的单位群可由至多 (log Q)^{1+ε} 个多对数大小的素数通过子集乘积覆盖。作者希望将所需素数个数降低到最优阶，因为 m 个素数最多只有 2^m 个子集乘积，因此下界约为 (1/log2)log Q。缩小这一上界与信息论下界之间的差距是自然的改进目标。

Method: 证明结合了有限阿贝尔群中随机子集乘积的 Erdős–Rényi 型加倍论证，并对其进行修改以容忍少量例外特征；同时对少数在 s=1 附近有 L-函数零点的本原特征进行一次性“修复”。此外还给出一种精确加权修复，得到一个素数个数至多 (2/log2+o(1))log Q、例外集显式描述的变体。

Result: 证明了 (1/log2+o(1))log Q 个素数足够，达到一阶最优；并给出一个加权修复变体，素数个数上界为 (2/log2+o(1))log Q，且例外集可显式描述。

Conclusion: 该工作将覆盖单位群所需素数个数上界改进到一阶最优，与信息论下界匹配，核心工具是加倍论证与针对坏 L-函数零点的修复技巧。

Abstract: Klurman, Shparlinski and Teräväinen showed that there is a set of at most $(\log Q)^{1+\varepsilon}$ primes of polylogarithmic size whose subset products cover the unit group $(\mathbb{Z}/q\mathbb{Z})^\times$ for almost all moduli $q\le Q$. We show that $(1/\log2+o(1))\log Q$ primes suffice, which is optimal to first order, since $m$ primes have at most $2^m$ subset products. The proof combines a doubling argument of Erdős--Rényi type for random subset products in a finite abelian group, modified to tolerate a small set of exceptional characters, with a one-time ``repair'' of the few primitive characters whose $L$-functions have zeros near $s=1$. An exact weighted repair gives a variant with at most $(2/\log2+o(1))\log Q$ primes and an exceptional set described explicitly.

</details>


### [196] [Borwein-Bradley-type identities for Ramanujan-type series for $1/π^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04770)
*Noam Shalev*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Z.-W. Sun关于含调和数的1/π^4与π^4型Ramanujan级数的十个猜想，方法是对新的Borwein-Bradley型恒等式作Taylor系数比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Z.-W. Sun提出的关于Ramanujan型π幂次级数（含调和数）的十个猜想尚待证明，同时需要为相关原始级数提供新的证明途径，并将恒等式方法推广到半整数情形与Apéry型级数。

Method: 对Cullen-Zhao级数作参数化推广，其乘积仅依赖x^3、x^4、x^6或x^8，从而得到新的Borwein-Bradley型恒等式；这些推广由K. C. Au的Wilf-Zeilberger种子导出，其和为三角形式。证明中使用Guillera的周期性论证。此外用Dougall的5F4求和给出Koecher、Borwein-Bradley与Cohen恒等式的半整数类比。

Result: 通过比较上述恒等式的Taylor系数，证明了Z.-W. Sun的十个猜想；同时给出了原始级数的新证明；并得到其Taylor系数为zeta值乘积的Apéry型级数的半整数类比恒等式。

Conclusion: 新的Borwein-Bradley型恒等式与WZ方法、Guillera周期性论证相结合，是证明Ramanujan型π幂次级数猜想的有效工具，并可自然推广至半整数与Apéry型级数情形。

Abstract: We prove ten conjectures of Z.-W. Sun on Ramanujan-type series for $1/π^4$ and $π^4$ with harmonic numbers. They follow by comparing Taylor coefficients in new Borwein-Bradley-type identities, whose products depend only on $x^3$, $x^4$, $x^6$ or $x^8$. These identities come from parametric extensions of the series of Cullen and Zhao. We derive the extensions from K. C. Au's Wilf-Zeilberger (WZ) seeds, and their sums are trigonometric. Our proofs use Guillera's periodicity argument and give new proofs of the original series. In the same way, Dougall's $_5F_4$ sum gives half-integer analogues of the identities of Koecher, Borwein-Bradley and Cohen, whose Taylor coefficients are Apéry-like series for products of zeta values.

</details>


### [197] [Supercongruences for Domb numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04772)
*Kevin Calderon*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文利用模形式机制证明了 Sun 关于 Domb 数的两个超同余猜想：一个涉及无权重截断和 $S_p$（判别式 $-60$），另一个涉及 Lucas 序列加权的 Domb 和（判别式 $-156$）。通过将扭曲 Domb 级数解释为 $Γ_0(6)+\langle 3\rangle$ 上的模周期，并关联 Zagier 的 $C$-序列，作者在惯性素数处得到模 $p^2$ 的同余，在分裂素数处通过构造行列式为 1 或 3 的矩阵识别 Beukers 单位根，得到模 $p^3$ 的更强同余，并修正了第二个猜想中 $p=3$ 的例外情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sun 提出了关于 Domb 数的两个超同余猜想，其中一个含有例外反例 $p=3$，其深层数学机制尚不明确。作者希望统一解释并证明这两个猜想，揭示其与模形式、复乘及超奇异约化之间的内在联系。

Method: 将扭曲 Domb 级数视为 $Γ_0(6)+\langle 3\rangle$ 上的模周期，并通过二次拉回与 Zagier 的 $C$-序列相联系；在相关复乘域中的惯性素数处，截断 $C$-周期等于 Hasse 不变量且在超奇异约化处消失，由此得到模 $p^2$ 整除性；在分裂素数处，显式构造行列式为 1 或 3 的矩阵来识别 Beukers 单位根，从而推导模 $p^3$ 的同余。

Result: 证明了 Sun 关于 $S_p=\sum_{n=0}^{p-1}D_n$ 的模 $p^2$ 同余猜想，并在分裂情形下给出了模 $p^3$ 的更强公式；对于 Lucas 序列加权的 Domb 和，在排除例外 $p=3$ 后证明了修正后的猜想，并在两个相关二次特征均为 $+1$ 时给出了模 $p^3$ 的精细化结果。两个应用分别对应判别式 $-60$ 和 $-156$ 的复乘序。

Conclusion: 两个超同余结果源自同一模机制：扭曲 Domb 级数的模周期性质、与 Zagier $C$-序列的二次拉回关系，以及惯性/分裂素数处 Hasse 不变量与 Beukers 单位根的分析，统一解释了判别式 $-60$ 和 $-156$ 的复乘现象，并为 Sun 的猜想提供了完整证明与修正。

Abstract: We prove two supercongruence statements proposed by Z.-W. Sun for Domb numbers. The first concerns the unweighted truncated sum
  $$S_p=\sum_{n=0}^{p-1}D_n$$
  and is governed by the two proper ideal classes of the order of discriminant $-60$ in $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-15})$. We prove Sun's conjectured congruences modulo $p^2$ and obtain stronger formulas modulo $p^3$ in the split cases. The second concerns Domb sums weighted by the Lucas sequences associated with $X^2-11X+1$. Sun's original formulation has the exceptional counterexample $p=3$; after excluding this prime, we prove the corrected statement, including its modulo-$p^3$ refinement when both relevant quadratic characters are $+1$. Both results arise from one modular mechanism. The twisted Domb series is a modular period on $Γ_0(6)+\langle 3\rangle$ and is related by a quadratic pullback to Zagier's sporadic $C$-sequence. At primes inert in the imaginary quadratic field underlying the relevant complex multiplication, the truncated $C$-period is the Hasse invariant and vanishes at supersingular reduction; a finite pullback then gives divisibility modulo $p^2$. At split primes, explicitly constructed determinant-$1$ or determinant-$3$ matrices identify the Beukers unit root, yielding congruences modulo $p^3$. The two applications correspond respectively to orders of complex multiplication of discriminants $-60$ and $-156$.

</details>


### [198] [Proofs of some OEIS conjectures on Wythoff sums, Fibonacci and Lucas words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04842)
*Alex Ashburn*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明并验证了整数序列在线百科全书（OEIS）中关于下/上Wythoff序列与Fibonacci字的多项猜想，多数证明通过Walnut自动定理证明器实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OEIS中积累了大量关于Wythoff序列、Fibonacci字及其补序列的猜想（如Kimberling、Mathar、Schmidt等提出者），这些猜想缺乏统一证明。本文旨在系统性地证明这些组合数论猜想，并厘清其间关系与已有结果的依赖。

Method: 以Wythoff序列、Fibonacci字与Lucas字为对象，采用Walnut自动定理证明器作为主要工具，通过判定过程（decision procedure）证明猜想；对部分猜想亦引用已有文献结果（如Shallit、Bosma等对A260317的Walnut结果，Carlitz、Scoville与Vaughan 1973年的定理），并给出完整命令文件以复现验证。

Result: 证明Kimberling关于A259598的三个猜想中的两个：n=⌊hφ⌋+⌊kφ²⌋（h,k≥1）恰有一种表示当且仅当n+1=2F（F≥2为Fibonacci数），恰有两种表示当且仅当n+1≥7为Lucas数；第三个（无表示当且仅当n+1为Fibonacci数）已由Kawsumarng等先前证明。指出A003622两项之和与其余项（A333308、A333309）的间隙猜想经平移2后可由A260317的已有Walnut结果推出，并重新验证。证明Kimberling 2025年关于Fibonacci字与Lucas字取指定值位置间隙的五个猜想（A383423–A383427）。证明Mathar关于A285383的猜想；指出Schmidt关于A003250的猜想可由Carlitz、Scoville与Vaughan 1973年定理推出，并用Walnut确认。

Conclusion: 本文系统解决了OEIS中多个组合数论猜想，展示了Walnut自动定理证明器在Wythoff序列与Fibonacci字相关猜想证明中的有效性，同时揭示了若干猜想与既有文献结果的推导关系，并提供了可复现的完整命令文件。

Abstract: We prove several conjectures from the On-Line Encyclopedia of Integer Sequences about the lower and upper Wythoff sequences and the Fibonacci word. Among them are two of Kimberling's three conjectures on the number of ways to write $n=\lfloor h\varphi\rfloor+\lfloor k\varphi^2\rfloor$ with $h,k\ge1$ (A259598): exactly one way if and only if $n+1=2F$ for a Fibonacci number $F\ge2$, and exactly two ways if and only if $n+1\ge7$ is a Lucas number. The third conjecture, that no way exists if and only if $n+1$ is a Fibonacci number, was proved earlier by Kawsumarng et al. We also observe that Kimberling's conjecture on the gaps of the sums of two distinct terms of A003622 and of their complement (A333308, A333309) follows, after a shift by $2$, from earlier Walnut results of Shallit and of Bosma et al. on A260317, and we re-verify it. Next, we prove Kimberling's five 2025 conjectures on the gaps between positions where the Fibonacci word and the "Lucas word" take prescribed values (A383423-A383427). Finally, we prove a conjecture of Mathar on A285383, and we point out that a conjecture of Schmidt on A003250 follows from theorems of Carlitz, Scoville and Vaughan (1973); we also confirm it with Walnut. Most proofs are decision procedures run in the free prover Walnut, and we supply the complete command file.

</details>


### [199] [Through the lens of the Eisenstein ideal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04943)
*Romyar Sharifi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过Eisenstein理想阐述模曲线几何与分圆域算术之间的关系，构造了X_1(N)同调Eisenstein约化的实部P与第N分圆整数环第二K群p部分的实部Y之间的两个猜想互逆映射，并给出第二个映射的完全等变构造及分圆单位Euler系统的等变构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨模曲线的几何与分圆域算术之间的深刻联系，借助Eisenstein理想作为桥梁，试图建立同调群与K群之间的互逆对应，并进一步发展分圆单位Euler系统的等变框架。

Method: 对整数N≥5及奇素数p，定义X_1(N)同调的Eisenstein约化实部P与第N分圆整数环第二K群的p部分实部Y之间的两个猜想互逆映射；回顾P到Y映射的motivic构造及模符号上底层映射Eisenstein性质的近期证明；利用X_1(N)第一étale上同调的Eisenstein约化给出Y到P映射的完全等变构造；并借助模曲线相对上同调群中的扩张类给出分圆单位Euler系统的等变构造。

Result: 建立了从Y到P的完全等变映射构造，并给出了分圆单位Euler系统的等变构造，将其纳入整体研究纲领之中。

Conclusion: 通过Eisenstein理想将模曲线几何与分圆域算术相联系的两个互逆映射构造有望深化对分圆单位Euler系统的理解，并为相关算术几何研究纲领提供新的等变工具。

Abstract: This paper exposits relationships between the geometry of modular curves and the arithmetic of cyclotomic fields through the Eisenstein ideal. For an integer $N \ge 5$ and an odd prime $p$, we define two conjecturally inverse maps between the real part $P$ of the Eisenstein reduction of the homology $P$ of $X_1(N)$ and the real part of the $p$-part $Y$ of the second $K$-group of the $N$th cyclotomic integer ring. We recall a motivic construction of the map from $P$ to $Y$ and describe the recent proof of the Eisenstein property of the underlying map on modular symbols. We provide a fully equivariant construction of the second map from $Y$ to $P$ using the Eisenstein reduction of the first étale cohomology of $X_1(N)$. We then explain an equivariant construction of the Euler system of cyclotomic units using extension classes in relative cohomology groups of modular curves and describe its place in our program.

</details>


### [200] [On the generalized Sierpiński and Riesel numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05149)
*Paulius Virbalas*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了在任意固定进制下，存在无穷算术级数中的正整数同时为广义Sierpiński数和广义Riesel数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究同时具有Sierpiński与Riesel性质的数，推广经典Sierpiński/Riesel问题到任意进制及广义（至少含两个不同素因子）情形。

Method: 采用覆盖系统（Erdős思想及Harrington构造），结合Zsigmondy本原素因子定理与乘法阶性质。

Result: 构造出无穷算术级数，使其每个k对任意n>0，kb^n+1与kb^n−1均至少有两个不同素因子。

Conclusion: 同时对任意进制b≥2存在无穷多广义Sierpiński-Riesel数，验证并推广了相关构造思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that for any fixed base $b \ge 2$, there exists an infinite arithmetic progression of positive integers $k$ that are simultaneously generalized Sierpiński and generalized Riesel numbers in base $b$. More precisely, we construct such an arithmetic progression so that both $k\cdot b^n + 1$ and $k\cdot b^n - 1$ have at least two distinct prime factors for every positive integer $n$. Our approach uses covering systems, following the ideas of Erdős and later constructions of Harrington. The argument is then completed using Zsigmondy's theorem on primitive divisors together with properties of multiplicative orders.

</details>


### [201] [One-level density of zeros of the Riemann zeta function weighted by its special values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05227)
*Shingo Sugiyama,Ade Irma Suriajaya*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究黎曼zeta函数零点的一级密度，其以Re(s)=σ上的特殊值为权，证明当且仅当σ=1/2时密度函数改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画黎曼zeta函数零点分布与特殊值之间的关系，探究权函数参数σ对零点一级密度的影响，特别是临界线σ=1/2的特殊地位。

Method: 引入以Re(s)=σ（1/2≤σ≤1为固定实数）上zeta特殊值加权的零点一级密度，并对不同σ值下的密度函数进行比较分析。

Result: 证明零点的一级密度函数发生变化当且仅当σ=1/2。

Conclusion: 临界线σ=1/2在加权零点密度行为中具有独特的临界性质，其余σ值下密度函数保持不变。

Abstract: We consider the one-level density of zeros of the Riemann zeta function weighted by its special values on the line ${\rm Re}(s)=σ$, where $σ$ is a fixed real number such that $1/2\le σ\le1$. We show that the density function for the Riemann zeta function changes if and only if $σ=1/2$.

</details>


### [202] [The main conjecture for a Vinogradov subsystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05515)
*Trevor D. Wooley*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文证明了 k≥2, s>0 时一般指数和的 L^{2s} 矩估计，主项 X^{s+ε} 与误差项 X^{2s-(k²+k-2)/2} 相匹配，从而在一类超出 Vinogradov（平移-伸缩不变）型的新指数和族上验证了主猜想；方法基于作者近期发展的嵌套有效同余（nested efficient congruencing）技术，并将结果推广到数域与函数域情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指数和的 L^{2s} 矩估计（均值估计）是解析数论与 Waring 问题（如 Vinogradov 均值定理、Hua 不等式）的核心工具。已知对 Vinogradov 型（平移-伸缩不变）指数和已有深刻的界，但对更一般的、仅具部分不变性的指数和，主猜想（即主项 X^{s+ε} 在适当参数范围内主导）尚未被完全验证。作者旨在扩大满足主猜想的指数和族，从而突破平移-伸缩不变性的限制。

Method: 采用嵌套有效同余方法（nested efficient congruencing），这是作者近期工作的关键技术。该方法将有效同余（efficient congruencing）中的同余切片思想嵌套使用，通过在多层模空间上对解的变量进行同余分类与递推，把积分矩的估计约化为若干"井"上的控制，并利用类适当分割与递推关系逐步压缩非主项，从而得到所期望的 X^{s+ε}+X^{2s-(k²+k-2)/2} 型上界；方法不依赖平移-伸缩不变性。

Result: 对任意 ε>0、k≥2、s>0，证明 ∫_{[0,1)^{k-1}} |Σ_{1≤x≤X} e(α₂x²+…+α_k x^k)|^{2s} dα ≪ X^{s+ε} + X^{2s-(k²+k-2)/2}。该估计在一类新的指数和族（超出 Vinogradov 平移-伸缩不变型）上确认了主猜想，并给出相应主项与误差项的明确指数。相应结果在数域与函数域中同样成立。

Conclusion: 嵌套有效同余方法能够处理不具备平移-伸缩不变性的指数和，证明其对一般系数多项式指数和同样成立主猜想形式的上界；该结论不仅适用于整数环情形，还可移植到数域与函数域，表明该方法具有一定的普适性与结构性。

Abstract: When $k\ge 2$ and $s>0$, we establish that for each $\varepsilon>0$, one has \[ \int_{[0,1)^{k-1}}\biggl| \sum_{1\le x\le X}e(α_2x^2+\ldots +α_k x^k)\biggr|^{2s}\,{\rm d}\boldsymbol α\ll X^{s+\varepsilon}+X^{2s-(k^2+k-2)/2}, \] confirming the main conjecture for a new family of exponential sums beyond those of Vinogradov (translation-dilation invariant) type. Our methods are based on very recent work of the author employing the nested efficient congruencing method. Consequently, analogues of our results hold also in the setting of number fields and function fields.

</details>


### [203] [Sharp mean value estimates in the absence of translation-dilation invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05525)
*Trevor D. Wooley*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了缺乏平移-伸缩不变性的指数和的精确均值估计，验证了一类新指数和族的主要猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典指数和均值估计（如Vinogradov均值定理）依赖于平移-伸缩不变性，而对于不具备该性质的新指数和族，缺乏相应结果，因此需要发展新的方法来建立精确估计并验证猜想。

Method: 方法基于作者近期关于嵌套有效同余（nested efficient congruencing）方法的工作，将其应用于缺乏平移-伸缩不变性的指数和均值估计。

Result: 对于 k≥1、s>0 及任意 ε>0，证明了积分估计 ∫_[0,1)^k |∑_{1≤h≤X}∑_{1≤z≤X} e(hzα_1+…+hz^kα_k)|^{2s} dα ≪ X^ε (X^s + X^{2s−k(k+3)/2})，从而验证了一类新指数和族的主要猜想。

Conclusion: 通过嵌套有效同余方法，成功建立了新指数和族的尖锐均值估计，确认了相应主要猜想，并拓展了指数和均值理论的应用范围。

Abstract: We establish sharp mean value estimates for various exponential sums lacking translation-dilation invariance properties. For example, suppose that $k\ge 1$ and $s>0$. Then, for each $\varepsilon>0$, one has \[ \int_{[0,1)^k}\biggl| \sum_{1\le h\le X}\sum_{1\le z\le X}e(hzα_1+\ldots +hz^kα_k)\biggr|^{2s}\,{\rm d}\boldsymbol α\ll X^\varepsilon\bigl( X^s+X^{2s-k(k+3)/2}\bigr), \] confirming the main conjecture for a new family of exponential sums. Our methods are based on the application of very recent work of the author employing the nested efficient congruencing method.

</details>


### [204] [Four-square polynomial triples: quadratic classification and equal-degree obstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05565)
*Mirela Jukić Bokun,Ana Jurasić*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究多项式三元组使其两两乘积加1及三者乘积加1均为平方。证明$\mathbb{R}[X]$中二次情形本质上唯一（源于Jurasić构造），$\mathbb{Q}[X],\mathbb{Z}[X]$中不存在；并给出次数模式限制、可约性条件及多项式Diophantine三元组的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式版本的Diophantine m-元组（Diophantine triples）问题：寻找多项式$a,b,c$使得$ab+1,ac+1,bc+1,abc+1$全为平方。此前Jurasić给出了一个二次构造，但这类三元组的分类、存在性以及次数结构的整体刻画尚不清晰，且在$\mathbb{Q}[X],\mathbb{Z}[X]$上的情形未知。

Method: 在$\mathbb{R},\mathbb{Q},\mathbb{Z}$上考察互异非常数多项式三元组，要求四个表达式均为多项式平方。先对二次情形在全等（共同的可逆仿射变量替换与置换）意义下进行分类；再对一般次数模式$(2n,2m,2l)$进行结构分析，推导因式分解系统与可约性约束；最后应用于参数化的多项式Diophantine三元组族。

Result: 1) 在$\mathbb{R}[X]$中，二次三元组在置换与共同可逆仿射变换下恰有一个（Jurasić构造），$\mathbb{Q}[X],\mathbb{Z}[X]$中无此类三元组。2) 对$\mathbb{R}$或$\mathbb{Q}$上一般次数$(2n,2m,2l)$，$1\le n\le m\le l$，必有$l=m$或$l>n+m$。3) $l=m$时得到因式分解系统与可约性限制；等次数情形在$\mathbb{Q}$上每个多项式至少有2个互异不可约因子。4) 在自然正则参数化多项式Diophantine三元组族中，当参数为常数或首元为平方时，$abc+1$永不为平方。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了满足四个表达式全为平方的多项式三元组：实数域上二次情形唯一（Jurasić构造），有理整数域上不存在；给出了次数的强结构约束（$l=m$或$l>n+m$）与可约性条件，并揭示了参数化Diophantine三元组中第四项不可能是平方的现象。

Abstract: We study triples of distinct nonconstant polynomials $a,b,c$ over $\mathbb{R}$, $\mathbb{Q}$ or $\mathbb{Z}$ for which $$
  ab+1,\qquad ac+1,\qquad bc+1,\qquad abc+1 $$ are all polynomial squares. We show that, up to permutation and a common invertible affine change of variable, there is exactly one such triple of quadratic polynomials in $\mathbb{R}[X]$, the one arising from a recent construction of Jurasić, and that none exists in $\mathbb{Q}[X]$ or $\mathbb{Z}[X]$. Moreover, for arbitrary nonconstant entries over $\mathbb{R}$ or $\mathbb{Q}$, we prove that the degree pattern $(2n,2m,2l)$ with $1\le n\le m\le l$ satisfies either $l=m$ or $l>n+m$. For the case $l=m$, we derive factorization systems and reducibility restrictions. In the equal-degree case over $\mathbb{Q}$, we further show that every entry has at least two distinct irreducible factors. As an application, we show that in a natural regular parametrized family of polynomial Diophantine triples in $\mathbb{Q}[X]$ the fourth expression $abc+1$ is never a square when the parameter is constant or the first entry is a square.

</details>


### [205] [On the factorization of $x^{d_{r}} + x^{d_{r-1}} + \cdots + x^{d_{2}} + x^{d_{1}} + p$ and a conjecture of C. Nicol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05568)
*Michael Filaseta,Lilit Martirosyan,London Cameron Swan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 对任意素数 p 和正整数 r、d_1<...<d_r，多项式 x^{d_r}+...+x^{d_1}+p 去掉所有首一不可约倒易因子后仍不可约；由此得到关于 Nicol 猜想（两个分圆多项式之和为分圆多项式乘积乘以 2 或一个不可约多项式）的若干结果，并对乘积情形 Φ_n(x)Φ_m(x)+1 给出类似结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究形如稀疏多项式加素数常数的不可约性，并借此推进 Charles Nicol 关于两个分圆多项式之和/积的分解结构猜想。

Method: 利用倒易多项式与首一不可约倒易因子的结构分析，证明 x^{d_r}+...+x^{d_1}+p 去掉全部首一不可约倒易因子后不可约，并将该结论应用于分圆多项式之和与乘积。

Result: 证明了该类稀疏多项式的不可约性结论，并据此建立了与 Nicol 猜想相关的多项结果，包括 Φ_n(x)+Φ_m(x) 以及 Φ_n(x)Φ_m(x)+1 的分解性质。

Conclusion: 该工作为 Nicol 猜想提供了新的证据与部分结果，说明稀疏多项式不可约性方法可用于研究分圆多项式的组合分解。

Abstract: For an arbitrary prime $p$ and arbitrary positive integers $r$ and $d_{1}, \ldots, d_{r}$ with $d_{r} > \cdots > d_{1}$, we show that $x^{d_{r}} + x^{d_{r-1}} + \cdots + x^{d_{2}} + x^{d_{1}} + p$ removed of all of its monic irreducible reciprocal factors is irreducible. As a consequence, we establish a number of results related to a conjecture of Charles Nicol that, over the rationals, the sum of two cyclotomic polynomials $Φ_{n}(x) + Φ_{m}(x)$ is a product of cyclotomic polynomials times either $2$ or an irreducible polynomial, where $n$ and $m$ are integers exceeding $1$. We also establish similar results for $Φ_{n}(x)Φ_{m}(x)+1$.

</details>


### [206] [Ab-faithfulness of absolute Galois groups in number theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05634)
*Naganori Yamaguchi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明混合特征离散赋值域及正维高局部域的绝对Galois群是ab-faithful，并给出slimness结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究绝对Galois群的ab-faithfulness与slimness性质，推广到混合特征离散赋值域和正维高局部域等类域。

Method: 通过有限分裂嵌入问题构造更多例子；建立形式Hilbert 90判别准则，用于判定有限步可解商的无中心性与slimness。

Result: 证明混合特征离散赋值域的绝对Galois群是ab-faithful；正维高局部域也具有ab-faithfulness；将形式Hilbert 90判别准则应用于ab-faithful绝对Galois群，得到若干类域（含正维高局部域）的slimness。

Conclusion: ab-faithfulness对混合特征离散赋值域及正维高局部域成立，并借助形式Hilbert 90判别准则导出多类域的slimness。

Abstract: We prove that the absolute Galois group of every discretely valued field of mixed characteristic is ab-faithful. We also prove ab-faithfulness for higher local fields of positive dimension and give further examples using finite split embedding problems. Finally, we give a formal Hilbert~90 criterion for center-freeness and slimness of finite-step solvable quotients. We apply this criterion to ab-faithful absolute Galois groups and obtain slimness for several classes of fields, including higher local fields of positive dimension.

</details>


### [207] [Slope Stability for ${\mathbf Z}_p$-Towers of Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05652)
*Daqing Wan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文系统研究特征p有限域上射影直线在无穷远分歧的$\mathbf{Z}_p$-塔的斜率稳定性，揭示几何稳定性（亏格序列、分歧断点序列）与Newton斜率算术稳定性之间的关系，给出正面结果并构造反例刻画稳定与失效的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解亏格序列或分歧断点序列的几何稳定性在多大程度上能推出塔中曲线zeta函数Newton斜率的算术稳定性，并厘清Kosters–Zhu关于斜率稳定性的猜想与问题。

Method: 通过构造具有最小分歧断点比但不斜率稳定的塔作为反例；在最小断点假设下证明在逼近第一块的增长区间上的精确部分斜率稳定性；使用有限接触块上半线性Frobenius的独立精度定理进行证明。

Result: 构造出具有最小断点比却非斜率稳定的塔，否定了Kosters–Zhu关于可移除度间隙条件及亏格稳定性蕴含斜率稳定性的问题；在最小断点假设下得到增长区间上的精确部分斜率稳定性与一致比较；进一步反例区分部分与完全第一块传播、第一块与两块传播等；改进了经典度间隙常数、部分稳定性损失及充分起始水平的界，均与常数域次数无关。

Conclusion: 几何稳定性一般不足以推出斜率稳定性；但在最小断点假设下可获得明确的精确部分稳定性与改进的一致界，从而精确刻画了斜率稳定性成立与失效的边界。

Abstract: This work is devoted to a systematic study of slope stability for $\mathbf Z_p$-towers of the projective line over finite fields of characteristic $p$, ramified only at infinity. Our aim is to understand to what extent geometric stability of the genus sequence, or of the ramification break sequence, implies arithmetic stability of the Newton slopes of the zeta functions of the curves in the tower. We prove positive results and construct counterexamples that clarify the boundary between slope stability and its failure, with sharp bounds in several cases.
  For towers with eventual minimal ramification break ratios, a condition stronger than genus stability, Kosters and Zhu proved slope stability under an additional degree-gap condition. We construct towers with minimal break ratios that are not slope stable, answering negatively both their question of whether the gap can be removed and their broader question of whether genus stability implies slope stability. Under the minimal-break hypothesis alone, we prove exact partial slope stability on growing intervals approaching the first block, with uniform comparisons over explicit boundary annuli and finite flat spectral pieces. Further counterexamples distinguish partial from complete first-block propagation, first-block from two-block propagation, and full classical stability from a uniform annular slope law below one.
  We obtain improved bounds for the classical degree-gap constant, the partial-stability loss, and the sufficient starting levels, all independent of the constant-field degree. The proof uses a separate precision theorem for the semilinear Frobenius on finite contact blocks.

</details>


### [208] [Amicable numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05661)
*Paul Pollack*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文报告了由ChatGPT Astra发现的一个证明，给出了友好数对计数函数A(x)的一个上界估计：A(x) ≤ x·exp{-(1/2+o(1))·log x·log₃x/log₂x}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 友好数对（amicable pairs）是数论中经典的研究对象，其余和函数s(n)相关的计数问题长期受到关注。确定友好数对计数函数A(x)的增长上界是数论中的核心问题之一，本文旨在给出一个新的、更精细的上界估计。

Method: 本文的方法是基于一个由ChatGPT Astra发现的证明。该证明涉及对真因数和函数s(n)的分布进行分析，估计满足s(n)=m且s(m)=n的整数对的个数，并利用对数迭代（log₂x、log₃x）等解析数论工具推导上界。

Result: 证明得到的结果为：当x→∞时，A(x) ≤ x·exp{-(1/2+o(1))·log x·log₃x/log₂x}。这表明友好数对在正整数中非常稀疏，其计数上界比x更快地衰减。

Conclusion: 本文给出了友好数对计数函数A(x)的一个新的上界，由ChatGPT Astra发现证明。该结果量化了友好数对的稀有性，为友好数分布的进一步研究提供了新的理论工具和界限。

Abstract: Let $s(n):=\sum_{d\mid n,\,d < n} d$ denote the sum of the proper divisors of $n$. Distinct positive integers $n,m$ form an amicable pair if $s(n)=m$ and $s(m)=n$. Let $A(x)$ count the positive integers not exceeding $x$ that belong to an amicable pair. We report on a proof, found by ChatGPT Astra, that $$ A(x)\le x\exp\{-(1/2+o(1))\log x\,\log_3 x/\log_2 x\}$$ as $x\to\infty$. Here $\log_k$ denotes the $k$th iterate of the natural logarithm.

</details>


### [209] [An Algebraic Proof of the Non-Modularity of Solutions to the Canonical Modular Differential Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05668)
*Yuichi Sakai,Hiroyuki Tsutsumi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文用代数方法研究Kaneko-Zagier微分方程权 $k$ 的模形式解存在性条件，借助同余子群 $Γ(N)$ 的连接矩阵，证明当 $(k+1)/6=n/m$ 的分母 $m=1$ 或 $m\ge7$ 时相关二维表示不可约，从而完成解的维数分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kaneko-Zagier微分方程模形式解的存在性此前主要依赖 $SL_2(\\mathbb{Z})$ 的表示论方法，本文希望给出代数的证明并得到更完整的分类。

Method: 利用主同余子群 $Γ(N)$ 的连接矩阵；从表示矩阵的换位子导出同时三角化的条件，并结合Galois理论，判定相关二维表示在 $m=1$ 或 $m\ge7$ 时不可约。

Result: 证明了当 $(k+1)/6=n/m$ 的分母 $m=1$ 或 $m\ge7$ 时，$Γ(N)$ 的二维表示不可约，由此确定不存在模形式解的权，并给出解空间维数的完整分类。

Conclusion: 该代数途径替代了 $SL_2(\\mathbb{Z})$ 表示论方法，实现了Kaneko-Zagier方程模形式解的权的完整分类。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide an algebraic proof of the condition for the existence of modular form solutions to the Kaneko-Zagier differential equation of weight $k$. Unlike previous representation-theoretic approaches relying on $SL_2(\mathbb{Z})$, our method employs the connection matrices of the principal congruence subgroup $Γ(N)$. By deriving a condition for simultaneous triangularizability from the commutators of the representation matrices and applying Galois theory, we prove that the associated two-dimensional representation of $Γ(N)$ is irreducible when the denominator $m$ of the fraction $(k+1)/6 = n/m$ satisfies $m=1$ or $m \ge 7$. Consequently, we identify the weights that admit no modular form solutions and obtain a complete classification of the dimensions of the spaces of such solutions.

</details>


### [210] [Ideal-Smooth Sets and an Explicit Upper Bound for the Real Sum-Product Exponent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05725)
*Kaiqiang Zhang,Yuanguo Zeng,Yuyu Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 构造任意大的实代数整数有限集A，使|A+A|和|AA|均不超过|A|^{1.95835}，突破了此前和积问题的指数界，且不使用任何未证明假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 和积问题（sum-product problem）中长期关注集合A在整数、实数或代数整数中的|A+A|与|AA|同时较小的下界。此前相关构造的指数约为1.98，本文希望进一步压低该指数，逼近更优的和积界，并在无条件假设下给出严格构造。

Method: 组合构造：将截断的理想光滑S-单位纤维（ideal-smooth S-unit fibres）与互素加法因子（coprime additive factor）相结合。用张量积秩论证（tensor-product rank argument），在每个选定素理想处使用二维局部特征来控制加法因子的损失；对一外层平行体（outer parallel body）作直接估计以改进和集填充界（sumset packing bound）。算术输入为已知十次域上的非分歧pro-2塔（unramified pro-2 tower），并用同时Frobenius切割控制小素理想。联类数—调节子代价用无条件Tsfasman–Vlăduţ不等式。所有进入指数的有限数值比较均由外向有理区间算术认证。

Result: 构造出任意大的实代数整数有限集A，满足|A+A|和|AA|均≤|A|^{1.95835}，即和积指数同时降至1.95835以下。

Conclusion: 在和积问题上给出新的无条件上界构造，指数为1.95835；方法融合代数数论（S-单位、类群、调节子、Tsfasman–Vlăduţ不等式）与加性组合工具（张量积秩、填充界），并通过区间算术严格验证数值，未使用任何未证明假设。

Abstract: We construct arbitrarily large finite sets $A$ of real algebraic integers such that both $|A+A|$ and $|AA|$ are at most $|A|^{1.95835}$. The construction combines truncated ideal-smooth $S$-unit fibres with a coprime additive factor. A tensor-product rank argument, using a two-dimensional local feature at each selected prime ideal, controls the loss in the additive factor, while a direct estimate for an outer parallel body improves the sumset packing bound. The arithmetic input is an unramified pro-$2$ tower over a known degree-ten field, with simultaneous Frobenius cuts controlling the small prime ideals. We use unconditional Tsfasman--Vlăduţ inequalities for the joint class-number--regulator cost. All finite numerical comparisons entering the exponent are certified by outward rational interval arithmetic. No unproved hypothesis is used.

</details>


### [211] [Automorphic commutator relations in relative geometric Langlands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05781)
*Shurui Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Liu-Wang猜想中的自守交换子关系，去除了原有附加上同调消没假设及亏格不等于一的限制，对任意光滑射影曲线成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该自守交换子关系是Liu-Wang框架中将高阶周期积分与函数域上L函数高阶导数联系起来的自守Clifford关系的几何输入。此前该关系仅在附加上同调消没假设下、且曲线亏格不等于一时才被建立，本文旨在移除这些限制。

Method: 引入"universal-local"中间阶段，位于局部与半局部设置之间：构造带两个有序标记截面的形式多重圆盘分类叠，并在其上构造Hecke叠、单位对象、Hecke作用及特殊上同调对应的universal-local类比。利用该叠上的Fusion与Purity得到universal-local交换子恒等式，其证明仅依赖于猜想中涉及的Plancherel代数的局部假设。再通过向曲线特化并结合六函子形式化方法得出结论。

Result: 在所给局部假设下，对任意光滑射影曲线（包括亏格为1的情形）证明了Liu-Wang猜想中的自守交换子关系，且无需额外的上同调消没假设。

Conclusion: 通过引入universal-local阶段与分类叠技术，成功去除了原有假设，使自守交换子关系在中性特征下对所有光滑射影曲线成立，为自守Clifford关系框架提供了更一般的几何基础。

Abstract: We prove the automorphic commutator relation conjectured by Liu and Wang arXiv:2504.00275, which is the geometric input for the automorphic Clifford relations in their framework relating higher period integrals to higher derivatives of $L$-functions over function fields. The relation was previously established only under additional cohomological vanishing assumptions and for curves of genus different from one; we remove these assumptions. The main new ingredient is a "universal-local" stage between the local and the semi-local settings: we introduce the classifying stack of formal multidisks with two ordered marked sections, and we construct universal-local analogues of the Hecke stacks, unit objects, Hecke actions and special cohomological correspondences. Fusion and purity on this stack yield a universal-local commutator identity, which is proved using only the local assumptions on the Plancherel algebra that enter the conjecture.
