Specialization to a curve and the six-functor formalism then give the conjecture for every smooth projective curve.

</details>


### [212] [Conflict-avoiding codes and near-primitive roots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05914)
*C. G. Karthick Babu,Pieter Moree,Sunil Kumar Pasupulati,Pietro Sgobba*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究冲突避免码中涉及4模d乘法阶的量，用Artin原根猜想技术刻画其在奇素数/奇整数上的分布，并给出与X^n-1在F_2上不可约分解的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冲突避免码理论中有一个关键量依赖于4模d的乘法阶（d遍历奇数n的正因子），其分布规律尚待刻画，且该量与其他数学对象的关系值得挖掘。

Method: 借助Artin原根猜想研究中的解析数论技术，分析该量在n取奇素数与奇整数时的分布；并探讨该量的其他解释。

Result: 建立了该量在奇数n（含奇素数情形）变化时的分布结果，并指出其一种解释与X^n-1在有限域F_2上的不可约分解相关联。

Conclusion: 该量不仅是冲突避免码理论的核心，还与F_2上多项式分解等经典问题存在联系，为后续研究提供了数论视角。

Abstract: An important role in the theory of conflict-avoiding codes is played by a quantity involving the multiplicative order of $4$ modulo $d$, with $d$ running over all positive divisors of an odd integer $n$. We establish results on its distribution as $n$ varies over the odd prime numbers, respectively odd integers, using techniques from the study of Artin's primitive root conjecture. We also point out some other interpretations of this quantity, with one of them making a connection with the factorization of $X^n-1$ into irreducibles over the finite field $\mathbb F_2$.

</details>


### [213] [A proper arithmetic surface with infinite étale fundamental group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05972)
*Pablo Chenal*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 构造了一个在 Spec Z 上固有、正则、整的算术曲面，带有一个截面，其 étale 基本群存在无限 pro-2 商，为数域整数环 Golod–Shafarevich 塔的几何类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数域整数环的 Golod–Shafarevich 塔给出无限非循环 p-扩张，其 Galois 群有无限 pro-p 商。作者希望在高维算术几何中寻找类似现象，即构造几何类比：一个算术曲面（2维）其 étale 基本群具有无限 pro-2 商。

Method: 几何类比 Golod–Shafarevich 塔的构造。取 Spec Z 上固有、正则、整的算术曲面，并赋予一个截面；通过该截面的存在控制基本群的生成元与关系，从而构造出无限 pro-2 商。

Result: 给出满足条件的算术曲面实例：固有、正则、整、带截面，且其 étale 基本群存在无限 pro-2 商。这为 Golod–Shafarevich 现象提供了几何（曲面）类比。

Conclusion: 数域整数环上的无限 p-扩张（Golod–Shafarevich 塔）在算术曲面范畴中存在几何对应物，说明 étale 基本群的无限 pro-p 商现象可由几何构造实现。

Abstract: We give an example of a proper, regular, integral arithmetic surface over $\mathrm{Spec}\ \Z$ endowed with a section, and whose étale fundamental group admits an infinite pro-$2$ quotient. The construction is a geometric analog of the Golod--Shafarevich towers for rings of integers of number fields.

</details>


### [214] [When the BSD Real Period Remembers Elliptic Curves over $\mathbb{Q}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06015)
*Asuka Shiga*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究BSD猜想中不变量对椭圆曲线的"记忆"能力：实周期决定K-同源类，结合连通分支数与二次扭的实周期可在Q上决定椭圆曲线同构类，且有限精度即可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BSD猜想中的不变量（如实周期、Tate-Shafarevich群）是否足以唯一刻画椭圆曲线，是一个自然且基础的问题。作者希望给出肯定性答案。

Method: 设K为含实嵌入σ的数域。证明E在K上的实周期决定其K-同源类；进而用实周期、E(R)连通分支数及负平方自由D≡1 mod 4的二次扭E^D的实周期，在Q上确定E的同构类；并分析所需十进制精度。

Result: 实周期决定K-同源类；在Q上，实周期、连通分支数与二次扭实周期共同决定同构类；两结论在实周期仅知足够多小数位（精度依赖E和D）时仍成立；举例E: y^2=x^3+17x被Ω_E=Γ(1/4)^2/(2√π·17^{1/4})与Sha(E/Q)≅(Z/2)^2记忆。

Conclusion: BSD不变量对椭圆曲线具有强"记忆"性质，且这种决定关系在有限数值精度下依然稳健。

Abstract: We study the extent to which the invariants appearing in the Birch and Swinnerton-Dyer conjecture remember the elliptic curve, and we give several affirmative answers to this question. For example, the real period $Ω_E =\frac{Γ(1/4)^2}{2\sqrtπ\,17^{1/4}}$ and the Tate--Shafarevich group $ \Sha(E/\mathbb{Q})\cong \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}\times \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$ remember the elliptic curve $E: y^2=x^3+17x$. Let $K$ be a number field admitting a real embedding, and fix one such embedding $σ\colon K\hookrightarrow\mathbb{R}$. We first prove that, for an elliptic curve $E$ over $K$, the real period of $E$ attached to $σ$ determines the $K$-isogeny class of $E$. We then prove that an elliptic curve $E$ over $\mathbb{Q}$ is determined up to isomorphism over $\mathbb{Q}$ by its real period, the number of connected components of $E(\mathbb{R})$, and the real period of the quadratic twist $E^{D}$ by a negative square-free integer $D\equiv 1 \bmod 4$ coprime to the minimal discriminant of $E$. Moreover, over $\mathbb{Q}$, both statements already hold when the real periods are known only to sufficiently many decimal places, where the required precision depends on $E$ (and $D$).

</details>


### [215] [A Variant of the Fractional Gamma-regularized Zeta Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06136)
*Hunduma Legesse Geleta*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究了一种“分数阶 gamma-正则化 zeta 核”的变体函数，其中低阶不完全 gamma 函数出现在分子位置，并分析其解析性质、收敛性及与 Riemann zeta 函数的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有“分数阶超几何 zeta 函数”中不完全 gamma 函数位于分母，本文旨在研究其互补形式（分子中出现的变体），以探索其与 Riemann zeta 函数的关系、零自由区域及形变现象。

Method: 通过引入低阶不完全 gamma 函数位于分子的核函数，推导其作为 Riemann zeta 函数乘性修正的表示，并构造其 Dirichlet 型级数表示；与已知零自由区域及形变现象进行比较。

Result: 该函数可表示为一类 Riemann zeta 函数的形变，在特定极限下可插值到交错 zeta 函数，并给出了其级数表示及与已知零自由区域的对比。

Conclusion: 该变体函数定义了一种 Riemann zeta 函数的形变，并给出了 Dirichlet 型级数表示；未来可进一步研究其函数方程与渐近分析等扩展方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce and study a special function, referred to as a variant of the fractional gamma-regularized zeta kernel, in which the lower incomplete gamma function appears in the numerator, contrasting with the fractional hypergeometric zeta function, where it appears in the denominator. We investigate its analytic properties, convergence behavior, and its connection to the Riemann zeta function. A representation is derived in terms of a multiplicative modification of the Riemann zeta function, and comparisons are made with known zero-free regions and deformation phenomena. We demonstrate that this function defines a deformation of the Riemann zeta function, which interpolates to the alternating zeta function in a specific limit. Additionally, a Dirichlet-type series representation is provided. Potential extensions, including a functional equation and asymptotic analysis, are proposed for future work.

</details>


### [216] [Tower of coverings and polylog-size coverings of rational points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06166)
*Kenneth Chung Tak Chiu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明：数域上光滑不可约真簇若存在满足特定条件的有限étale覆盖塔，则其有界高度有理点可被多对数规模覆盖；并验证阿贝尔簇满足这些条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究数域上有界高度有理点的计数/覆盖问题，寻求用多对数规模（polylogarithmic-size）的覆盖来刻画有理点分布，推广或强化已有的覆盖型结果。

Method: 利用有限étale覆盖塔的构造，假设其满足若干条件（其中包括对有限étale覆盖扭曲的高度比较条件），由此导出多对数规模的覆盖；并针对阿贝尔簇证明这些条件成立。

Result: 证明了主定理：在所述条件下，光滑不可约真簇的有界高度有理点存在多对数规模覆盖；并证明阿贝尔簇满足这些条件，因而不变量结果适用于阿贝尔簇。

Conclusion: 覆盖型结果可归约为有限étale覆盖塔及其高度比较条件，阿贝尔簇是一类满足该框架的重要实例。

Abstract: We prove that a smooth irreducible proper variety over a number field admits polylogarithmic-size coverings of rational points of bounded height if it admits a tower of finite étale coverings satisfying several conditions. One of the conditions is a height comparison on twists of the finite étale covers. We prove that these conditions hold for abelian varieties.

</details>


### [217] [Theta Series with Half-integral Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06323)
*Riccardo Salvati Manni*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究奇正定幺模格上的半整数特征theta级数；偶数秩时得到奇数特征的非零theta级数，并着重讨论四的倍数秩，其中涉及的rational格互为整幺模邻格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨半整数特征theta级数在奇正定幺模格上的构造与性质，尤其在偶数秩情形下寻找具有奇数特征的非零theta级数。

Method: 将半整数特征theta级数附着于奇正定幺模格进行研究；针对四的倍数秩，借助相关有理格之间的整幺模邻格关系进行分析。

Result: 在偶数秩情形下，得到了具有奇数特征的非零theta级数。

Conclusion: 奇正定幺模格在偶数秩（特别是秩为4的倍数）时可产生奇数特征的非零半整数特征theta级数，且相关有理格表现为整幺模邻格。

Abstract: We study theta series with half-integral characteristics attached to odd positive-definite unimodular lattices. In even rank, we get non-vanishing theta series with odd characteristics.
  We emphasize ranks divisible by four, where the involved rational lattices are integral unimodular neighbors.

</details>


### [218] [A Bogomolov property for moduli spaces of polynomials over abelian extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06382)
*Geng-Rui Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明临界高度在整数环阿贝尔扩张上的小值集有限，特别地，度-d多项式的后临界有限有理点仅有限个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究MPoly^d上临界高度的小值分布，刻画后临界有限（PCF）点的有限性，推广算术动力系统中高度为零点的有限性结果到K^ab。

Method: 利用普适临界除子族、带显式轨道高度公式的 nef adelic 线丛、缠绕关系（intertwined relation）、局部分歧估计，以及 Ji–Song–Xie 的对应方法。

Result: 对任意数域K和d≥2，存在ε_{K,d}>0，使临界高度小于ε_{K,d}的K^ab-有理点集等于临界高度为零的点集，且该集合有限。

Conclusion: MPoly^d(K^ab)中后临界有限点只有有限多个，临界高度具有小值有限性/离散性。

Abstract: Let $d\geq2$ be an integer, and let $\operatorname{MPoly}^d$ be the moduli space of degree-$d$ polynomials. For every number field $K$, we prove that there exists $ε_{K,d}>0$ such that \[ \left\lbraceα\in\operatorname{MPoly}^d(K^{\mathrm{ab}})\colon h_{\mathrm{crit}}(α)<ε_{K,d}\right\rbrace=\left\lbraceα\in\operatorname{MPoly}^d(K^{\mathrm{ab}})\colon h_{\mathrm{crit}}(α)=0\right\rbrace \] is finite, where $h_{\mathrm{crit}}$ is the critical height. In particular, only finitely many $K^{\mathrm{ab}}$-rational points of $\operatorname{MPoly}^d$ are postcritically finite. The proof uses a universal critical-divisor family, a nef adelic line bundle with an explicit orbit-height formula, the intertwined relation, local ramification estimates, and the correspondence method of Ji--Song--Xie.

</details>


### [219] [How close can rational points get to a manifold?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06422)
*Victor Beresnevich,Shreyasi Datta*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明解析非退化流形附近有理点数量的下界，在τ≤3/(2m+1)范围内成立，改进了先前猜想范围τ≤1/m的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究解析非退化流形周围有理点分布的下界问题，验证启发式预测的下界，并突破先前猜想范围τ≤1/m的限制。

Method: 针对R^n中固定的解析非退化流形，考虑高度至多Q且距流形ε/Q内的有理点数量，设定ε≍Q^{-τ}，其中τ≤3/(2m+1)，m为流形余维数，通过解析数论方法证明下界。

Result: 证明了启发式预测的有理点数量下界，在τ≤3/(2m+1)范围内成立，超越了先前猜想的τ≤1/m范围，并改进了Schindler、Srivastava和Technau最新结果中τ≤3/(2n-1)的界限。

Conclusion: 该结果显著扩展了下界成立的τ范围，从τ≤1/m提升至τ≤3/(2m+1)，并优于近期相关研究，推动了对解析非退化流形附近有理点分布的理解。

Abstract: We prove the heuristically predicted lower bound for the number of rational points of height at most $Q$ lying within $ε/Q$ of a fixed analytic nondegenerate manifold in $\mathbb{R}^n$, provided that $ε\asymp Q^{-τ}$ for some $τ\leq 3/(2m+1)$, where $m$ is the codimension of the manifold. Our result establishes the lower bound well beyond the previously conjectured range $τ\leq 1/m$, and improves upon a recent result of Schindler, Srivastava, and Technau, who established the same lower bound for $τ\leq 3/(2n-1)$.

</details>


### [220] [Counting zero-sum subspaces for the multiplicative inverse function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06445)
*Kaimin Cheng*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明有限域上子空间逆元和 S(E)=0 的渐近计数估计，进而证实 Carlet 关于存在性的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限域 F_q（q=2^n）中 k 维 F_2-子空间 E 上元素逆元和 S(E)=sum_{x in E\\{0}} x^{-1} 是否为零。Carlet 猜想：对每个 3<=k<=n-3 都存在这样的 E 使 S(E)=0，需要给出证明。

Method: 令 N_{n,k} 为满足 S(E)=0 的 k 维子空间个数。对 k>=3 且 n>=2k+1，证明 N_{n,k} 与高斯二项系数 [n choose k]_2 的偏差估计：|2^n N_{n,k}/[n choose k]_2 - 1| < 2^{r^2+r} 2^{n(1-r/2)}，其中 r=floor((k-1)/2)。由此得到 N_{n,k} ~ 2^{-n}[n choose k]_2（n->无穷，对 n/3<k<=(n-1)/2 一致）。结合低维情形、k 与 n-k 的对称性以及中维子域构造，覆盖所有 3<=k<=n-3。

Result: 当 k>=3、n>=2k+1 时，得到 N_{n,k} 相对高斯二项系数的指数型偏差上界；n->无穷时 N_{n,k} ~ 2^{-n}[n choose k]_2 对 n/3<k<=(n-1)/2 一致成立。结合其他情形证明了对所有 3<=k<=n-3 存在 E 使 S(E)=0。

Conclusion: 证实了 Carlet 的猜想：对每个 3<=k<=n-3，均存在 k 维 F_2-子空间 E 使得 S(E)=0。

Abstract: Let $q=2^n$ with $n\ge6$, and let $\mathbb{F}_q$ be the finite field with $q$ elements. For a subspace $E$ of $\mathbb{F}_q$, define \[ S(E)=\sum_{x\in E\setminus\{0\}}x^{-1}.\] Let $N_{n,k}$ denote the number of $k$-dimensional $\mathbb{F}_2$-subspaces $E$ for which $S(E)=0$. We prove that, if $k\ge3$ and $n\ge2k+1$, then \[ \left|\frac{2^nN_{n,k}}{\genfrac{[}{]}{0pt}{}{n}{k}_{2}}-1\right|<2^{r^2+r}2^{n(1-r/2)},\] where $r=\lfloor\frac{k-1}{2}\rfloor$, and $\genfrac{[}{]}{0pt}{}{n}{k}_{2}$ is the Gaussian binomial coefficient. In particular, \[ N_{n,k}\sim2^{-n}\genfrac{[}{]}{0pt}{}{n}{k}_{2}\quad (n\to\infty)\] uniformly for $n/3<k\le(n-1)/2$. Combined with the known low-dimensional cases and the symmetry between dimensions $k$ and $n-k$, and the elementary middle-dimensional subfield construction, this proves a conjecture of Carlet: for every $3\le k\le n-3$, there exists a $k$-dimensional $\mathbb{F}_2$-subspace $E$ such that $S(E)=0$.

</details>


### [221] [Golod--Shafarevich for arithmetic surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06461)
*Timo Keller,Carlo Pagano*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 构造整数上的 Golod–Shafarevich 塔，得到几何 étale 基本群无限的算术曲面及有理点完全分裂的曲线塔，回答了 Bost–Charles、Ihara、Frey–Kani–Völklein 及 Venkatesh 的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是回答若干公开问题：Bost–Charles 关于存在带截面且几何 étale 基本群无限的算术曲面；Ihara 与 Frey–Kani–Völklein 关于有理曲线上有理点完全分裂的无限几何 étale 塔；以及 Venkatesh（PCMI 2022）关于亏格趋于无穷而对数导子线性增长的曲线序列。

Method: 方法是在整数上构造 Golod–Shafarevich 塔的算术曲面，并取一般纤维（generic fibres），从而把曲面塔的结果传递到有理数域上的曲线塔。

Result: 获得带截面且几何 étale 基本群无限的算术曲面；得到有理数上几何 étale 塔无限的曲线且其中一有理点完全分裂；并构造出亏格趋于无穷、对数导子随亏格线性增长的曲线序列。

Conclusion: 这些构造统一地解决了上述多个公开问题，说明利用 Golod–Shafarevich 塔与一般纤维取法可有效控制算术曲面及曲线塔的群论与不变量性质。

Abstract: We construct Golod--Shafarevich towers of arithmetic surfaces over the integers. In particular, we obtain an arithmetic surface with a section and infinite geometric etale fundamental group, answering a question raised by Bost and Charles about the existence of such surfaces. Taking generic fibres gives curves over the rationals with infinite geometric etale towers in which a rational point splits completely. This answers questions posed by Ihara and by Frey, Kani and Völklein. Taking the generic fibres of the tower, we also obtain sequence of curves over the rationals whose genera goes to infinity and whose logarithmic conductors grows no more than linearly in the genera, answering a question asked by Venkatesh at PCMI in 2022.
  The authors worked together on this problem since 2022 and developed a general strategy. They were able to bring it to fruit only after a collaboration with GPT5.6 Sol, Fable and Aletheia a Gemini-powered internal agent developed at Google DeepMind.

</details>


### [222] [Chowla Conjecture for the truncated Möbius function over $\mathbb{F}_q[t]$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06471)
*Abhirup Chatterjee*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在固定 $q$ 的函数域情形下，给出了截断 Möbius 函数 $\mu_M$ 的两点 Chowla 猜想的解析证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chowla 猜想是数论中的核心问题之一，研究 Möbius 函数在平移下的相关性。函数域情形因其几何与代数结构，常为整数情形提供可借鉴的思路。作者旨在固定 $q$ 设定下验证截断 Möbius 函数的两点 Chowla 猜想。

Method: 将问题重述为研究 $(\omega_M,\omega_M\circ T_a)$ 的分布；借助 Kubilius 模型，针对小实数对 $(u,v)$，以特征函数形式建立一致的双变量 Erdős-Kac 定理；为此还发展并运用了多项式的纯 Brun 筛法。

Result: 建立了截断 Möbius 函数的两点 Chowla 猜想的解析证明，并得到一致的双变量 Erdős-Kac 定理（特征函数形式），同时构造了适用于多项式的纯 Brun 筛。

Conclusion: 通过概率模型（Kubilius 模型）与筛法工具，在函数域固定 $q$ 情形下证实了两点 Chowla 猜想，为相关解析数论问题提供了新工具与思路。

Abstract: In this paper we give an analytic proof of the Two-point Chowla conjecture for the truncated Möbius function $μ_M$ for the function field case in the fixed $q$ setting. We reformulate the problem into studying the distribution of $(ω_M,ω_M\circ T_a)$. Thus we develop a uniform bivariate Erdős-Kac theorem in terms of the characteristic function for small pair of real numbers $(u,v)$ via the Kubilius model. For this purpose we also develop and employ the pure Brun sieve for polynomials in this paper.

</details>


### [223] [Degeneracy loci and unlikely intersections in abelian schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06518)
*Fabrizio Barroero,Laura Capuano,Tangli Ge,Francesco Tropeano*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究数域上阿贝尔概型的子簇与低维平坦群子概型的交性，证明在 t-非退化性之外该交集至多有限，并给出相对 Mordell–Lang 猜想等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Mordell–Lang 与 Pila–Zannier 方法处理的是固定阿贝尔簇上子簇与子群（或有限秩群）的交集。本文关注随参数变化的相对情形：在数域上的阿贝尔概型 A→S 中，子簇 X 与相对维数小于 t 的平坦群子概型的交，希望刻画其非 Zariski 稠密性，并将结论推广到族的层次。

Method: 遵循 Pila–Zannier 策略：结合有界高度定理与一致的大 Galois 轨道估计，使用 o-minimal 点计数以及混合 Ax–Schanuel。算术输入为自同态关系复杂度的显式上界，由几何数论和阿贝尔簇的高度估计得到。

Result: 证明对任意整数 t>0，在 X 的 t-退化轨迹之外，至多有限个点落在相对维数小于 t 的平坦群子概型中；特别地，当 X 为 t-非退化时该交集不 Zariski 稠密。

Conclusion: 提出相对 Mordell–Lang 猜想；对某些阿贝尔概型幂中的截面像证明该猜想；在变动与维数假设下，证明截面群的特殊化在适当闭子集外是单射。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{A}\to S$ be an abelian scheme over a normal quasi-projective variety over a number field, and let $\mathcal{X}\subseteq\mathcal{A}$ be a positive-dimensional subvariety. For every integer $t>0$, we prove that, outside the $t$-degeneracy locus of $\mathcal{X}$, at most finitely many points lie in flat group subschemes of relative dimension less than $t$. In particular, this intersection is not Zariski dense whenever $\mathcal{X}$ is $t$-nondegenerate. The proof combines a bounded height theorem with uniform large Galois orbit estimates, o-minimal point counting, and mixed Ax--Schanuel, following the Pila--Zannier strategy. The arithmetic input is an explicit bound for the complexity of endomorphism relations, obtained from geometry of numbers and height estimates for abelian varieties. As applications, we formulate a relative Mordell--Lang conjecture, prove it for section images in powers of certain abelian schemes, and establish injectivity of specialization of the group of sections outside a proper closed subset under variation and dimension hypotheses.

</details>


### [224] [$7$-adic Galois representations of elliptic curves over the rationals via Kummer descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06521)
*Santiago Arango-Piñeros,David Zureick-Brown*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明剩余两条亏格9、水平49的模曲线无非CM有理点，从而完成了Q上非CM椭圆曲线7-adic Galois像的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rouse–Sutherland–Zureick-Brown及Furio–Lombardo的工作已将Q上非CM椭圆曲线的7-adic Galois像分类问题化归到少数剩余情形，其中水平49的两条亏格9模曲线尚未解决；这一遗留情形阻塞了Mazur Program B中7-adic部分的最终完成。

Method: 对Furio–Lombardo的超椭圆方程F(x,y)=k w^7在其未处理的7|k情形上作Kummer下降。相应覆盖曲线是Fermat septicity的twist，并映射到Klein四次曲线的twist；通过7-adic计算证明由任一有理解所对应的twist为平凡的，从而将有理点问题归约到Klein四次曲线，后者由Hurwitz归结为指数7的Fermat大定理（Lamé证明）。

Result: 所考虑的两条亏格9、水平49模曲线均无(非CM)有理点；结合Furio–Lombardo关于X_ns^+(49)的定理，完全确定了Q上非CM椭圆曲线的7-adic Galois像。

Conclusion: Mazur Program B的7-adic部分最后一个开放情形最终依赖并归结为指数7的Fermat大定理。

Abstract: We show that the two modular curves of level $49$ and genus $9$ left open by
  the work of Rouse, Sutherland and Zureick-Brown, and of Furio and Lombardo,
  have no non-CM rational points. Together with the theorem of Furio and
  Lombardo on $X_{ns}^{+}(49)$, this completes the classification of the
  $7$-adic images of Galois of non-CM elliptic curves over $Q$. The proof is a
  Kummer descent on the superelliptic equations $F(x,y) = k\,w^{7}$ of Furio
  and Lombardo, in the cases $7 \mid k$ that they left open. The covering
  curves are twists of the Fermat septic, they map to twists of the Klein
  quartic, and a $7$-adic computation shows that the twist attached to a
  solution is trivial. The rational points of the Klein quartic were
  determined by Hurwitz, who reduced the question to Fermat's Last Theorem for
  exponent $7$, proved by Lamé. Thus, the last open case of the
  $7$-adic part of Mazur's Program B rests on Fermat's Last Theorem
  for exponent $7$.

</details>


### [225] [The linear independence of $1$, $ζ(3)$, and $ζ(4)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06838)
*Li Lai*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 结合Fauzan的行列式方法与经典zeta值联立逼近，证明了1、ζ(3)、ζ(4)在Q上线性无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fauzan近期证明了ζ(5)的无理性，本文旨在借鉴其行列式方法并扩展至多个zeta值之间的线性无关性研究。

Method: 将Fauzan的行列式方法与Zudilin及Dauguet–Zudilin研究的经典zeta值联立逼近相结合。

Result: 证明了1、ζ(3)与ζ(4)在Q上线性无关。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了行列式方法在多重zeta值线性无关性证明中的有效性。

Abstract: In a recent breakthrough, Fauzan proved the irrationality of $ζ(5)$. In this paper, we combine Fauzan's determinant method with classical simultaneous approximations of zeta values studied by Zudilin and Dauguet--Zudilin to prove the linear independence of $1$, $ζ(3)$, and $ζ(4)$ over $\mathbb{Q}$.

</details>


### [226] [Dyadic Structure of the Two-Block Odd Partition Function and Theta-Weighted Congruences for $pod(n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05020)
*Mircea Merca*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究两分块奇分区函数 \(a(n)\) 的算术性质，通过按两部分尺寸的 \(2\)-进赋值进行带符号分解，证明了结构关系 \(a(2^km)=a(m)+(2^{k-1}-1)\sigma(m)\)，并由此得到一系列同余式及模奇素数的归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: \(a(n)\) 是恰由两个不同部件尺寸、每个出现奇数次构成的分区的带符号计数，其结构关系 \(a(2^km)=a(m)+(2^{k-1}-1)\sigma(m)\) 暗示存在深层的生成函数与 theta 函数机制。作者希望给出该关系的结构性解释，并将其推广以导出新的同余式与模奇素数的统一归约。

Method: 将底层表示按两个部件尺寸的 \(2\)-进赋值分离，得到带符号分解，并用两个经典 theta 函数求值完成论证。利用涉及 \(\operatorname{pod}(n)\) 的生成函数因子分解，对相关辅助级数建立模每个奇素数的一致归约，得到以 theta 函数加权的 \(\operatorname{pod}(n)\) 求和。

Result: 证明了 \(a(2^km)=a(m)+(2^{k-1}-1)\sigma(m)\)（\(m\) 为奇数，\(k\ge1\)），结合已有算术公式得到沿二进进列 \(2^k(4n+3)\) 的同余式，以及 \(a(2^\alpha3^\beta(12n+11))\equiv0\pmod3\) 和 \(a(2^\alpha(18n+15))\equiv0\pmod3\)。此外，theta 加权求和与 \(a(n)\) 同余，可用除数和函数及模 \(4\) 非主 Dirichlet 特征的字符除数和显式求值，作为特例得到模 \(3\)、\(5\)、\(13\) 的无限族加权同余式。

Conclusion: 按 \(2\)-进赋值的带符号分解为 \(a(n)\) 的结构关系提供了机制性解释，并连同生成函数因子分解统一处理了模奇素数的归约，产生了沿二进进列及多种模数的无限族同余式。

Abstract: The two-block odd partition function \(a(n)\) is the signed enumeration of partitions into exactly two distinct part sizes, each occurring an odd number of times. By separating the underlying representations according to the \(2\)-adic valuations of the two part sizes, we obtain a signed decomposition, completed by two classical theta-function evaluations, which explains the structural relation \(a(2^km)=a(m)+(2^{k-1}-1)σ(m)\), where \(m\) is odd, \(k\ge1\), and \(σ(m)\) is the sum-of-divisors function. Combined with a previously established arithmetic formula for \(a(n)\), this relation yields congruences along the dyadic progressions \(2^k(4n+3)\), as well as the families \(a\!\left(2^\alpha3^β(12n+11)\right)\equiv0\pmod3\) and \(a\!\left(2^α(18n+15)\right)\equiv0\pmod3\). We also use a factorization of the generating function of \(a(n)\) involving \(\operatorname{pod}(n)\), the number of partitions in which odd parts are distinct and even parts are unrestricted, to establish a uniform reduction modulo every odd prime of the associated auxiliary series. The resulting theta-weighted sums involving \(\operatorname{pod}(n)\) are congruent to \(a(n)\) and therefore admit explicit evaluations involving the sum-of-divisors function and the character divisor sum associated with the nonprincipal Dirichlet character modulo \(4\). As special cases, we obtain infinite families of weighted congruences modulo \(3\), \(5\), and \(13\).

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Block numerical ranges for linear relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04431)
*Rabeb Aydi,Hannes Gernandt,Omaima Kchaou,Carsten Trunk,Mitsuru Wilson*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文将块数值域概念从算子推广到线性关系，通过值域表示方法定义块数值域，建立基本性质及谱包围结果，并应用于端口-哈密顿微分代数方程的左半平面谱界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性关系的谱性质与微分代数方程的稳定性密切相关，因此可靠的谱包围（spectral enclosure）具有重要价值。经典数值域是凸的，无法检测谱中的间隙（gaps），需要引入非凸的块数值域以获得更精细的谱信息。

Method: 通过值域表示（range representation）将已有的块数值域概念从算子推广到线性关系，建立该新概念的基本性质，并推导相应的谱包围结果；最后用数值算例比较不同构造下的块数值域。

Result: 所得块数值域可能非凸，因而能够检测谱中的间隙；建立了基本性质并得到谱包围定理；数值算例对比了不同构造的块数值域；作为应用，给出了一类端口-哈密顿微分代数方程的显式左半平面谱界。

Conclusion: 块数值域可成功推广到线性关系，提供了比经典数值域更强的谱定位能力，并在端口-哈密顿微分代数方程的稳定性分析中获得具体应用。

Abstract: Linear relations are multivalued generalizations of linear operators whose spectral properties are closely linked to the stability of differential-algebraic equations. Consequently, reliable spectral enclosures are of considerable interest. In this note, we extend the established concept of the block numerical range from operators to linear relations by means of a range representation. Unlike the classical numerical range, the resulting block numerical range may be nonconvex and can therefore detect gaps in the spectrum. We establish fundamental properties of this new notion and derive corresponding spectral enclosure results. Finally, we illustrate the theory with a numerical example comparing the block numerical ranges obtained from different constructions. As an application, we obtain an explicit left-half-plane spectral bound for a class of port-Hamiltonian DAEs.

</details>


### [228] [Pólya's conjecture for Neumann eigenvalues of annuli](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05146)
*Guowei Dai,Yingxin Sun*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文证明了欧氏圆环域上每一个Neumann特征值的Pólya不等式。证明通过径向作用量恒等式保留精确的Weyl面积项，并结合变分界与一致的Bessel相位及角度和估计。当环宽不超过内半径四分之一时证明完全解析；对任意半径，显式高频界与小孔界将问题约化为紧参数区域，并用在整个参数矩形上有效的精确有理与向外区间证书加以验证。经转移原理，结果推广至环状乘积与全等环状扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pólya猜想（Weyl渐近的逐特征值不等式）在欧氏圆环域上尚未对全部Neumann特征值成立。环域的曲率与内外边界耦合使精确Weyl面积项难以保留，且高频与薄环、小孔等极端几何情形需分别处理，因此需要一套能覆盖任意半径比、并在整个参数区域上严格验证的方法。

Method: 核心是保持精确Weyl面积项的径向作用量恒等式；将变分上界与一致的Bessel相位估计、角度和估计相结合。环宽≤内半径1/4时给出纯解析证明；对一般半径，构造显式高频界与小孔界，把问题归约为紧参数区域，再用精确有理证书与向外区间证书（在整块参数矩形上有效）完成验证。最后通过既有的转移原理推广到环状乘积与全等环状扇区。

Result: 对任意欧氏圆环域的所有Neumann特征值证明了Pólya不等式；即每个Neumann特征值不低于对应的精确Weyl面积项。证明覆盖任意内外半径比，并在紧参数区域上获得严格的多项式/区间验证。由此得到环状乘积与全等环状扇区的Neumann Pólya不等式。

Conclusion: 圆环域的Neumann Pólya不等式成立，且证明可在薄环情形完全解析、在任意半径下借助区间证书严格闭合；结合转移原理，结论进一步适用于环状乘积与全等环状扇区，为Pólya猜想的对称域情形提供了完整证据。

Abstract: We prove Pólya's inequality for every Neumann eigenvalue of every Euclidean circular annulus. The proof preserves the exact Weyl area term through a radial action identity and combines variational bounds with uniform Bessel-phase and angular-sum estimates. It is entirely analytic when the width is at most one quarter of the inner radius. For arbitrary radii, explicit high-frequency and small-hole bounds reduce the problem to a compact parameter region, which is verified by exact rational and outward interval certificates valid on whole parameter rectangles. Established transfer principles yield the Neumann inequality for annular products and congruent annular sectors.

</details>


### [229] [Planar Dirichlet eigenvalue bounds from a trace identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05636)
*Romain Speciel*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文对平面有界开集上 Dirichlet Laplacian 的特征值 λ_k 给出了渐近最优的下界，并由此推出 λ_k ≥ (2/3)·4πk/A，即 Pólya 猜想界的三分之二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pólya 曾猜想平面上面积为 A 的区域，其 Dirichlet Laplacian 特征值满足 λ_k ≥ 4πk/A。该猜想长期未完全解决，因此寻求渐近最优的下界、并尽可能逼近该猜想常数具有重要价值。

Method: 证明使用了一个关于某个乘法算子与其伴随算子之间 commutator 的迹恒等式（trace identity）。

Result: 得到了 λ_k 的渐近正确（asymptotically sharp）下界；作为推论，对每个 k 成立 λ_k ≥ (2/3)·4πk/A。

Conclusion: 该结果为 Pólya 猜想提供了目前常数意义上的强下界，达到猜想常数的 2/3，且所用方法（commutator 迹恒等式）可望进一步推进该问题。

Abstract: We prove an asymptotically sharp lower bound for the eigenvalues $λ_k$ of the Dirichlet Laplacian on bounded open sets in the plane with area $A>0$. The proof employs a trace identity for the commutator between a certain multiplication operator and its adjoint. As a corollary, we obtain $(2/3)\cdot 4πk/A\leq λ_k$ for every $k$, which is two thirds of the bound conjectured by Pólya.

</details>


<div id='math.FA'></div>

# math.FA [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [An Extremal Formula for Elliptical Width and the Bourin--Lee Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04377)
*Mohammad Sababheh*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文解决了 Bourin 与 Lee 关于正分块矩阵范数不等式的猜想，证明最优范数缺陷恰等于椭圆宽度 δ₂(X)，且通用范数不等式成立当且仅当 X 本质 Hermitian，并确定了正规非对角块特征值估计中半径常数的尖锐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bourin 与 Lee 已证明正分块矩阵满足 ‖M‖ ≤ ‖A+B‖ + δ₂(X)，并猜想：对固定非对角块 X，若 ‖M‖ ≤ ‖A+B‖ 对所有正分块矩阵成立，则 X 必为本质 Hermitian。该猜想刻画了范数不等式的等式边界，但其最优常数与必要性此前未被确定。

Method: 定义 δ₂(X) 为所有二维压缩 X_S 的数值域 W(X_S) 中最大圆盘直径的上确界。通过上确界构造正面方向：将 X 的任意二维压缩提升为一族显式正分块矩阵，得到误差为 O(t⁻¹) 的定量双侧估计；反面方向结合已有的 ‖M‖ ≤ ‖A+B‖ + δ₂(X) 得精确公式。对正规非对角块情形，构造显式 3×3 例子并由直和放大推广到各特征值层级。

Result: 证得精确公式 sup{‖M‖ − ‖A+B‖ : M ≥ 0} = δ₂(X)，即椭圆宽度恰为固定非对角块对应的最优范数缺陷；由此推出通用范数不等式 ‖M‖ ≤ ‖A+B‖ 成立当且仅当 X 本质 Hermitian，从而证明 Conjecture 3.3。此外构造 3×3 正规例子说明正规非对角块特征值估计中的半径常数在首特征值层级最优，并经直和放大证明其对所有层级 j ≥ 0 均尖锐。

Conclusion: 椭圆宽度 δ₂(X) 完全刻画了正分块矩阵相对 ‖A+B‖ 的最优范数缺陷，解决了 Bourin–Lee 猜想，并确定了相关正规非对角块特征值估计中半径常数在所有特征值层级的尖锐性。

Abstract: Let \(X\in M_n(\mathbb C)\), \(n\geq2\), and let
  \[
  δ_2(X)
  =
  \sup_{\dim S=2}δ(X_S),
  \]
  where \(X_S\) denotes the compression of \(X\) to \(S\), and
  \(δ(X_S)\) is the diameter of the largest disk contained in
  the numerical range \(W(X_S)\). Bourin and Lee proved that every
  positive block matrix
  \[
  M=
  \begin{pmatrix}
  A&X
  X^*&B
  \end{pmatrix}
  \]
  satisfies
  \[
  \norm{M}\leq\norm{A+B}+δ_2(X).
  \]
  In the same work, they conjectured that the inequality
  \[
  \norm{M}\leq\norm{A+B}
  \]
  for every positive block matrix with prescribed off-diagonal block
  \(X\) characterizes essentially Hermitian matrices.
  We resolve this conjecture by proving the stronger exact formula
  \[
  \sup_{\left(\begin{smallmatrix}A&X\\X^*&B\end{smallmatrix}\right)\geq0}
  \left\{
  \norm{\begin{pmatrix}A&X\\X^*&B\end{pmatrix}}
  -\norm{A+B}
  \right\}
  =
  δ_2(X).
  \]
  Thus the elliptical width is precisely the optimal norm defect
  associated with a fixed off-diagonal block. The reverse inequality
  is obtained by lifting an arbitrary two-dimensional compression
  of \(X\) to an explicit family of positive block matrices, yielding
  quantitative two-sided estimates with error of order \(t^{-1}\).
  As a consequence, the norm inequality above holds universally
  if and only if \(X\) is essentially Hermitian, proving Conjecture 3.3
  of Bourin and Lee. We also construct an explicit \(3\times3\)
  normal example showing that the radius constant in their
  normal-off-diagonal eigenvalue estimate is optimal at the leading
  eigenvalue level. By direct-sum amplification of this construction,
  we further prove that the same radius constant is sharp at every
  eigenvalue level \(j\geq0\), thereby resolving their corresponding
  sharpness question.

</details>


### [231] [A New insight into the Ambrosio--Reshetnyak approach to Sobolev maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04384)
*Alexander Tyulenev*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 在满足局部加倍与弱(1,p)-Poincaré不等式的度量测度空间上，Borel映射u属于Ambrosio–Reshetnyak–Sobolev类W_p^1(X,Y)当且仅当对每个1-Lipschitz函数h:Y→R，复合h∘u∈W_p^1(X)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画取值于度量空间Y的Sobolev映射，通常依赖较难验证的能量泛函定义；作者希望给出一个仅通过实值Sobolev空间并用Lipschitz检验函数即可等价判定的特征，从而简化W_p^1(X,Y)的辨识与后续分析。

Method: 设X为度量测度空间，μ一致局部加倍且支持弱局部(1,p)-Poincaré不等式；Y为完备带基点度量空间。通过1-Lipschitz函数h:Y→R作检验，将u∈W_p^1(X,Y)的判定归结为标量函数h∘u∈W_p^1(X)，并证明该刻画与u模μ-a.e.相等意义下的等价类相对应。

Result: 证明了Borel映射u:X→Y（模μ-a.e.相等意义下）属于Ambrosio–Reshetnyak–Sobolev类W_p^1(X,Y)的充要条件是：对每个1-Lipschitz函数h:Y→R，复合函数h∘u属于Sobolev空间W_p^1(X)。

Conclusion: 给出了度量空间-valued Sobolev映射的一个简洁、纯Lipschitz检验的等价特征，将向量（度量空间）值情形归约到实值情形；在弱局部(1,p)-Poincaré与一致局部加倍假设下成立。

Abstract: Given $p \in (1,\infty)$, let $\operatorname{X}=(\operatorname{X},ρ,μ)$ be a metric measure space such that the measure $μ$ is uniformly locally doubling and $\operatorname{X}$ supports a weak local $(1,p)$-Poincaré inequality. Let $(\operatorname{Y},\operatorname{d},\underline{y})$ be a complete pointed metric space. We prove that the equivalence class of a Borel map $ u:\operatorname{X} \to \operatorname{Y}$ (modulo coincidence $μ$-a.e.) belongs to the Ambrosio--Reshetnyak--Sobolev class $W_{p}^{1}(\operatorname{X},\operatorname{Y})$ if and only if $h \circ u$ belongs to the Sobolev space $W_{p}^{1}(\operatorname{X})$ for every $1$-Lipschitz function $h:\operatorname{Y} \to \mathbb{R}$.

</details>


### [232] [Effective Dimensions in Grothendieck--Hölder Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04398)
*Joedson Santos,Diana Serrano-Rodríguez*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究将Grothendieck不等式中的Hilbert空间向量替换为ℓ_p^r和ℓ_{p'}^r向量后的最优常数,证明普适维数损失由有效维数d=min{r,m,n}决定,最优常数上下界(忽略无维因子)为d^{|1/p-1/2|}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Grothendieck不等式在Hilbert空间向量情形成立,但将其向量替换为ℓ_p^r与ℓ_{p'}^r空间向量时,最优常数如何随维数变化尚不清楚;作者希望刻画维数依赖的精确规律,区分普适幂律与矩形几何的精细端点信息。

Method: 通过逐点Hilbert化稳定化G_2^{(r)}(A)=G_2^{(d)}(A),结合Lewis欧氏扭曲估计得到有效维数Grothendieck–Hölder上界;在p=∞时证明精确压缩公式Γ_∞^K(r;m,n)=ρ(ℓ_1^m(K),ℓ_1^{min{r,n}}(K));复域上利用Hilbert问题与精确矩形端点间的插值得到上界;并分析p→2时的有界性阈值|1/p-1/2|log d。

Result: 最优常数由d^{|1/p-1/2|}上下界控制(相差无维因子);得到p=∞的精确压缩公式及全Hölder尺度的端点敏感下界;复域上插值给出相应上界;p→2的阈值由|1/p-1/2|log d决定;复二行矩阵的端点张量比为显式,一般矩形估计给出1≤p≤∞的定量双侧界。

Conclusion: 有效维数决定普适指数,而张量范数端点保留额外的纵横比信息;该框架统一刻画了ℓ_p型Grothendieck不等式从Hilbert情形到矩形端点的维数依赖行为。

Abstract: What becomes of Grothendieck's inequality when its Hilbert-space vectors are replaced by vectors in $\ell_p^r$ and $\ell_{p'}^r$? For an $m\times n$ matrix, we show that the universal dimensional loss is governed not by $r$ alone, but by the effective dimension \[ d=\min\{r,m,n\}. \] More precisely, the optimal constant is bounded above and below, up to dimension-free factors, by \[ d^{|1/p-1/2|}. \] The upper bound rests on a pointwise Hilbertian stabilization: for every matrix $A$, \[ G_2^{(r)}(A)=G_2^{(d)}(A). \] Combining this stabilization with Lewis's Euclidean distortion estimate gives the effective-dimensional Grothendieck--Hölder bound.
  Beyond the universal power law, we retain the finer rectangular geometry. At $p=\infty$ we prove the exact compression formula \[ Γ_\infty^\K(r;m,n) =
ρ\!\left( \ell_1^m(\K), \ell_1^{\min\{r,n\}}(\K) \right), \] and derive from it endpoint-sensitive lower bounds throughout the full Hölder scale. Over the complex field, interpolation between the Hilbertian problem and the exact rectangular endpoints yields corresponding upper bounds. Thus the effective dimension determines the universal exponent, whereas the tensor-norm endpoint retains additional aspect-ratio information.
  We determine the boundedness threshold when $p$ approaches $2$: it is governed by \[ |1/p-1/2|\log d. \] For complex two-row matrices, the endpoint tensor ratios are explicit, and the general rectangular estimates give quantitative two-sided bounds for every $1\le p\le\infty$.

</details>


### [233] [Simultaneous Williamson's normal form for positive operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04583)
*Rudra Kamat,Anmary Tonny*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 证明了实辛Hilbert空间上两个辛交换正算子(A,B)的同时Williamson标准形，在自然交换条件下存在（一般无界的）算子Φ同时对角化A、B并将J映为标准辛算子，将Williamson标准形推广到无穷维并给出(A,B)的完整谱不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Williamson标准形仅适用于有限维辛空间的单个正算子，而无穷维辛Hilbert空间上多个辛交换算子的同时标准化及其谱分类问题尚未解决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在自然交换条件下，构造一个（一般为无界的）算子Φ，使得 A = Φ^t M_A Φ、B = Φ^t M_B Φ，并将辛结构J变换为规范辛算子，从而在辛框架下同时对角化A与B。

Result: 得到了两个辛交换正算子的同时Williamson标准形；该Φ可将A、B同时对角化并将J约化为规范形式，由此获得了算子对(A,B)的完整谱不变量。

Conclusion: 该工作将Williamson标准形从有限维推广至无穷维辛Hilbert空间，并为一对辛交换正算子提供了完整的谱分类工具。

Abstract: We prove a simultaneous Williamson's normal form for two symplectically commuting positive operators $A, B$ on a real symplectic Hilbert space $(\mathbf{H}, J)$. Under a natural commutativity condition, there exists a (generally unbounded) operator $Φ$ that simultaneously diagonalizes $A$ and $B$ in the symplectic setting as $A = Φ^t M_A Φ$, $B = Φ^t M_B Φ$, and maps $J$ to a canonical symplectic operator. The result generalizes Williamson's normal form to infinite dimensions and yields a complete spectral invariant for the pair $(A,B)$.

</details>


### [234] [A relaxation to the post-composition approach to metric-valued Sobolev maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04435)
*Roman D. Oleinik*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究从度量测度空间到度量空间的Sobolev映射的定义，证明在源空间有限Hausdorff维数且满足Sobolev-to-Luzin-Lipschitz性质，或目标空间有限Hausdorff维数的条件下，Ambrosio-Reshetnyak型Sobolev映射定义中仅需"定性"检验即可，无需附加"定量"检验，从而推广了已有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于后复合（post-composition）方法的Sobolev映射定义通常包含"定性"与"定量"两部分检验，验证繁琐。作者希望找到在何种空间假设下，可仅凭较弱的"定性"部分即可判定映射为Sobolev映射，从而简化定义并推广既有成果。

Method: 采用Ambrosio-Reshetnyak的后复合定义框架，在关于度量测度空间与度量空间的透明假设下，建立Sobolev映射定义的一个等价重构，该重构显著弱于原始定义。核心手法是证明在某些奇异情形条件下，"定量"检验可由"定性"检验自动推出。

Result: 证明该现象在以下任一条件成立时出现：源空间具有有限Hausdorff维数且满足Sobolev-to-Luzin-Lipschitz性质，或目标空间具有有限Hausdorff维数。此时判定Sobolev映射只需满足"定性"部分。

Conclusion: 所得等价弱化定义依赖于空间本身的奇异性质假设，显著推广了此前关于该主题的多项成果，为度量测度空间上Sobolev映射的判定提供了更简洁的准则。

Abstract: We establish that the definition of Sobolev mappings from metric measure spaces to metric spaces relying on the post-composition approach of Ambrosio-Reshetnyak admits, under certain transparent assumptions on the spaces, an equivalent reformulation that is substantially weaker than the principal version. In detail, the corresponding definition requires for maps of such a kind to be called Sobolev to meet first a ''qualitative'' test and then a ''quantitative'' test. At the same time, our result allows one to draw the relevant conclusion solely based on the ''qualitative'' part. Remarkably, the conditions on the spaces sufficient for this to hold fit entirely into the singular flavor of the subject. More explicitly, such a phenomenon emerges if either the source space is of finite Hausdorff dimension and satisfies the Sobolev-to-Luzin-Lipschitz property or the target space is of finite Hausdorff dimension. All this provides a significant generalization of several earlier achievements on the topic.

</details>


### [235] [Sharp Hardy inequalities for the Vladimirov--Taibleson operator on $\mathbb{Q}_p^n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04463)
*Yaojia Sun*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文在 $\mathbb{Q}_p^n$ 上证明了三类形式的尖锐 Hardy 不等式：Vladimirov–Taibleson 算子二次型、其分数阶导数的 $L^q$ 范数、以及非线性差分能量，并确定了各自的精确常数、极小化序列与等号情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 $p$-进欧氏空间上建立与欧氏空间 Hardy 不等式相对应的尖锐结果，统一处理二次型、$L^q$ 与非线性差分能量三类泛函，并厘清参数适用范围（如去掉 $s<1$ 的限制）。

Method: 利用自对偶 Fourier 归一化；通过加权 Schur 估计确定加权 Riesz 势 $\|\boldsymbol{x}\|_p^{-s}I_s$ 的精确 $L^q$ 范数；采用两点 Picone 不等式与壳层分解处理 Gagliardo 型能量；用临界幂的环状截断构造极小化序列。

Result: 对 $0<α<n$，二次型尖锐常数为 $[(1-p^{-(n-α)/2})/(1-p^{-(n+α)/2})]^2$，余项由基态表示给出；对 $1<q<\infty$、$0<s<n/q$，尖锐线性 Hardy 常数为 $[\Gamma_{p}^{(n)}(n/q'+s)/\Gamma_{p}^{(n)}(n/q')]^q$；核为 $\|\boldsymbol{x}-\boldsymbol{y}\|_p^{-n-sq}$ 的 Gagliardo 型能量尖锐常数为 $2(1-p^{-n})\sum_{m\ge1}p^{-sqm}(1-p^{-(n/q-s)m})^q$。$\mathbb{Q}_p^n$ 上的一致局部常性保证差分能量对每个 Bruhat–Schwartz 函数与任意 $s>0$ 有限。

Conclusion: 当 $q=2$ 时两个 $L^q$ 不等式退化为二次型不等式；在各自函数类中等号仅由零函数取得；全测试函数空间上的 Hardy 不等式成立当且仅当 $sq<n$，无需 $s<1$ 的额外限制。

Abstract: We prove sharp Hardy inequalities on $\mathbb{Q}_p^n$ for the quadratic form of the Vladimirov--Taibleson operator, the $L^q$ norm of its fractional derivative, and a nonlinear difference energy. Under the self-dual Fourier normalization, the sharp quadratic-form constant for $0<α<n$ is $[(1-p^{-(n-α)/2})/(1-p^{-(n+α)/2})]^2$, with a remainder given by the ground state representation. For $1<q<\infty$, $q'=q/(q-1)$, and $0<s<n/q$, a weighted Schur estimate determines the exact $L^q$ norm of the weighted Riesz potential $\|\boldsymbol{x}\|_p^{-s}I_s$, giving the sharp linear Hardy constant $[Γ_{p}^{(n)}(n/q'+s)/Γ_{p}^{(n)}(n/q')]^q$. The two-point Picone inequality and shell decomposition give the sharp constant $2(1-p^{-n})\sum_{m\ge1}p^{-sqm}(1-p^{-(n/q-s)m})^q$ for the Gagliardo-type energy with kernel $\|\boldsymbol{x}-\boldsymbol{y}\|_p^{-n-sq}$. Annular truncations of the critical powers give explicit minimizing sequences with error estimates. Equality is attained only by the zero function in each stated class. For $q=2$, both $L^q$ inequalities reduce to the quadratic-form inequality. Uniform local constancy makes the difference energy finite for every Bruhat--Schwartz function and every $s>0$. The Hardy inequalities on the full test-function space therefore hold precisely when $sq<n$, without the restriction $s<1$.

</details>


### [236] [Sharp discrete Hardy constants in low dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05421)
*Natanael Alpay*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了在 $\mathbb Z^d$（$5\le d\le8$）上带欧氏反平方权的最邻近 Hardy 不等式的尖锐常数为 $C_d=\frac{(d-2)^2}{4}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作确定了连续 Hardy 系数在 $d=3,4$ 时尖锐、在 $d\ge9$ 时不尖锐，但 $5\le d\le8$ 的剩余情形尚未解决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用 reciprocal edge fields（互逆边场）以及相关顶点权的逐点估计；外部区域的估计通过多项式不等式得到，原点附近的权则通过显式估计与局部修正处理。

Result: 证明了对于 $5\le d\le8$，有 $\lambda_d=C_d$，即连续 Hardy 系数在这些维度下是尖锐的。

Conclusion: 结合已有结果，最邻近 Hardy 不等式在 $\mathbb Z^d$（$d\ge3$）上的尖锐常数在 $3\le d\le8$ 时等于连续 Hardy 系数 $C_d$，而 $d\ge9$ 时不再等于该系数。

Abstract: For $d\ge3$, let $λ_d$ denote the sharp constant in the nearest-neighbor Hardy inequality on $\mathbb Z^d$ with Euclidean inverse-square weight for functions vanishing at the origin. Recent work established that the continuous Hardy coefficient \[ C_d=\frac{(d-2)^2}{4} \] is sharp in dimensions three and four, while it is not sharp for $d\ge9$. We prove $λ_d=C_d$ for the remaining cases $5\le d\le8$. The proof uses reciprocal edge fields and pointwise estimates for the associated vertex weights. The exterior estimates follow from polynomial inequalities, and the weights near the origin are treated by explicit estimates and local corrections.

</details>


### [237] [Crossed-Heinz Inequalities: The Two-Dimensional Threshold and Higher-Dimensional Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04567)
*Mohammad Sababheh,Jean-Christophe Bourin*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究 Bourin 提出的 crossed-Heinz 不等式问题：对正矩阵 A、B 与 0<t<1，是否有 ‖C_t(A,B)‖ ≤ ‖A+B‖ 对任意酉不变范数成立。作者通过削弱直接比较 C_t 与普通 Heinz 项 H_t 的强不等式，转而证明 2×2 情形下的弱majorization 关系，并厘清该比较在维数上的精确失效阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最直接的证明路径是比较 crossed-Heinz 表达式 C_t(A,B)=A^tB^{1-t}+B^tA^{1-t} 与普通 Heinz 表达式 H_t(A,B)=A^tB^{1-t}+A^{1-t}B^t，再借助经典 Heinz 不等式 ‖H_t(A,B)‖≤‖A+B‖ 得到结论。但该强比较 ‖C_t‖≤‖H_t‖ 对 3×3 正定矩阵已失效，经块对角嵌入后在所有 n≥3 维均失败，形成真实的维数障碍。因此需要确定该障碍的精确阈值，并寻找在低维情形可行的替代比较方式。

Method: 针对 2×2 正矩阵情形，不再要求算子范数意义下的强比较 ‖C_t(A,B)‖≤‖H_t(A,B)‖，而是证明其奇异值向量满足弱 majorization 关系 s(C_t(A,B))≺_w s(H_t(A,B))，对任意 0<t<1 与任意 2×2 正矩阵对成立。该方法通过在奇异值层面建立较弱但足以支撑酉不变范数比较的序关系，规避高维中已被证明失效的直接比较路径。

Result: 对任意 0<t<1 及任意一对 2×2 正矩阵 A、B，证明了弱 majorization 关系 s(C_t(A,B))≺_w s(H_t(A,B))。该结果给出了直接比较 ‖C_t‖≤‖H_t‖ 失效的精确维数阈值：在 2×2 情形以弱 majorization 形式成立，而该强比较自 3×3 起失效并可嵌入到所有 n≥3 维。

Conclusion: 本文确定了 crossed-Heinz 与普通 Heinz 表达式之间比较的精确障碍阈值。2×2 情形下弱 majorization 成立，意味着相应酉不变范数比较在低维可行；而高维（n≥3）中直接比较失效。这一结论为研究 Bourin 问题提供了关键的结构性分界，并提示需在高维采用新的证明策略。

Abstract: For positive matrices $A,B$ and $0<t<1$, Bourin asked whether the crossed-Heinz expression
  \[
  C_t(A,B)=A^tB^{1-t}+B^tA^{1-t}
  \]
  satisfies
  \[
  \UIN{C_t(A,B)}\le \UIN{A+B}
  \]
  for every unitarily invariant norm. The most direct route to this
  inequality is to compare $C_t(A,B)$ with the ordinary Heinz expression
  \[
  H_t(A,B)=A^tB^{1-t}+A^{1-t}B^t,
  \]
  because the classical Heinz inequality gives
  $\UIN{H_t(A,B)}\le\UIN{A+B}$. This route, however, encounters a
  genuine dimensional obstruction: the stronger comparison
  $\UIN{C_t(A,B)}\le\UIN{H_t(A,B)}$ is known to fail for positive
  definite $3\times3$ matrices, and hence, by block-diagonal embedding,
  in every dimension $n\ge3$.
  We first determine the exact threshold for this obstruction. For every
  pair of positive $2\times2$ matrices and every $0<t<1$, we prove the
  weak-majorization relation
  \[
  s(C_t(A,B))\prec_w s(H_t(A,B)).
  \]

</details>


### [238] [Unique operator space structures on a family of two dimensional Banach spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04608)
*Samya Kumar Ray*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 证明 $E_\theta=\mathrm{span}\{1,z\}$ 上任意收缩线性映射可经谱含于 $K_\theta$ 的酉算子分解，从而完全收缩，$E_\theta$ 具唯一算子空间结构；并给出不可数多个唯一算子空间结构的二维复Banach空间，否定Pisier问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维多项式空间 $E_\theta$ 的算子空间结构唯一性，以及等距同构分类问题，并回应Pisier关于二维空间算子空间结构的提问。

Method: 利用 $E_\theta$ 的解析/几何结构，构造经谱含于圆弧 $K_\theta$ 的酉算子的分解，证明其完全收缩性；通过谱参数 $\theta$ 与等距不变量比较证明等距分类。

Result: 每个从 $E_\theta$ 到 $\mathcal B(H)$ 的收缩线性映射可因子化通过谱含于 $K_\theta$ 的酉算子，故完全收缩，$E_\theta$ 有唯一算子空间结构；$E_\theta\cong E_\varphi$ 等距当且仅当 $\theta=\varphi$；$E_\theta$ 均不等距同构于 $\ell_1^2$ 或 $\ell_\infty^2$。

Conclusion: 得到不可数多个互不等距、具唯一算子空间结构的二维复Banach空间，否定Pisier的相关问题。

Abstract: For $0<θ<π$, let $K_θ=\{e^{is}:|s|\leθ\}$ and $E_θ=\operatorname{span}_{\mathbb C}\{1,z\}\subset C(K_θ)$. We prove that every contractive linear map from $E_θ$ into $\mathcal B(H)$ admits a factorization through a unitary operator whose spectrum is contained in $K_θ$, and is therefore completely contractive. Consequently, $E_θ$ has a unique operator space structure. For $0<θ,\varphi<π$, we also show that $E_θ$ and $E_\varphi$ are isometrically isomorphic if and only if $θ=\varphi$, and that none of these spaces is isometrically isomorphic to $\ell_1^2$ or $\ell_\infty^2$. Thus we obtain uncountably many mutually non-isometric two-dimensional complex Banach spaces with a unique operator space structure, giving a negative answer to a question of Pisier.

</details>


### [239] [Noncommutative maximal inequalities for polynomial ergodic averages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06455)
*Guixiang Hong,Wenbo Li,Eric Ricard,Liang Wang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了沿多项式序列的平均在半有限von Neumann代数上的非交换极大遍历不等式，将已知的强极大不等式的p范围从受限区间扩展到所有1<p<∞，证明过程中发展了非交换Stein外推、新型组合方法以及多线性Doob极大不等式等新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于多项式序列平均的非交换极大遍历不等式仅在受限的p范围内成立，作者希望将其扩展到所有1<p<∞，从而完善非交换遍历理论中多项式平均的极大不等式理论。

Method: 证明遵循Bourgain的大弧（major-arc）策略，但需要为算子情形发展新的思路与工具：建立非交换版本的Stein外推以获得局部化极大不等式，引入新颖的组合方法，以及一个出人意料的多线性Doob极大不等式；对于所需的衰减L_2逼近，将Bourgain的两种构造结合，从而避免使用标量证明中的多频极大不等式。

Result: 对于半有限von Neumann代数(𝒩,τ)上的保迹自同构γ，定义的多项式平均A_Nf=1/N Σ_{n=1}^N γ^{P(n)}(f)在L_p(𝒩)上对每个1<p<∞满足强极大不等式，将此前已知的受限p范围扩展至全部1<p<∞。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了多项式平均的非交换强极大不等式在全范围1<p<∞上成立，所发展的非交换Stein外推、组合方法和多线性Doob极大不等式等工具可能在非交换分析中有进一步应用。

Abstract: We prove a noncommutative maximal ergodic inequality for averages along polynomial sequences. Let $γ$ be a trace-preserving automorphism of a semifinite von Neumann algebra $(\mathcal N,τ)$. We show that the associated polynomial averages \begin{equation*}
  A_Nf:=\frac1N\sum_{n=1}^Nγ^{P(n)}(f),
  \qquad N\in\mathbb N, \end{equation*} satisfy a strong maximal inequality on $L_p(\mathcal N)$ for every $1<p<\infty$, extending the previously known restricted range of $p$.
  The proof follows Bourgain's major-arc strategy but requires substantially new ideas and tools for operators that may have further applications in noncommutative analysis. More precisely, we obtain a localized maximal inequality by developing a noncommutative version of Stein's extrapolation, novel combinatorial methods, and a surprising multilinear version of Doob's maximal inequality. For the required decaying $L_2$-approximation, we combine two of Bourgain's constructions in a way that avoids the multi-frequency maximal inequality used in the scalar proof.

</details>


### [240] [Optimal Embeddings of Campanato and Morrey Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04622)
*Przemysław Górka,Zuzanna Stępień*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究度量测度空间上的Campanato空间与Morrey空间，建立其与Hölder空间的联系，证明Campanato空间到Hölder空间的嵌入等价于底层测度存在下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Campanato空间、Morrey空间与Hölder空间在欧氏空间上的关系已有成熟理论，但在一般度量测度空间上这些空间的关系尚不清晰，尤其是嵌入性质成立的充要条件有待刻画。

Method: 在一般度量测度空间框架下研究Campanato空间与Morrey空间，通过分析底层测度的性质（特别是测度下界）来刻画这些空间与Hölder空间之间的嵌入关系，并构造具体例子加以说明。

Result: 证明了Campanato空间嵌入到Hölder空间这一性质等价于底层测度存在下界，并给出若干例子说明所得结果。

Conclusion: Campanato空间与Hölder空间的嵌入关系可由底层测度的下界性质完全刻画，该结论推广了欧氏空间中的经典结果。

Abstract: We investigate Campanato and Morrey spaces on metric-measure spaces and establish their relationships with Hölder spaces. In particular, we show that the embedding of a Campanato space into a Hölder space is equivalent to the existence of a lower bound for the underlying measure. Several examples are provided to illustrate the results.

</details>


### [241] [Operator representations of Herglotz-Nevanlinna functions in several variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04637)
*Erik Avelin,Mitja Nedic*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文利用自伴线性关系给出多变量Herglotz-Nevanlinna函数的算子表示，并刻画其对应的再生核Hilbert空间，同时与Loewner函数类的已有结果进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Herglotz-Nevanlinna函数在多变量情形下的算子表示尚需系统刻画，且其对应的再生核Hilbert空间结构有待明确，并需与Loewner函数类的相关结果建立联系。

Method: 采用自伴线性关系（self-adjoint linear relations）作为核心工具，构建多变量Herglotz-Nevanlinna函数的算子表示，并据此描述相应的再生核Hilbert空间。

Result: 给出了多变量Herglotz-Nevanlinna函数的自伴线性关系算子表示，并得到了与其对应的再生核Hilbert空间的描述。

Conclusion: 所提表示方法统一刻画了多变量Herglotz-Nevanlinna函数及其再生核Hilbert空间，并与Loewner函数类的已有结果进行了对比。

Abstract: In this paper, we present an operator representation for Herglotz-Nevanlinna functions in several variables using self-adjoint linear relations. Moreover, a description of the reproducing kernel Hilbert space corresponding to a Herglotz-Nevanlinna function of several variables is also presented, along with a comparison to some existing result concerning the class of Loewner functions.

</details>


### [242] [Sharp inequalities for the Newtonian and logarithmic capacities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04811)
*Mengwei Su,Qiang Sun,Ge Xiong*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该论文建立了 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 中紧集的 Riesz 容量与对数容量之间的精确不等式，并完整刻画了等号成立条件，从而推广了 Pólya 与 Szegő 于 20 世纪 40 年代提出的关于 Newton 容量与对数容量之比的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pólya 与 Szegő 在 20 世纪 40 年代提出了 $\mathbb{R}^2$ 中 Newton 容量与对数容量之比的猜想，该问题长期未获完全解决，且其在高维情形下的自然推广缺乏系统研究。本文旨在建立更一般的 Riesz 容量与对数容量之间的精确不等式，并给出等号成立条件。

Method: 通过势论与容量理论的分析方法，对 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 中紧集的 Riesz 容量与对数容量进行比较，建立二者之间的尖锐不等式，并对等号成立的情形进行完整刻画，进而将结果应用于二维 Newton 容量与对数容量之比的情形。

Result: 在 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 中获得了 Riesz 容量与对数容量之间的尖锐不等式，并完整刻画了等号成立条件；该结果自然地推广了 Pólya–Szegő 关于 $\mathbb{R}^2$ 中 Newton 容量与对数容量之比的猜想。

Conclusion: 本文为高维容量理论中不同容量之间的定量比较提供了统一框架，并将经典的 Pólya–Szegő 猜想纳入更广泛的结论之中；但摘要未提及精确的常数形式、等号取到的具体集合类别及证明技术细节。

Abstract: Sharp inequalities between the Riesz and logarithmic capacities of compact sets in $\mathbb{R}^n$ are established, and the equality conditions are fully characterized. These results naturally extend Pólya and Szegő's conjecture on the ratio of Newtonian to logarithmic capacities in $\mathbb{R}^2$, which was posed in the 1940s.

</details>


### [243] [The \(L^2\)-Cartesian Numerical Radius of Hilbert Space Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04844)
*Wasim Audeh,Hamid Reza Moradi,Satyajit Sahoo*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文引入算子 \(T=H+iK\) 的 \(L^2\)-笛卡尔数值半径 \(\omega_2(T)=\big(\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_0^{2\pi}\|\cos\theta H+\sin\theta K\|^2 d\theta\big)^{1/2}\)，研究其基本性质并与经典数值半径 \(\omega(T)\) 比较，建立尖锐不等式及由笛卡尔分量给出的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典数值半径 \(\omega(T)=\sup_{\|x\|=1}|\langle Tx,x\rangle|\) 是算子理论中的重要量，但其与算子的笛卡尔分解（实部与虚部）之间的联系尚可用一个新的"平均型"半径来刻画。作者希望通过沿单位圆周对 \(\cos\theta H+\sin\theta K\) 的范数平方取平均，定义一种 \(L^2\)-型数值半径，从而给出与 \(\omega(T)\) 的定量比较，并用 H、K 给出界。

Method: 在复 Hilbert 空间上考察笛卡尔分解 \(T=H+iK\)（H、K 自伴），定义 \(\omega_2(T)\) 为 \(\|\cos\theta H+\sin\theta K\|\) 关于 θ 的 \(L^2\) 平均。通过积分恒等式与算子范数不等式，研究其基本性质（如范数性、酉不变性等），并与 \(\omega(T)\) 建立双向尖锐估计，同时推导以 H、K 表述的上下界。

Result: 建立了尖锐估计 \(\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\omega(T)\le \omega_2(T)\le \omega(T)\)，并给出以笛卡尔分量 H、K 表示的下界与上界。作为推论得到 \(\frac{1}{4}\|T^*T+TT^*\|\le \omega_2(T)^2\le \omega(T)^2\)。

Conclusion: \(L^2\)-笛卡尔数值半径 \(\omega_2(T)\) 是介于 \(\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\omega(T)\) 与 \(\omega(T)\) 之间的新半径量，能够由 T 的实部与虚部刻画，并导出关于 \(T^*T+TT^*\) 的范数下界，为数值半径理论提供了新的比较工具与不等式。

Abstract: Let \(\mathcal{H}\) be a complex Hilbert space, and let \(T=H+iK\in\mathcal{B}(\mathcal{H})\) be the Cartesian decomposition of \(T\), where \(H=H^{*}\) and \(K=K^{*}\). We introduce the \(L^2\)-Cartesian numerical radius \[ ω_2(T)=\left(\frac{1}{2π}\int_0^{2π}\|\cosθH+\sinθK\|^2\,dθ\right)^{1/2}. \] We study its basic properties and compare it with the classical numerical radius \(ω(T)\). In particular, we establish the sharp estimate \[ \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}ω(T)\le ω_2(T)\le ω(T), \] and establish lower and upper bounds in terms of the Cartesian parts \(H,K\). As a product, we obtain \[ \frac{1}{4}\left\|T^{*}T+TT^{*}\right\|\le ω_2(T)^2\le ω(T)^2. \]

</details>


### [244] [From Sharp Self-Improvement to Exact Perimeter Formulas in Bourgain--Brezis--Mironescu Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04849)
*Hongchao Jia,Xianjie Yan*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文定义并用$L^q$型平均振荡替代局部$L^1$平均振荡，引入空间$\mathcal{B}_q(Q_0)$，研究了其与$\mathcal{B}(Q_0)$、$\mathrm{BMO}$的关系，并给出一般函数、特征函数与周长公式三类端点结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bourgain--Brezis--Mironescu空间$\mathcal{B}$以局部$L^1$平均振荡刻画，其$L^q$对应物在$n=1$时等同于$\mathrm{BMO}$，但高维$n\geq2$时的端点行为尚不清晰，需要确定$\mathcal{B}_q$与$\mathcal{B}$等价的临界指标及周长刻画。

Method: 通过定义以$L^q$平均振荡替代局部$L^1$平均振荡的$\mathcal{B}_q(Q_0)$；分别利用稀疏中位控制（general functions）、原子块分解（characteristic functions）与相对等周不等式证明三个sharp self-improvement；对周长公式采用一致De Giorgi blow-up归约为几何立方截面不等式，并通过几何平均与显式packing分别给平面延拓与反例。

Result: (i) 一般函数：$\mathcal{B}_q(Q_0)=\mathcal{B}(Q_0)$当且仅当$q\in(0,\frac{n}{n-1})$，并得到Hardy型predual的sharp self-improvement与对偶结果。(ii) 特征函数：$\|\mathbf{1}_E\|_{\mathcal{B}_q}\sim\|\mathbf{1}_E\|_{\mathcal{B}}\sim\min\{1,P(E,Q_0)\}$对可测$E\subset Q_0$一致成立当且仅当$q\in(0,\frac{n}{n-1}]$。(iii) 各向同性泛函$I_{\varepsilon,q}^*(E)$当$\varepsilon\to0^+$时收敛到$\frac{1}{2}\min\{1,P(E,Q_0)\}$，对$q\in(0,\frac{n}{n-1}]$成立；二维可延拓到$q\in(0,q_*]$，$q_*>\frac{19}{9}$，而在任意维对$q>q_0$失败，$q_0\approx2.15391$（变分定义，数值估计）。

Conclusion: $\mathcal{B}_q$在一般函数与特征函数情形呈现不同的临界指标（$\frac{n}{n-1}$与闭端点），周长泛函在低$q$下具有稳定极限并在二维获得部分延拓，而高$q$存在维数无关的反例，完整刻画由$q_0$控制。

Abstract: Let $Q_0:=(0,1)^n$. We define $\mathcal{B}_q(Q_0)$ by replacing the local $L^1$ mean oscillation in the Bourgain--Brezis--Mironescu space $\mathcal{B}(Q_0)$ with its $L^q$ counterpart, which coincides with $\mathrm{BMO}(Q_0)$ when $n=1$. For $n\geq2$, our results reveal different endpoint behaviors for general functions, characteristic functions and the exact perimeter formula. (i) For general functions, $\mathcal{B}_q(Q_0)=\mathcal{B}(Q_0)$ precisely when $q\in(0,\frac{n}{n-1})$. We also establish the corresponding sharp self-improvement and duality results for Hardy-type preduals. (ii) For characteristic functions, the endpoint is attained: the equivalence $\|\mathbf{1}_E\|_{\mathcal{B}_q(Q_0)}\sim\|\mathbf{1}_E\|_{\mathcal{B}(Q_0)}\sim\min\{1,P(E,Q_0)\}$ holds uniformly over measurable $E\subset Q_0$ precisely when $q\in(0,\frac{n}{n-1}]$, where $P(E,Q_0)$ denotes relative perimeter. (iii) The isotropic functional $I_{\varepsilon,q}^{*}(E)$, defined using arbitrarily oriented cubes, converges to $\frac{1}{2}\min\{1,P(E,Q_0)\}$ as $\varepsilon\to0^+$ throughout $q\in(0,\frac{n}{n-1}]$. In dimension two, the formula extends to $q\in(0,q_*]$ for some universal $q_*>\frac{19}{9}$, whereas it fails in every dimension for every $q>q_0$, where $q_0>2$ is defined variationally and numerical computation suggests that $q_0\approx2.15391$. The proofs of the three sharp self-improvements use sparse median domination, atomic block decompositions, and relative isoperimetry, respectively. For the perimeter formula, a uniform De Giorgi blow-up reduces the problem to a geometric cube-section inequality; geometric averaging and an explicit packing yield the planar extension and the obstruction, respectively.

</details>


### [245] [A semi-greedy Markushevich basis which is not quasi-greedy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05052)
*Pablo M. Berná*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 构造了一个具有半贪心Markushevich基但非准贪心的拟Banach空间,给出相关问题在拟Banach框架下的否定回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨Banach空间中Markushevich基的"半贪心性与几乎贪心性等价"这一结论能否推广到一般拟Banach空间。

Method: 在拟Banach空间框架下构造反例:建立一个具有半贪心Markushevich基的空间,该基不是准贪心的,从而也不是几乎贪心的。

Result: 构造出这样的拟Banach空间:其Markushevich基为半贪心但非准贪心,故非几乎贪心。

Conclusion: 半贪心与几乎贪心在Markushevich基上的等价性不能推广到一般拟Banach空间。

Abstract: We construct a quasi-Banach space with a semi-greedy Markushevich basis which is not quasi-greedy, and hence not almost greedy. This gives a negative answer to the question of whether the equivalence between semi-greediness and almost greediness for Markushevich bases in Banach spaces extends to the general quasi-Banach setting.

</details>


### [246] [Trace-Controlled Operator Selection and Discretization of Continuous Frames in Hilbert \(C^*\)-Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05073)
*Hicham Tarif*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文在有限维 $C^*$-代数上的Hilbert $C^*$-模中建立正紧算子的迹控制选择定理（含二元选择原理的模版本），并将其应用于连续Hilbert $C^*$-模框架的离散化，得到具有显式框架界与受控分离性的采样族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续框架的离散化在Hilbert空间中已有成熟理论，但在Hilbert $C^*$-模中，由于内积取值为 $C^*$-代数元素、且紧算子结构更为复杂，相应问题存在额外困难，因此需要发展新的算子选择框架来处理模框架的离散化。

Method: 构建面向有限维 $C^*$-代数上Hilbert $C^*$-模的算子选择框架；证明正紧算子的迹控制选择定理；建立正迹控制紧算子的二元选择原理的模版本。证明手段结合有限秩逼近、有限维 $C^*$-代数的矩阵表示以及适配 $C^*$-模设定的算子估计。

Result: 得到有限维 $C^*$-代数上可数生成Hilbert $C^*$-模中正紧算子的迹控制选择定理及二元选择原理的模版本；并将其应用于连续Hilbert $C^*$-模框架的离散化，在度量测度空间满足适当假设、系数代数 $\mathcal A$ 有限维、且 $\mathcal H$ 的每个闭子模均正交可补的条件下，所得采样族构成具有显式框架界和受控分离性的Hilbert $C^*$-模框架。

Conclusion: 该工作将框架离散化的选择方法从Hilbert空间推广到有限维 $C^*$-代数上的Hilbert $C^*$-模，给出迹控制算子选择与连续模框架离散化的统一处理，并为采样族的框架界与分离性提供定量控制。

Abstract: The discretization of continuous frames in Hilbert spaces is well established, while the corresponding problem for Hilbert $C^*$-modules presents additional difficulties arising from the $C^*$-algebra-valued inner product and the structure of compact operators on Hilbert modules. In this paper, we develop an operator-selection framework for the discretization of continuous frames in Hilbert $C^*$-modules over finite-dimensional $C^*$-algebras. Our main result is a trace-controlled selection theorem for positive compact operators acting on countably generated Hilbert $C^*$-modules over finite-dimensional $C^*$-algebras. In particular, we establish a module version of the binary selection principle for positive trace-controlled compact operators. The proof combines finite-rank approximation, the matrix representation of finite-dimensional $C^*$-algebras, and operator estimates adapted to the $C^*$-module setting. We then apply these selection results to the discretization of continuous Hilbert $C^*$-module frames. Under suitable assumptions on the underlying metric measure space, with the coefficient algebra $\mathcal A$ finite-dimensional, and assuming that every closed submodule of $\mathcal H$ is orthogonally complemented. The resulting sampling family is a Hilbert $C^*$-module frame with explicit frame bounds and controlled separation.

</details>


### [247] [Incoherent filter banks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05302)
*William J. Brinkley,Matthew Fickus,John Jasper,Dustin G. Mixon*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文刻画了来自互易交叉Gram矩阵的滤波器组等等交紧融合框架（EITFF）和等弦紧融合框架（ECTFF），证明前者等价于等角紧框架（ETF）的直和，后者等价于一类新的"分层单位范数紧框架"（SUNTF），并建立SUNTF理论给出显式构造与存在性必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等等交（EI）与等弦（EC）紧融合框架是压缩感知等应用中出现的极小相干子空间填充，其构造困难、存在性刻画基本未解决；作者希望针对滤波器组这类交叉Gram矩阵互易的特殊情形给出可刻画的结构与显式构造。

Method: 限定等距映射的交叉Gram矩阵互易（对应滤波器组）这一特殊情形，将EITFF与ECTFF分别归约为ETF直和与新型框架；随后建立分层单位范数紧框架（SUNTF）的一般理论，给出多种显式构造并推导存在性必要条件。

Result: 证明每个滤波器组EITFF均等价于ETF的直和；每个滤波器组ECTFF均等价于ETF的新型推广——SUNTF；建立SUNTF的一般理论，提供显式构造及若干存在性必要条件；所有技术均为显式的，可直接构造非相干滤波器组。

Conclusion: 在交叉Gram矩阵互易的滤波器组情形下，EITFF与ECTFF的存在性可归约到ETF与SUNTF的存在性，从而把原本开放的EITFF/ECTFF存在性问题简化为更易处理的结构，并给出显式、可直接构造非相干滤波器组的方法。

Abstract: Equi-isoclinic (EI) and equichordal (EC) tight fusion frames (TFFs) are types of minimally coherent (maximally orthogonal) subspace packings that arise in several applications, such as compressed sensing. They seem challenging to construct, and the problem of characterizing their existence remains mostly open. In this paper, we characterize those special complex EITFFs and ECTFFs that arise from isometries whose cross-Gram matrices commute, as is the case with filter banks. In particular, we show that every filter bank EITFF is equivalent to a direct sum of equiangular tight frames (ETFs). More generally, we show that every filter bank ECTFF is analogously equivalent to a novel generalization of ETFs that we dub stratified unit norm tight frames (SUNTFs). We further establish a general theory of SUNTFs, providing various explicit constructions of them, and some necessary conditions on their existence. All of our techniques are explicit, yielding direct constructions of incoherent filter banks.

</details>


### [248] [Presenting a proof of the Kannan-Lovasz-Simonovits conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05474)
*Pierre Bizeul,Boaz Klartag,Joseph Lehec*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文证明了高维几何中著名的Kannan-Lovász-Simonovits (KLS)猜想，关键步骤是应用Song和Zhang关于倾斜平均值高阶导数的近期判别准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KLS猜想与Bourgain切片问题、方差猜想并列为近几十年高维几何数学发展的核心驱动力，长期悬而未决，证明该猜想具有重大意义。

Method: 核心方法是运用Song和Zhang最近提出的关于倾斜平均值(tilt averages)高阶导数的判别准则。

Result: 给出了KLS猜想的一个证明。

Conclusion: 通过Song和Zhang的高阶导数准则成功证明了KLS猜想，为高维几何领域提供了重要突破。

Abstract: Together with Bourgain's slicing problem and the variance conjecture, the Kannan-Lovász-Simonovits (KLS) conjecture has been a driving force for mathematical developments in high dimensional geometry in recent decades. We present here a proof of this conjecture. The crucial step in the proof is the very recent criterion from Song and Zhang involving high-order derivatives of tilt averages.

</details>


### [249] [Lower bounds and Poincaré inequality for the Brezis-Seeger-Van Schaftingen-Yung functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05929)
*Nicola Picenni*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文研究Brezis等人引入的含三实参(γ,p,λ)的非局部非凸泛函，在γ<-p时给出λ→0⁺的Gamma-liminf下界与Poincaré型不等式，并揭示γ>0时不成立、需修正版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该泛函族由Brezis、Seeger、Van Schaftingen和Yung引入，其渐近行为与相应不等式此前存在未决问题（包括Nguyen提出的问题），本文旨在解决这些公开问题。

Method: 对含参(γ,p,λ)的非局部非凸泛函族，在γ<-p情形分析λ→0⁺时的Gamma-liminf并建立下界估计，进而证明Poincaré型不等式；在γ>0情形构造反例否定该不等式，并给出修正形式。

Result: 在γ<-p时得到了Gamma-liminf的下界及Poincaré型不等式；证明当γ>0时此类不等式不成立；并针对γ>0建立了修改后的不等式版本。

Conclusion: 结果回答了Brezis、Seeger、Van Schaftingen、Yung及Nguyen提出的若干公开问题，刻画了参数γ对泛函渐近行为与不等式有效性的临界影响。

Abstract: We consider a family of non-local and non-convex functionals depending on three real parameters $(γ,p,λ)$, which was introduced by H. Brezis, A. Seeger, J. Van Schaftingen and P. L. Yung. In the case $γ<-p$ we prove a lower bound for the Gamma-liminf of these functionals as $λ\to 0^+$ and a Poincaré-type inequality. We also show that such inequality cannot hold if $γ>0$, and we establish a modified version in this case.
  These results answer some open questions posed by these authors and by H. M. Nguyen.

</details>


### [250] [Von Kármán theories for thin elastic shells subject to pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06048)
*Trung Hieu Giang,Filippo Riva*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该论文在常压载荷下，从三维非线性弹性理论出发，通过变分方法推导二维von Kármán壳能量，将已有压力载荷板的分析推广到壳的弯曲情形，并以非线性压力项补充经典壳理论变分推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作已对压力载荷下的板完成了类似变分推导，但壳的弯曲几何情形尚待处理；同时经典壳理论的变分推导通常只含线性化压力项，需纳入非线性压力力以完善理论。

Method: 采用变分方法（Γ-收敛类能量展开框架），从三维非线性弹性出发，在常压载荷下对薄壳进行渐近展开，导出二维von Kármán壳能量泛函。

Result: 得到了含非线性压力贡献的二维von Kármán壳能量，建立了三维弹性与二维壳模型之间在压力载荷下的严格变分联系。

Conclusion: 该工作把压力载荷板的变分推导推广到壳，并以非线性压力项补充了经典壳理论推导，为压力作用下的薄壳建模提供了严格的理论依据。

Abstract: The variational derivation of two-dimensional von Kármán shell energies from three-dimensional nonlinear elasticity is investigated in the presence of constant pressure loads. On the one hand, the paper extends the existing analysis of pressure-loaded plates to the curved setting of shells. On the other hand, it complements the classical variational derivation of shell theories by incorporating a nonlinear pressure force.

</details>


### [251] [From the Sphere to the Grassmannian: Continuous Brascamp--Lieb Inequalities via Measure Approximation and Heat Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05890)
*Minguo Li*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究球面 $S^{n-1}$ 与 Grassmann 流形 $G(n,k)$ 上各向同性测度的连续 Brascamp--Lieb 不等式，并给出两种证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典（等秩）几何 Brascamp--Lieb 不等式推广到连续各向同性测度，特别是 Grassmann 流形的高秩情形。

Method: 证明一：用离散各向同性测度逼近连续测度——秩一情形借助 Barthe 的构造，高秩情形用 Tchakaloff 型定理构造有限逼近各向同性测度，再结合弱收敛、几何 BL 不等式与 Fatou 引理。证明二：将 Barthe--Huet 的热半群保持方法推广到 $G(n,k)$ 上的各向同性测度，证明 Grassmann 情形下的热半群保持定理。末尾给出混合秩情形的梗概。

Result: 建立了球面与 Grassmann 流形上连续各向同性测度的 Brascamp--Lieb 不等式；给出离散逼近与热半群保持两条独立证明路线；证明了 Grassmann 情形热半群保持定理并推导出 Grassmannian BL 不等式；补充混合秩情形思路。

Conclusion: 连续各向同性测度的 BL 不等式可由离散逼近或热半群保持两种途径得到，二者分别依赖 Tchakaloff 型定理与热半群保持定理，并覆盖秩一、高秩及混合秩情形。

Abstract: We study continuous Brascamp--Lieb inequalities associated with isotropic measures on the sphere $S^{n-1}$ and on the Grassmannian $G(n,k)$. We give two proofs of the continuous Grassmannian inequality. The first proof approximates the continuous isotropic measure by discrete isotropic measures and then applies the equal-rank geometric Brascamp--Lieb inequality. In the rank-one case, the construction of the discrete isotropic measures follows from Barthe's work, which is standard, whereas in the higher-rank case the construction of finite approximate isotropic measures is given by a Tchakaloff-type theorem. Weak convergence, geometric Brascamp--Lieb inequality and Fatou's lemma then yield the continuous inequalities. The second proof generalizes the heat-semigroup preservation method of Barthe and Huet to isotropic measures on $G(n,k)$. We prove a heat-semigroup preservation theorem in the Grassmannian setting and show that this theorem yields a Grassmannian Brascamp--Lieb inequality. At the end, we also give a sketch for the mixed-rank case of the continuous Brascamp--Lieb inequality.

</details>


### [252] [Exactness of weighted exponential systems with a defect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06114)
*Ivan Arefev,Andrei V. Semenov*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文证明了在自然且指数尺度上最优的衰减条件下，任意Schauder基与任意缺项缺陷集导致加权函数系必完备但非极小，并给出了有限缺陷集情形完备且极小的组合判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究$L^2(0,1)$中加权指数型函数系的完备性与极小性，刻画权重衰减条件与缺陷集结构对其性质的影响，并寻找简洁判定准则。

Method: 利用Schauder基与缺项缺陷集$A\subset\mathbb{Z}$的构造性分析，结合加权函数系$\{w(t)r_n(t)\}$与$\{w(t)e^{2\pi i n t}\}$的完备性、极小性论证及组合判据。

Result: 对满足自然衰减条件（指数尺度上最优）的任意权重$w$，$\{w(t)r_n(t)\}_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus A}$总是完备且永不极小；对有限缺陷集$A$，给出$\{w(t)e^{2\pi i n t}\}_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus A}$在$L^2(0,1)$中完备且极小的简单组合判据。

Conclusion: 权重衰减条件与缺陷集结构共同决定加权指数系的完备与极小性质；所得条件在指数尺度上最优，且有限缺陷情形可由组合准则判定。

Abstract: Let $w \colon (0,1) \to \mathbb{R}_{+}$ be a weight. We prove that for an arbitrary Schauder basis $\{e^{i λ_n t}\}_{n \in \mathbb{Z}}$ in $L^2(0,1)$ and an arbitrary lacunary defect set $A \subset \mathbb{Z}$ the system $\{w(t) r_n(t)\}_{n \in \mathbb{Z} \setminus A}$ is always complete and never minimal for any weight $w$ satisfying a natural decay condition, which is sharp on the exponential scale.
  Moreover, we establish a simple combinatorial criterion for systems of the form $ \{ w(t) e^{2πi n t} \}_{n \in \mathbb{Z} \setminus A} $ to be complete and minimal in $L^2(0,1)$ for an arbitrary weight $w$ and a finite defect set $A \subset \mathbb{Z}$.

</details>


### [253] [Characterization of the boundedness of the discrete Fractional maximal operator between weighted in Lorentz sequence spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06124)
*Amiran Gogatishvili,Nurzhan A. Bokayev,Nurgul K. Kuzeubayeva*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文给出了定义在 $\mathbb{Z}^n$ 上的离散分数次极大算子 $M_\gamma$ 在经典 Lorentz 序列空间 $\lambda_p(v)$ 与 $\lambda_q(w)$ 之间有界性的完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究离散分数次极大算子 $M_\gamma$ 在加权 Lorentz 序列空间之间的有界性刻画问题。

Method: 首先获得 $M_\gamma x$ 非增重排的精确上估计，该估计涉及一个含离散 Hardy 算子的上确界算子与序列 $\{x(k)\}$ 的非增重排，并通过特殊序列的下估计证明该估计是精确的；进而将原问题归约为加权 Lebesgue 序列空间上离散上确界算子在非增序列限制下的有界性问题。

Result: 得到了 $M_\gamma x$ 非增重排的精确上估计（含匹配的下估计），并借助已有工作完成了有界性的最终刻画。

Conclusion: 通过精确重排估计与归约方法，完整刻画了离散分数次极大算子在经典加权 Lorentz 序列空间间的有界性。

Abstract: In this paper, we give the complete characterization of the boundedness of the discrete fractional maximal operator $M_γ$, defined on $\mathbb{Z}^n$, $n\in\mathbb{N}$, $γ\in [0,n)$, between the classical Lorentz sequence spaces $λ_p(v)$ and $λ_q(w)$, where $0<p,q<\infty$, $\{v(m)\}$ and $\{w(m)\}$ are non-negative sequences defined on $\mathbb{N}$. We first obtain a sharp upper estimate for the nonincreasing rearrangement of the discrete fractional maximal operator $M_γx$, with a supremal operator involving a discrete Hardy operator and the nonincreasing rearrangement of the sequence $\{x(k)\}$, and we show that this estimate is sharp by showing a lower estimate for special sequences. Using this result, we can reduce the characterization of the boundedness of the discrete fractional maximal operator $ M_γ$ between the discrete weighted Lorentz sequence spaces to the boundedness of the discrete supremum operator between weighted Lebesgue sequence spaces, restricted to nonincreasing sequences. This problem was investigated in the authors' recent paper, which enables us to obtain the final characterization.

</details>


### [254] [Projection Constants and Banach-Mazur Distance in Codimension Two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06141)
*Beata Deregowska,Barbara Lewandowska*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 证明了余维一或二的子空间 $V\subset\ell_\infty^N$，若绝对投影常数 $\lambda(V)<4/3$，则 $d(V,\ell_\infty^{\dim V})\le \lambda(V)/(4-3\lambda(V))$，并猜想该估计对任意子空间成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 $\ell_\infty^N$ 子空间与 $\ell_\infty^{\dim V}$ 的Banach-Mazur距离，在给定绝对投影常数上界下寻求定量估计；已知结果可能受限于余维条件，作者希望放宽限制。

Method: 利用绝对投影常数与Banach-Mazur距离的定量关系，对余维一或二的子空间证明所需不等式。

Result: 对余维一或二且 $\lambda(V)<4/3$ 的子空间 $V$，得到 $d(V,\ell_\infty^{\dim V})\le \lambda(V)/(4-3\lambda(V))$。

Conclusion: 猜想余维假设可以去掉，同一估计对任意满足 $\lambda(V)<4/3$ 的 $V\subset\ell_\infty^N$ 均成立。

Abstract: We prove that every subspace $V\subset\ell_\infty^N$ of codimension one or two with absolute projection constant $λ(V)<4/3$ satisfies $$ d(V,\ell_\infty^{\dim V}) \leq\frac{λ(V)}{4-3λ(V)}. $$
  We conjecture that the codimension assumption can be removed and that the same estimate holds for every subspace $V\subset\ell_\infty^N$ with $λ(V)<4/3$.

</details>


### [255] [Spectral Barron spaces of periodic functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06169)
*Mourad Choulli,Shuai Lu,Hiroshi Takase*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文将谱Barron空间推广到周期函数情形，覆盖高维环面及更一般的局部紧Abel群，并讨论PDE适定性与两个反问题的条件稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谱Barron空间多定义在全空间或具光滑边界的有界域上，缺乏对周期函数及更一般群结构的处理，限制了其在周期问题与反问题中的应用。

Method: 通过考虑与基于ℓ¹序列空间的加权序列空间等距同构的Fourier级数展开，构造周期谱Barron空间，将其推广至高维环面与局部紧Abel群，并用于研究PDE适定性与反问题。

Result: 建立了周期函数谱Barron空间的框架，将其扩展到高维环面及局部紧Abel群；讨论了该类空间中PDE的适定性与两个演化方程反问题的条件稳定性。

Conclusion: 谱Barron空间可自然推广至周期与群结构背景，为周期PDE及反问题提供统一的分析框架，并在环面情形下给出条件稳定性结果。

Abstract: Spectral Barron spaces are Fourier-Lebesgue-type spaces that arise in the approximation of functions by neural networks. Conventional spectral Barron spaces are mostly considered on the whole space or on a bounded domain with certain smoothness assumptions on the boundary. In this work, we investigate spectral Barron spaces for periodic functions, thereby extending the study of spectral Barron spaces to high-dimensional tori and, more generally, to locally compact Abelian groups. These extensions are carried out by considering Fourier series expansions that are isometrically isomorphic to weighted sequence spaces built upon the $\ell^1$ sequence space. We discuss the well-posedness of PDEs in these newly introduced spaces. Two specific inverse problems for evolution equations are investigated, yielding conditional stability within the framework of spectral Barron spaces on the torus.

</details>


### [256] [Uniform Embeddings of Banach Space Spheres into Hilbert Spaces and Property (H)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06543)
*Qingjin Cheng,Yue Wang,Yipeng Zhang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 证明了可分无限维实Banach空间的单位球面若一致嵌入Hilbert空间，则该空间具有Kasparov–Yu性质(H)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Banach空间单位球面的Hilbert一致嵌入性质与Kasparov–Yu性质(H)是粗几何与Banach空间几何中的重要概念，二者之间的联系尚待明确。

Method: 采用泛函分析与粗几何方法，通过单位球面的Hilbert一致嵌入性这一度量几何条件，推导Kasparov–Yu性质(H)的成立。

Result: 若可分无限维实Banach空间单位球面可一致嵌入Hilbert空间，则该Banach空间具有Kasparov–Yu性质(H)。

Conclusion: 单位球面的Hilbert一致嵌入性足以保证Kasparov–Yu性质(H)，建立了Banach空间局部几何与粗几何性质之间的新联系。

Abstract: We prove that if the unit sphere of a separable infinite-dimensional real Banach space embeds uniformly into a Hilbert space, then the Banach space has Kasparov--Yu's Property (H).

</details>


### [257] [Poly and Raby's theorem splits in infinite dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06595)
*David Salas*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该论文研究无穷维Hilbert空间中平方距离函数的光滑性与集合子流形光滑性之间的刻画关系，证明了经典结论在此分裂为两种情形：弱子流形由平方距离的C^k光滑性刻画，而真子流形还需额外的最优阶导数逐点等连续性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Poly与Raby (1984)的经典结果表明，在有限维欧氏空间中，闭集上一点附近平方距离函数的C^k光滑性（k≥2）等价于该集合为同阶可微的子流形。作者希望在无穷维Hilbert空间中推广并考察这一刻画是否仍然成立，因为无穷维设置下几何与拓扑性质可能发生本质变化。

Method: 作者利用Poly与Raby的微分同胚、其关联的图表示及其逆映射的描述作为共同的几何基础，在无穷维Hilbert空间中分析平方距离函数的C^k光滑性，并引入关于平方距离最高阶导数的逐点等连续性条件，由此分别刻画弱C^k子流形与C^k子流形。

Result: 在无穷维Hilbert空间中，Poly-Raby的刻画发生分裂：平方距离函数的C^k光滑性刻画的是弱C^k子流形；而要刻画（强）C^k子流形，除平方距离的C^k光滑性外，还需附加关于最高阶导数的逐点等连续性条件。Poly-Raby的微分同胚、图表示及其逆的描述同时构成两种刻画的共同几何基础。

Conclusion: 该工作表明Poly-Raby经典刻画在无穷维Hilbert空间中不再单一成立，而是分化为弱子流形与子流形两个层次，等连续性条件成为区分二者的关键。此结果深化了对无穷维空间中距离函数光滑性与集合正则性关系的理解。

Abstract: A classic result of Poly and Raby \cite{Poly-Raby:1984} states that, in finite-dimensional Euclidean spaces, $\mathcal{C}^k$-smoothness (with $k\geq2$) of the squared distance function near a point of a closed set characterizes smoothness of the set as a submanifold, with the same order of differentiability. In this work, we show that this characterization splits in infinite-dimensional Hilbert spaces: $\mathcal{C}^k$-smoothness of the squared distance function characterizes weakly $\mathcal{C}^k$-submanifolds, while $\mathcal{C}^k$-submanifolds are characterized by this smoothness together with an additional pointwise equicontinuity condition on the highest-order derivative of the squared distance. The diffeomorphism of Poly and Raby, its associated graph representation, and the description of its inverse remain the common geometric basis of both characterizations. The development of this work was assisted by GPT-6 Astra.

</details>


### [258] [Quantitative $Γ$-Convergence: A Variational Extrapolation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06645)
*Oscar Dominguez,Mario Milman*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文将De Giorgi的Γ-收敛与Gagliardo相对完备化结合，建立变分法中的外推框架，通过变分K-泛函刻画Γ-极限与收敛速率，并应用于ROF与Chan-Esedoglu等图像处理模型的反正则性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立变分法中定量化的Γ-收敛理论，将De Giorgi变分收敛与Gagliardo完备化统一，解决图像处理中离散近似的逆正则性问题并统一经典收敛与Γ-型结果。

Method: 在变分法中引入外推框架，结合De Giorgi的Γ-收敛方法与Gagliardo在插值论中的相对完备化；核心工具是变分K-泛函的外推，将任意泛函族联系到动态罚方法以刻画Γ-极限并给出极小元的显式收敛速率；并证明涉及Gagliardo完备化的紧性外推定理。

Result: 建立了变分K-泛函与紧性外推理论；在ROF与Chan-Esedoglu模型上给出逆正则性贡献，表明保证最优收敛率所需的正则性由变分K-泛函衰减度量，并用Brezis-Van Schaftingen-Yung空间显式刻画；统一并推广了Bourgain-Brezis-Mironescu与Maz'ya-Shaposhnikova公式的经典逐点收敛与Γ-版本及相关结果。

Conclusion: 通过变分K-泛函和外推紧性桥接De Giorgi变分收敛与Gagliardo完备化，本工作建立了变分法的定量化方法，并在图像处理与多种函数空间设置中统一和扩展了已有结果。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new extrapolation framework within the Calculus of Variations. We develop a robust quantitative theory of the $Γ$-convergence approach of De Giorgi by combining it with Gagliardo's work on relative completions in interpolation theory. A central new tool is the extrapolation of \emph{variational $K$-functionals}, which associates to an arbitrary family of functionals a dynamic penalty method that simultaneously characterizes their $Γ$-limits and yields explicit rates of convergence for minimizers. As a technical foundation we prove compactness extrapolation theorems also involving Gagliardo completions. To illustrate the mechanism, we apply the technique to variational models arising in image processing, notably the Rudin--Osher--Fatemi (ROF) and Chan--Esedoglu models. Our main result in this setting is a contribution to the \emph{inverse regularity problem} associated to discrete approximations of the ROF model: we show that the required regularity of the observed image that guarantees a prescribed optimal rate of convergence is measured in terms of the decay of variational $K$-functionals. Surprisingly, our characterization is explicitly captured by using the recently introduced spaces of Brezis, Van Schaftingen, and Yung. Moreover, our methods unify, and considerably extend, classical pointwise convergence and $Γ$-versions of the Bourgain--Brezis--Mironescu and Maz'ya--Shaposhnikova formulae, as well as related results in a variety of settings that involve BMO-type functionals, heat kernels, Sobolev spaces on metric measure spaces, ... By bridging De Giorgi's variational convergence with Gagliardo's completion through variational $K$-functionals and extrapolation of compactness, this work establishes a quantitative approach to the Calculus of Variations.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [259] [Cartan inverse semigroups and twisted Steinberg algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04548)
*Nathan Brownlowe,Lisa Orloff Clark,Ying-Fen Lin,Lynnel D. Naingue*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文引入Cartan逆半群与(代数)半Cartan子代数的概念，作为C*-代数中Cartan半群与半Cartan子代数的纯代数类比，将研究焦点从指定子代数转向指定子半群，并刻画了twisted Steinberg代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在C*-代数中已有Cartan半群与半Cartan子代数理论，且quasi-Cartan子代数理论已得到发展，但缺乏纯代数框架下以子半群为核心的Cartan型理论，本文旨在填补这一空白并统一刻画twisted Steinberg代数。

Method: 在R-代数A中定义Cartan逆半群与代数半Cartan子代数，构造相应的twist结构；借助该结构将R-代数的同构问题转化为其是否含有Cartan逆半群的问题，并利用ample Hausdorff groupoid上的twisted Steinberg代数验证构造的twist与原twist的关系。

Result: 证明了主要刻画定理：一个R-代数含有Cartan逆半群当且仅当它同构于某twisted Steinberg代数；并且当构造应用于ample Hausdorff groupoid上的twisted Steinberg代数时，所构造的twist与原twist自然同构。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Cartan逆半群与半Cartan子代数的代数理论，成功将Cartan型刻画从C*-代数推广到纯代数范畴，给出了twisted Steinberg代数的一个全新代数刻画，并验证了twist构造在标准情形下的自然性。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of a Cartan inverse semigroup and an (algebraic) semi-Cartan subalgebra in an $R$-algebra A, providing a purely algebraic analogue of Cartan semigroups and semi-Cartan subalgebras in a C*-algebra. This extends the theory of quasi-Cartan subalgebras, shifting the focus from designated subalgebras to designated subsemigroups. Our main result characterises twisted Steinberg algebras in terms of the existence of a Cartan inverse semigroup: we show that an $R$-algebra admits a Cartan inverse semigroup if and only if it is isomorphic to a twisted Steinberg algebra. Moreover, when our construction is applied to a twisted Steinberg algebra over an ample Hausdorff groupoid, the constructed twist is naturally isomorphic to the original one.

</details>


### [260] [Embeddings of matrix algebras into the universal Roe algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05410)
*Hiroto Nishikawa*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究Manuilov引入的universal Roe代数及其变体，通过C*-代数的可嵌入性性质，证明了universal Roe代数能嵌入∏ₙ𝕄ₙ，并得到Birkhoff第111号问题的算子范数类比等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Manuilov引入universal Roe代数（ℕ上最大一致局部有限粗结构Univ对应的一致Roe代数）及若干变体。作者希望通过C*-代数的可嵌入性来研究这些代数，并检验Ozawa关于一致局部有限度量空间的uniform Roe代数中∏ₙ𝕄ₙ不可嵌入的定理能否推广到任意一致局部有限粗空间。

Method: 以C*-代数的嵌入性质（embeddability）为核心工具，研究universal Roe代数与Manuilov各变体及Univ的quasi-local代数、乘子代数M(𝕀)的嵌入关系；利用置换算子与双随机（双亚随机）算子的凸包刻画；并对可分C*-代数、可分quasidiagonal C*-代数的嵌入性进行分析。

Result: 1) universal Roe代数可嵌入∏ₙ𝕄ₙ，回答Farah的问题，说明Ozawa的不可嵌入定理不能推广到任意一致局部有限粗空间；2) 嵌入性质可区分Manuilov各变体与Univ的quasi-local代数（乘子代数M(𝕀)除外）；3) 得到Birkhoff第111号问题的算子范数类比：置换算子的范数闭凸包恰为双亚随机算子集合；4) 每个可分C*-代数嵌入Univ的quasi-local代数；5) 每个可分quasidiagonal C*-代数嵌入universal Roe代数，从而嵌入某étale局部紧第二可数groupoid的约化C*-代数。

Conclusion: universal Roe代数与其quasi-local变体在嵌入性上存在区别，且quasi-local代数能容纳所有可分C*-代数，而universal Roe代数至少能容纳所有可分quasidiagonal C*-代数并嵌入某约化groupoid C*-代数；universal Roe代数的完全嵌入性问题仍待解决。

Abstract: V. Manuilov introduced the universal Roe algebra, namely the uniform Roe algebra associated with the largest uniformly locally finite coarse structure $\mathrm{Univ}$ on $\mathbb{N}$, together with several variants. We study these algebras through embeddability properties of $\mathrm{C}^*$-algebras.
  Among other results, we show that the universal Roe algebra admits an embedding from $\prod_n\mathbb{M}_n$, answering a question by Farah. Thus, Ozawa's non-embeddability theorem for $\prod_n\mathbb{M}_n$ in uniform Roe algebras of uniformly locally finite metric spaces does not extend to arbitrary uniformly locally finite coarse spaces. Nevertheless, embeddability properties distinguish the variants introduced by Manuilov and the quasi-local algebra of $\mathrm{Univ}$, apart from the multiplier algebra $M(\mathbb{I})$. As a by-product, we also obtain an operator-norm analogue of Birkhoff's Problem 111: the norm-closed convex hull of permutation operators is exactly the set of doubly substochastic operators.
  Moreover, we show that every separable $\mathrm{C}^*$-algebra embeds into the quasi-local algebra of $\mathrm{Univ}$.
