Although we do not know whether the analogous statement holds for the universal Roe algebra, we prove that every separable quasidiagonal $\mathrm{C}^*$-algebra embeds into it, and hence into the reduced $\mathrm{C}^*$-algebra of some étale locally compact second countable groupoid.

</details>


### [261] [Metric Spectral Geometry of $\mathbb{Z}^d$-Crossed Products beyond Equicontinuity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05952)
*Frederic Latremoliere*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 在C*-交叉积上构造线性扭曲谱三元组，证明其κ-度量性及与谱邻近性的收敛准则，并应用于量子2-环面交叉积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将谱三元组与非交换度量几何推广到C*-交叉积，同时放宽对作用α等连续性的要求，以涵盖非等连续及Anosov型动力学等更一般情形。

Method: 从基谱三元组(𝔄,ℋ,D)和容许适配权的作用α出发，直接在原交叉积𝔄⋊_αℤ^d上构造线性扭曲谱三元组；用权补偿Dirac算子沿动力学增长并保留ℤ^d方向的微分结构；建立谱邻近性收敛的有限模判据。

Result: 基谱三元组度量时，所得扭曲谱三元组对任意κ>0为κ-度量的，其Monge–Kantorovich度量可度量化状态空间上的弱*拓扑；保持Lipschitz半范定义域的作用可选指数权，使诱导度量几何精确限制为基代数原有度量几何；等连续时权取常数、扭曲消失并回复标准交叉积谱三元组；对量子2-环面被SL(2,ℤ)中固定自同构（含Anosov自同构）作用的交叉积，证明其扭曲谱三元组关于形变参数及合适平度量扰动具联合连续性。

Conclusion: 该工作为交叉积提供了统一的扭曲谱三元组框架，实现非等连续作用下的度量几何推广，并通过谱邻近性刻画其连续依赖性。

Abstract: We construct linearly twisted spectral triples on $C^\ast$-crossed products $\mathfrak{A}\rtimes_α\mathbb{Z}^d$, starting from a spectral triple $(\mathfrak{A},\mathcal{H},D)$ and an action $α$ admitting an adapted weight. The weight compensates for the growth of the base Dirac operator under the dynamics while preserving the canonical differential structure in the $\mathbb{Z}^d$-directions. The construction takes place directly on the original crossed product and does not require the action to be equicontinuous. We prove that, whenever the base spectral triple is metric, the resulting twisted spectral triple is $κ$-metric for every $κ>0$: its associated Monge--Kantorovich metric metrizes the weak* topology on the state space of the crossed product. Our framework applies, in particular, to every action preserving the domain of the Lipschitz seminorm of the base triple. In this case, an exponential weight may be chosen so that the induced metric geometry restricts exactly to the original metric geometry on the base algebra. In the equicontinuous case, the weight may be chosen constant, the twist disappears, and the standard crossed-product spectral triple is recovered. We also establish a general finite-mode criterion for convergence in the spectral propinquity. As an application, we prove joint continuity, with respect to the deformation parameter and suitable fluctuations of the flat metric, for the twisted spectral triples associated with crossed products of quantum $2$-tori by fixed automorphisms in $\operatorname{SL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$, including Anosov automorphisms.

</details>


### [262] [$\boldsymbol{\mathrm{K}_1^Δ}$ for $\boldsymbol{\mathrm{C}^\ast}$-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06187)
*James Gabe,Alistair Miller*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 引入拓扑阿贝尔群 K₁^Δ(A)，可区分等变 KK-理论与迹无法区分的群作用；其 Hausdorff 化构成全稳定不变量 K̲T_s(A)，可在特定稳定 C*-代数间分类 *-同态至近似酉等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变 KK-理论与迹等已有不变量无法区分某些群作用（如无理旋转代数上的强外作用），需要构造更精细的不变量以刻画此类作用并分类 *-同态。

Method: 对每个 C*-代数 A 构造拓扑阿贝尔群 K₁^Δ(A)，具函子性与稳定性；取 Hausdorff 化得 K̄₁^Δ(A)，并与单位代数 K₁ 群比较；构造全稳定不变量 K̲T_s(A)。

Result: 在无理旋转代数上给出 Z 的强外作用，经 K₁^Δ 不（稳定）余循环共轭，但在 KK^Z 与 Hausdorff 化酉代数 K₁ 群上平凡；K̲T_s 分类含非零投影的特定稳定 C*-代数间的 *-同态至近似酉等价。

Conclusion: K₁^Δ 在单位情形下亦为新不变量，在额外假设下可还原酉代数 K₁ 群，且 K̄₁^Δ 与 K̄₁^alg 对任意单位 A 一致。

Abstract: We introduce a topological abelian group $\mathrm{K}_1^Δ(A)$ for every $\mathrm{C}^\ast$-algebra $A$, which is functorial in $A$ and invariant under stabilisations. $\mathrm{K}_1^Δ$ can distinguish group actions indistinguishable by equivariant (K)K-theory and traces --- we illustrate this on the irrational rotation algebra by exhibiting strongly outer actions of $\mathbb Z$ which, via $\mathrm{K}_1^Δ$, are not (stably) cocycle conjugate. These actions are moreover trivial in $\mathrm{KK}^{\mathbb Z}$ and on the Hausdorffised unitary algebraic $\mathrm{K}_1$ group $\overline{\mathrm{K}}_1^{\mathrm{alg}}(A)$.
  The Hausdorffisation $\overline{\mathrm{K}}_1^Δ(A)$ of $\mathrm{K}_1^Δ(A)$ forms the final part of the total stable invariant $\mathrm{\underline{K}T}_{\mathrm{s}}(A)$, which we also introduce. We prove that $\mathrm{\underline{K}T}_{\mathrm{s}}$ classifies $*$-homomorphisms up to approximate unitary equivalence between certain stable $\mathrm{C}^\ast$-algebras which contain a non-zero projection.
  $\mathrm{K}_1^Δ(A)$ is new even in the unital setting, but under further assumptions on $A$ recovers the unitary algebraic $\mathrm{K}_1$ group $\mathrm{K}_1^{\mathrm{alg}}(A)$, whereas the Hausdorffisations $\overline{\mathrm{K}}_1^Δ(A)$ and $\overline{\mathrm{K}}_1^{\mathrm{alg}}(A)$ agree for every unital $A$.

</details>


### [263] [Classifiability of crossed products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06586)
*Eusebio Gardella*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文综述了由拓扑动力系统产生的C*-代数交叉积，围绕Elliott分类纲领探讨其可分类性，重点讨论了核性、单性与$\mathcal{Z}$-稳定性等性质与动力系统性质的对应关系，并指出两个核心猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Elliott分类纲领框架下，交叉积的可分类性需要将分类不变量条件翻译为动力系统语言。核性、单性等条件已有明确的动力系统对应，但张量吸收Jiang-Su代数$\mathcal{Z}$这一条件尚需系统梳理。作者希望提供既易于入门又全面的参考，阐明该领域的最新进展与核心开放问题。

Method: 综述性研究。首先一般性地引入离散群作用的交叉积构造，然后聚焦交换系统，建立基本结构结果：交叉积的核性等价于作用的顺从性，单性等价于极小性与拓扑自由的结合。随后整理已知的获得$\mathcal{Z}$-稳定性的动力系统工具，涵盖顺从群与非顺从群两种情形，并指出两个被认为能刻画完整图景的猜想。

Result: 建立了交换系统下核性$\leftrightarrow$顺从性、单性$\leftrightarrow$极小性加拓扑自由性的等价关系；系统呈现了在顺从与非顺从群情形下获取$\mathcal{Z}$-稳定性的主要动力系统工具；明确标识了两个刻画完整分类图景的中心猜想。

Conclusion: 该综述为交叉积分类提供了一个易于入门且内容全面的参考，刻画了领域现状，并列举了若干核心开放问题，特别是关于$\mathcal{Z}$-稳定性动力系统刻画的两个猜想。

Abstract: This survey studies C*-algebraic crossed products arising from topological dynamical systems with an eye toward their classifiability in the sense of the Elliott program. We introduce the crossed product construction for actions by discrete groups in full generality, and then focus on commutative systems to establish some of the fundamental structural results: nuclearity of the crossed product is equivalent to amenability of the action, and in this setting simplicity is equivalent to the combination of minimality and topological freeness. With these properties in place, the only condition left to translate into dynamical terms is tensorial absorption of the Jiang-Su algebra $\mathcal{Z}$. We present the main dynamical tools known to obtain $\mathcal{Z}$-stability, both in the settings of amenable and nonamenable groups, and highlight the two conjectures that are believed to capture the full picture. The aim of the survey is to provide both an accessible entry point and a comprehensive reference on the classification of crossed products, describing the state of the art and a number of central open problems in the field.

</details>


### [264] [Ranks of operators and pureness of C*-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06761)
*Hannes Thiel*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文证明简单非初等C*-代数中算子秩函数具有一致稠密性，由此推出迹严格比较蕴涵几乎可除性与纯性；进一步证明局部有限核维数的可分简单非初等C*-代数中严格比较蕴涵Z-稳定性，从而约化次齐次C*-代数满足Toms–Winter猜想，并在全局Glimm性质下刻画精确C*-代数的纯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Toms–Winter猜想（严格比较是否蕴涵Z-稳定性）是C*-代数分类理论的核心问题之一。此前该猜想仅在特定情形下得到验证，缺乏对一般简单非初等C*-代数的统一处理。作者希望从算子秩函数的稠密性这一更基本的性质出发，建立严格比较与纯性、Z-稳定性之间的联系，并覆盖约化次齐次（ASH）代数这一重要类。此外，作者试图在更一般的全局Glimm性质框架下刻画精确C*-代数的纯性。

Method: 对简单非初等C*-代数证明可实现为算子秩的函数类具有一致稠密性；利用该稠密性结果，论证迹的严格比较强制几乎可除性进而得到纯性；将该稠密性推广至具有全局Glimm性质的C*-代数；结合局部有限核维数条件，由严格比较导出Z-稳定性；最后在精确C*-代数情形下给出纯性的等价刻画。

Result: 1) 简单非初等C*-代数中算子秩函数一致稠密；2) 迹严格比较蕴涵几乎可除性，从而蕴涵纯性；3) 可分、简单、非初等且局部有限核维数的C*-代数中严格比较蕴涵Z-稳定性；4) 约化次齐次C*-代数满足Toms–Winter猜想；5) 具有全局Glimm性质的C*-代数中算子秩一致稠密；6) 精确C*-代数纯当且仅当同时具有严格比较与全局Glimm性质。

Conclusion: 本文以算子秩函数的一致稠密性为核心工具，统一处理了严格比较、纯性与Z-稳定性之间的关系，证明了Toms–Winter猜想在约化次齐次C*-代数类中成立，并在全局Glimm性质下给出了精确C*-代数纯性的充要条件。

Abstract: For simple, nonelementary C*-algebras, we show uniform density of functions realized as ranks of operators. It follows that strict comparison by traces forces almost divisibility, and hence pureness. As an application, we show that strict comparison implies $\mathcal{Z}$-stability for separable, simple, nonelementary C*-algebras with locally finite nuclear dimension. In particular, the Toms--Winter conjecture holds for approximately subhomogeneous C*-algebras.
  More generally, we obtain uniform density of ranks for C*-algebras with the Global Glimm Property. Consequently, an exact C*-algebra is pure if and only if it has strict comparison together with the Global Glimm Property.

</details>


### [265] [Quantum integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06771)
*David P. Blecher,Louis E. Labuschagne*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该文在ZFC下解决了von Neumann代数上"正交投影可数可加 ⇔ 序列弱*连续（序列正规）"的猜想，并借助直接积分理论给出态的重整化定理，进一步推广到权的情形与Haagerup问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一作者与Weaver十年前提出的问题：von Neumann代数上的态在正交投影上可数可加是否等价于序列弱*连续（序列正规），此前未在ZFC中解决；同时希望将结果推广到权并处理Haagerup问题1.11。

Method: 运用1970年代直接积分理论的思想，证明sigma-有限von Neumann代数乘积上态的重整化（disintegration）定理；构造适配给定序列的分解，将连续性断言化归为经典控制收敛定理；论证不依赖可分性假设。

Result: 在ZFC中证明该等价性（包括无可分性假设）；给出Gleason定理的新变体，刻画无type I_2直和项的任意von Neumann代数上的可数可加投影测度；对权的情形，在强或严格半有限时解决Haagerup问题，或对特定类别的von Neumann代数无限制解决。

Conclusion: 主结果统一处理了态与权的序列正规性，并给出多个应用；后续工作将进一步应用于量子测度与积分理论，如von Neumann代数上Lebesgue控制收敛定理的变体。

Abstract: We first solve in ZFC a problem that the first author and Weaver posed ten years ago: a state on a von Neumann algebra is countably additive on orthogonal projections if and only if it is sequentially weak* continuous, equivalently, sequentially normal. Such states may be considered the quantum (countably additive) probability measures, or rather their extension to a noncommutative integral. The principal tools for this are known ideas from direct integral theory from the 1970s. Indeed we prove a disintegration theorem for states on products of sigma-finite von Neumann algebras. A decomposition adapted to a given sequence reduces the continuity assertion in our main result to the classical dominated convergence theorem. The argument needs no separability hypothesis. We give several applications. For example we give a new variant of Gleason theorem, describing the countably additive projection measures for any von Neumann algebra with no type $I_2$ direct summand. We then turn to the weight case, discussing sequentially normal weights and the famous related Haagerup's Problem 1.11, giving some partial results (which may conceivably may turn out to be best possible in a certain sense). For example we solve Haagerup's problem if our weight is strongly or strictly semifinite, or with no restrictions for certain classes of von Neumann algebras. We give several applications of our main result to weights. In forthcoming work we consider many applications to `quantum measure and integration theory', such as variants of Lebesgue's dominated convergence theorem for von Neumann algebras.

</details>
